### [1] [The Role Of Recurrent Refinement In Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09436)
*Joao D. S. Marques,David Calhas,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在前馈分割骨干上附加轻量循环细化模块（CRF、现代Hopfield网络、卷积GRU），验证测试时迭代加深可提升噪声与小样本条件下的分割鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈分割网络在样本少、存在成像伪影的临床受限场景下性能退化；人类视觉因皮层循环与反馈连接而保持鲁棒，故作者希望用循环机制提升分割网络的鲁棒性。

Method: 在分割骨干后接轻量循环细化模块：条件随机场（CRF）、现代Hopfield网络与卷积GRU；在测试时增加迭代次数（包括超出训练时迭代视野），考察其行为。在开源数据集Kvasir-SEG与ARCADE上评估。

Result: 参数量增加不足1.5%；干净数据上最高提升约1.4个百分点，噪声条件下最高提升约17个百分点，并降低HD95。

Conclusion: 受视觉皮层循环/反馈机制启发的轻量循环细化模块能以极小参数代价显著增强分割网络在噪声与数据受限条件下的鲁棒性，且测试时迭代可超出训练迭代范围继续获益。

Abstract: While feed-forward segmentation networks perform well on large cohorts, they degrade in constrained clinical settings with few training samples and imaging artifacts. Human vision, by contrast, remains robust under these conditions, a property partly attributed to the recurrent and feedback connectivity of the visual cortex, which refines visual information over time. Inspired by this, we append lightweight recurrent refinement blocks to segmentation backbones, namely Conditional Random Fields (CRFs), modern Hopfield networks and convolutional GRUs, and study their behaviour as the number of iterations increases at test time (including beyond the training horizon). Evaluated on two open-source datasets, Kvasir- SEG and ARCADE, our recurrent blocks add less than 1.5% parameters and yield gains on clean data (up to 1.4 pp) and under noise (up to 17 pp) while reducing HD95. Code is available at https://github.com/joaodsmarques/recurrencehelpsegmentation .

</details>


### [2] [Evaluating Anterior Cruciate Ligament Risk of Injury Using a Graph Convolutional Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09724)
*Athanasios Babouras,Paul A. Martineau,Thomas Fevens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种完全运行于智能手机的无监督自编码器模型（多层图卷积网络），利用姿态估计数据评估ACL损伤风险，在DVJ和SLH两项测试上取得约75%–78%的敏感性与特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ACL损伤风险评估依赖昂贵的动作捕捉实验室或专家对专项运动应激测试（如DVJ、SLH）的主观评价，资源成本高、可及性差，亟需一种低成本、可广泛部署的实时筛查方案。

Method: 构建基于自编码器架构的无监督学习模型，编码器/解码器由多层图卷积网络组成，以姿态估计数据（人体关键点图结构）为输入，模型可完全在智能手机端运行，输出受试者的损伤风险状态。

Result: 在两项运动应激测试上均能准确识别高风险运动员：DVJ的平均敏感性与特异性为78%和78%，SLH为75%和74%。

Conclusion: 结果支持开发一种可广泛获取的智能手机端ACL损伤风险实时筛查方案，为缺乏实验室资源的场景提供可行替代。

Abstract: Current solutions for assessing the risk of anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) injury typically rely on expensive motion-capture labs that require substantial resource investment or subjective evaluations of specific athletic stress tests by experts. The Drop Vertical Jump (DVJ) and the Single Leg Hop (SLH) are two examples of such tests typically used to track progress in ACL rehabilitation. In this paper, we develop an unsupervised learning model that runs entirely on a smartphone for two tasks, based on an autoencoder architecture with multiple layers of graph convolutional networks, which utilizes pose estimation data to determine a person's injury risk status. Our model accurately predicts athletes at high risk of injury for both athletic stress tests, with average sensitivities and specificities of 78% and 78% for the DVJ, and 75% and 74% for the SLH. Our findings support the development of a widely accessible smartphone solution for real-time screening of ACL injury risk.

</details>


### [3] [Direct Regression and Geometric Recovery for Event-based Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09717)
*Pruthvi Nandan Janga,Kashyap Hegde Kota,Bharatesh Chakravarthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文对两种独立开发的“合成到真实”事件相机航天器6-DoF位姿估计系统做对比研究：直接回归法在姿态误差上更优，模块化几何法在平移误差上更优，二者呈互补错误分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备高时间分辨率与大动态范围，适合非合作航天器位姿估计；但仅用合成事件流训练的模型需泛化到真实传感器的噪声、背景活动与对比度变化，因此需要系统比较不同技术路线在真实事件数据上的表现。

Method: 在统一评测协议（SPARK 2026 隐藏测试、SPADES 真实事件划分，覆盖全部测试帧）下比较两个系统：其一为模块化流水线（学习式关键点检测＋无标注目标域自适应＋几何位姿恢复＋时序平滑）；其二为直接回归（基于全局与裁剪事件表示、表观尺度线索、物理驱动的合成退化与特征一致性正则，直接回归平移与旋转）。并结合存档配置与一个定性失败案例进行分析。

Result: 聚合挑战分数掩盖了分量层面的反转：直接回归将朝向误差降低22.7%（1.1767→0.9101 rad），综合分数低13.4%；模块化流水线将相对平移误差降低78.7%（0.1335→0.0285）。

Conclusion: 两类方法在旋转与平移上呈现互补的错误特征；存档配置与失败案例仅提示可能的解释，未隔离其成因。据此提出可检验的混合设计：直接估计朝向＋几何约束的平移恢复。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and dynamic range for uncooperative spacecraft pose estimation, but models trained on synthetic event streams must generalize to the noise, background activity, and contrast variation of physical sensors. We present a comparative systems study of two independently developed approaches to synthetic-to-real, event-based 6-DoF spacecraft pose estimation. The first is a modular pipeline that combines learned keypoint detection and unlabeled target-domain adaptation with geometry-based pose recovery and temporal smoothing. The second directly regresses translation and rotation from global and cropped event representations using an apparent-scale cue, physics-motivated synthetic corruptions, and feature-consistency regularization. We evaluate both systems under the same SPARK 2026 hidden-test protocol on the real-event split of SPADES, with predictions for all test frames. The results reveal a component-wise reversal concealed by the aggregate challenge score: direct regression reduces orientation error by 22.7% (1.1767 to 0.9101 rad) and obtains a 13.4% lower composite score, whereas the modular pipeline reduces relative translation error by 78.7% (0.1335 to 0.0285). Archived configurations and a qualitative failure case identify plausible explanations for these complementary error profiles, without isolating their causes. These findings motivate a testable hybrid design that combines direct orientation estimation with geometry-constrained translation recovery.

</details>


### [4] [CRTrack-Cap: A New Benchmark for Cross-View Dense Video Object Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09617)
*Xiangqun Zhang,Likai Wang,Zekun Qian,Rui Zhou,Ruize Han,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出跨视角密集视频目标描述任务（CVDVOC），构建CRTrack-Cap数据集并提出融合VECA与CEG的基线框架，实现跨相机轨迹关联与统一描述，并提出CV-CHOTA评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集视频目标描述（DVOC）方法对每个相机视角独立处理，对同一人物在不同相机下生成各自独立的相机局部轨迹片段与描述，导致描述不统一，且未能利用多视角间的互补外观证据。

Method: 形式化跨视角DVOC（CVDVOC）任务：将相机局部轨迹片段关联为场景级全局轨迹，并基于同步视角为每条轨迹生成单一描述。基于CRTrack构建CRTrack-Cap数据集（审核文本-身份对应关系，将可用指代表达转为规范描述），含13个场景、425条全局轨迹、838条局部轨迹片段、335个身份的规范描述。将CHOTA改造为跨视角的CV-CHOTA指标。提出参考框架：VECA（视角互斥的跨相机关联）用于轨迹片段分组，CEG（冲突证据门控）依据跨视角可靠性与分歧度整合描述证据。

Result: 与三种经MvMHAT改造为跨视角关联的单视角方法相比，所提框架在场景不相交的域内测试和跨域测试上CV-CHOTA分别为83.43和33.48。

Conclusion: CRTrack-Cap与所提框架共同为跨相机视角一致描述同一人物提供了基准数据集和强基线。

Abstract: Dense video object captioning (DVOC) localizes, tracks, and describes object trajectories within a video. Existing methods process each camera view independently, producing separate camera-local tracklets and captions for the same person across cameras. These outputs lack a unified description and leave complementary appearance evidence across views unused. We therefore formulate cross-view DVOC (CVDVOC), which associates these tracklets into scene-level global trajectories and generates one caption for each trajectory from synchronized views. To support this task, we construct CRTrack-Cap from CRTrack by auditing text-identity links and converting usable referring expressions into canonical captions. It retains 425 global trajectories and 838 local tracklets across 13 scenes, with canonical captions for 335 identities. We adapt CHOTA to the cross-view setting as CV-CHOTA to jointly evaluate tracking and caption quality. Our reference framework combines View-Exclusive Cross-Camera Association (VECA) for grouping tracklets with Conflict-Evidence Gating (CEG) for integrating caption evidence using its reliability and disagreement across views. Against three single-view methods adapted with MvMHAT for cross-view association, our framework achieves CV-CHOTA scores of 83.43 and 33.48 on the scene-disjoint in-domain and cross-domain tests, respectively. Together, CRTrack-Cap and our framework provide a benchmark and a strong baseline for describing the same person consistently across camera views.

</details>


### [5] [MSR-ABC: Complementary Feature Fusion for Clear Cell Renal Cell Carcinoma Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09583)
*Yuan Liang,Sourav Bhattacharjee,Abraham Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全局-局部框架MSR-ABC，融合MedVAE、MedSigLIP与放射组学表征用于CT图像上透明细胞肾细胞癌(ccRCC)分类，内部AUROC 0.806、外部0.879。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT图像分类ccRCC可从多视角肿瘤表型表征中获益，但简单拼接重建特征、图文嵌入与放射组学描述符无法充分利用其互补信息，需结构化的融合机制。

Method: 构建全局-局部框架：空间匹配的局部图像描述符经跨表征注意力交互；放射组学辅助监督约束池化后的局部表征保留定量肿瘤属性；门控残差机制将局部信息与全局摘要整合；配合区域感知池化及视觉/软文本提示。

Result: 在396例KiTS23（患者级划分，3个训练种子均值与95%患者bootstrap置信区间）内部AUROC 0.806，111例TCGA/TCIA外部队列0.879；内部较全局MedVAE-放射组学与三源拼接分别提升3.35与3.61个百分点；去除任一来源在兩队列均降性能（放射组学去除降幅最大）；单独去除跨表征注意力、辅助监督或门控融合亦降低内部AUROC。

Conclusion: 结果与集成架构内各来源的互补贡献一致，支持进一步研究结构化表征融合用于无创肾肿瘤表征。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) classification from computed tomography (CT) may benefit from representations that capture different aspects of tumour phenotype. However, simply concatenating reconstruction-derived features, image--text embeddings, and radiomic descriptors may not fully exploit their complementary information. We propose MSR-ABC, a global--local framework integrating MedVAE, MedSigLIP, and radiomics. Spatially matched local image descriptors interact through cross-representation attention, while radiomic auxiliary supervision encourages the pooled local representation to retain quantitative tumour attributes. A gated residual mechanism integrates local information with global summaries. Area-aware pooling and visual and soft-text prompting complete the framework. We evaluate discrimination using a patient-level split of 396 KiTS23 cases and an external cohort of 111 TCGA/TCIA cases, reporting mean AUROC over three training seeds with 95% patient-bootstrap confidence intervals. MSR-ABC achieves mean AUROCs of 0.806 internally and 0.879 externally. Internally, it exceeds global MedVAE--radiomics and three-source concatenation by 3.35 and 3.61 AUROC percentage points, respectively. Removing any source lowers mean AUROC in both cohorts, with the largest decreases following radiomics removal. Individual removal of cross-representation attention, auxiliary supervision, or gated fusion also lowers internal mean AUROC. These findings are consistent with complementary contributions within the integrated architecture and support further investigation of structured representation fusion for non-invasive renal tumour characterisation.

</details>


### [6] [ExQuery: Explicit Structured Query Priors for Temporal 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09527)
*Honglin Sun,Yifei Zhang,Junyang Zhang,Kenji Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ExQuery显式结构化查询框架：将每个解码查询划分为共享语义上下文切片与中心/尺寸/朝向/速度等物理状态切片，并贯穿编码、分支回归与时序查询重建全过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稀疏查询的3D检测器把语义、几何与运动等异质信息编码进统一的隐式查询（即便使用显式3D锚点也是如此），导致信息耦合、结构不可解释。

Method: 将查询显式分解为共享语义切片与各物理状态专属切片，并在编码端到解码端的分支特定回归中保持该结构；时序重建时用自车运动变换预测的物理状态、用分类分数构造软语义先验，再通过门控仿射调制与存储的潜在实例特征融合。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上取得有竞争力的检测性能，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式结构化查询设计能在不牺牲性能的前提下解耦异质信息并有效支持时序查询重建，具备良好的可解释性与模块化优势。

Abstract: Sparse query-based 3D detectors commonly encode heterogeneous semantic, geometric, and motion information into unified latent queries, including when explicit 3D anchors are used. We introduce ExQuery, an explicit structured query framework for temporal 3D object detection. Each decoded query is organized into a shared semantic context slice and physical-state-specific slices for center, dimensions, orientation, and velocity. This structure is preserved from prior encoding to branch-specific regression and is reused to reconstruct temporal queries: predicted physical states are transformed by ego motion, classification scores form soft semantic priors, and the reconstructed query is fused with the stored latent instance feature through gated affine modulation. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that our method achieves competitive detection performance, and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our proposed modules.

</details>


### [7] [GlobalPuzzle: Globally Informed Genetic Search for Eroded Jigsaw Puzzle Reassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09077)
*Lunyan Wang,Chuanzhi Xu,Ziyuan Tao,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GlobalPuzzle框架，将全局画布级评估、动作引导与遗传搜索相结合，用于拼图重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GAP基准上，现有方法对5×5拼图的完美拼装准确率仅0.3%，远低于人类。人类通过识别潜在相邻拼块、评估当前拼装状态并迭代执行添加、替换、整体移动等复杂操作求解；而现有方法存在根本局限：利用全局视觉信息的方法直接预测拼块布局，难以显式执行此类迭代操作；基于搜索的方法虽可重排拼块，却主要依赖成对边界相容性，缺乏全局视觉信息来评估和引导搜索。

Method: GlobalPuzzle框架融合三项要素：全局画布级评估、动作引导（action guidance）与遗传搜索（genetic search），把全局视觉信息引入候选拼装方案的评估与优化过程。

Result: 通过引入全局视觉信息，可缓解仅凭不规则侵蚀边界难以判定的邻接歧义，实现灵活且具全局信息的重建（摘要未给出具体定量指标）。

Conclusion: 将全局视觉评估与可迭代的搜索式重排相结合是弥合人与机器拼图能力差距的有效途径，为受损文物类不规则边界拼图重组提供了新思路。

Abstract: Jigsaw puzzle reassembly remains a highly challenging task, particularly when piece boundaries are irregularly eroded to simulate damage to cultural artifacts. On the GAP benchmark, existing methods achieve a perfect assembly accuracy of only 0.3% on $5 \times 5$ puzzles, whereas humans can solve jigsaw puzzles with remarkably high accuracy. Humans typically approach puzzle solving by identifying potentially adjacent pieces, evaluating the current assembly, and iteratively performing complex operations such as adding pieces, replacing incorrectly placed pieces, and moving entire connected groups of pieces. However, existing methods face a fundamental limitation: approaches that leverage global visual information typically predict piece arrangements directly, making it difficult to explicitly perform such complex iterative operations, while search based methods can rearrange pieces but primarily rely on pairwise boundary compatibility, lacking global visual information to evaluate and guide the search process. To bridge this gap, we propose GlobalPuzzle, a jigsaw puzzle reassembly framework that integrates global canvas level evaluation, action guidance, and genetic search. By incorporating global visual information into the evaluation and optimization of candidate assemblies, GlobalPuzzle aims to resolve ambiguous adjacency relationships that are difficult to determine from irregularly eroded piece boundaries alone, enabling flexible, globally informed reconstruction.

</details>


### [8] [Video Stitching from Multiple Moving Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09570)
*Shira Ifergane,Roy Amoyal,Shahar Benishay,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MViS，一个轻量级测试时优化框架，实现多自由运动相机的联合多视视频几何对齐并保持时序一致性；同时构建首个多于两相机的标注基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频对齐方法（基于图像拼接或视频稳像）多局限于两相机，或必须假设相机存在固定的线性排序，难以应对多台自由运动、视域交集关系随时间变化的真实场景。

Method: MViS 采用受 spatial transformer 启发的网络，在短时间窗内联合处理所有视角的帧，预测逐视角、逐时刻的单应矩阵。优化分两阶段：几何引导对齐 + 基于外观的精化，两阶段均施加多视角与时序一致性约束；无需场景相关的超参调优，可处理任意且时变的视角相交图，对快速相机运动鲁棒。

Result: 在标准两视视频基准上与SOTA持平或更优；为更复杂场景引入含多于两台自由运动相机的新标注数据集，并建立该设定的首个基准。

Conclusion: MViS 以轻量测试时优化方式突破了双视/线性排序限制，实现多视视频的几何一致对齐，并开辟了多相机视频对齐的新评测方向。

Abstract: We address geometric video alignment for the same scene captured by multiple freely moving cameras. Existing methods, which are based on image stitching or video stabilization, are typically limited to two cameras or must assume the restrictive setting of a fixed linear ordering of the cameras. We propose MViS, a lightweight test-time optimization framework for joint multi-view alignment with temporal consistency. MViS uses a spatial-transformer-inspired net to jointly process frames from all views within a short temporal window and predict per-view, per-time homographies. Optimization proceeds in two stages: geometry-guided alignment followed by appearance-based refinement, with both stages enforcing multi-view and temporal consistency across the sequence. The method requires no scene-specific hyperparameter tuning, handles arbitrary and time-varying view-intersection graphs, and remains robust under rapid camera motion. On a standard two-view video benchmark, MViS matches or surpasses the state of the art. To facilitate evaluation in more complex scenarios, we introduce an annotated dataset with more than two freely moving cameras and establish the first benchmark for this setting. Project page: https://bgu-cs-vil.github.io/MViS-project/ .

</details>


### [9] [Region-aware error triage for medical image segmentation with groupwise conformal risk control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08962)
*Nishi Kanta Paul,Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Hoileong Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RACE-Triage，一种不改变分割掩码的事后误差分流框架：用预测轮廓将图像划分为边界带与非边界区，在两个区域分别施加共形风险控制，从而更好地覆盖远离轮廓的漏检误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割掩码更多是被"复核"而非重绘，因此需知道掩码可能出错的位置。已有学习式误差图能按风险排序像素，但告警像素数量靠经验设定，且用单一阈值校准全部误差像素，会使远离预测轮廓的误差告警不足（漏警）。

Method: 风险网络在骨干无关输入上、基于折外（out-of-fold）预测进行训练。以预测轮廓把每幅图划分为边界带与非边界区域，在两区域内分别做共形风险控制，约束"存在误差时被漏掉的误差比例"的期望上限。在 ISIC 2018 皮肤镜与 BUSI 乳腺超声（无患者标识，假设图像独立）上评估，并做三随机种子、第二骨干、面积匹配启发式对比及外部 PH² 集检验。

Result: 合并（pooled）控制在总体目标上达标，但非边界误差平均漏报 31.8%（ISIC）与 36.4%（BUSI）；分区控制将其降至 19.3% 与 20.9%，尺寸过滤后的假阴性连通域召回率由 0.722→0.818、0.611→0.763。平均告警面积增加 1.6 与 4.9 个百分点，95 分位达 23.5% 与 38.5%。结论在三种子与第二骨干下稳定，面积匹配启发式无法复现。外部 PH² 集上冻结阈值超出两个目标，而用 100 张本地图像重校准后，重复划分的平均值低于目标。

Conclusion: 分区共形校准以可量化的告警面积增加为代价，显著改善非边界误差的覆盖（漏警率下降），且效应稳定、非启发式可比，但阈值存在数据集偏移问题，需少量本地数据重校准。

Abstract: Segmentation masks are increasingly reviewed rather than redrawn, which requires knowing where a mask is likely wrong. Learned error maps rank pixels by risk, but the number of pixels to warn is chosen ad hoc, and calibrating one warning threshold over all error pixels may leave errors far from the predicted contour under-flagged. We propose RACE-Triage, a post-hoc error triage framework that leaves the segmentation mask unchanged. A risk network is trained on out-of-fold predictions from backbone-independent inputs. The predicted contour partitions each image into a boundary band and a non-boundary region, and conformal risk control is applied separately in each region to bound the expected fraction of missed errors among cases with errors there. On ISIC 2018 dermoscopy and BUSI breast ultrasound (where patient identifiers are unavailable, so independence across images is assumed), pooled control met its overall target but missed on average 31.8% and 36.4% of non-boundary errors. Regional control reduced these fractions to 19.3% and 20.9% and raised the recall of size-filtered false-negative components from 0.722 to 0.818 and from 0.611 to 0.763. The mean warned area grew by 1.6 and 4.9 percentage points, and its 95th percentile reached 23.5% and 38.5%. The effect held across three seeds and a second backbone, and area-matched heuristics did not reproduce it. On the external PH$^2$ set, frozen thresholds exceeded both targets, whereas recalibration with 100 local images brought the mean over repeated splits below them. Regional calibration improves non-boundary error coverage at a measured increase in warned area.

</details>


### [10] [MESAM: Enhancing SAM2 for Few-Shot Segmentation through Semantic-Visual Memory Generation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08943)
*Tianyu Zou,Shengwu Xiong,Rui Wu,Ruilin Yao,Yi Rong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出记忆增强的 SAM2 方法 MESAM，通过构建鲁棒的语义记忆以缓解支持-查询外观差异，实现少样本分割新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 SAM2 用于少样本分割（FSS）时，支持集与查询图像间存在显著外观差异，会削弱其记忆匹配过程的有效性。

Method: 系统分析发现基于类别语义信息生成的记忆对外观差异更鲁棒、与 SAM2 更兼容；据此设计 SVMG 模块分别构建查询语义记忆、查询前景视觉记忆与支持自适应视觉记忆，并用 ISMR 模块以细粒度视觉细节增强语义记忆、抑制误激活背景响应。

Result: 在 PASCAL-5^i、COCO-20^i、LVIS-92^i 三个数据集上一致优于现有 FSS 方法，取得新的 SOTA 分割性能。

Conclusion: 以语义记忆为核心的记忆增强策略能有效适配 SAM2 于少样本分割任务，代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to leverage the powerful capabilities of the Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) to tackle few-shot segmentation (FSS) tasks. One major challenge lies in the substantial appearance differences between the support and query images, which may diminish the effectiveness of the memory-matching process in SAM2. Through systematic analysis, we find that the memory generated based on the categorical semantic information is robust to this appearance divergence and is more compatible with SAM2. Based on these observations, we propose a new Memory-Enhanced SAM2 (MESAM) method, which first introduces a Semantic-Visual Memory Generation (SVMG) module to separately construct a query semantic memory, a query foreground visual memory, and a support adaptive visual memory. Then, in our designed Interactive Semantic Memory Refinement (ISMR) module, we further enrich the obtained semantic memory with fine-grained visual details, and suppress its misatcivated background responses. Extensive experiments on the PASCAL-$5^i$, COCO-$20^i$, and LVIS-$92^i$ datasets demonstrate that MESAM consistently outperforms existing FSS methods, achieving new state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Our source code is available at: \href{https://github.com/tianyu-zou/MESAM}{https://github.com/tianyu-zou/MESAM}.

</details>


### [11] [Align3D: Image-Structure Co-Aligned 3D Scene Generation with Observability-Aware Fidelity-Preserving Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08861)
*Chenliang Zhou,Yifan Wang,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Align3D：以单张照片为条件的扩散模型，生成既忠实于图像又物理合理的三维室内布局，通过置信度校准场景图与图像-结构协同对齐实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时满足两点：布局需忠实于单张输入照片，且在不可见视角外仍保持物理合理性（遮挡、支撑关系等未被观测的自由度需要先验补全）。

Method: 从照片推断置信度校准的场景图；训练集合式扩散（set-diffusion）布局去噪器，并引入图像-结构协同对齐：(1) 置信度加权模态融合，用预测置信度平衡图像对齐与场景图对齐；(2) 可观测性感知的保真保持引导，将冲突的场景质量梯度投影并以物体级置信度掩码重加权，使欠观测自由度吸收物理先验而不破坏图像-图匹配。

Result: 在 3D-FRONT 上，图像对齐与场景质量指标均优于近期基线；训练完成后可单次前馈将未见照片映射为布局。

Conclusion: 置信度驱动的多模态融合与可观测性感知引导可有效统一图像保真与物理合理性，并具备单次前馈的实用推理效率。

Abstract: We present Align3D, an image-conditioned diffusion model that generates 3D indoor layouts that are both faithful to a single input photograph and physically plausible beyond the observed view. From the photograph we infer a confidence-calibrated scene graph and train a set-diffusion layout denoiser with image-structure co-alignment where confidence-weighted modality fusion balances image and scene-graph alignment using predicted confidences, while observability-aware fidelity-preserving guidance projects conflicting scene-quality gradients and reweighs them with an object-level confidence mask so under-observed degrees of freedom absorb physical priors without undoing the image-graph match. On 3D-FRONT, Align3D improves image-alignment and scene-quality metrics over recent baselines; once trained, it maps previously unseen photographs to layouts in a single feed-forward pass.

</details>


### [12] [Federated training performs as well as centralized for 3D Latent Diffusion for Diagnosis-Conditioned Brain MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08771)
*Abhijith Shaji,Paul Kurian,Greg Ver Steeg,Paul M. Thompson,Jose-Luis Ambite*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究验证了在联邦学习框架下训练3D条件隐变量生成模型（脑MRI）与集中式训练性能相当，说明联邦训练是构建多站点脑MRI生成模型的可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI生成模型有助于理解脑部疾病并缓解神经影像数据稀缺与不均衡问题，但其训练依赖大规模多站点数据，数据集中共享存在困难，因此需要在不共享数据的前提下进行训练。

Method: 采用联邦学习（数据保留在各站点本地）训练3D条件隐变量生成模型，并与集中式训练（数据汇总至单站点）进行对比；使用三项指标评估：条件保真度（生成MRI在分类任务中的表现）、多样性（以MultiScale-SSIM衡量生成图像与真实数据分布的匹配程度）、真实性（以FID衡量生成图像与真实图像的接近程度）。

Result: 联邦训练与集中式训练在条件保真度、多样性和真实性三项指标上表现相近。

Conclusion: 联邦训练可作为开发多站点脑MRI生成模型的可行方案，在保护数据隐私的同时获得与集中式训练相当的生成质量。

Abstract: Generative models of brain MRI are useful to understand brain conditions, and to enrich scarce and imbalanced neuroimaging data. However, training these models requires large, multi-site training sets, which is challenging. Federated learning (FL) allows for training deep learning models without sharing data. We show that training a 3D conditional latent model federatedly (where data is kept private at each site) performs similarly to centralized training (where all the data have been copied to a single site). We evaluate three metrics: conditional fidelity (how well generated MRIs perform in classification), diversity (how the generated images match the real data distribution, using MultiScale-SSIM), and realism (how close generated images are to real images, using Fréchet Inception Distance). Our results show that federated training is a viable route to develop multi-site generative models of brain MRI.

</details>


### [13] [BEVCast: Path-Adaptive BEV for Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08631)
*Qiren Wang,Mingyu Guo,Zixuan Wang,Weisong Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEVCast 提出一种分辨率随预测行驶路径自适应的两阶段 BEV 表示：先以 4.096 m 粗网格编码全场景，再由路径热力头选出高热度单元在固定预算下细化为 1.024 m，形成混合分辨率 BEV 供轨迹头使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机 BEV 模型对整个稠密网格均等分配计算，但车辆在规划时域内仅经过极少单元，存在显著计算冗余；同时方法希望摆脱 HD 地图与导航指令依赖。

Method: 两阶段自适应分辨率：粗通道在 4.096 m 网格编码全场景；基于记录路径训练的热力头为每个粗单元打分；固定预算下取热度最高的单元细化为 1.024 m，其余保持粗分辨率；轨迹头读取覆盖全场景的混合分辨率 BEV，无需 HD 地图与导航命令。

Result: 与相同架构和训练方案的 1.024 m 全网格基线相比，10% 细化预算下仅编码 28% 的 BEV 查询、BEV 编码时间为 66%（batch=1）。nuScenes 上平均位移误差 1.078 m vs 1.149 m，可行驶区域违规率 1.46% vs 2.29%；NAVSIM navtest 上误差 0.577 m vs 0.598 m，EPDMS 82.51 vs 81.28。在无导航指令条件下达到已发表规划器的可比误差。

Conclusion: 驾驶 BEV 的分辨率可以随任务而非随网格均匀分布，在保持全场景覆盖的同时显著降低 BEV 计算量并提升精度与安全性指标。

Abstract: Camera-based driving models build a dense bird's-eye-view (BEV) grid and spend the same computation on every cell of it, although the vehicle crosses only a few of those cells within the planning horizon. We present BEVCast, a two-stage BEV whose resolution adapts to the predicted driving path. A coarse pass encodes the whole scene on a grid of 4.096 m cells. A heat head trained on the recorded path assigns a heat to every coarse cell and the cells with the highest heat are refined into 1.024 m cells under a fixed budget. The remaining cells keep their coarse resolution, so the trajectory head reads a mixed-resolution BEV that still covers the entire scene and needs neither an HD map nor a navigation command. Against a full grid of 1.024 m cells with the same architecture and training recipe, BEVCast at a 10% refinement budget encodes 28% of the BEV queries and needs 66% of the BEV encoder time at batch size 1. On nuScenes its mean displacement error is 1.078 m against 1.149 m and its drivable area violation rate is 1.46% against 2.29%. On NAVSIM navtest its mean displacement error is 0.577 m against 0.598 m and its extended predictive driver model score is 82.51 against 81.28. Next to published planners BEVCast reaches a comparable error on both benchmarks without a navigation command. The resolution of a driving BEV can therefore follow the task instead of the grid.

</details>


### [14] [Bootstrap Your Own AV-Proxies: Adaptive Contrastive and Prototype Learning for Audio-Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09191)
*Junbo Zhang,Hang Su,Zhaofan Li,Hang Dong,Chao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出BYOAVP框架用于音视频分割（AVS），通过自监督音频增强（SSAE）对比学习对齐音视频表征缩小语义鸿沟，并用动态原型约束（DPC）优化像素级类别感知；在两个基准、六个子任务上取得SOTA，鲁棒性与泛化性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVS任务受限于单模态语义不完整及音视频表征间的语义鸿沟；传统特征融合解码难以有效抑制融合噪声，而引入数据依赖先验的近期方法增加了音视频相关性建模复杂度，导致跨域泛化能力差。

Method: 提出BYOAVP自适应对比与原型学习框架：1) 自监督音频增强（SSAE）模块，引入对比学习，使音频表征与梯度阻断的视觉嵌入自适应对齐，缩小模态语义鸿沟；2) 动态原型约束（DPC）模块，基于动量更新的原型细化像素级类别感知，并通过跨模态交互增强发声区域定位。

Result: 在两个AVS基准和六个子任务上均达到SOTA性能，并表现出较强的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过对比对齐与动态原型约束协同建模，可在抑制融合噪声、降低建模复杂度的同时提升AVS的精度与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Segmentation (AVS) aims to accurately segment sounding objects in video frames by leveraging audio-visual correspondence cues. However, it remains challenging due to the intrinsic semantic incompleteness within a single modality and the semantic gap between audio and visual representations. Traditional feature-fusion decoding approaches struggle to suppress fusion noise effectively, while recent methods that incorporate data-dependent priors often increase the complexity of modeling audio-visual correlations, leading to poor cross-domain generalization. To address these issues, we propose a novel adaptive contrastive and prototype learning framework, BYOAVP, for AVS. Specifically, we design a Self-Supervised Audio Enhancement (SSAE) module that introduces contrastive learning to adaptively align audio representations with gradient-blocked visual embeddings, thus narrowing the semantic gap between modalities. Furthermore, a Dynamic Prototype Constraint (DPC) module is developed to refine pixel-wise category perception via momentum-based prototype updating, while enhancing localization of sounding regions through cross-modal interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves SOTA performance across two AVS benchmarks and six sub-tasks, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization ability.

</details>


### [15] [FIND: Frequency Invariance Disentanglement for Test-Time Adaptation in LiDAR 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09170)
*Dongxiao Li,Zixuan Hu,Ling-Yu Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FIND框架，在频域中解耦域不变内容与域特定干扰，用于LiDAR 3D目标检测的测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L3OD在传感器配置与环境变化下存在域偏移；现有TTA方法多在空间域做自监督，受点云稀疏、非欧几里得特性影响，易将语义内容与域风格混淆。

Method: 基于B样条拟合构建局部自适应滤波器，嵌入双流架构：Invariant Stream提取鲁棒特征引导伪标签生成；Specific Stream对域扰动施加一致性约束；结合不变性驱动对齐与稳定性引导正则化。

Result: 在跨数据集自适应与鲁棒性损坏基准上显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 频域解耦可有效提取域不变特征并抑制域特定干扰，为L3OD的TTA提供了新范式；代码已开源。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D Object Detection (L3OD) is a fundamental 3D perception task, yet its performance is often compromised by domain shifts arising from diverse sensor configurations and environmental conditions. Existing Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods primarily leverage self-supervision in the spatial domain, yet they frequently confound semantic content with domain style due to the sparse, non-Euclidean nature of point clouds. To overcome this limitation, we propose FIND (Frequency INvariance Disentanglement), a novel TTA framework that precisely disentangles domain-invariant content from domain-specific interference within the frequency domain. Our methodology utilizes B-Spline fitting to construct locally adaptive filters integrated into a dual-stream architecture: an Invariant Stream extracts robust features to guide pseudo-labeling, while a Specific Stream enforces consistency against domain perturbations. By combining invariance-driven alignment with stability-guided regularization, our approach dynamically extracts robust domain-invariant features while suppressing domain-specific interference. Extensive experiments on cross-dataset adaptation and robust corruption benchmarks demonstrate that FIND significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/llldx03/FIND.

</details>


### [16] [TouchAnnotator: Multi-Source Tactile Supervision with Privileged-View Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09129)
*Ao Li,Zhihao Yuan,Chubin Zhang,Kunrui Huang,Sheng Xu,Zengjue Chen,Junjie Wang,Jiawei Li,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TouchAnnotator：仅用第一人称视频推理的双臂触觉标注器，通过多源触觉监督与特权视角蒸馏生成稠密触觉标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频可大规模记录人-物交互，但缺少隐藏接触与压力的触觉标签，难以直接用于触觉标注。

Method: 构建规范手部场（canonical hand field），统一6个数据集、3种具身的手套压力、几何推导的人体接触与机器人指尖形变；用源感知掩码保证目标有效性。训练时由EMA教师观察同步自我视角与腕部视角，将手局部表征与触觉预测蒸馏给学生；学生推理仅需自我视频与估计位姿，并用紧凑的过去片段记忆提供时序上下文。

Result: 在EgoTouch、零样本FPHA迁移及860段同步手套录制上评测；可迁移至裸手接触，并在裸手、戴手套手与机器人具身上产生定性一致的触觉响应；实测录制上时序准确率提升5.38点，Volumetric IoU提升5.02点。

Conclusion: 异构的人-机器人监督与特权训练视角可将第一人称视频转化为稠密触觉标注。

Abstract: Egocentric video captures human-object interaction at scale but rarely includes tactile labels for hidden contact and pressure. We introduce TouchAnnotator, an ego-only bimanual tactile annotator trained with multi-source tactile supervision and privileged-view distillation. A canonical hand field unifies glove pressure, geometry-derived human contact, and robot fingertip deformation from six datasets and three embodiments, while source-aware masks preserve valid targets. During training, an EMA teacher observes synchronized ego and wrist views and distills hand-local representations and tactile predictions into a student that uses only ego video and estimated pose at inference. Compact past-clip memory supplies temporal context. We evaluate on EgoTouch, zero-shot FPHA transfer, and 860 synchronized glove recordings. The model transfers to bare-hand contact and produces qualitatively coherent tactile responses across bare-hand, gloved-hand, and robot embodiments. On measured recordings, it improves temporal accuracy by 5.38 points and Volumetric IoU by 5.02 points under a shared protocol. These results show that heterogeneous human--robot supervision and privileged training views can turn egocentric video into dense tactile annotations.

</details>


### [17] [CoMET: Tracking the Shared Pose Error of Roadside Messages for Asynchronous Vehicle-Infrastructure Cooperative 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09121)
*Shuwei Sun,Jian Huang,Hui Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoMET，用无学习参数的卡尔曼滤波在线估计路侧检测的公共平移误差，提升异步车路协同3D检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧传感器可探测自车视野盲区内的车辆，但位姿误差会使同一条消息中所有检测整体平移；且仅38%的验证消息存在匹配对，需在推理时无真值、无未来帧、无逐对标定偏移的条件下仅用决策时可得信息解决问题。

Method: CoMET在每条消息到达时匹配车辆与路侧检测，由互为最近邻的配对估计平移量，并对每个发送方维护卡尔曼滤波；无匹配时用滤波预测值外推，修正应用于全部路侧框（含自车未见目标）；估计器无学习参数，合并后的检测由独立的冻结头排序。

Result: 在21条验证序列、0–400 ms四种延迟下，CoMET平均BEV AP@IoU0.7为66.63，优于未校正在线位姿的63.18与最佳中间融合基线的62.96；在三种架构五个检测器上较在线位姿提升2.2–3.5 AP，较仅用当前消息估计提升0.3–0.6；并发现基准中28%消息以零偏移作占位、其余45%偏移搜索触及边界。

Conclusion: CoMET在无真值条件下稳健校正位姿误差，在两种替代标签参考下均优于未校正在线位姿；用CoMET位姿与校正标签重训练中间融合，在三种架构上均提升检测，其中两种架构增益统计显著。

Abstract: A roadside sensor can detect vehicles hidden from the ego vehicle, but a pose error shifts every detection in the same message. We study asynchronous vehicle-infrastructure cooperative 3D detection on V2X-Seq using only information available at decision time, without ground truth, future frames, or per-pair calibration offsets at inference. Objects seen by both sensors reveal the shared translation, but even at zero added delay, 38% of validation messages have no matched pair. We propose CoMET (Common-Mode Error Tracking), which estimates this translation over time. At each message arrival, CoMET matches vehicle and roadside detections, estimates the offset from mutually nearest pairs, and updates a Kalman filter for that sender. When no pair matches, the filter carries its estimate forward. The correction applies to all roadside boxes, including those unseen by the ego vehicle. The estimator has no learned parameters; a separate frozen head ranks the merged detections. Across four delays from 0 to 400 ms on 21 validation sequences, CoMET averages 66.63 bird's-eye-view AP at IoU 0.7, compared with 63.18 for the same late fusion using uncorrected online poses and 62.96 for the best intermediate-fusion baseline. Across five detectors from three architectures, CoMET gains 2.2 to 3.5 AP points over online poses and 0.3 to 0.6 over estimating the offset from the current message alone. We find that the benchmark uses a zero offset as a placeholder in 28% of validation messages and that its offset search reaches the boundary in 45% of the rest. These offsets affect the labels for objects seen only from the roadside. Under two alternative label references, CoMET improves on uncorrected online poses in every evaluated population. Retraining intermediate fusion with CoMET poses and corrected labels improves detection on all three architectures tested, with statistically significant gains on two.

</details>


### [18] [Finite-sample Calibration Guarantees for Binary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09120)
*Bruno Viti,Martin Holler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对分割模型置信度失准问题，提出一种具有形式化校准保证的事后校准方法，并给出显式考虑分割输出空间依赖性的改进理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习分割模型普遍存在校准不良问题：像素级预测的置信度无法准确反映真实概率，这在医学图像分割等高风险场景中尤为关键。现有校准方法中仅有少数能提供校准误差的理论保证（即以高概率保证低校准误差）。

Method: 提出一种事后（post-hoc）校准方法，从标准集中不等式（concentration inequalities）出发，推导出显式刻画二值分割输出中空间依赖性的改进理论界，从而使该方法具备形式化校准保证。

Result: 实验表明所提校准器满足所推导的保证，且性能优于现有事后校准方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为分割模型的置信度校准提供了可证明的理论保障，并实证验证了其在精度与保证一致性上均优于现有事后校准方案。

Abstract: Machine learning based segmentation models are frequently miscalibrated, producing for each pixel a label prediction whose associated confidence does not accurately reflect the true underlying probability. This limitation is particularly critical in high-stakes applications such as medical image segmentation. Although several calibration methods have been proposed, only a small subset of them provides theoretical guarantees on the calibration error, i.e., they do not typically ensure that, with high probability, the model exhibits a low calibration error. In this work, we develop a post-hoc calibration method for which formal calibration guarantees can be established. To achieve this, we derive from standard concentration inequalities an improved theoretical bound that explicitly accounts for the spatial dependence inherent in binary segmentation outputs. We empirically demonstrate that the proposed calibrator satisfies the derived guarantee and outperforms existing post-hoc calibration approaches.

</details>


### [19] [AdaAlloc: Adaptive Visual Token Allocation for Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09092)
*Joungbin An,Kristen Grauman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaAlloc将长视频问答重构为视觉token分配问题，提出免训练推理框架，自适应地在全局概览与高分辨率局部证据之间分配视觉token，在多个基准上超越预算匹配的均匀采样最多10.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答要求多模态大模型在严格的视觉token预算下推理，存在"广泛时间覆盖"与"细粒度空间细节"之间的权衡：均匀采样覆盖面广却掩盖关键局部线索，基于字幕或帧相似度的检索依赖有损代理，易漏掉运动、状态变化和前后关系。

Method: 提出AdaAlloc免训练推理框架：先规划全局/局部token分配策略；再用时间定位（temporal grounding）定位候选证据片段并精炼为紧凑的时间记忆；所选策略同时控制帧采样密度与分辨率，即用稀疏低分辨率全局帧搭配从精炼记忆中提取的更密、更高分辨率的局部帧；最终回答器在分配好的全局上下文与精炼局部证据上推理。

Result: 在LVBench、MLVU、VideoMME和LongVideoBench上，相比预算匹配的均匀采样最多提升10.5个百分点，并在所报告基准上优于对比的帧选择方法与agentic方法。

Conclusion: 以视觉token分配视角重新刻画长视频推理，通过全局/局部自适应分配结合时间定位，可在固定预算下兼顾覆盖与细节，显著提升长视频问答性能。

Abstract: Long-video question answering forces multimodal large language models to reason under strict visual-token budgets, creating a tradeoff between broad temporal coverage and fine-grained spatial detail. Uniform sampling preserves coverage but often obscures decisive local cues, while caption-based or frame-similarity retrieval operates over lossy proxies that can miss motion, state changes, and before-after relations. We recast long-video reasoning as a visual-token allocation problem: given a fixed budget, decide how much to spend on global context versus high-resolution local evidence, and where in the video to draw that local evidence from. We introduce AdaAlloc, a training-free inference framework that adaptively allocates visual tokens between a global video overview and localized visual detail. AdaAlloc first plans a global/local allocation policy, then uses temporal grounding to locate candidate evidence segments and refines them into a compact temporal memory. The selected policy controls both frame sampling density and resolution, combining sparse, lower-resolution global frames with denser, higher-resolution local frames drawn from the refined memory. The final answerer reasons over the allocated global context and refined local evidence. Across LVBench, MLVU, VideoMME, and LongVideoBench, AdaAlloc improves over budget-matched uniform sampling by up to 10.5 percentage points and outperforms the compared frame-selection and agentic methods on their reported benchmarks.

</details>


### [20] [Seeing vs. Counting: Complexity Twins Show Where Multimodal LLMs Fail on Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08998)
*Yin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过在同一张图上配对不同计数复杂度类别的计数题（如生成树数=行列式 vs 简单路径数=#P-完全），并加入"转录探针"和纯文本对照，将多模态LLM计数失败解耦为"看错"与"算错"两个来源；发现CoT下Gemini等模型多能正确读图却在计数上失败，且失败率主要由答案数量级而非复杂度类别决定；给定代码与精确转录后大网格亦能算对，此时瓶颈转为读图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉图/puzzle基准同时改变视觉密度与算法难度，导致"看图失败"与"计数失败"混杂，无法判断模型究竟错在哪一环。作者希望在受控条件下分离视觉读取与算法计算两个误差来源。

Method: 控制变量设计：对同一幅图提出属于不同计数复杂度类的计数问题（生成树数=行列式、简单路径数=#P-完全），固定输入图形；引入转录探针（让模型只输出图的文本表示）与纯文本对照（直接给文本图），以区分读取与计算；测试Gemini、Gemma、Qwen3-VL；并在给定代码+精确转录条件下考察大规模网格（含10×10）与旋转副本+裁剪校验的二次读取策略。

Result: CoT下多数Gemini模型能正确读取小图但对计数失败；精确作答的几率随答案数量级每增加一个十进制位下降约2.38倍（主模型），而复杂度类别影响较小。给定代码与精确转录后，主模型即使在10×10网格上也能正确计数；此时读取成为瓶颈：单次观察会漏读水平边，对旋转副本进行带裁剪校验的二次读取可达86/96大网格完全正确，而仅重复正向读取为57/96。

Conclusion: 多模态LLM的图计数失败主要不是"看不见"，而是计算环节的失效，且受答案规模主导；当计算被代码外包后，错误转移到视觉读取侧，可通过多视角/旋转+裁剪校验的重复读取显著缓解。该配对设计为诊断多模态推理瓶颈提供了可复用的实验范式。

Abstract: When a multimodal LLM miscounts in a diagram, does it fail at seeing or at counting? Existing visual graph and puzzle benchmarks vary visual density and algorithmic hardness together, confounding the two. We pair counting questions about the same drawing that sit in different counting-complexity classes, such as its spanning trees (a determinant) and its simple paths (#P-complete), and use a transcription probe and a text-only control to separate reading from computing. We test Gemini, Gemma and Qwen3-VL models; for the Gemini models, the answer depends on how the model computes. In chain of thought, most of them read small drawings correctly and then fail at counting, at a rate set far more by the size of the answer than by its complexity class (for the primary model, the odds of an exact answer fall 2.38-fold per decade of the answer). Given code and an exact transcription, the primary model counts correctly even on 10x10 grids, and reading becomes the bottleneck: a single view misses horizontal edges, and a second reading of a rotated copy with crop checks reads 86/96 large grids exactly, against 57/96 when the upright reading is simply repeated.

</details>


### [21] [LEF-JEPA: Denoising-Trained Latent Energy Fields for Spatial Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08989)
*Zhiyuan Liu,Zhihao Xu,Sicheng Liu,Huanhuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEF-JEPA通过目标引导的隐空间复原学习空间结构，并用相对能量约束将该监督传递给视觉编码器，能量模块仅用于预训练、无推理开销；同时构造条件Ising哈密顿量以便专用求解器进行低能态搜索。在统一的CIFAR-10预训练与冻结编码器评测下，该方法在分类、前景分割和视觉规划上均优于V-JEPA2.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅在被掩码位置施加预测监督的JEPA难以在隐表征中清晰捕捉物体形状与边界；而保留更多低层纹理与颜色信息并不必然提升表征对预测与动作任务的适配性。核心问题是：如何在不"过度编码"细节的前提下强化支撑预测与动作的空间结构。

Method: 提出LEF-JEPA：1）通过目标引导的隐空间复原（target-guided latent recovery）学习空间结构；2）以相对能量约束将上述监督迁移至视觉编码器；3）能量模块仅用于预训练，不引入推理开销；4）为降低能量优化代价，构造条件Ising哈密顿量，使低能态搜索可由专用Ising求解器完成，实现更少局部更新与更低前向延迟。

Result: 在物理硬件上验证了以更少局部更新实现更低前向延迟的可行性；在统一的CIFAR-10预训练与冻结编码器评测协议下，两种实现均在图像分类、前景分割和视觉规划任务上超过V-JEPA2.1。

Conclusion: 将空间结构监督置于隐空间复原并经由相对能量约束回传，可在避免过度编码低层细节的同时提升表征对预测与动作任务的适配性；能量优化可借助Ising硬件加速，且不增加部署阶段成本。

Abstract: A joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) that applies prediction supervision only at masked positions struggles to clearly capture object shapes and boundaries in its latent representations. We further observe that some existing methods improve spatial structure while also retaining rich variations in texture and color. However, preserving more low-level visual information does not necessarily make representations better suited to prediction and action. This raises a key question: how can we strengthen the spatial structure in latent representations that supports prediction and action without over-encoding these details? We introduce LEF-JEPA, which learns spatial structure through target-guided latent recovery and transfers this supervision to the visual encoder through a relative-energy constraint; the energy module is used only during pretraining and adds no inference overhead. To reduce the cost of energy optimization during pretraining, we further construct a conditional Ising Hamiltonian that allows low-energy-state search to be performed by specialized Ising solvers. On physical hardware, we validate the feasibility of achieving lower forward latency with fewer local updates. Under a unified CIFAR-10 pretraining and frozen-encoder evaluation protocol, both realizations outperform V-JEPA2.1 in image classification, foreground segmentation, and visual planning.

</details>


### [22] [Overview of the ImageCLEFmedical 2026 GANs task: Exploring fingerprint detection, privacy leakage, and synthetic medical image generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09455)
*Alexandra-Georgiana Andrei,Mihai Gabriel Constantin,Mihai Dogariu,Dzmitry Karpenka,Yuri Prokopchuk,Ahmedkhan Radzhabov,Dan-Cristian Stanciu,Liviu-Daniel Ştefan,Vassili Kovalev,Henning Müller,Bogdan Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 概述ImageCLEFmedical GANs 2026任务（第四届），围绕合成医学图像中的隐私与安全问题设置三个子任务：训练数据指纹检测、潜向量反演识别、隐私保护CT生成，共37队注册、10队提交结果，并总结方法与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用患者数据训练GAN生成合成医学图像存在隐私泄露与安全隐患，需系统评估GAN训练数据可追溯性、潜空间可逆性以及隐私保护生成的可行性。

Method: 构建基准数据集（真实与GAN生成的肺部CT切片）并提供标注；设三个子任务：Subtask 1检测哪些真实图像被用于训练给定GAN；Subtask 2由合成图像识别其对应潜向量；Subtask 3生成隐私保护CT切片并权衡保真度与隐私。评测分别采用Cohen's Kappa、匹配准确率、FID与隐私保护分数（PPS）组合。

Result: 37支队伍注册，10支队伍在子任务中提交运行结果，6支队伍提交工作笔记论文；论文汇总了任务设置、数据集、指标及各队方法与结果。

Conclusion: 该基准任务为合成医学图像的隐私与安全风险提供了统一的评测框架，揭示了GAN训练数据可检测性、潜空间可逆性与隐私-保真权衡之间的关系。

Abstract: The 2026 ImageCLEFmedical GANs Task Exploring Fingerprint Detection, Privacy Leakage, and Synthetic Medical Image Generation continues to investigate privacy and security concerns around using patient data to generate synthetic medical images, now in its fourth edition. This edition introduces three complementary subtasks: the first extends prior editions by asking participants to detect which real images were used in training a Generative Adversarial Network to produce given synthetic outputs; the second is a novel subtask requiring participants to identify which latent vector produced each synthetic image, probing the faithfulness and reversibility of the latent-to-image mapping; the third asks participants to generate privacy-preserving CT slices, quantifying the trade-off between image fidelity and patient privacy. Ground-truth annotations and benchmark datasets of real and GAN-generated lung CT slices are provided for all subtasks. Evaluation is based on Cohen's Kappa for Subtask 1, matching accuracy for Subtask 2, and a combination of FID and Privacy Preservation Score (PPS) for Subtask 3. Overall, 37 teams registered for the task, with 10 teams submitting runs across subtasks and 6 teams submitting working notes papers. This paper presents an overview of the task setup, datasets, evaluation metrics, and summarises the approaches and results of participating teams.

</details>


### [23] [PathXAI: Evidence-guided structured pathology reporting by transferable tissue segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09117)
*Zhaorui Tan,Jun Aun Tan,Weixiang Zhong,Xiaoxi Wang,Tan Pan,Xingdong Xue,Li Liu,Jing Zhang,Jing Zhang,Weimiao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PathXAI，将分割得到的组织成分信息与 H&E 图像及可选领域知识结合，引导诊断推理、针对性复核与结构化报告撰写；分割性能优于基线，报告质量在与参考诊断和形态描述的一致性上超过纯 H&E 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理报告需把跨区域、跨切片的组织观察转化为具体诊断及形态学描述，但如何用显式的组织成分测量来指导基于 H&E 的报告生成仍研究不足。

Method: PathXAI 由分割模块与报告生成模块组成：分割提取组织成分信息，与 H&E 图像及可选领域知识共同输入自托管预训练视觉-语言模型（冻结权重、不更新参数），生成带证据链接的报告，支持诊断推理与靶向复核。评估覆盖内部与外部分割测试，以及 1,200 例公开、151 例私有病例的自动报告评价。

Result: 分割模块在内部与外部测试的平均 Dice 均超过基线；报告与参考诊断及记录形态的一致性高于基于 H&E 的对照方法；公开队列中 PathXAI-Report 较 Qwen + H&E 的癌种家族匹配率提升 4.4 个百分点，发现项 F1 提升 5.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式组织成分信息可作为有效中间表示提升 H&E 病理报告生成质量，且无需微调视觉-语言模型即可产出可追溯至证据的报告。

Abstract: Pathology reporting requires translating tissue observations across regions and slides into specific diagnoses and supporting morphological descriptions. However, the use of explicit tissue-composition measurements to guide H&E-based report generation remains underexplored. Thus, we developed PathXAI, which combines segmentation-derived tissue information with H&E images and optional domain knowledge to guide diagnostic reasoning, targeted reinspection and structured report writing. Its segmentation component exceeded baselines in mean Dice on internal and external tests. In automated report evaluation on 1,200 public and 151 private cases, PathXAI achieved higher agreement with reference diagnoses and documented morphology than the H&E-based comparators. On the public evaluation cohort, PathXAI-Report exceeded Qwen + H&E in cancer family match rate by 4.4 percentage points and in finding F1 by 5.7 percentage points. PathXAI generates evidence-linked reports with a self-hosted pretrained vision-language model, without updating its weights.

</details>


### [24] [OmniLatent: Unified Latent for 3D Part Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09055)
*Yunhan Yang,Yunfei Zhao,Zeqiang Lai,Jiachen Xu,Minxiu Wang,Xintong Han,Xiangyu Yue,Xihui Liu,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniLatent 提出统一的潜在建模框架做3D部件分解，联合编码几何、形变、外观与二值部件ID，单图一次前向即生成部件感知3D资产，并可扩展到点提示分割与不完整部件补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D部件分解多为对已完成网格的后处理分割，结果易受几何伪影、纹理歧义影响，且部件级标注稀缺。作者希望让数据丰富的几何/视觉先验反哺数据稀缺的部件学习，而非孤立预测部件标签。

Method: 学习共享隐空间，联合编码几何、形变、外观与二值部件ID；基座模型由单张图像一次生成几何、形变、纹理和表面部件ID，无需独立分割阶段。再对预训练模型做两项微调：(1) 点提示适配器，将带二值部件ID的任意3D点提示映射为latent token，实现全分割、精修与子部件细分；(2) 不完整部件补全模型，通过动态逐部件token分配、对给定几何隐变量的cross-attention以及部件局部/全局注意力交替，将部分表面掩码补全为一致的完整分解。

Result: 大量实验表明，OmniLatent 产生的部件分割语义合理、边界精确，支持交互式部件编辑，并能在保持全局物体一致性的前提下补全部分部件观测。

Conclusion: 将部件分解内嵌于统一潜在生成建模，可借助几何与外观先验缓解部件标注稀缺，形成生成—分割—编辑—补全一体化的3D部件感知资产创建范式。

Abstract: 3D part decomposition is a central capability for editable asset creation, yet existing workflows typically segment a completed mesh as a post-processing step, making the result sensitive to geometric artifacts, texture ambiguity, and limited part-level annotations. We present OmniLatent, a unified latent modeling framework for 3D part decomposition. Instead of predicting part labels in isolation, OmniLatent learns a shared latent space that jointly encodes geometry, deformation, appearance, and binary part IDs, allowing data-rich geometric and visual priors to support data-scarce part learning. Given a single input image, the base model generates geometry, deformation, texture, and surface part IDs in one pass, producing a part-aware 3D asset without a separate segmentation stage. We further tune the pretrained model for two extensions: a point-prompt adapter that maps arbitrary 3D point prompts with binary part IDs into latent tokens for full segmentation, refinement, and part subdivision, and an amodal part completion model that completes partial surface masks into a coherent full-object decomposition through dynamic per-part token allocation, cross-attention to the given geometry latent, and alternating part-local and global attention. Extensive experiments show that OmniLatent produces semantically meaningful part segmentations with precise boundaries, supports interactive part editing, and completes partial part observations while preserving global object consistency.

</details>


### [25] [Did I See It or Think It? Probing Reality Monitoring in Vision-Language Models with Concept Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09014)
*Tina Khezresmaeilzadeh,Seyedarmin Azizi,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出"现实监控"范式的注入实验，检验视觉语言模型能否区分内部注入的想法与图像中实际看到的内容。六个VLM中仅Gemma-3-27B能较可靠识别注入概念，但其来源报告脆弱：强注入或视觉通路注入被误报为"看到"，且注入物体最多出现在87%的真实照片描述中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于语言模型内省能力的研究均为纯文本设定，通过向激活注入概念方向并询问模型是否察觉来检验。但视觉语言模型面临认知心理学中的"现实监控"难题：必须将内部生成内容与真实所见区分开。因此需要检验VLM能否区分被注入的概念与图像内容。

Method: 引入独立操控图像与注入概念的实验范式：每个试次中模型需报告所见内容、是否被注入想法、以及该想法是什么。该设计同时对"是否识别出概念"与"将概念归属到哪个来源"进行评分。在六个VLM上进行测试，并进一步在常规图像描述任务中检验注入物体是否会泄漏到对真实照片的描述中。

Result: 仅Gemma-3-27B能较可靠识别注入概念（35% 对 仅steering的3%），且几乎无误报。但其来源报告很脆弱：强注入或视觉通路注入被报告为"看到"，可见物体的存在几乎完全消除了正确的想法报告。同样的失败出现在普通图像描述中，注入物体最多出现在87%的真实照片描述里。

Conclusion: VLM的自我报告不仅可能错误识别内容，还会混淆内容来源。评估多模态自我报告时必须同时检查其内容与所报告的来源，仅凭内容正确性不足以判断内省可靠性。

Abstract: Recent work tests whether language models can introspect by injecting a concept direction into their activations and asking whether they notice it and can name it. These studies are text-only. Vision-language models (VLMs) face an additional problem, known in cognitive psychology as reality monitoring: they must keep internally generated content separate from what they actually see. We therefore ask: can VLMs distinguish injected concepts from image content? We introduce a paradigm that varies the image and the injected concept independently. On each trial, the model reports what it sees, whether a thought was injected, and what that thought is. This design scores both whether the model identifies the concept and which source it assigns to it. Across six VLMs, only Gemma-3-27B reliably identifies injected concepts (35% vs. 3% for steering alone), with almost no false alarms. Even then, the source it reports is fragile. Strong or visual-pathway injections are reported as seen, and a visible object nearly eliminates correct thought reports. The same failure appears in ordinary captioning, where injected objects appear in up to 87% of captions of real photographs. Evaluating multimodal self-reports therefore requires checking both their content and their reported source.

</details>


### [26] [FishSense Lite: A Camera-Based, Single Laser Hardware Framework with Software System for In Situ Fish Length Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08732)
*Christopher L. Crutchfield,Kyle Hu,Nathan Hui,Omair Qazi,Jennifer Loch,Alli Candelmo,Brice X. Semmens,Curt Schurgers,Ryan Kastner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FishSense Lite 利用休闲潜水相机、潜水激光笔和3D打印支架，从单张水下照片中恢复鱼体长度；经公民科学潜水员在野外采集73条鱼的数据，与水下立体成像系统无显著偏差（平均差+0.8%），刚性目标误差在1.3%以内，并提出规范化现场采集协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼类资源评估需要大范围、多地点地将鱼体长度与物种配对，但受训调查人员覆盖范围有限。休闲潜水员虽已到达这些地点，但水下测鱼此前依赖受训观察者或专用设备，成本与门槛高。

Method: 提出 FishSense Lite 系统：潜水员用激光笔对准鱼体并拍照，依据激光光斑在图像中的位置反演目标距离，再由距离将图像中的鱼体像素尺寸换算为实际长度；硬件仅需休闲潜水相机、潜水激光笔与3D打印支架。由系统开发者培训的公民科学项目组织潜水员在七次开放水域潜水中采集数据。通过刚性已知长度目标与水下立体成像系统进行精度与偏差验证，并对误差来源建模以推导现场采集协议。

Result: 在73条野生鱼上完成长度与物种配对采集；与水下立体成像系统对同7条野生鱼的测量无可检测偏差（平均差+0.8%）；刚性标定目标全部恢复误差在1.3%以内，1,055帧中99%落在15%误差预算内。

Conclusion: 建议的现场协议为：每次潜水用潜水记录板进行标定；在距鱼1 m以外、与鱼体夹角15°以内拍摄；每条鱼至少采集10帧图像。该方法使休闲潜水员可低成本、可扩展地提供鱼类长度—物种配对数据。

Abstract: Fish stock assessments need fish lengths paired with species from far more places than trained surveyors can reach. Recreational divers already visit many of those places, but measuring a fish underwater has required trained observers or specialist equipment. We present FishSense Lite, which recovers fish length from a single photograph taken with a recreational dive camera, a dive laser pointer, and a 3D-printed mount. The diver aims the laser at a fish and takes a picture; afterward, the dot's position in the image gives the range to the fish, and the range turns the fish's size in the image into a length. A citizen-science program, trained by the system's builders, taught its own divers and collected lengths paired with species for 73 wild fish across seven open-water dives. On seven wild fish also measured by an underwater stereo-imaging system, the two instruments agreed without detectable bias (mean difference +0.8%). On rigid targets of known length, FishSense Lite recovered every target to within 1.3%, and 99% of 1,055 frames fell within a 15% error budget. From the error sources we measure, we derive a field protocol: calibrate against a dive slate on every dive, photograph fish within 15 degrees of perpendicular and beyond 1 m, and take at least ten frames of each.

</details>


### [27] [Scoring, Not Learning: Decomposing Catalog-Consolidation Gains in Web-Scraped Retail Product Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08655)
*Kaden Leis,Xiang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在16.5万张奶酪网页图像的噪声目录上，把条目规范化到基础类别带来的"提升"主要来自评估标签空间变化（类别粗化占94%），而非识别器本身改善；重新训练与增大骨干网均无显著交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络图像搜索构建的零售商品目录廉价但噪声大：查询返回包装照而非商品本体，条目按无法视觉区分的名称泛滥。整合目录常被视为"扩大模型规模"的替代方案，作者要澄清这实为"改进识别器"与"改变正确性定义"两种不同性质的操作。

Method: 使用165,769张、1,824个查询命名条目的奶酪图像公开档案；经包装过滤与近重复去除得到998类识别集，固定分组感知划分；将条目规范化到基础类型（844类）；对未改动模型采用组内概率求和（canonical group probability aggregation）再评分；并在28M/50M/88M三档骨干上重训练对比，辅以随机合并对照（相同规模剖面）与跨种子统计。

Result: 规范化后top-1提升4.0点；不变模型组内概率求和即得+4.27点（六个种子+4.25），无需重训。在原目录整合后重训仅得-0.10±0.53、+0.08±0.65、-0.10±0.65点，各容量下与零无差异。随机合并仅+0.68点，三个骨干差异<0.1点。37个合并组中类型粗化贡献94%、别名合并仅7%。骨干参数三倍化：原目录+1.50±0.57、规范化目录+1.42±0.44，种子内交互-0.08 [-0.79,+0.63]。

Conclusion: 该档案上报告的"目录整合增益"主要反映评估标签空间的变化（以类别粗化为主），而非识别能力提升；整合与模型规模之间无交互，即不能替代模型扩展。分离这两者只需一次评估代价，提示后续工作须明确区分标签空间效应与模型改进。

Abstract: Retail product catalogs assembled from web image search are cheap to build and pervasively noisy: queries return packaging shots instead of products, and entries proliferate under names that cannot be distinguished visually. Consolidating such a catalog can look like an alternative to scaling the model. We show this is not a comparison between two ways of improving a recognizer, but between improving a recognizer and changing what counts as correct. We study a public archive of 165,769 cheese images across 1,824 query-named entries. A packaging filter and near-duplicate removal reduce it to a 998-class recognition set on one fixed, group-aware split. Canonicalizing entries to their base type (844 classes) raises top-1 accuracy by 4.0 points. Scoring the unchanged model against the merged label space, by summing its predicted probability within each canonical group, recovers +4.27 points with no retraining (+4.25 over six seeds). Retraining on the consolidated catalog then adds -0.10$\pm$0.53, +0.08$\pm$0.65 and -0.10$\pm$0.65 points at 28, 50 and 88 M parameters, indistinguishable from zero at every capacity ($\pm$ is the across-seed standard deviation). That component is specific, not mechanical: random merges with the identical size profile yield only +0.68 points, and the three backbones agree to within 0.1 points. But it is overwhelmingly category coarsening, not alias correction: across the 37 merged groups, type coarsenings supply 94% of the gain and alias collapses only 7%. We also detect no interaction between consolidation and backbone size: tripling backbone parameters is worth +1.50$\pm$0.57 points on the raw catalog and +1.42$\pm$00.44 on the canonical one, a within-seed interaction of -0.08 [-0.79,+0.63]. On this archive, the reported consolidation gain primarily reflects the evaluation label space, and separating that out costs one evaluation

</details>


### [28] [An Anatomy-guided 3D Segmentation Pipeline for Prostate Surgical Phantoms from CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08652)
*Iven Chen,Kuan-I Chung,Ayberk Acar,Susheela Sharma Stern,Alejandro O. Chara,Jie Ying Wu,Daniel Moyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练语料的自动化3D分割流程，利用经典医学图像处理与显式解剖先验，实现前列腺手术体模的可迁移分割与病灶检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人前列腺手术自主化需在体模上快速迭代测试，但体模迭代产生的小规模、不一致数据集使深度学习难以在合理规模下使用，人工标注耗时且解剖不可靠。

Method: 将体模已知解剖结构编码为几何与强度的显式先验，采用经典医学图像处理技术构建自动化3D分割流水线，无需训练语料，可迁移至每个新体模版本而无需重新标注。

Result: 在跨多个体模版本的57次扫描中，11例对比度抑制扫描上囊内区域中位Dice为0.83；32个标注局灶病灶全部检出且无误报；单次扫描2–8分钟（普通CPU）对比人工1–2小时。

Conclusion: 基于先验的经典方法在小样本、快速迭代的体模场景中可替代深度学习和人工标注，为手术自动化系统的规划与性能评估提供高效可靠的分割支持。

Abstract: Full autonomy has been a long-term goal in robotic surgery and a near-to-medium-term goal for prostatectomy. Due to the relatively closed structure of the procedure, its standard-of-care robotic/laparoscopic solutions, and procedure economics, prostate procedures are an optimal early target for surgical automation. Prototyping and testing of possible solutions is done in constructed laboratory phantoms, either synthetic tissues or animal phantoms. To enable measurement of perceptual and robotic motion error, phantoms undergo volumetric imaging; these images are critical to both a potential deployed automatic system, used in planning motion and treatment (cutting, ablation, suturing, etc.), as well as post-hoc assessment of system performance. Rapid iteration on these phantom models yields small, inconsistent datasets, while the current workflow for human annotation is often anatomically unreliable and prohibitively time-consuming, rendering deep learning unsuitable at reasonable scales. To address these limitations, we present an automated 3D segmentation pipeline that uses classical medical image-processing techniques to encode the known anatomy of the phantoms as explicit geometric and intensity priors, which requires no training corpus and is transferred to each new phantom revision without re-annotation. Across 57 scans spanning multiple phantom revisions, our pipeline recovers the capsule interior at 0.83 median Dice on 11 contrast-suppressed scans and detects all 32 annotated focal lesions without false positives, completing a full scan in 2-8 minutes on commodity CPU hardware versus 1-2 hours by hand.

</details>


### [29] [Turkish Multimodal Video Fact-Checking: A New Dataset and a Comparison of Zero-Shot Agentic and Fine-Tuned Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08651)
*Azra Açıl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个土耳其语多模态视频事实核查数据集TeViD，并基于同一开源基座模型构建两条核查流水线：多角色框架适配的3MFact-TR（含语义不一致检查、5W1H问题管理、排除核查站点的双语证据检索三个模块）与LoRA微调的闭卷模型，报告准确率、宏F1等指标及消融实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频类虚假信息在社交媒体上快速传播，但现有事实核查系统几乎只面向英文文本，土耳其语等低资源语言工具严重匮乏，且缺少土耳其语多模态视频核查数据集。

Method: 构建TeViD数据集（来自IFCN认证机构Teyit.org的文章，辅以翻译的Snopes文章与合成样本以缓解类别不平衡）；提出两条共享同一开源基座模型的流水线：Pipeline 1将多角色3MFact框架适配土耳其语，采用Qwen2.5-7B与Qwen2-VL-7B，并新增语义不一致检查器、5W1H问题管理器和排除事实核查网站以防泄漏的双语证据检索器；Pipeline 2用LoRA微调单一Qwen2.5-7B，基于声明与视频文本描述给出闭卷判定。

Result: 报告了两系统的准确率、宏F1、True类F1与True类召回率，并给出消融实验结果。

Conclusion: TeViD填补了土耳其语视频事实核查的资源空白，所提两条流水线在统一开源基座上提供了可比较的多模态核查基线，模块消融验证了各组件的贡献。

Abstract: Video-based misinformation spreads rapidly on social media, but almost all existing fact-checking systems focus exclusively on English text. Tools for low-resource languages such as Turkish are severely limited. In this work, we present TeViD (Turkish Video Fact-checking Dataset), to the best of our knowledge the first multimodal video fact-checking dataset for Turkish. We compiled the dataset from articles published by Teyit.org, an IFCN-certified organisation, and enriched it with translated Snopes articles and synthetic examples to reduce class imbalance. Building on TeViD, we develop two verification pipelines that share the same open-source base model. First, in 3MFact-TR (Pipeline 1), we adapt the multi-role 3MFact framework to Turkish with the Qwen2.5-7B large language model and the Qwen2-VL-7B vision-language model, and add three modules of our own: a semantic inconsistency checker, a 5W1H question manager, and a bilingual evidence retriever that excludes fact-checking sites to prevent leakage. Second, as our Fine-Tuned Model (Pipeline 2), we fine-tune a single Qwen2.5-7B with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to produce closed-book verdicts from the claim and the video's textual description. We report accuracy, macro F1, True class F1, and True class recall for both systems, together with ablation studies.

</details>


### [30] [Refer-CityFlow and M3Track: A Benchmark Dataset and Reference Framework for Language-Driven Multi-Target Multi-Camera Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08976)
*Phuong T. Nguyen,Hao Vo,Tam Bang,Hoang K. Tran,Hoang-Minh Dau,Mahshid Malazizi,Tuan-Anh Hoang,Mina Sartipi,Thanh-Nam Doan,Ngan Le,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将Referring MOT扩展到多摄像头跨视角场景，提出Refer-MTMCT任务与新基准Refer-CityFlow（40路摄像头、660+跨相机轨迹、4362条轨迹描述、7699条查询，含1699条真实用户查询），并提供LLM智能体驱动的模块化框架M3Track作为参考基线，在真实用户查询上达到44.10 HOTA / 65.40 IDF1，显著优于单相机参考跟踪器（14.95–18.28 HOTA）与跨视角方法的直接迁移（约0.05 HOTA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Referring MOT基准几乎都限于单摄像头设定，用户以自然语言指定的目标无法在稀疏重叠的多摄像头网络中保持身份一致地恢复；缺少面向自由文本查询、需要语言落地+跨相机关联+时空推理联合建模的评测基准与参考方法。

Method: 构建Refer-CityFlow：基于CityFlow的全局跨相机身份标注，扩展轨迹级描述与自由形式查询（含真实用户采集查询）；任务形式化为在时序、稀疏重叠相机网络中恢复身份一致轨迹。提出M3Track：以LLM智能体为调度核心的模块化多模态参考框架，将可替换的检测、单相机跟踪与跨相机关联模块编排成统一流程。

Result: 在四相机Scenario 02参考实验中，单相机referring跟踪器在真实用户查询上仅14.95–18.28 HOTA，所评估跨视角方法直接迁移约0.05 HOTA；M3Track在同一真实用户查询集上达44.10 HOTA与65.40 IDF1。

Conclusion: Refer-MTMCT是一个更具挑战性且现有方法难以直接迁移的新任务；Refer-CityFlow提供了含真实用户查询的评测基准，M3Track确立了有竞争力的参考性能并展示LLM智能体编排模块化组件的有效性。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking enables users to specify targets through natural language, but existing Refer-MOT benchmarks primarily focus on single-camera settings. We formalize Referring Multi-Target Multi-Camera Tracking (Refer-MTMCT), where free-form queries specify identity-consistent trajectories to be recovered across a network of sequential, sparse-overlap cameras. We introduce Refer-CityFlow, a benchmark built on CityFlow's global cross-camera identity annotations and augmented with language queries requiring joint language grounding, cross-camera association, and spatio-temporal reasoning. Refer-CityFlow covers 96 videos from 40 roadside cameras with 660+ identity-consistent cross-camera trajectories, 4,362 trajectory-level descriptions, and 7,699 queries, including 1,699 real-user queries collected from 50 participants. Reference experiments on the four-camera Scenario 02 show that single-camera referring trackers reach 14.95-18.28 HOTA on real-user queries, while the evaluated cross-view method achieves about 0.05 HOTA in direct transfer. To establish reference performance, we provide M3Track, a modular multimodal reference framework in which a large language model (LLM)-based agent orchestrates interchangeable detection, tracking, and cross-camera association modules. M3Track reaches 44.10 HOTA and 65.40 IDF1 on the same real-user query set. The project repository is available at https://github.com/phuongnguyen194/Refer-CityFlow-and--M3Track.

</details>


### [31] [MetricPose: Flexible 3D Human Pose Estimation with Depth and Geometric Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08954)
*Shifan Zhu,Sabad Modi,Hang Yang,Arushi Agrawal,Steven Gao,Meghan Huber,Nathan Wycoff,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个预测相机坐标系下度量精确人体关节位置的3D人体姿态估计器，通过融合RGB先验、直接深度测量和几何约束（如地面接触）来缓解单目透视投影导致的体型-距离歧义，得到统一模型支持RGB、RGB-Ground、RGB-D三种模式并共享参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目图像中，人体尺寸与相机距离经透视投影耦合，无法仅凭图像唯一确定，因此需要恢复度量尺度和相机局部放置；同时仅用骨盆相对误差无法衡量相对相机的绝对位置精度。

Method: 扩展一个预训练全身姿态估计器，加入可选的度量深度模块与几何处理模块，改进相机距离估计与深度引导的人体细化，同时保留纯RGB运行能力；三种模式共享学习参数。

Result: 相比现有基础模型SAM 3D Body，RGB、RGB-Ground、RGB-D三种模式的相机局部关节位置误差分别降低12.16%、31.00%和54.61%。

Conclusion: 多源信息融合（RGB先验+深度+几何约束）可有效消解单目尺度歧义，统一模型在保持纯RGB可用性的同时显著提升相机坐标系下的度量精度，且增益随额外信息增多而递增。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a 3D human pose estimator that predicts metrically accurate human joint positions in the camera coordinate frame. This task requires recovering the configuration of the body joints, scale, and metric camera-local placement. In monocular images, body size and camera distance are coupled through perspective projection and therefore cannot be determined uniquely from image alone. This ambiguity can be reduced by combining three complementary sources of information: learned RGB priors, direct depth measurements, and geometric constraints (e.g., ground contact) from the environment. We extend a pretrained full-body estimator with optional metric depth and geometry-processing modules that improve camera-distance estimation and depth-guided body refinement while preserving RGB-only operation. The resulting unified model supports RGB, RGB-Ground, and RGB-D using shared learned parameters. Unlike pelvis-relative error, camera-local error also measures placement relative to the camera. Compared with the existing foundation model SAM 3D Body, our methods with RGB, RGB-Ground, and RGB-D modes reduce camera-local joint-position error by 12.16%, 31.00% and 54.61% respectively.

</details>


### [32] [FANO: Anchored Forward-Noise Optimization for Diffusion Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08923)
*Shilong Han,Hongxia Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FANO，通过锚定前一步去噪估计、只优化已知分布的前向噪声e而非x_t，并用极坐标软势约束噪声半径，使x_t保持在扩散前向分布的高密度区，抑制径向漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退火类解耦方法直接优化去噪器输入x_t，其层内优化会改变x_t的噪声分量范数，使其偏离典型值（径向漂移），导致去噪器脱离高性能工作区间、重建质量下降。

Method: 在每一退火层固定上一层去噪估计为锚点，仅优化前向噪声e，从而保持去噪器输入的前向扩散结构；将e按极坐标分解为方向与径向幅值，对半径施加软势正则，使其集中于标准高斯先验的典型范数附近。

Result: 在多个线性与非线性逆问题上验证：线性任务上达到当前最强基线水平，非线性任务上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 从优化变量选择与噪声范数分布约束的角度修正退火优化的结构性缺陷，FANO为扩散先验求解图像逆问题提供了更稳健的范式。

Abstract: Pre-trained diffusion models have become a widely adopted zero-shot prior for image inverse problems, among which annealed decoupling methods achieve leading reconstruction performance on nonlinear tasks. We find, however, that annealed methods that directly optimize the denoiser input $x_t$ exhibit radial drift in later layers: within-layer optimization drives the noise-component norm of $x_t$ away from its typical value, pushing the denoiser out of its high-performance regime and degrading reconstruction quality. To address this, we propose FANO (Anchored Forward-Noise Optimization). At each annealing layer, FANO fixes the previous denoiser estimate as an anchor and optimizes only the forward noise $e$, whose distribution is known, instead of $x_t$, thereby preserving the forward-diffusion structure of the denoiser input. The standard Gaussian prior on $e$ concentrates its norm near the typical value, and deviations degrade denoiser performance. We accordingly decompose $e$ in polar coordinates into a direction and a radial magnitude, and apply a soft potential to regulate the radius, keeping $x_t$ in the high-density region of the forward distribution. On multiple linear and nonlinear inverse problems, FANO matches the strongest current baselines on linear tasks and achieves consistent improvements on nonlinear tasks.

</details>

*Tri Nhut Do,Quoc Tuan Vo*

### [34] [AtariBench: Evaluating Spatiotemporal Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in Atari Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08755)
*Mingjia Huo,Yao Fu,Bo Chang,Yaqing Wang,Dawei Zhu,Dylan Zhang,Xinyang Yi,Lichan Hong,Ed H. Chi,Hao Zhang,Jiaxi Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AtariBench——覆盖20款Atari 2600游戏、基于高时间分辨率游戏流的受控基准，用于评估VLM在动态环境中的感知、时空推理与动作落地能力，并揭示其在被动感知与主动决策之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在静态视觉理解基准上表现优异，但在动态环境中行动的能力仍缺乏充分认知；需要有能解耦"游戏内快速动态"与"实际推理速度"的受控测试平台来检验模型能否感知快速视觉状态变化、进行时空推理并将动作建立在当前观测之上。

Method: 构建 AtariBench：20款Atari 2600游戏，要求模型从高时间分辨率游戏流中做序贯决策；将游戏内快节奏动态与真实时钟推理速度解耦；引入"可分性分析"（separability analysis）识别哪些游戏得分可作为模型能力的可靠信号；并借助该基准系统研究记忆设计与提示策略的影响。

Result: 实验显示当前最先进VLM存在显著局限：难以推断物体运动与方向；序贯决策脆弱；即使追加了新视觉反馈，仍过度依赖文本形式的历史交互信息。可分性分析给出各游戏得分的可靠性判别。

Conclusion: AtariBench 暴露了被动视觉感知与主动决策之间的持续鸿沟，为开发能在动态环境中推理与行动的VLM提供了一个轻量且严谨的测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on static visual understanding benchmarks, yet their ability to act in dynamic environments remains less well understood. We introduce AtariBench, a controlled benchmark that evaluates VLMs across 20 Atari 2600 games, requiring models to make sequential decisions from high-temporal-resolution gameplay streams. By decoupling fast in-game dynamics from wall-clock inference speed, AtariBench focuses on whether models can perceive rapid visual state changes, reason over spatiotemporal dynamics, and ground actions in the current observation. Our experiments reveal substantial limitations in state-of-the-art VLMs: models often fail to infer object motion and direction, exhibit brittle sequential decision-making, and over-rely on textual interaction history despite newly appended visual feedback. We further introduce a separability analysis to identify which in-game scores provide reliable signals of model competence, and use the benchmark to study how memory design and prompting strategies affect performance. Overall, AtariBench exposes a persistent gap between passive visual perception and active decision-making, providing a lightweight and rigorous testbed for developing VLMs that can reason and act in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [35] [Graded convolutional networks: scale invariance without the scale and robustness to unknown blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09878)
*Tanush Shaska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究图像高斯射流(graded jet)的膨胀不变特征：局部特征在膨胀不变当且仅当它经由尺度归一化射流或加权球面retraction分解；在未知高斯模糊下retraction仅改变尺度，而尺度归一化射流会引入无法通过径向归一化消除的grading因子；基于retraction的网络对不变特征是通用的，在模糊图像上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像特征常需对尺度/膨胀(如模糊导致的尺度变化)保持不变。现有常用做法是尺度归一化导数，但在未知高斯模糊下其grading因子会引入无法通过径向归一化消除的变化，导致不变性被破坏，因此需要一个更稳健的特征构造。

Method: 将图像高斯射流视为分次向量空间(膨胀λ使k阶导数乘λ^{-k})；证明局部分次卷积恰为齐次微分算子，局部特征是膨胀不变的当且仅当它经由尺度归一化射流或(无尺度时)经由到加权球面的retraction分解；分析未知高期模糊下retraction的尺度变化与尺度归一化射流的grading因子；构造基于retraction的网络并证明其对不变特征的通用性。

Result: 在零射流处，retraction能区分径向归一化和连续分类器无法区分的图像族。MNIST上两像素模糊下，基于retraction的网络保持88.4%准确率，而基于尺度归一化导数的同网络仅66.2%；在Lindeberg高斯导数网络中，retraction将该模糊下准确率从91.5%提升到95.1%。

Conclusion: 加权球面retraction是构造膨胀/模糊不变特征的更优途径：理论上对不变特征通用、在高斯模糊下仅表现为尺度变化，实践中显著优于尺度归一化导数方案。

Abstract: The Gaussian jet of an image is a graded vector space: a dilation by $λ$ multiplies derivatives of order $k$ by $λ^{-k}$. Local graded convolutions are exactly homogeneous differential operators, and a local feature is dilation-invariant if and only if it factors through the scale-normalized jet or, without the scale, through the retraction onto the weighted sphere of the grading. Under unknown Gaussian blur the retraction changes only by a change of scale, whereas the scale-normalized jet acquires a grading factor that no radial normalization removes. At the zero jet the retraction separates image families that radial normalizations and continuous classifiers cannot. Networks built on the retraction are universal for invariant features. On MNIST such a network keeps 88.4% accuracy under a two-pixel blur, against 66.2% for the same network on scale-normalized derivatives; in Lindeberg's Gaussian derivative network the retraction raises accuracy at that blur from 91.5% to 95.1%.

</details>


### [36] [Health-System-Wide Mapping of Demographic Information in Routine Clinical Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09851)
*Shengjia Chen,Ruchika Verma,Kevin Clare,Paula Restrepo,Jannes Jegminat,Eugenia Alleva,Kuan-lin Huang,Aryeh Stock,Brandon Veremis,Thomas Fuchs,Gabriele Campanella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用病理基础模型与多实例学习，系统分析了25个器官、257,322名患者的130万张H&E全切片图像，绘制了人口学属性（性别、年龄组、自报种族）在常规组织病理学中的可检测性图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口学相关信息在常规组织病理图像中跨器官、跨疾病状态的可检测程度尚不清楚；厘清这一问题有助于识别潜在捷径学习来源并提升计算病理模型的可解释性。

Method: 对来自25个器官的130万张H&E全切片图像（257,322名患者）提取病理基础模型表征，结合多实例学习，系统映射性别、年龄组与自报种族相关信息；覆盖69个器官–人口学组合，并进行人口学类别平衡、按诊断文本聚类评估及基于诊断分组训练等稳健性分析。

Result: 人口学属性普遍可恢复：全器官平均AUROC为性别0.828、年龄组0.733、自报种族0.662，且跨器官差异显著。类别平衡仅引起较小性能变化，诊断文本聚类内评估出现轻度衰减。在基于诊断分组训练下信号仍可检测，如胃组织性别0.865、乳腺年龄组0.816、皮肤自报种族0.728。

Conclusion: 研究提供了常规组织病理学中人口学相关信息的系统性图谱，为追溯其来源、评估潜在捷径学习风险及开发更具可解释性的计算病理模型提供了依据；文献引导分析亦给出待验证的组织形态学与情境解释假设。

Abstract: The extent to which demographic-associated information is detectable across organs and disease states in routine clinical histopathology remains unclear. Here, we analyzed 1.3 million hematoxylin and eosin whole-slide images from 257,322 patients across 25 organs, using pathology foundation-model representations and multiple-instance learning to systematically map information associated with sex, age group, and self-reported race. Across 69 organ-demographic combinations, demographic attributes were broadly recoverable, with pan-organ mean AUROCs of 0.828 for sex, 0.733 for age group, and 0.662 for self-reported race, and substantial variation across organs. Demographic-class balancing produced relatively small changes in performance, while evaluation within diagnosis-text clusters showed modest attenuation. Under training with diagnosis-based grouping, demographic-associated signals remained detectable across multiple organs, including sex in gastric tissue (AUROC 0.865), age group in breast (0.816), and self-reported race in skin (0.728). Literature-guided analysis of these settings generated candidate histomorphological and contextual explanations for further investigation. Together, these findings provide a systematic map of demographic-associated information in routine histopathology, informing investigations of its sources, assessments of potential shortcut learning, and the development of more interpretable computational pathology models.

</details>


### [37] [A lightweight convolutional neural network for target detection in an edge environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09756)
*Hughes Perreault,Ola Ahmad,Daniel Czuboka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向边缘设备的轻量化CNN，结合实时热点检测算法，用于目标识别与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备算力受限，需要轻量化且可实时部署的目标检测/分类模型，以扩展安防覆盖范围。

Method: 设计轻量化CNN，并集成实时热点检测算法；采用典型热成像态势感知传感器，在边缘端部署。

Result: 推理速度最高20 fps，探测距离超过400米；实验验证了高准确率、高灵敏度和低误报率。

Conclusion: 该模型轻量易部署，可为边缘设备提供可靠的目标检测方案，适用于监控、安防与目标识别等场景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel, lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for edge device deployment. The CNN is developed to recognize and classify threats or targets of interest. The model is equipped with a real-time hotspot detection algorithm, which enables it to process information quickly and accurately, resulting in improved inference time of up to 20 fps and a detection range of more than 400 meters with typical thermal situational awareness sensors. The proposed CNN has been rigorously tested and validated to demonstrate its high accuracy, sensitivity, and low false positive rate, providing a reliable solution for edge devices to detect potential targets. Additionally, the lightweight design of the CNN allows for easy deployment, making it an ideal solution for extending the security bubble for edge devices. The proposed model can be used for a variety of applications, including surveillance, security, and object recognition.

</details>


### [38] [TriNetra: Thermal-Reliability-Guided RGB-Thermal Fusion and Tracking for Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09725)
*Sadique Khatib,Aditya Ahirrao,Vedant Sonawane,Disha Kapse,Y. D. Bhise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriNetra：用无标签热可靠性系数（TRC）动态门控RGB-T检测器热特征，提升热模态损坏时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光相机夜间失效、热红外传感器在饱和/模糊/低对比度下失效，而多数RGB-T检测器以固定权重融合两路信息，无法判断某模态何时变得不可靠。

Method: 基于5个无标签图像线索计算帧级热可靠性系数TRC；用其对双分支YOLOv8m的热分支特征进行门控（可见分支、neck、head冻结且为COCO预训练）；同一分数传入ByteTrack跟踪器，并驱动基于规则的行为分析器。在M3FD（2940/630/630）上评估。

Result: 检测：87.6% P、79.4% R、70.0% mAP@50、47.8% mAP@50:95，42.9M参数中仅训练17.0M。TRC在干净帧约0.78（σ=0.012），随热饱和与模糊加剧下降（全严重度0.53/0.69），对RGB-T错位不敏感。热流损坏时自适应门控保持43.6% mAP@50，对比固定门控38.4%、全量热准入29.2%、闭合门控25.0%，增益5.1点（95% bootstrap CI +3.6~+6.5）。跟踪端TRC门控关联与ByteTrack持平，热饱和下略不稳定。

Conclusion: 自适应门控在干净数据上与固定门控相当、在热模态损坏时显著更鲁棒；但跟踪环节实测收益有限，增益局限于检测器，论文同时报告了当前评估的局限性。

Abstract: Visible cameras fail at night and thermal sensors fail under saturation, blur or loss of contrast, yet most RGB-thermal (RGB-T) detectors mix the two streams with fixed weights and cannot tell when one modality has become unreliable. TriNetra is a reliability-aware surveillance framework built around this problem. The proposed solution computes a frame-level Thermal Reliability Coefficient (TRC) from five label-free image cues, uses it to gate the thermal features of a dual-branch YOLOv8m detector whose visible branch, neck and head are frozen and COCO-pretrained, and passes the same score to a ByteTrack-based tracker that feeds a rule-based behaviour analyser. On the M3FD benchmark (2,940/630/630 split) the detector reaches 87.6% precision, 79.4% recall, 70.0% mAP@50 and 47.8% mAP@50:95 on the test split, with 17.0 M of its 42.9 M parameters trained. TRC stays near 0.78 on clean frames (standard deviation 0.012), decreases with the severity of thermal saturation and blur, reaching 0.53 and 0.69 at full severity, and is insensitive to RGB-thermal misalignment. With identical weights, the adaptive gate matches a fixed gate on clean data (70.0% versus 69.8% mAP@50) but keeps 43.6% mAP@50 when the thermal stream is corrupted, against 38.4%, 29.2% and 25.0% for a fixed gate, full thermal admission and a closed gate; the 5.1-point gain over the fixed gate has a 95% bootstrap confidence interval of +3.6 to +6.5 points.
