In tracking, the TRC-gated association performs on par with ByteTrack on a synthetic benchmark with exact ground truth and on 14 M3FD clips, and is slightly less stable under thermal saturation, so its measured benefit is confined to the detector. The limitations of the current evaluation are reported together with the results.

</details>


### [39] [The APL Doesn't Fall Far From The Tree: Unsupervised Artefact Prototype Learning in Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08838)
*Emilia Agasi,Charlie Gourley,C. Simon Herrington,Robert L. Hollis,T. Ian Simpson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Artefact Prototype Learning（APL），利用预训练病理基础模型嵌入空间的几何结构，无需人工标注或微调即可检测全切片图像中的技术伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织病理学基础模型虽提供丰富的形态学表征，但全切片图像中的技术伪影会引入噪声、影响诊断准确性；现有伪影检测方法依赖标注或大量计算资源。

Method: 将伪影视为分布外（out-of-distribution）图块，计算其原型（prototypes），再用原型区分伪影相关模式与组织区域；直接复用冻结的预训练嵌入，无需标签与微调。

Result: 在单中心数据集上，APL在模糊、暗斑、切片边缘、笔迹等伪影检测上优于GrandQC与HistoART；但在组织褶皱与血液检测上表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 冻结的预训练嵌入本身已蕴含足以支持伪影检测的信息，可在无需昂贵标注和大量算力的条件下实现伪影识别。

Abstract: Histopathology foundation models provide rich representations of tissue morphology, but technical artefacts in whole slide images introduce noise that may impact diagnostic accuracy. We introduce Artefact Prototype Learning (APL), a novel approach to detecting these artefacts that exploits the geometry of pre-trained embedding spaces without manual labels or fine-tuning. Rather than treating artefacts as isolated outliers, APL computes prototypes of out-of-distribution patches and uses them to distinguish artefact-associated patterns from tissue. Evaluation on a single-centre dataset shows that APL outperforms prior methods GrandQC and HistoART in detecting artefacts such as blur, dark spots, slide edges and pen marks, but struggles with tissue folds and blood. These results show that frozen pre-trained embeddings already carry information that allows artefact detection without expensive labels or vast computational resources.

</details>


### [40] [InstaGen: Group-Shared Importance Sampling for Fast Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08610)
*Kislay Aditya Oj,Kumar Krishna Agrawal,Sri Surya Varma Datla,Aditya Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstaGen 将 Permuted Block-Sparse Attention 的块选择重新表述为重要性采样，用"精确 top 块集合 + 按估计注意力概率加权采样的其余块"替代 top-p 选择，在 Wan2.1 视频生成模型上以相同密度/时间取得显著 PSNR 提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers 是视频生成的主流架构，但二次注意力复杂度带来高昂计算与延迟。PBSA 通过语义置换后只计算部分注意力块来降本，然而现有方法普遍采用 top-p 范式，仅保留每个查询块的少数高分键块。作者发现在视频生成常见的高熵注意力分布下，top-p 选择样本效率极低，同等计算预算下采样能给出更好的近似。

Method: 将块选择推广为重要性采样框架：top 分键块构成精确集合被完整计算，其余键块按估计注意力比例采样并以逆采样概率重加权。据此提出 InstaGen，可用任意 PBSA 方法参数化——将其块选择替换为"精确集合 + 加权采样"。为兼顾 GPU 效率，每个查询簇只抽一个被簇内所有查询共享的样本，使被采样键构成规整的块结构。论文以 SVG 系列（具体为 SVG2、SVG-EAR）作为基础 PBSA 方法进行实例化。

Result: 在 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 与 Wan2.1-T2V-14B 上，与 SVG2 相比：注意力密度相同时 PSNR 分别提升 3.4 dB 与 2.0 dB；实际耗时相同时提升 1.6 dB 与 2.2 dB。在 14B 模型上，InstaGen 在近相同密度下较 SVG-EAR 提升 1.1 dB PSNR，同时更快（相对稠密注意力加速 1.65x，SVG-EAR 为 1.48x）。

Conclusion: 块稀疏注意力的选择策略应从"仅取 top 块"转向"精确集合 + 重要性采样"的统一框架；InstaGen 在高熵视频注意力场景下以无额外计算代价（同密度或同时延）显著改善生成保真度，并可插拔式适配现有 PBSA 方法。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) are the dominant architecture for video generation, but their quadratic attention complexity results in substantial computational cost and latency. Permuted block-sparse attention (PBSA) offers a promising approach to reducing this cost by selectively computing blocks of a semantically permuted attention matrix. However, existing approaches typically select only the top few key blocks for each query block, following a top-$p$ paradigm. We find that top-$p$ selection can be highly sample inefficient in high-entropy attention regimes, which are common in video generation. In these regimes, we show that sampling can provide substantially better approximations under the same computational budget. More generally, we cast block selection as importance sampling, where the top-scoring keys form an exact set that is computed in full and the remaining keys are represented by a sample drawn in proportion to their estimated attention and reweighted by inverse sampling probability. Building on this formulation, we present InstaGen, which can be parameterized by any PBSA method: it replaces the method's block selection with an exact set plus a weighted sample. Each query cluster draws one sample shared by all its queries, so the sampled keys form blocks amenable to efficient GPU execution. In this paper, we take the SVG family of PBSA methods and show that, on Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B and Wan2.1-T2V-14B, InstaGen parameterized by SVG2 improves PSNR over SVG2 by 3.4 dB and 2 dB, respectively, at matched attention density, and by 1.6 dB and 2.2 dB at matched wall-clock time. On Wan2.1-T2V-14B, InstaGen also beats SVG-EAR by 1.1 dB PSNR at nearly the same density while running faster (1.65x against 1.48x over dense).

</details>


### [41] [Multimodal Training with Knowledge Traction for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09536)
*Xiao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出引入外部知识与大语言模型先验的弱监督视频异常检测新方法，通过字典学习压缩文本先验、用LLM保证相似帧文本描述一致性，并结合先验知识进行跨模态注意力特征处理，性能超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督视频异常检测方法主要仅建模帧特征并依据特征幅值判断异常，缺乏任务相关的外部先验，导致性能、鲁棒性与泛化性受限；同时发现图像描述模型对相似视频帧生成的文本描述存在不一致问题。

Method: 1）利用文本提示与大语言模型（LLM）为特定任务构建先验信息，并用字典学习对该信息进行归一化与压缩；2）借助LLM及辅助信息设计一致性保障方法，使同一视频内相似帧的文本描述保持一致；3）提出融合先验知识与跨模态注意力机制的特征处理方案，学习多模态间关系与特性以构建更准确可靠的特征表示。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在弱监督视频异常检测任务上优于其他先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 将外部语言知识引入弱监督视频异常检测训练是有效的：LLM先验与字典学习可提供压缩化的任务先验，跨模态一致性约束与注意力融合能显著提升模型的准确性、鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: The task of weakly supervised video anomaly detection is challenging. Existing approaches mainly model video frame features and determine whether anomalies are present based on their magnitudes. In this work, we propose a novel method that incorporates external knowledge into training to improve model performance, robustness, and generalization. We leverage textual prompts and a large language model (LLM) to establish prior information for the specific task, and employ dictionary learning to normalize and compress this information. Moreover, during experiments, we observed inconsistencies in the textual descriptions extracted from similar video frames by image caption models. To address this, we devise a method that leverages an LLM and auxiliary information to ensure consistency in the textual descriptions of similar frames within a video. Finally, we introduce a feature processing approach that incorporates prior knowledge and cross-modal attention mechanisms. This approach learns the relationships and characteristics among different modalities to construct more accurate and reliable feature representations, thereby improving the accuracy and stability of downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that our method surpasses other state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [42] [RadarDINO: Distilling a Visual Foundation Model into mmWave Radar for Human Mesh Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09524)
*Furkan Mert Algan,Zhifan Ni,Eckehard Steinbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RadarDINO框架，通过将DINOv3视觉语义蒸馏到3D雷达体素表示并引入显式几何监督，实现高保真毫米波雷达人体网格重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达在隐私保护和光照鲁棒性上优于视觉方案，但高保真雷达人体网格重建仍困难：基于稀疏点云的方法存在信息丢失与漏检，基于原始雷达张量的方法（如RT-Mesh）缺乏细粒度语义与显式的局部身体建模。

Method: 利用标定的RGB-雷达几何关系将DINOv3描述子迁移到雷达体素上，提供体素级与身体部位级监督，构建部件感知的雷达表示；同时引入顶点损失与关节点损失，直接约束重建几何。

Result: 在M4Human数据集的三个评测划分上均优于SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 视觉语义蒸馏结合显式几何监督可有效弥补雷达表示的语义与结构缺陷，提升雷达人体网格重建的保真度。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers a privacy-preserving and illumination-robust alternative for human mesh reconstruction (HMR). However, high-fidelity radar HMR remains challenging. Sparse point cloud-based methods suffer from information loss and missed detections, while raw radar tensor methods like RT-Mesh lack fine-grained semantics and explicit local body modeling. In this paper, we propose RadarDINO, a radar HMR framework that distills visual semantics from a DINOv3 teacher into 3D radar representations and introduces explicit geometric supervision. DINOv3 descriptors are transferred to radar voxels via calibrated RGB-radar geometry, providing voxel- and body-part-level supervision for part-aware radar representations. We further incorporate vertex- and joint-losses to directly constrain reconstructed geometry. On the M4Human dataset, our method outperforms the SOTA baseline across all three evaluation splits.

</details>


### [43] [Video-Coder: Reinforcing Agentic Coding for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09894)
*Chaoyang Wang,Sicheng Gao,Junan Hu,Kunyang Li,Dongyang Chen,Yiyang Zhou,Huaxiu Yao,Yong Jae Lee,Radu Timofte,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Video-Coder：首个"代码驱动"的视频生成智能体，自主编写并执行代码构建动态3D场景（Draft Video），为下游视频生成器提供结构与运动信号，并经SFT+RL训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成在视觉质量与时序一致性上进展显著，但精确的空间结构、连贯运动动力学与可控场景组成仍具挑战，缺乏显式结构/运动引导。

Method: 1) 构建专用数据管线，整理Video-Coder-SFT-6k与Video-Coder-RL-2k两个数据集（含多样提示与真值参考视频）；2) 设计Video-Coder Harness统一交互环境，集成代码执行、3D场景渲染与执行反馈；3) 训练流程为SFT后接harness原生智能体强化学习，采用执行奖励+视频奖励的双重反馈；4) 设计退火训练策略，逐步降低执行奖励权重，使后期更依赖视频奖励。

Result: Video-Coder在PhyGenBench上较Minimax-H3提升约7分，在VBench-2.0上提升约24分。

Conclusion: 代码驱动3D结构/运动引导是提升视频生成结构与可控性的有效范式；作者开源数据、模型与代码，作为视频生成编码智能体的开放基础。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable progress in video generation in terms of visual quality and temporal consistency. However, generating videos with precise spatial structure, coherent motion dynamics, and controllable scene composition remains challenging. In this paper, we introduce Video-Coder, the first attempt to train a code-driven video generation agent, which autonomously writes and executes code to construct a dynamic 3D scene (Draft Video) that provides structure and motion signals to downstream video generators. To support this paradigm, we design a dedicated data pipeline and curate two high-quality datasets, Video-Coder-SFT-6k and Video-Coder-RL-2k, which contain diverse prompts and ground-truth reference videos. We further develop the Video- Coder Harness, a unified interactive environment integrating code execution, 3D scene rendering, and execution feedback for agent training and inference. We then train Video-Coder with SFT fol- lowed by harness-native agentic reinforcement learning with dual reward feedback, which combines execution-based and video-based rewards. Additionally, we design an annealing training strategy that gradually reduces the execution reward over time, allowing the model to rely more on the video reward in the later training stages. Experiments show that Video-Coder achieves significant gains, improving Minimax-H3 by approximately 7 points on PhyGenBench and 24 points on VBench-2.0. We hope this work can serve as an open foundation for coding agents in video generation, and we fully open-source our data, models, and code

</details>


### [44] [MotionCore: A Latent Neural Simulator for Video Generation with Rigid-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08904)
*Zheng Wang,Zhengwei Gong,Yuanzhi Liang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MotionCore，用可学习的偏微分方程求解器在潜空间学习刚体动力学，并将预测的状态演化转化为空间对齐的潜控制信号驱动可控视频扩散模型，从而在保持视觉质量的同时提升物理一致性与轨迹服从度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型能产出高度逼真的视频，但物体运动常违反物理规律，说明仅靠视觉建模难以获得可迁移的物理动力学表征。

Method: 引入潜空间神经模拟器（可学习PDE求解器），从观测运动预测物体级物理状态演化；将预测转换为空间对齐的潜控制注入可控视频扩散模型，生成器仅作视觉界面提供外观与场景细节，轨迹由动力学模型指定；训练上结合扩散匹配目标与针对物体身份和位置的低容量可解码性约束。

Result: 在公开物理一致性基准及已知轨迹的配对数据上评估，在刚体设定下提升了物理一致性与轨迹服从度，视觉质量保持可比；并通过受控消融比较学习动力学与解析先验、以及潜演化处理器选择的影响。

Conclusion: 将显式可学习动力学与视频扩散生成解耦是提升生成视频物理合理性的有效途径，低容量可解码性约束有助于在视觉界面中保留指定轨迹。

Abstract: Video generation models can produce highly realistic videos while generating physically inconsistent object motion. This limitation suggests that visual modeling alone may not provide a transferable representation of physical dynamics. We introduce MotionCore, a framework for learning physical dynamics via a learnable partial differential equation solver and focuses on rigid-body motion. We introduce a latent neural simulator learning to predict the evolution of object-level physical states from observed motion. Its predictions are then converted into spatially aligned latent controls for a controllable video diffusion model. The generator serves as a visual interface: it provides appearance and scene detail, while the learned dynamics model specifies the physical trajectory. To preserve this trajectory through the interface, MotionCore combines a diffusion-matching objective with a low-capacity decodability constraint on object identity and position. We evaluate MotionCore on public physical-consistency benchmarks and on paired data with known trajectories. Results indicate that MotionCore improves physical consistency and trajectory obedience in the evaluated rigid-body settings, while maintaining comparable visual quality. A controlled ablation protocol compares learned dynamics with an analytic prior and studies the choice of latent evolution processor.

</details>


### [45] [InstantHMR : Real-Time Human Mesh Recovery on Your Phone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09195)
*Mohamed Adjel,Kahina Chalabi,Vincent Bonnet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstantHMR 是一族面向移动端推理的开源单图像人体网格回归器，基于 MHR 参数模型并仅用 SAM 3D Body 公开标注训练，在三星 Galaxy S23 NPU 上单人 6,890 顶点网格仅需 5.5 ms，同时保持有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像实时 3D 人体网格恢复在机器人、生物力学、AR/VR、健身游戏等场景需求广泛，但现有 SOTA 模型要么在移动设备上运行过慢，要么为提速而牺牲精度，缺乏兼顾速度与精度的移动端方案。

Method: 采用 Momentum Human Rig (MHR) 参数化人体模型，构建一组开源单图像回归器，仅使用 SAM 3D Body 数据集的公开标注进行训练；并在统一协议下重新评测 11 种 SOTA 方法（含 Meta 官方 MHR-to-SMPL 转换），在 8 类目标平台（桌面 TensorRT 至两款手机 NPU）测量延迟。

Result: 在三星 Galaxy S23 NPU 上每人 5.5 ms 恢复 6,890 顶点网格，NVIDIA RTX 4070 GPU 上 0.98 ms，速度超过所评测的全部现有方法；移动端比唯一略高精度的移动兼容方法快 12 倍；精度在 3DPW 为 40.0 mm PA-MPJPE、EMDB 48.8 mm、RICH 41.7 mm。

Conclusion: InstantHMR 在移动端实现了显著的速度优势并保持有竞争力的精度，提供了统一协议下的复现基准与多平台延迟测量，代码与模型开源，为移动端实时人体网格恢复提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Real-time 3D human mesh recovery from a single image on mobile devices is a challenging problem with many applications, including robotics, biomechanics, AR/VR, and exergames. Current state-of-the-art models are either too slow to run efficiently on mobile devices or sacrifice accuracy for speed. We present InstantHMR, a family of open-source single-image regressors suited for mobile inference, based on the Momentum Human Rig (MHR) parametric model and trained exclusively on publicly available annotations from the SAM 3D Body dataset. Our approach achieves remarkable performance on a Samsung Galaxy S23 NPU, recovering a 6,890-vertex body mesh in 5.5 ms per person, and 0.98 ms on an NVIDIA RTX 4070 GPU. This speed surpasses all existing methods we evaluated. On mobile, it is 12x faster than the single mobile-compatible method that slightly outperforms ours in accuracy, while achieving competitive accuracy with 40.0 mm PA-MPJPE on 3DPW, 48.8 mm on EMDB and 41.7 mm on RICH. To ensure fair comparisons, we re-evaluate 11 state-of-the-art methods under a unified protocol aligned with the closest MHR-based competitors, including Meta's official MHR-to-SMPL conversion, and report their reproduction results. Additionally, we measure latency across eight different targets, ranging from desktop TensorRT to two phone NPUs. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Naturalpad-studio/InstantHMR.

</details>


### [46] [BayesCrop: Real-Time Probabilistic Multi-Crop Video Reframing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09100)
*Cheng-Han Lee,Rajesh Sureddi,Neil Birkbeck,Yilin Wang,Balu Adsumilli,Alan Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BayesCrop，一个实时概率化多裁剪视频重构图框架，用二分量各向同性高斯混合建模裁剪偏好，并结合锚帧预测与运动矢量传播实现高效处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从横屏内容适配到异构显示格式时，视频自动重构图日益重要。现有方法通常只预测单一确定性裁剪，忽略了重构图的主观性以及多个合理感兴趣区域的存在。

Method: 在裁剪位置与尺度上构建二分量空间各向同性高斯混合，每个分量给出一个代表性重构图模式及其局部不确定性，从而支持双视图预测与额外候选裁剪采样；采用"锚点+传播"策略，仅在稀疏锚帧上做神经预测，利用压缩码流中的运动矢量传播裁剪轨迹；再用轻量级周期性视角切换策略将多条轨迹合成单一输出。

Result: 在LIVE-YT-MC、LIVE-YT-VC++及多目标长视频基准上，单输出定位具有竞争力，多裁剪参考覆盖率较高，裁剪假设多样且连贯，感知质量稳定；默认工作点下达到112 FPS的实时吞吐，且从学习分布中采样可进一步提升参考覆盖率。

Conclusion: 概率化建模结合锚帧传播可在保证实时性的同时兼顾重构图的主观多样性，多裁剪假设与分布采样优于单一确定性裁剪。

Abstract: Automatic video reframing is increasingly important for adapting landscape content to heterogeneous display formats. Existing methods typically predict a single deterministic crop, despite the subjective nature of reframing and the presence of multiple plausible regions of interest. We present \textbf{BayesCrop}, a real-time probabilistic framework for multi-crop video reframing. BayesCrop represents crop preferences using a two-component spatially isotropic Gaussian mixture over crop position and scale. Each component defines a representative reframing mode together with its local uncertainty, enabling both dual-view prediction and sampling of additional plausible crop candidates. For efficient video processing, we introduce an anchor-and-propagate strategy that performs neural prediction only on sparse anchor frames and propagates crop trajectories using motion vectors available from the compressed video bitstream. The resulting trajectories can be combined into a single output using a lightweight periodic view-switching strategy. Experiments on LIVE-YT-MC, LIVE-YT-VC++, and a multi-target long-video benchmark demonstrate competitive single-output localization, high multi-crop reference coverage, diverse yet coherent crop hypotheses, and stable perceptual quality. BayesCrop achieves real-time throughput of 112 FPS at the default operating point, while sampling from the learned distributions further improves reference coverage beyond the two representative crop means.

</details>


### [47] [Left-Right-Consistent Iterative Stereo Matching via Cross-Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09458)
*Manuel Knott,Diogo Luvizon,Daniel Scharstein,Elias Vansteenkiste*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 cross-injection (CI)，一种可加在任意预训练“左视图单目式”迭代立体匹配模型上的轻量扩展：通过把显式带符号的左右不一致量作为额外输入线索注入细化阶段每一步，使双向视差预测相互耦合，显著降低左右不一致并降低单视图误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流立体匹配模型只预测左（参考）视图视差，但每眼 AR/VR 渲染、新视角合成、双视图深度融合等应用需要左右两视图视差且二者需一致。常规做法是把网络在水平翻转并交换的输入上再跑一次，得到两个独立预测，在透明表面、弱纹理区和遮挡边界易不一致。

Method: CI 针对迭代/细化类模型设计，可挂接到任意预训练的左视图迭代匹配器；将两次前向（原对与翻转交换对）耦合，在细化阶段每一步把显式的、带符号的左右不一致量作为额外输入线索注入，从而在迭代过程中相互校正。

Result: 在全部基准上显著降低左右不一致（FoundationStereo 在 Booster 上减半以上），同时较好保持物体边界锐利度；在两个先进立体匹配模型上，CI 还降低了单视图视差误差。

Conclusion: CI 是一种轻量、即插即用的扩展：既满足需要双向一致视差的应用，也提升单视图精度，即使只需左视差亦值得采用。

Abstract: State-of-the-art stereo matching models predominantly predict the disparity of only the left (reference) view. However, applications such as per-eye AR/VR rendering, novel view synthesis, or two-view depth fusion require disparity for both views, and need those two maps to agree. The usual workaround of running the network a second time on the horizontally flipped and swapped pair gives two independent predictions that can be inconsistent, especially on transparent surfaces, in textureless regions, and at occlusion boundaries. We introduce cross-injection (CI), a lightweight extension for iterative (refinement-based) models that can be added to any pretrained left-only iterative matcher. It couples the two passes by injecting the explicit, signed left-right inconsistency as an additional input cue at every step of the refinement stage. We empirically show that CI substantially reduces left-right disagreement on every benchmark (more than halving it on Booster for FoundationStereo), while largely preserving sharp object boundaries. Furthermore, using two state-of-the-art stereo matching models, we show that CI also reduces the single-view disparity error, making it a worthwhile extension even if only left disparity is needed.

</details>


### [48] [Interpretable Context-Aware Multi-Label Retinal Classification with Dual-Hub Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09309)
*Proshanto Chanda,Munib Mesinovic,Tingting Zhu,Abhirup Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双中心异构图神经网络，通过跨模态消息传递耦合眼底图像空间特征与结构化临床协变量，实现多标签视网膜筛查的固有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签视网膜筛查需同时捕捉共发病理与临床背景，但标准多模态融合过程不透明，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 双中心异构图神经网络：通过跨模态消息传递耦合眼底空间特征与结构化临床协变量；采用空间偏置自注意力建模病灶级patch交互，并由模态特定的图注意力系数显式量化视觉区域与临床风险因素对各疾病节点的贡献；在前向聚合中暴露多层级决策路径，而非依赖事后显著性近似。

Result: 在BRSET数据集（8,524名患者的16,266张照片）上，七种病变的Macro-AUPRC达76.1%，匹配或超过仅图像（75.9%）与ResNet-50+MLP融合（75.9%）基线；上下文增益最大见于黄斑水肿（+3.5%）与糖尿病视网膜病变（+2.1%）。

Conclusion: 所提框架在保持与基线相当性能的同时，提供前向过程中可追溯的多层级决策路径，验证了临床协变量的增益价值，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-label retinal screening requires capturing co-occurring pathologies and clinical context, yet standard multimodal fusion remains opaque. We propose a dual-hub heterogeneous graph neural network (GNN) that couples spatial fundus features and structured clinical covariates through cross-modal message passing while providing inherent interpretability. Rather than relying on post-hoc saliency approximations, our architecture exposes multi-level decision pathways during forward aggregation: spatially biased self-attention captures lesion-level patch interactions, while modality-specific graph attention coefficients explicitly quantify the contribution of visual regions and clinical risk factors to each disease node. Evaluated on the BRSET dataset (16,266 photographs from 8,524 patients), the proposed model achieves 76.1% Macro-AUPRC across seven conditions, matching or exceeding image-only (75.9%) and ResNet-50+MLP fusion (75.9%) baselines, with largest contextual gains in macular edema (+3.5%) and diabetic retinopathy (+2.1%). Codebase is available at: https://github.com/proshanto-c/ca-mlc-refactor.

</details>


### [49] [T2I Models Rarely Invert Negation but Often Fail to Suppress Negated Concepts: Benchmarking Negation Suppression in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09304)
*Akansha Gautam,Aparna Bharati,Mayank Vatsa,Richa Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 T2I-NSI 基准与指标，量化文生图模型对显式否定概念的抑制能力，发现主流模型否定敏感性普遍很低（开源≤0.038，闭源约0.09），主要失效模式是"抑制不足"而非"反向理解"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型常无法抑制被显式否定的概念（如"没有灯的桌子"仍画出灯），但社区缺乏系统、可分解的否定敏感性度量标准。

Method: 构建 T2I-NSI 基准与指标：每条否定提示配以规范正面对照，按语言形式（词汇/语义、形态、句法）与目标类型（对象、属性、关系、组合、嵌套）分解否定；含 759,869 对提示原子、5,082 对象目录、916,272 张图像（911,982 开源 + 4,290 闭源）；用 Basic CLIP 估计器与 CLIP+VQA+开放词表检测的 Robust 集成评分；辅以盲测人类研究（420 原子、9 名标注者）与匹配提示对照实验。

Result: 否定敏感性低：开源生成器 NSI ≤ 0.038，GPT-Image-1.5 与 Gemini-2.5-Flash-Image 约 0.09。主导失效为抑制不足而非误导；反向理解几乎为零（>45 万次原子-模型评估中 0.00%）；8–19% 原子在正负提示下均渲染该概念，另有 9–25% 仅部分抑制，其余原子渲染过弱难以标注。盲测人类研究印证上述结论；匹配提示对照显示开源-闭源约 3.5 倍差距非提示生成器伪影。

Conclusion: 现有文生图模型基本"忽视"而非"误解"否定，否定敏感性应作为独立能力被系统评测；T2I-NSI 提供了可分解、可复现的基准、指标与数据，代码数据已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models often fail to suppress explicitly negated concepts, such as a lamp for "a desk without a lamp." We introduce T2I-NSI, a benchmark and metric for measuring negation sensitivity in text-to-image generation. It pairs each negated prompt with a canonical positive counterpart and factorizes negation by linguistic form (lexical/semantic, morphological, syntactic) and target type (objects, attributes, relations, compositions, and nested cases). The benchmark contains 759,869 paired prompt atoms over a 5,082-object catalog, with 916,272 images (911,982 open-weights, 4,290 closed-source), scored by a Basic CLIP estimator and a Robust ensemble of CLIP, VQA and open-vocabulary detection. Negation sensitivity is low: open-weights generators score NSI <= 0.038, while GPT-Image-1.5 and Gemini-2.5-Flash-Image reach about 0.09. The dominant failure is under-suppression rather than misdirection. Inverse interpretation is essentially absent (0.00% across over 450K atom-model evaluations), whereas models render the concept under both prompts on 8-19% of atoms and only partly suppress it on a further 9-25%; most remaining atoms are too weakly rendered to label. A blinded human study (420 atoms, nine annotators) corroborates the near-absence of inversion and the high persistence rate, and a matched-prompt comparison shows the open-versus-closed gap (~3.5x) is not a prompt-generator artifact. Code and data: https://github.com/IAB-IITJ/T2I-NSI

</details>


### [50] [A Bridge from Paste to Place: Physics-Guided One-Step Image Composition via Object-Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09387)
*Hongzhen Li,Youwei Pan,Yingfang Zhu,Sen Zhu,Fengjie Zhu,Leilei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paste2Place 提出一种端到端、物理引导的流模型，通过单步潜在桥接将粘贴式前景直接转为真实感合成结果，并配套构建 86,273 对训练数据与 1,500 例基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在割裂：基于参考的插入方法会改变物体姿态与外观，前景协调与阴影合成方法仅解决任务的一部分；同时缺少高效统一方案以及大规模高质量的数据集与评测基准。

Method: 提出 Paste2Place：端到端、物理引导的基于流的模型，学习从粘贴输入到真实合成结果的一步（one-step）潜空间桥接；模型内部显式进行 albedo-shading-residual 分解与重组以注入物理先验。数据方面，搭建 Blender 渲染与验证流水线生成带物理标注的成对合成数据，并基于开源物体移除数据构造真实场景配对。

Result: 共获得 86,273 对训练数据及 1,500 例基准；定性与定量实验显示该方法在合成质量和推理速度上均取得明显优势。

Conclusion: 工作给出了清晰的任务定义与有效解决方案，并填补了图像合成领域大规模高质量数据集与基准的空白。

Abstract: Realistic image composition must make a pasted object appear naturally present in a scene while preserving the fine details of both foreground and background. Reference-based insertion methods can change object pose and appearance, while foreground harmonization methods and shadow-specific synthesis methods each address only part of this task. An efficient, unified solution to this task remains an open challenge. We introduce Paste2Place, an end-to-end, physics-guided flow-based model that learns a direct, one-step latent bridge from pasted inputs to realistic composites, guided by explicit albedo-shading-residual decomposition and recomposition within the model. To support model training and evaluation, we develop a Blender rendering and validation pipeline to generate paired composites with physical annotations, and construct real-scene pairs from open-source object-removal data. This effort yields 86,273 training pairs and a 1,500-example benchmark. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate that Paste2Place achieves substantial advantages in image composition quality and inference speed. Overall, our work provides a clear task definition and an effective solution for image composition, and fills a gap in large-scale, high-quality datasets and benchmarks for training and evaluating image composition.

</details>


### [51] [SPSC: Zero-Shot Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Reconstruction via Semantic Preservation and Subspace Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09380)
*Yutang Lu,Shuai Jiang,Fei Su,Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义保持与子空间校准（SPSC）框架，用于无标签、零样本的跨被试EEG到图像重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑到图像生成已有进展，但EEG重建仍受低信噪比与跨被试语义漂移制约。零样本跨被试场景下，源被试学习到的EEG语义嵌入偏离未见目标被试的图像嵌入流形，且目标标签与目标图像嵌入均不可得，问题更为严峻。

Method: SPSC包含两部分：（1）对象掩码对比正则（OMCR），在训练阶段利用物体身份构造对象级正样本，对条件空间进行正则化，以在重建先验与低层分支中保持对象级特征邻域；（2）子空间候选门（SCG），推理阶段利用训练图像原型与无标签目标EEG的批统计量，在图像原型子空间内为每个任务挑选候选，实现无标签语义校正。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG数据集10名被试的留一被试（LOSO）设置下，CLIP上跨被试重建指标由0.552提升至0.713，Alex.5上由0.612提升至0.745。

Conclusion: 语义校准后的条件能更好支撑无标签推理下目标被试的生成，显著提升零样本跨被试EEG到图像重建性能。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models and diffusion generators have driven rapid progress in brain-to-image generation, while EEG-based reconstruction is still constrained by low signal-to-noise ratio and cross-subject semantic shifts. Zero-shot cross-subject reconstruction poses even greater challenges: EEG semantic embeddings learned from source subjects deviate from the manifold of image embeddings of the unseen target subject, particularly when neither target labels nor target image embeddings are available. To address this issue, we introduce the Semantic Preservation and Subspace Calibration (SPSC) framework, which first uses Object Mask Contrastive Regularization (OMCR) with object identity for backend training, and then constructs Subspace Candidate Gate (SCG) to achieve label-free semantic correction for target subjects during inference. Specifically, OMCR regularizes the conditioning space with object-level positive samples to preserve object-level feature neighborhoods within the reconstruction prior and low-level branch, while SCG exploits training image prototypes and batch statistics of unlabeled target EEG to select candidates for each task within subspaces of image prototypes. Under the leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) setting across ten subjects on the THINGS-EEG dataset, SPSC improves the cross-subject reconstruction from 0.552 to 0.713 on CLIP and from 0.612 to 0.745 on Alex.5. These significant gains indicate that the semantically calibrated conditions can better support generation for the target subjects under label-free inference.

</details>


### [52] [DINO-Nucleus: Multi-Level Self-Supervised Features for Nucleus Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09133)
*Mohammad Sarhangzadeh,Elyar Esmaeilzadeh,Doruk Oner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DINO-Nucleus，一个参数高效框架，利用多层自监督 DINOv2 表征联合预测细胞核区域、像素级实例流与显式细胞核中心，实现细胞核实例分割与计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量显微成像需要可靠的细胞核实例分割与计数，但相互粘连的细胞核难以准确分离，现有方法多依赖复杂模型或敏感的后处理流程，成本高且鲁棒性不足。

Method: 采用多层 DINOv2 自监督特征提供互补的空间与语义信息；模型联合预测细胞核区域、像素级实例流和显式细胞核中心，并引入一致性目标促使预测流收敛到对应中心；主干网络通过轻量 LoRA 模块适配，降低微调开销。

Result: 实验表明，DINO-Nucleus 在参数高效的主干适配下取得了良好的分割与计数性能。

Conclusion: 多层自监督特征的参数高效适配可为显微图像中的细胞核实例分割提供有效方案。

Abstract: Nucleus instance segmentation and counting are essential for quantitative microscopy, enabling reliable analysis of cell populations, tissue organization, and disease-related changes. However, accurately separating touching nuclei remains difficult, and many existing approaches rely on complex models or sensitive post-processing procedures. We present DINO-Nucleus, a parameter-efficient framework that leverages multi-level self-supervised DINOv2 representations for nucleus instance segmentation and counting. The model jointly predicts nucleus regions, pixel-wise instance flows, and explicit nucleus centers, while a consistency objective encourages the predicted flows to converge toward their corresponding centers. Multi-level transformer features provide complementary spatial and semantic information, and the pretrained backbone is adapted using lightweight LoRA modules to reduce fine-tuning cost. Experimental results show that DINO-Nucleus achieves strong segmentation and counting performance with parameter-efficient backbone adaptation. These findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation of multi-level self-supervised features can provide an effective solution for nucleus instance segmentation in microscopy images.

</details>


### [53] [Trajectory Forcing: Exploiting Diffusion Trajectories for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09096)
*Bohan Wang,Shuo Chen,Yunzhi Li,Zhaozheng Chen,Junzhe Zhang,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Trajectory Forcing (TraF)，将前序视频块的去噪轨迹（而非仅最终预测块）作为自回归上下文，支持有真值视频与有教师模型两种训练范式，在VBench 5秒与30秒长视频生成上均取得更优质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散方法仅用已生成的前一个chunk作为下一chunk的上下文，丢弃了构成该chunk生成历史的中间预测；而LLM等传统自回归模型以全部历史token为条件。因此需要让视频AR扩散也能利用完整的生成历史信息。

Method: TraF以先前视频块的去噪轨迹作为条件上下文，包含两种训练机制：(1) 有真值视频时，用高斯插值构造伪轨迹并由模型重新预测；(2) 有教师模型时，从学生自身的去噪rollout中获取真实轨迹。第一种机制为第二种提供良好初始化。

Result: 在5秒VBench基准上总分为85.15，超过Self Forcing (+0.84)与Causal Forcing (+1.11)，且质量与语义指标同时提升；在30秒生成（训练时长的6倍）上，在所有对比方法中总体质量最高。

Conclusion: 利用去噪轨迹作为自回归上下文可有效扩展固定长度视频扩散模型至长视频/流式视频生成，两种训练机制互补，TraF在短片段质量与长时段外推上均优于现有强制对齐类方法。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) modeling is arguably the only way to extend a fixed-length video diffusion model to longer or even streaming videos. However, existing AR diffusion methods use only the previously predicted chunk as context for the next, discarding the intermediate predictions that constitute the chunk's generation history. In contrast, conventional AR models such as LLMs condition on the entire history of preceding tokens. To this end, we propose Trajectory Forcing (TraF), an AR diffusion method that exploits the denoising trajectories of previous video chunks as context. TraF operates under two training regimes: (1) with ground-truth videos, where pseudo-trajectories are constructed from Gaussian interpolations and re-predicted by the model, and (2) with a teacher model, where real trajectories are obtained from the student's own denoising rollout. The first regime naturally provides a strong initialization for the second. On the 5-second VBench benchmark, TraF achieves a Total score of 85.15, outperforming Self Forcing (+0.84) and Causal Forcing (+1.11), with Quality and Semantic improving simultaneously. On 30-second generation---6$\times$ the training horizon---TraF achieves the highest overall quality among all compared methods.

</details>


### [54] [Training-Free Active Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09084)
*Jihwan Bang,Sumyeong Ahn,Hwanjun Song,Jae-Gil Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Active VLM-TTA（未来标签约束下的主动测试时适应）新设定，并给出免训练插件模块 ACTOR，通过在历史查询标签上做一致性与可靠性校验，将不确定样本路由到基础预测、标签查询或记忆修正三条路径，在十个跨域基准与 ImageNet 域迁移基准上稳定提升强基线且推理开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 测试时适应多处于全无监督区间，依赖伪标签、置信度或熵自证，对强分布偏移样本是"让模型无外部证据地自修复"的严苛限制；另一极端是有监督在线适应，假设标签可直接训练或纠正当前模型。二者之间存在未被研究的中间部署区间：有显式标注预算但标签延迟生效。

Method: 形式化 Active VLM-TTA：当前样本的预测必须在其标签可用之前输出，查询得到的标签只能作为未来样本的有代价、延迟证据。提出 ACTOR——免训练即插即用模块，基于对既往查询标签的一致性—可靠性校验，把不确定样本在基础预测、标签查询、基于记忆的修正三者间路由。

Result: 在十个跨域基准及基于 ImageNet 的域迁移基准上，ACTOR 在 ResNet 与 ViT 骨干上均一致优于强 VLM-TTA 基线，推理开销可忽略；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 稀疏标签应在"仅对未来生效"约束下保守复用；引入受预算约束的主动查询可有效突破无监督 TTA 的自证瓶颈，且无需训练即可与现有 VLM-TTA 方法组合。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) for vision-language models (VLMs) is usually studied in a fully unsupervised regime, where adaptation relies on pseudo-labels, confidence scores, or entropy computed by the model itself. This is a severe restriction on hard shifted samples: the model is asked to repair its own uncertain predictions without any external evidence. At the other extreme, supervised online adaptation assumes labels can directly train or correct the model. We study the intermediate deployment regime between these two extremes. In Active Test-time Adaptation for VLMs (Active VLM-TTA), a pretrained VLM may spend an explicit labeling budget on selected test samples, but the prediction for the current sample must be emitted before its label can be used; queried labels are therefore costed, delayed evidence for future samples rather than free correction for the present one. Under this future-only constraint, the central question is how to reuse sparse labels conservatively. We instantiate this setting with ACTive VLM-TTA for Online Retrieval (ACTOR), a training-free plug-in module that routes uncertain samples among base prediction, label query, and memory-based correction using a consistency-and-reliability check over previously queried labels. Across ten cross-domain benchmarks and a domain-transfer benchmark based on ImageNet, ACTOR consistently improves strong VLM-TTA baselines on ResNet and ViT backbones with negligible inference overhead. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/ACTOR.

</details>


### [55] [Emo-LiveSync: Real-Time Universal Lip Synchronization with Explicit Emotion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09074)
*Sangjoon Yu,Seungeun Lee,JoonHeyok Shin,Ji-Su Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出高效通用的唇同步视频生成框架，基于LivePortrait将音频对齐到隐式运动空间，实现实时推理并支持风格化角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GAN方法计算高效但视觉保真度不足，扩散方法质量高但计算开销大；且多数方法仅限真实人脸，难以适用于风格化角色与非人类实体。

Method: 以基于warp的人像动画模型LivePortrait为基础，引入训练策略，通过从预训练video-to-motion模型蒸馏知识，将音频信号对齐到隐式运动空间；并设计表达控制门（expressive control gate）以显式操控情绪表情。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在视觉质量与唇同步精度上均达到SOTA，同时保证实时推理，并能泛化到多样风格化角色。

Conclusion: 在保持计算效率的前提下实现高保真、通用的唇同步生成，兼顾实时性与风格泛化能力，并支持表情的可控编辑。

Abstract: Lip synchronization, the task of generating realistic lip movements synchronized with audio signals, is fundamental to applications such as film dubbing, virtual avatars, and digital content creation. Recent GAN-based methods offer computational efficiency but often lack visual fidelity, while diffusion-based approaches achieve high-quality results at the cost of substantial computational overhead. Moreover, most existing methods are limited to photo-realistic human faces, restricting their applicability to stylized characters and non-human entities. In this paper, we propose an efficient universal lip-sync video generation framework that achieves high-quality results without compromising computational efficiency. We build on LivePortrait, a warp-based portrait animation model, and introduce a training strategy that aligns audio signals to an implicit motion space by distilling knowledge from a pre-trained video-to-motion model. This enables real-time inference while maintaining high visual fidelity, and naturally generalizes to diverse stylistic characters beyond photo-realistic humans. Furthermore, we introduce an expressive control gate that enables explicit manipulation of emotional expressions for content enrichment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both visual quality and lip-sync accuracy while ensuring real-time inference.

</details>


### [56] [Frontier3DBench: Benchmarking Coding Agents for 3D Creation across Objects, Scenes, and Interactive Open Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09017)
*Xinjie Lin,Qingle Liu,Wenyi Li,John Lam,Xiaoqiu Wang,Deyao Hong,Mingju Gao,Zheng Jiang,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Kaisen Yang,Huan-ang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出统一基准 Frontier3DBench，用 145 个任务、9 个类别覆盖物体、场景与交互式开放世界三维创作，从外观、结构、行为三方面评测编程智能体，发现参考保真与协调活动完成是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在任务范围与评测协议上相互割裂，难以判断编程智能体在不同构建层级（物体、场景、交互世界）上的能力差异，因此需要一个统一、多层级、多维度联合评测的基准。

Method: 构建 Frontier3DBench：145 个任务、9 个类别，覆盖 object / scene / interactive open-world 三个构建层级，并对外观（appearance）、结构（structure）、行为（behavior）进行联合评测；在 11 组模型-框架（model-harness）配置上进行系统实验。

Result: 优势随层级分化：GPT-6.1-sol 在物体与场景层级领先，总分最高 66.02/100；GPT-6-astra 在交互式世界构建上领先。所有配置在物体与场景任务中，参考重建分数均低于文本驱动生成，说明生成"看似合理"的内容比精确复现几何、布局和视觉细节更容易。生成世界在局部机制上平均 83.08/100，但运动间隙（motion clearance）仅 20.82、有状态工作流（stateful workflows）仅 30.26。

Conclusion: 单个机制往往能正常运行，但需要协同动作与持久状态的完整活动仍难以实现；参考保真度与协调活动完成度是编程智能体进行三维创作的两大核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Coding agents combine visual understanding with program execution, offering a new route to automated 3D creation. By translating images and instructions into editable programs, they bring reconstruction, generation, and editing into a shared workflow. Yet existing benchmarks fragment evaluation across task scopes and protocols, leaving their capabilities across construction levels unclear. We propose Frontier3DBench, a unified benchmark of 145 tasks across 9 categories spanning object, scene, and interactive open-world creation, with joint evaluation of appearance, structure, and behavior. Experiments with 11 model-harness configurations reveal strengths that vary by level: GPT-6.1-sol leads the object and scene levels and achieves the highest overall score of 66.02/100, whereas GPT-6-astra leads interactive world construction. However, all configurations score lower on reference reconstruction than on text-driven generation in object and scene tasks. Producing plausible content thus remains easier than reproducing specified geometry, layouts, and visual details. Moreover, generated worlds average 83.08/100 on local mechanisms but only 20.82 on motion clearance and 30.26 on stateful workflows: individual mechanisms often function, whereas complete activities that require coordinated actions and persistent state remain difficult. These results identify reference fidelity and coordinated activity completion as central bottlenecks for coding agents in 3D creation.

</details>


### [57] [eSharp: Diffusion-based Sharp Monocular Metric Event-to-Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08731)
*Qiushi Dai,Aniket Dashpute,Yuhao Liu,Rishubh Parihar,Guha Balakrishnan,Ashok Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: eSharp：复用预训练扩散先验实现事件相机单目度量深度估计，通过跨模态蒸馏解决稀疏LiDAR监督，在真实数据上取得更准确、更锐利的深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于帧的单目深度估计（MDE）虽质量提升，但在快速运动与高动态范围场景下失效；同时真实事件数据集仅有稀疏LiDAR深度真值，难以支撑稠密预测。

Method: 构建事件相机驱动的MDE框架：将预训练扩散模型的先验改造用于纯事件输入的度量深度估计；并采用跨模态蒸馏，用与事件相机空间配准的帧相机经LiDAR标定生成的稠密伪标签作为监督。

Result: 在真实数据上，eSharp在度量深度精度与空间保真度（锐利度）两方面均优于已有事件到深度基线方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可作为事件驱动3D感知的有力基础范式；跨模态蒸馏能有效缓解事件数据稀疏监督瓶颈。

Abstract: Depth perception is fundamental for autonomous navigation, industrial inspection, and mixed-reality systems. It often operates under strict constraints on power and form factor. In such cases, monocular depth estimation (MDE) is often used. Even as recent advances continue to improve monocular depth quality, conventional frame-based MDE systems remain challenged by fast motion and high-dynamic-range scenes. This work introduces eSharp, an MDE framework that addresses these problems using an event camera. eSharp repurposes pretrained diffusion priors for event-only MDE and produces accurate metric depth with unparalleled sharpness on real data. Real-world event datasets provide only sparse LiDAR depth maps for ground-truth supervision, making dense prediction challenging. We address this limitation through cross-modal distillation, using LiDAR-calibrated dense pseudo-labels generated from a spatially co-registered frame camera to train eSharp. Experiments show that eSharp outperforms prior event-to-depth baselines in both metric-depth accuracy and spatial fidelity, establishing diffusion models as a promising foundation for event-driven 3D perception.

</details>


### [58] [Towards Label-Efficient Geospatial Foundation Model Adaptation via Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Building Damage Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08561)
*Hengwei Zhao,Qirui Wang,Zhuo Zheng,Xiaoyan Lu,Rusty Feagin,Wenzhe Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PUMA框架，仅需少量受损建筑标注即可将地理空间基础模型高效适配到建筑损毁制图任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFM结合PEFT虽参数高效，但参数效率不等于标签效率；灾害建筑损毁制图面临已有标注稀缺、标注时间有限的问题。引入弱监督缓解标签瓶颈后，关键挑战在于难以学到足够判别性的表征。

Method: PUMA采用正-无标签（Positive-Unlabeled）学习设定，仅标注少量受损建筑；①非对比式弱监督表征精炼，从梯度视角抑制噪声监督对表征学习的影响；②基于隐状态的标签消歧，实现可逆的监督精炼且无需估计类先验。

Result: 在全球多个灾害基准数据集上实验验证了有效性与鲁棒性；在2025年洛杉矶野火真实应用中，在极少监督条件下仍能准确制图损毁。

Conclusion: 弱监督PEFT为GFM走向真实应用提供了标签高效路径。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GFMs) are reshaping remote sensing by shifting the field from task-specific modeling toward general-purpose geospatial intelligence. Mapping the damage to buildings stands to benefit substantially from this shift, as GFMs pretrained on large-scale Earth observation data provide generalizable geospatial representations that can facilitate effective responses to sudden-onset disasters. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) facilitates GFM adaptation, parameter efficiency does not necessarily translate into label efficiency. Building damage mapping remains challenging due to scarce pre-existing labeled data or limited time for annotation. When weak supervision is introduced to alleviate the labeling bottleneck, we identify the difficulty of learning sufficiently discriminative representations as a key challenge for label-efficient GFM adaptation. To address this challenge, we propose a Positive-Unlabeled GFM Adaptation (PUMA) framework that requires annotations for only a small number of damaged buildings. More specifically, PUMA integrates non-contrastive weakly supervised representation refinement to mitigate the impact of noisy supervision on representation learning from a gradient perspective, and latent-state-based label disambiguation to enable reversible supervision refinement without requiring class prior estimation. Extensive experiments on disaster benchmark datasets from around the world demonstrated the effectiveness and robustness of PUMA. A real-world application to the 2025 Los Angeles wildfires further highlighted its potential to accurately map damage under highly limited supervision. More broadly, these findings suggest that weakly supervised PEFT offers a label-efficient pathway from GFMs to real-world applications.

</details>


### [59] [GeoJeV: Unified Spatial Probability for Training-Free Efficient Evidence Acquisition in Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08879)
*Xinyi Niu,Lingfeng Chen,Jiehua Zhang,Panhe Hu,Li Liu,Zhen Liu,Yongxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 GeoJeV，将超高分辨率遥感图像的证据获取建模为空间概率驱动的决策过程，避免依赖生成式 MLLM 的中间解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHR 遥感影像细节丰富但多模态理解困难；现有选择性区域搜索方法依赖生成式 MLLM 进行搜索控制，而 RLHF 主要对齐人类偏好响应而非优化区域级相关性判断，且重复自回归解码带来额外计算开销。

Method: GeoJeV 免训练：在每个被评估区域上由冻结的 JeV 模型输出候选区域概率分布；Region Rank 对候选区域排序，Sparse Branch 依据稀疏概率支持自适应确定分支宽度，Depth Gate 依据分支稀疏度与分布集中度控制细化深度；三者通过共享分布协同搜索优先级、分支宽度与细化深度。

Result: 在 LRS-GRO、MME-RealWorld-RS、LRS-VQA 上分别达 60.59%、52.79%、34.32%；三基准平均使 Qwen3-VL-8B 精度提升 +5.32pp，每题仅增加 1.12 秒，相较近期方法实现 7.8 倍推理加速。

Conclusion: 以概率分布驱动的免训练决策替代生成式中间控制，可在几乎不增加时延的前提下显著提升 UHR 遥感多模态理解精度与推理效率。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing imagery provides rich spatial details but poses challenges for accurate and efficient multimodal understanding. Existing evidence acquisition methods selectively explore relevant image regions, yet largely rely on generative multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for intermediate search control. However, reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) primarily aligns these models with human-preferred responses rather than explicitly optimizing region-level relevance judgments, while repeated autoregressive decoding introduces additional computational overhead. To address these limitations, we propose GeoJeV, a training-free method that formulates evidence acquisition as spatial probability-driven decision making. At each evaluated region, a frozen JeV model produces a probability distribution over candidate regions. Specifically, Region Rank prioritizes candidate regions, Sparse Branch adaptively determines branching width through sparse probability support, and Depth Gate controls refinement depth based on branch sparsity and distribution concentration. By coordinating search priority, branching width, and refinement depth through a shared distribution, GeoJeV reduces reliance on RLHF-aligned generation and eliminates intermediate decoding. Experiments on LRS-GRO, MME-RealWorld-RS, and LRS-VQA achieve accuracies of 60.59%, 52.79%, and 34.32%, respectively. Across the three benchmarks, GeoJeV improves the average accuracy of Qwen3-VL-8B by +5.32pp with only 1.12 additional seconds per question, while achieving a 7.8-fold inference speedup over the recent method.

</details>


### [60] [Impact of color in computer and human-based object recognition under occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08808)
*Courtney M. King,Elissa Aminoff,Daniel D. Leeds*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较人类与CNN、Transformer在伪装型部分遮挡下的识别表现，并提出NOP、NOR、AIO三项可解释性指标，揭示人类与Transformer受场景伪装遮挡负面影响更大、经典CNN反受益于语境相关颜色的相反趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分遮挡对人和计算机视觉的识别均有挑战，但影响程度取决于遮挡性质与模型设计；颜色属性在生物识别中至关重要且常被用于伪装，然而"何种颜色使伪装有效"以及这些效应如何迁移到CV模型仍是开放问题，且缺乏刻画遮挡下模型表征与激活的量化手段。

Method: 构建产生伪装的生成式部分遮挡（包括与目标物体融合的object-based camouflage和与场景融合的scene-based camouflage），在人类被试与从CNN到Transformer的多类模型上评估识别性能；对模型进行额外分析，提出三项新指标：NOP与NOR衡量有/无遮挡时模型激活的对齐程度，AIO衡量激活落在被遮挡区域的比例；并将生成伪装遮挡用于训练增强。

Result: 人类识别与大多数Transformer在生成伪装遮挡下性能下降，且场景型伪装劣于物体型伪装；经典CNN呈强烈相反趋势，在neutral-colored遮挡下表现最差，说明语境相关颜色（尤其场景型）更利于识别而非破坏形状/边界线索；对特定CNN，用生成伪装遮挡做训练增强可提升对未见伪装的鲁棒性，遮挡指标亦揭示可学习的机制趋势。

Conclusion: 人类视觉与Transformer在伪装遮挡下的表现更一致，而经典CNN依赖颜色—语境线索，二者存在对齐差异；研究对理解人机视觉对齐、模型脆弱性与可解释性（NOP/NOR/AIO指标）具有启示意义。

Abstract: Partial occlusions challenge object recognition in both human and computer vision (CV), but the impact is largely dependent on the occlusion and model design. Properties relating to color are particularly important in biological recognition and frequently leveraged for camouflage, however there are still open questions about what makes them effective and how they translate to CV. This work studies partial occlusions that create camouflage, including those that blend in with target objects or with scenes, which alter object representations in distinct ways. Experiments evaluate recognition performance on both human participants and models ranging from convolutional neural networks (CNNs) to transformers, and additional analysis is performed on models to uncover trends in processing and object representations under multiple occlusion types. Novel metrics offer explainability of model behavior under occlusion: non-occlusion precision (NOP) and non-occlusion recall (NOR) measure alignment of model activations with and without occlusion, and activation in occlusion (AIO) measures the percentage of activations that occur in occluded regions. Findings show that human recognition, and most transformers, are negatively impacted by generated camouflaging occluders, with scene-based camouflage performing worse than object-based. Classical CNNs show a strong opposite trend, performing the worst on neutral-colored occlusions, suggesting that contextually-related colors, particularly scene-based, benefit object recognition rather than disrupting shape or boundary cues. Additionally, generated camouflaging occluders for training augmentation improves robustness to unseen camouflage for selected CNN model, and occlusion metrics suggest trends about learned mechanisms. Findings have interesting implications for alignment in human and computer vision, model vulnerabilities, and explainability.

</details>


### [61] [Scale as a Modality: Multi-Scale Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08802)
*Ruibo Chen,Kaishen Wang,Tianyi Xiong,Chenxi Liu,Yanshuo Chen,Heng Huang,Jian Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MS-pDiT：一种多尺度像素空间扩散Transformer，将空间尺度视为一种"模态"，通过多尺度分词与跨尺度联合注意力，把尺度数量确立为新的架构扩展维度，在ImageNet 256/512上取得FID 1.43/1.44。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间扩散依赖预训练自编码器，带来有损压缩并限制空间粒度；像素空间扩散虽可规避该瓶颈并支持多尺度分词，但现有方法多为两级粗到细架构，跨尺度交互有限。

Method: MS-pDiT对同一噪声图像在多种patch尺寸下分词，每个尺度由独立的Transformer流处理，并通过压缩化的MM-DiT式联合注意力实现双向信息交换，且注意力开销与最细patch尺寸无关。

Result: ImageNet 256×256上尺度数从2增至5，FID由4.39依次降至2.31、2.26、2.19；同等参数预算下新增中间尺度显著优于加深网络。ImageNet 256/512最终FID为1.43/1.44；文生图GenEval 0.86、DPG-Bench 83.9。

Conclusion: 空间尺度数可作为新的架构扩展维度；双向跨尺度交互与细粒度流的"晚引入"策略对性能至关重要，且该框架可自然扩展至文本到图像生成。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models rely on pretrained autoencoders that introduce lossy compression and constrain spatial granularity. Pixel-space diffusion avoids this bottleneck and enables flexible tokenization at multiple spatial scales, yet existing approaches largely adopt two-level coarse-to-fine architectures with limited cross-scale interaction. We introduce MS-pDiT, a multi-scale pixel-space diffusion transformer that treats spatial scale as a modality. MS-pDiT tokenizes the same noisy image at multiple patch sizes, processes each scale in a dedicated transformer stream, and enables bidirectional information exchange through compacted MM-DiT-style joint attention, with attention cost independent of the finest patch size. This design establishes the number of scales as a new architectural scaling dimension. On ImageNet $256\times256$, increasing the number of scales from two to five consistently improves FID from 4.39 to 2.31, 2.26, and 2.19, while adding an intermediate scale substantially outperforms increasing depth under comparable parameter budgets. Further analysis highlights the importance of bidirectional cross-scale interaction and late introduction of fine-grained streams. MS-pDiT achieves FID scores of 1.43 and 1.44 on ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$, respectively, and naturally extends to text-to-image generation, achieving 0.86 on GenEval and 83.9 on DPG-Bench.

</details>


### [62] [Extrapolation Matters: Sparse-Only Depth Completion Beyond LiDAR Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08782)
*Maximilian Jansen,Gasser Elazab,Praveen Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究LiDAR覆盖范围外的深度补全，仅用单帧稀疏LiDAR原始监督，借助冻结的单目深度基础模型+轻量逐帧对齐+精化模块，并引入高频法向一致性损失与新评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实相机-LiDAR系统中传感器重叠有限、遮挡与视场受限，导致大量图像区域被LiDAR弱观测或完全未观测；而该"超出覆盖"的外推场景在现有深度补全中评估不足。

Method: 不使用稠密深度标签、LiDAR累积或伪LiDAR，仅用原始单扫稀疏LiDAR监督：冻结单目深度基础模型，进行轻量逐帧对齐到稀疏LiDAR几何，再用精化模块融合对齐先验与稀疏LiDAR锚点预测稠密度量深度；提出高频法向一致性损失（逐像素法向余弦差异的图像空间梯度，惩罚标量角误差的空间变化）；并设计区分LiDAR支持区与覆盖外区域的评测协议。

Result: 在LiDAR覆盖外区域取得较强深度精度，同时在覆盖区内保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 为在多传感器系统中部署单目基础先验提供了实用途径，并揭示了传统深度补全指标掩盖的外推行为。

Abstract: Extrapolation beyond LiDAR coverage is an important yet under-evaluated challenge of depth completion. In real camera--LiDAR systems, partial sensor overlap, occlusions, and limited field coverage often leave large image regions weakly observed or completely unobserved by LiDAR. Thus, we investigate depth completion in this beyond-coverage regime using only sparse supervision from raw single-sweep LiDAR, without dense depth labels, LiDAR accumulation, or pseudo-LiDAR generation. Our approach leverages a frozen monocular depth foundation model, lightweight per-frame alignment to sparse LiDAR geometry, and a refinement module that fuses the aligned prior with sparse LiDAR anchors to predict dense metric depth. To regularize learning under raw sparse supervision, we introduce a high-frequency normal-consistency loss defined as the image-space gradient of the per-pixel normal cosine discrepancy. This term penalizes spatial variation in scalar angular error; it does not directly match changes in neighboring normal vectors. We further introduce an evaluation protocol that explicitly separates LiDAR-supported and beyond-coverage regions, revealing extrapolative behavior that conventional depth-completion metrics can obscure. Our method achieves strong depth accuracy beyond LiDAR coverage while remaining competitive in LiDAR-covered regions, providing a practical way to deploy monocular foundation priors in multi-sensor systems.

</details>


### [63] [BEiTScore++: Reference-free Image Captioning Evaluation with BEiT-3 and Conformalized Quantile Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09505)
*Gonçalo Gomes,Bruno Martins,Chrysoula Zerva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BEiTScore++，一种带不确定性感知的图像描述评价指标，在单次前向传播中同时输出对齐分数与经共形校准的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署场景中图像描述评价指标面临输入歧义与域偏移，仅给出点估计分数无法反映可靠性，需要原则化的不确定性量化。

Method: 在BEiTScore基础上建模对人类判断的预测分布，实现一次性前向推理即得分数与不确定性；并采用共形预测（conformal）方法对置信区间进行校准。

Result: 在多个含人工标注评分的基准上，BEiTScore++保持BEiTScore的排序相关性；在多数基准上，其区间宽度与预测误差的对应关系优于Monte Carlo dropout；在图像退化及域外艺术作品图像上，不确定性相应增大。

Conclusion: BEiTScore++可作为不确定性感知的图像-描述对齐评价实用工具，实现准确打分与有校准保证的区间估计。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is important for deploying evaluation metrics in real-world settings, where both input ambiguity and domain shifts are common. In this work, we propose an uncertainty-aware extension of the BEiTScore image captioning metric, producing conformally calibrated confidence intervals alongside alignment scores. Our approach models predictive distributions over human judgments, enabling both accurate scoring and principled uncertainty quantification in a single forward pass. We evaluate our BEiTScore++ metric across multiple benchmarks with human-annotated ratings, showing that it preserves the ranking correlation of BEiTScore, while producing intervals whose width tracks the prediction error more closely than Monte Carlo dropout on most benchmarks. The estimated uncertainty also grows under image degradation and on out-of-domain artwork images, making BEiTScore++ a practical tool for uncertainty-aware evaluation of image-caption alignment.

</details>


### [64] [FusionCLIP: Multimodal Contrastive Pretraining for Fusion Plasma Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09493)
*Qiang Chen,Xiao Wang,Qingquan Yang,Hao Si,Zikang Yan,Guosheng Xu,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FusionCLIP 多模态对比预训练框架，对齐 EAST 托卡马克可见光视频与异步多变量诊断信号，并构建 EAST-MMD 数据集，在破裂预测、ELM 分类、H98 回归三任务上取得 89.07% 综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚变预训练多依赖重建或预测观测，忽视可见光视频与诊断信号之间的模态对应关系；同时等离子体相关区域仅占视频帧局部、诊断变量采样率差异大，给表征学习带来挑战。

Method: FusionCLIP：①时空稳定性选择（Spatiotemporal Stability Selection）保留信息量高的视觉 token；②时间查询编码器（Temporal Query Encoder）依据观测时间戳融合异步诊断信号；③以对比学习对齐两种模态表征。并构建含 1307 次 EAST 放电的 EAST-MMD 数据集。

Result: 在三个独立微调任务（破裂预测、边缘局域模 ELM 分类、H98 回归）上综合得分 89.07%，分别比最佳通用预训练配置和无时空稳定性选择的域内对比预训练配置高 6.27 与 2.12 个百分点；消融实验与注意力可视化验证关键组件贡献。

Conclusion: 跨模态对比对齐是聚变多模态预训练的有效范式，时空稳定性选择与基于时间戳的异步信号编码是提升性能的关键设计。

Abstract: Visible-light videos and multivariate diagnostic signals provide complementary observations of tokamak discharges. However, existing fusion pretraining methods, primarily learn representations by reconstructing or predicting observations, leaving the correspondence between these modalities underexplored. Representation learning is further challenged by the fact that plasma-related regions occupy only part of each video frame and diagnostic variables have substantially different sampling rates. We propose FusionCLIP, a multimodal contrastive pretraining framework. Spatiotemporal Stability Selection retains informative visual tokens, while a Temporal Query Encoder integrates asynchronous diagnostic signals using their observation timestamps. Contrastive learning then aligns the two modality representations. We construct EAST Multimodal Diagnostics (EAST-MMD), comprising 1307 EAST discharges, and evaluate the model on three independently fine-tuned tasks: disruption prediction, edge-localized mode (ELM) classification, and $H_{98}$ regression. FusionCLIP achieves an aggregate score of 89.07\%, exceeding the best general-purpose pretraining configuration and an in-domain contrastive pretraining configuration without Spatiotemporal Stability Selection by 6.27 and 2.12 percentage points, respectively. Ablations and attention visualizations further support the contributions of its key components.

</details>


### [65] [ZeoBench: A Benchmark for Self-Supervised Learning on 3D Zeolite Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09880)
*Aaron Sun,Yachan Liu,Ping Yang,Peng Bai,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ZeoBench 基准，系统比较多种 3D 表示学习策略在全硅沸石吸附性质预测（低数据情形）中的效果，发现自监督 3D 表示优于现有方法并达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沸石结构数量庞大但性质标签获取昂贵、仅覆盖有限的结构-分子对，导致面向目标应用的优选困难；已有工作表明 3D 神经表示可有效预测沸石性质，但在材料发现所关心的低数据量条件下，哪种表示学习策略最有效尚不明确。

Method: 构建 ZeoBench：针对全硅沸石稠密 3D 表示的自监督学习基准，在六个现有下游吸附性质预测任务上、跨多种训练集规模评估特征。对比对象包括：体素沸石数据上的自监督 3D 预训练；通过多视图与通道膨胀（channel inflation）将图像预训练 2D ConvNet 与 ViT 迁移到 3D；在域外体数据上预训练的 3D ConvNet；以及手工描述符与有监督基线。

Result: 自监督 3D 表示优于现有方法，在域内标签体制与未见域（金属有机框架 MOF）上均达到 SOTA；学习得到的表示一致优于手工描述符；在该设定下卷积架构总体优于视觉 Transformer。

Conclusion: ZeoBench 可作为沸石 3D 表示学习的实用基准，并为数据受限条件下的吸附性质预测提供策略指导。

Abstract: Zeolites are an important class of porous crystalline materials with broad applications in gas storage, separations, and catalysis. Although hundreds of thousands of experimentally realized and hypothetical zeolite structures are known, identifying materials optimized for a target application remains challenging because property labels are expensive to obtain and available only for limited structure-molecule pairs. While prior work has demonstrated that 3D neural representations can predict zeolite properties effectively, it remains unclear which representation-learning strategies are most effective in the low-data regime relevant to materials discovery. We present ZeoBench, a benchmark for self-supervised learning on dense 3D representations of all-silica zeolites, and evaluate the resulting features on six existing downstream adsorption-property prediction tasks, across a range of training set sizes. Our study compares self-supervised 3D pretraining on volumetric zeolite data, adaptations of image-pretrained 2D ConvNets and vision transformers to 3D via multiview and channel-inflation strategies, and 3D ConvNets pretrained on out-of-domain volumetric datasets, alongside hand-crafted descriptors and supervised baselines. Using our pretraining and evaluation framework, we find self-supervised 3D representations outperform existing methods and achieve state-of-the-art performance across both in-domain label regimes and for the unseen domain of metal organic frameworks. Learned representations consistently outperform hand-crafted descriptors, and convolutional architectures are generally more effective than vision transformers in this setting. These results establish ZeoBench as a practical benchmark for 3D representation learning in zeolites and provide guidance for data-limited adsorption property prediction.

</details>


### [66] [GRAC: Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning for Surgical Scene Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09866)
*Rochak Dhakal,Chen Zhao,Soup Cobb,Xin Qian,Xiaofeng Yang,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAC 将手术场景表示为图，并检索相似的图-文本对，用器械-组织交互比较特征条件化生成，提升手术场景描述的质量与事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术记录支持文书记录、培训、绩效评估与流程回顾，但从中提取有效信息需大量人工审阅与临床专业知识；利用手术活动重复性与先验知识实现自动化场景描述可减轻该负担。

Method: 提出 GRAC（Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning）：以图结构表示器械、动作与解剖结构间关系；给定输入场景图，学习从训练数据检索相关图-文本对，比较检索样例与输入场景的器械-组织交互，并以比较特征对检索到的文本表示进行条件化以生成描述。

Result: EndoVis-18 上 CIDEr-D 为 5.88（SGT 4.23，最强传统 Transformer 基线 3.36）；器械-动作-目标三元组集合精确匹配准确率 52.6%（SGT 32.7%，最强传统基线 26.2%），语言质量与事实一致性均有提升。TORS 上 CIDEr-D 为 5.06（最强基线 3.80）。

Conclusion: 结构化手术场景表示与检索样例相结合可改进手术场景描述生成，产生更具上下文意义的描述。

Abstract: Purpose: Surgical operative reports support procedural documentation, surgical training, performance assessment, and workflow review. However, extracting meaningful information often requires extensive manual review and clinical expertise. Automated surgical scene descriptions could reduce this burden by leveraging recurring surgical activities and prior knowledge.
  Methods: We propose GRAC (Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning), a framework that represents surgical scenes as graphs capturing relationships among instruments, actions, and anatomical structures. Given an input scene graph, GRAC learns to retrieve relevant graph-caption pairs from training data. It compares instrument-tissue interactions between retrieved examples and the input scene, using comparison features to condition retrieved-caption representations during generation.
  Results: On the EndoVis-18 benchmark, GRAC achieved a CIDEr-D of 5.88, compared with 4.23 for SGT and 3.36 for the strongest conventional Transformer baseline. Exact instrument-action-target triplet-set accuracy reached 52.6%, compared with 32.7% for SGT and 26.2% for the strongest conventional baseline. These results demonstrate improvements in language quality and factual agreement. On TORS, GRAC achieved a CIDEr-D of 5.06, compared with 3.80 for the strongest baseline.
  Conclusion: GRAC demonstrates that combining structured surgical scene representations with retrieved examples improves surgical scene captioning and produces more contextually meaningful descriptions.

</details>


### [67] [BEV3SR: Evidence-Aware 3D Spatial Reasoning from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09816)
*Fan Zhu,Rui Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEV3SR：一个免训练、骨干无关的框架，将RGB-D视频转为可查询的度量BEV场景记忆，并按证据需求分派几何推理或视觉证据对齐，显著提升VLM的3D空间问答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频提供的是序列化、视角局部的观测，而非持久的度量场景记录；许多问题需在查询指定视角而非观测相机视角下作答；固定RGB视角提示容易遗漏稀疏或被遮挡的视觉线索。

Method: 构建免训练、骨干无关的RGB-D视频+相机参数框架。将视频观测转为可查询的度量BEV场景记忆；由空间任务形式化器解析查询的参考系、空间约束、目标与证据需求并决定推理路径：需几何证据者交由几何推理器在查询指定参考系下用BEV度量记录确定性计算；需视觉证据者交由视觉证据对齐器，借助BEV索引的物体记录及其支持视角检索物体一致、含场景上下文的RGB帧用于VLM接地。

Result: 在标准16帧预算下，跨多个3D场景QA基准对多种VLM骨干均有一致提升；空间密集问题上增益显著，开放式场景QA亦有明显改进。

Conclusion: BEV3SR为基于视频的显式3D空间推理提供了有效、高效且可解释的框架。

Abstract: 3D spatial reasoning from video remains challenging for VLMs because videos provide sequential, view-local observations rather than persistent metric scene records. Many questions must be answered in a query-specified perspective rather than the observed camera viewpoint, and prompting with a fixed set of RGB views may miss sparse or occluded visual cues. To address these gaps, we introduce BEV3SR, a training-free and backbone-agnostic framework for RGB-D videos with camera parameters. BEV3SR turns video observations into queryable metric bird's-eye-view (BEV) scene memory and formalizes each query into an evidence-aware spatial task. The Spatial Task Formalizer specifies the query's reference frame, spatial constraints, objective, and evidence requirement. The evidence requirement determines the appropriate reasoning pathway. A query treated as requiring geometric evidence is handled by the Geometric Reasoner, which deterministically computes the answer using the metric BEV records in the query-specified reference frame. Otherwise, a query treated as requiring visual evidence is passed to the Visual Evidence Aligner, which uses BEV-indexed object records and their associated supporting views to retrieve object-consistent and scene-context RGB frames for VLM grounding. Within a standard 16-frame budget, BEV3SR consistently improves different VLM backbones across multiple 3D scene QA benchmarks, delivering large gains on spatially intensive questions and significant improvements on open-ended scene QA. These results demonstrate that BEV3SR offers an effective, efficient, and interpretable framework for explicit 3D spatial reasoning from video.

</details>


### [68] [AR-Edit: Training-Free Autoregressive Video Editing without Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09795)
*Hovhannes Margaryan,Quentin Bammey,Christian Sandor,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AR-Edit：一种无需训练、无需反演的实时流式视频编辑方法，基于 DMD 蒸馏自回归视频扩散模型中自注意力的“键/查询稳定、值发散”非对称性，仅需一次前向缓存源视频的 value 特征并在去噪中注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式应用需要在无未来帧可用的条件下实时编辑视频流。现有免训练视频编辑方法假设可离线访问完整序列，而流式方法依赖额外训练或仅支持特定编辑技术，因此缺乏通用、免训练、实时的流式编辑方案。

Method: 分析 DMD 蒸馏自回归（AR）视频扩散模型的自注意力动态，发现非对称性：Q/K 在去噪时间步间保持稳定且与源视频对齐，而 V 随内容演化发生偏移，且该现象在高噪声（少步流式模型的工作区间）最强、继承自未蒸馏的双向教师模型。据此只需一次前向即可在 V 特征中捕获源身份信息，从纯高斯噪声出发仅注入源 V 即可重构输入，从而免除反演；实现为 AR-Edit，一次缓存源 V 并在去噪中按需选择性注入。

Result: 从纯高斯噪声出发仅注入源 value 特征，即可重构输入，保真度与基于反演的方法相当或更优；在多种 AR 骨干网络上，AR-Edit 实现实时流式编辑，源保真度与色调保真度更优，额外开销极小。

Conclusion: 免训练、免反演的实时流式视频编辑是可行的；注意力中 value 特征承载源身份这一洞见为流式编辑提供了简洁高效的新范式，可跨 AR 骨干泛化。

Abstract: Editing a video stream in real time, without access to future frames, is essential for interactive applications. Existing training-free video editing methods, however, assume full offline access to the entire sequence, while streaming approaches either rely on additional training or are limited to specific editing techniques. We show that this limitation can be removed by analyzing the self-attention dynamics of DMD-distilled autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. We uncover a key asymmetry: queries and keys remain stable and aligned with their source counterparts across denoising timesteps, while values diverge and encode the evolving content. Inherited from the non-distilled bidirectional teacher, this asymmetry is strongest at high noise, where few-step streaming models operate. The resulting mechanism is simple: a single forward pass suffices to capture source identity in the value features, which can be reused throughout generation. Starting from pure Gaussian noise and injecting only these source values reconstructs the input with fidelity matching or surpassing inversion-based methods, effectively eliminating inversion. Based on this insight, we introduce AR-Edit, a training-free, inversion-free method that caches source values once and selectively injects them during denoising. Across multiple AR backbones, AR-Edit enables real-time streaming editing with superior source preservation and tonal fidelity at minimal overhead.

</details>


### [69] [Dual Architecture Fusion Vehicle Lidar 3D Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09664)
*Muyun Li,Weizheng Jin,Yuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合卷积与Transformer双分支的3D主干网络SpconvTransformer，以解决3D CNN感受野受限问题，并在3D目标检测任务上取得优于现有算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D卷积神经网络感受野有限，难以建模体素间的远程依赖与丰富上下文信息，制约了3D目标检测的精度。

Method: 构建卷积分支与Transformer分支并行的双分支3D主干网络：卷积分支采用3D稀疏卷积与子流形卷积学习局部体素特征与语义信息；Transformer分支利用自注意力机制建模体素间远程关系，以大感受野捕获全局上下文；最后融合两分支的局部特征与全局特征。

Result: 在3D目标检测与鸟瞰图（BEV）检测任务上平均精度分别达81.45%和88.09%，平均方向相似度达91.10%，显著优于现有其他算法。

Conclusion: 局部卷积特征与全局自注意力特征的融合可有效互补两种架构优势，扩展有效感受野，显著提升3D检测性能。

Abstract: In this paper, a 3D backbone network (SpconvTransformer) based on the fusion of convolutional architecture branch and Transformer architecture branch is proposed. The convolution branch uses 3D sparse convolution and submanifold convolution to learn local voxel features and semantic information, and the Transformer branch uses the self-attention mechanism to model the remote relationship between voxels, uses the broad receptive field to capture rich context information, and complementing the advantages of the two architectures by fusing the local features extracted from the two branches and the global features solves the problem of limited receptive fields of 3D convolutional neural networks. The experimental results show that the average accuracy of SpconvTransformer in 3D object detection and bird's-eye view detection tasks reaches 81.45% and 88.09%, respectively, and the average directional similarity reaches 91.10%, which is significantly better than other existing algorithms.

</details>


### [70] [Decoupled Mode Connectivity for Base-to-Novel Generalization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09655)
*Imad Eddine Marouf,Khalid Oublal,Enzo Tartaglione,Stéphane Lathuilière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMC将专业化与泛化目标解耦到两个独立prompt，通过文本特征空间线性模式连通与视觉锚点KL正则实现融合，以单prompt推理代价平移base-novel前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉熵目标使prompt趋向基类专门化，而泛化到新类需保持接近CLIP零样本特征空间；二者作用于同一prompt导致收敛时梯度冲突，实验表明单prompt损失设计难以稳定超越余弦基线。

Method: 从基类训练得到的prompt出发，分别训练专门化prompt与泛化prompt，测试时以固定系数融合二者文本特征；在文本特征空间构建线性模式连通（LMC）走廊，使两端点间任意插值分类器均保持低分类损失；并引入Visual Anchor KL项，在专门化过程中保持每张图像对CLIP零样本类相似度分布。理论上论证正则项应满足类置换不变性（CPI），并借Fano不等式在理想化模型下证明违反CPI时新类精度下降的下界与prompt和基类标签间互信息成正比。

Result: 在11个数据集上，DMC对CoOp与KgCoOp均提升了基类与新类精度调和平均（相对MergeTune），且插值曲线越过单prompt工作点，实现前沿平移而非沿前沿权衡。

Conclusion: 双prompt解耦+特征空间模式连通+CPI正则可有效缓解梯度冲突，兼顾基类专门化与新类泛化，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Prompt learning adapts vision-language models such as CLIP by optimizing a short learnable prompt. The cross-entropy objective pulls this prompt toward
  base-class specialization, whereas generalization to unseen classes benefits from staying close to CLIP's zero-shot feature space. Because both objectives
  act on the same prompt, their gradients conflict at convergence, and in our experiments no single-prompt loss design clearly improves on the cosine
  baseline. We propose Decoupled Mode Connectivity (DMC), which gives each objective its own prompt. Starting from a base-trained prompt, DMC trains a
  specialization prompt and a generalization prompt, and merges their text features with a fixed coefficient at test time, at the inference cost of a single
  prompt. A linear mode connectivity (LMC) corridor in text-feature space keeps the classification loss low for every classifier interpolated between the two
  endpoints, and a Visual Anchor KL term preserves CLIP's zero-shot class-similarity distribution of each image during specialization. We further argue that
  such regularizers should satisfy class-permutation invariance (CPI), and show via Fano's inequality, in an idealized model, that violating CPI
  lower-bounds the drop in novel accuracy by a term proportional to the mutual information between the prompt and the base-class labels. Across 11 datasets,
  DMC improves the average harmonic mean of base and novel accuracy over MergeTune for both CoOp and KgCoOp, and its interpolation curves pass beyond the
  single-prompt operating point, shifting the base-novel frontier rather than trading along it. Code is available at https://github.com/IemProg/DMC.

</details>


### [71] [Breaking the Privacy Promise: Gradient Inversion Attacks on Federated Low-Rank Adapted Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09652)
*Ritik K. Badiya,Siladittya Manna,Ashmit Sinha,M Yashwanth,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究 LoRA 适配的 Vision Transformer 在联邦学习中的隐私泄露风险，指出 LoRA 梯度信息量减少与冻结位置嵌入缺失空间线索构成两重歧义，提出“空间锚定生成式反演”框架，通过在客户端微调时注入服务器指定的可训练位置嵌入，使位置梯度充当逐层空间锚，显著提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEFT（如 LoRA）因低计算与通信开销在联邦学习中被广泛采用，但其对隐私泄露风险的影响尚缺乏研究。LoRA 的低秩更新削弱了服务器可获取的梯度信号，使得基于梯度的图像重建（梯度反演攻击）效果受限，作者希望厘清该场景下的泄露边界并揭示潜在风险。

Method: 提出空间锚定生成式反演框架（在 GIAS 基础上扩展）：在客户端微调阶段，将服务器指定的可训练位置嵌入注入 ViT 块，产生的位置梯度作为逐层空间锚，补充因冻结位置嵌入而缺失的空间线索；同时结合生成先验将重建约束在真实图像流形上，抑制重复数据伪影与 patch 置换伪影（这些伪影会产生与真实目标图像相似的梯度）。

Result: 在 FedSGD 设置下，相比原 GIAS，CIFAR-10 上 PSNR 提升 10.14 dB、SSIM 提升 0.45、LPIPS 提升 0.098；高分辨率 CelebA 与 CARS 数据集上 PSNR 分别提升 7.83 dB 和 11.74 dB。在更大 batch size 及 FedAvg 设置下，重建图像质量指标的增益依然保持。

Conclusion: 基于 LoRA 的 PEFT 本身并不能防止联邦学习中的数据泄露；经过精心设计融入的位置嵌入能够帮助暴露私有信息，说明 PEFT 场景下的隐私风险评估仍需重视梯度反演威胁。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has been increasingly adopted to fine-tune pretrained models with low computational cost and is therefore also preferred in the federated learning paradigm to reduce computational and communication overhead. In this work, we investigate the sparsely explored problem of the risk of private data leakage in LoRA-adapted Vision Transformers (ViTs), where low-rank updates weaken the gradient signal available to a server with access to individual client updates. We show that quality of reconstruction is limited by two coupled ambiguities: reduced information in LoRA gradients and missing spatial cues from frozen positional embeddings. To address this, we propose a spatially anchored generative inversion framework that incorporates server-prescribed trainable positional embeddings into ViT blocks during fine-tuning at clients. The resulting positional gradients act as layer-wise spatial anchors, providing additional positional cues, while the generative prior keeps reconstructions on a realistic image manifold, reducing duplicate-data and patch-permutation artifacts, {which produce similar gradients as those produced when the target client images are used}. Adding our proposed spatial anchoring to GIAS improves PSNR by 10.14 dB, SSIM by 0.45, and LPIPS by 0.098 on CIFAR-10, as well as PSNR gains of 7.83 and 11.74 dB on high-resolution CelebA and CARS datasets, respectively, in the FedSGD setting. Such gains in reconstructed image quality metrics are also maintained at higher batch sizes and in FedAvg settings. These results indicate that LoRA-based PEFT does not, by itself, prevent data leakage in FL, and carefully incorporated positional embeddings can help in exposing private information.

</details>


### [72] [Keyline Detection with YOLO: Runway Perception for Vision-Based Aircraft Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09316)
*Leander Fritsch,David Rügamer,Alexej Klushyn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"目标关键线检测"任务：为每个目标实例预测一组固定、语义有序的无限直线（Hesse法式参数化），扩展YOLO并引入OKLS指标，在跑道感知中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键点作为目标的几何特征易受遮挡和截断影响；而目标边缘信息具有互补性，即使部分被遮挡或端点超出视野仍可观测。通用线段检测器输出与目标实例无关联，缺少类似关键点的语义索引形式。

Method: 1) 定义关键线检测任务：每个实例预测固定、语义有序的无限直线集合；2) 扩展YOLO，以连续Hesse法式 $[\rho,\cos\theta,\sin\theta]$ 参数化关键线；3) 提出Object Keyline Similarity (OKLS)，同时用作评估指标、目标分配信号与回归损失；4) 在基于视觉着陆的跑道感知中实例化。

Result: 在四种评测的直线参数化中，该表示取得最高检测精度；关键线可作为额外输出通道直接集成到标准YOLO检测头，不降低关键点性能，也不引入明显延迟。

Conclusion: 关键线是关键点的有效互补表示，OKLS统一了指标、分配与损失，且方法可低成本集成到现有检测框架，适用于存在语义线性特征且持续部分可观测的场景。

Abstract: Modern object detectors can predict keypoints as per-object geometric features, but keypoints are inherently vulnerable to occlusion and truncation. Object edges can provide complementary information, often remaining observable even when partially occluded or when their defining endpoints leave the field of view. We therefore introduce object keyline detection, the task of predicting a fixed, semantically ordered set of infinite lines per object instance. Unlike the outputs of general line-segment detectors, each keyline is associated with an object instance and identified by a semantic index, providing a line-level analog of keypoints. To solve this task, we extend YOLO with keyline prediction parameterized in continuous Hesse-normal form $[ρ,\cosθ,\sinθ]$, and introduce Object Keyline Similarity (OKLS), which serves as an evaluation metric, target assignment signal, and regression loss. We instantiate this formulation on runway perception in vision-based landing, a motivating example containing semantic linear features that remain partially observable throughout the approach. Experiments show that our representation achieves the highest detection accuracy among four evaluated line parameterizations, and keylines integrate directly into standard YOLO heads as additional output channels without degrading keypoint performance or adding meaningful latency.

</details>


### [73] [UAV-Based Computer Vision for Cattle Detection and Feed Bunk Spatial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08928)
*Pedro Oliveira Torrente,Diego Giaretta de Paulo,Lucas Pascotti Valem,Jianglong Yan,Everton Castelão Tetila,Jayme Garcia Arnal Barbedo,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于无人机(UAV)计算机视觉的肉牛饲槽空间分布量化框架：用YOLOv8/YOLOv11检测牛头与牛背，SAM分割饲槽区域，再以牛头位置构建对齐、间距规律性与聚类几何指标。YOLOv8s达84.30% mAP50、35.50% mAP50-95，单图推理约2.7 ms；指标能区分规则与不规则分布(RMSD 1.06 vs 4.26 m，CV 42.70% vs 96.31%，聚类数0 vs 2)，为精准畜牧(PLF)提供饲槽空间模式评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肉牛生产对效率、可持续性与动物福利要求提升，饲喂管理决定生产性能；但现有采食行为/空间分布评估依赖主观、劳动密集、难扩展且易有偏的目视检查，缺乏在真实育肥场条件下可规模化、客观量化的工具。

Method: 在巴西Mato Grosso do Sul商业育肥场开展UAV飞行采集数据，人工标注"牛头"与"牛背"两类以支持目标检测与图像级空间分析；评估YOLOv8与YOLOv11，选用YOLOv8s做下游分析；用Segment Anything Model (SAM)分割饲槽区域；基于检测到的牛头位置构建几何指标刻画对齐度、间距规律性与聚类。

Result: YOLOv8s达84.30% mAP50、35.50% mAP50-95，NVIDIA RTX 5060 Ti上约2.7 ms/图。指标可区分代表性规则与不规则分布：RMSD 1.06 m vs 4.26 m，CV 42.70% vs 96.31%，聚类数0 vs 2。

Conclusion: UAV+目标检测+SAM分割+几何指标的组合可行，能为PLF中饲槽采食空间模式提供客观、可扩展的量化指标；但本文为指标构建与可行性验证，尚未与生产性能或福利结局建立统计关联。

Abstract: Beef cattle production is central to Brazilian agribusiness and faces growing demands for efficiency, sustainability, and animal welfare. Precision Livestock Farming (PLF) offers tools to address these challenges, particularly through monitoring feeding-related spatial patterns in confined systems. Feeding management plays a decisive role in animal performance, yet its evaluation still relies largely on subjective, labor-intensive visual inspections that are hard to scale and prone to bias. This work proposes a computer vision framework based on Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) to quantify cattle alignment and spatial dispersion at the feed bunk under real-world feedlot conditions. A UAV dataset was collected during flight campaigns at a commercial feedlot in Mato Grosso do Sul, Brazil. Images were manually annotated into cattle head and cattle back classes to support object detection and image-level spatial analysis of cattle distribution. You Only Look Once (YOLO) models, namely YOLOv8 and YOLOv11, were evaluated, with YOLOv8 small used for the downstream spatial analysis. The feed bunk region was segmented using the Segment Anything Model (SAM), and geometric metrics based on detected head positions were developed to characterize alignment, spacing regularity, and clustering. The selected detector achieved 84.30% mAP50 and 35.50% mAP50-95, with an inference time of approximately 2.7 ms per image on an NVIDIA RTX 5060 Ti GPU. The resulting metrics distinguished representative regular and irregular spatial distributions by Root Mean Square Deviation - RMSD (1.06 m and 4.26 m), Coefficient of Variation - CV (42.70% and 96.31%), and clustering (0 and 2 clusters), respectively. These findings indicate that the proposed approach can provide indicators for assessing feed bunk spatial patterns in PLF.

</details>
