### [74] [Decoding spatial and semantic information from large-scale visual cortical activity for natural-movie reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08887)
*Yukai Huang,Haoyu Albert Wang,Jiawei Zhang,Jialun Ma,Jiayao Ding,Yiran Lin,Xingyu Chen,Ziqi Gao,Yuguo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ca2REAL：以钙成像群体活动为条件、用预训练视频扩散模型（布局+语义双通路控制）重建自然电影片段，小鼠V1上全配对刺激辨识准确率81.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从皮层群体活动重建自然视觉经验是神经解码的核心难题；钙成像信号噪声大、时间被滤波、神经-视频配对数据稀缺，确定性解码基线难以复原动态自然场景的感知与语义内容。

Method: 提出Ca2REAL（Calcium Activity to Reconstruct Events, Appearance, and Layout）神经→视频框架：由钙活动分别导出布局控制与语义控制，条件化预训练视频扩散模型；在单只小鼠7,863个V1神经元的实测反应上做留出自然电影片段重建，并在由11个独立数据集编码模型预测响应拼装的116,705神经元刺激对齐虚拟群体上做泛化评估，配合扰动与消融分析。

Result: 实测数据上全配对刺激辨识准确率81.4%，相对确定性基线在感知与语义指标上提升最明显；虚拟群体中重建依赖分布式、刺激特异的群体结构，布局与语义通路分别提供互补的空间与身份信息；时间分析显示重建片段对语义动态的保持更优。

Conclusion: 大规模皮层群体活动约束自然视觉经验的空间、语义与时间结构，Ca2REAL为动态视觉场景的高保真神经重建提供了生成式框架。

Abstract: Reconstructing natural visual experience from cortical population activity is a central challenge in neural decoding, particularly for calcium imaging, where signals are noisy, temporally filtered, and paired neural-video data are limited. Here we present Ca2REAL (Calcium Activity to Reconstruct Events, Appearance, and Layout), a neural-to-video framework that conditions a pretrained video diffusion model through separate layout and semantic controls derived from calcium activity. Using measured responses from 7,863 V1 neurons in one mouse, Ca2REAL reconstructed held-out natural-movie clips with 81.4% all-pairs stimulus-identification accuracy, with its clearest gains over deterministic baselines in perceptual and semantic measures. We further evaluated the framework in a stimulus-aligned virtual population of 116,705 neurons assembled from encoding-model-predicted responses across 11 independently recorded datasets. Perturbation and ablation analyses showed that reconstruction depends on distributed, stimulus-specific population structure, with layout and semantic pathways providing complementary spatial and identity information. Temporal analyses further showed improved preservation of semantic dynamics across reconstructed clips. These results show that large-scale cortical population activity constrains the spatial, semantic, and temporal structure of natural visual experience, providing a generative framework for high-fidelity neural reconstruction of dynamic visual scenes.

</details>


### [75] [JEPAhead: From Inpainting to Causal Forecasting for Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08657)
*Daniel G. P. Petrini,Davi Pincinato,Fabio Grassiotto,Pedro L. F. Prado,Alexandre F. S. Osorio,Raphael V. Ramos,Felipe Pamboukian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 微调V-JEPA预测器实现无动作因果潜空间预测，一次前向预报16帧，CARLA Town12上检索准确率69.0%（持久性基线55.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注的问题是什么？探索冻结编码器的潜空间预测能否用于驾驶感知——即不依赖动作输入、仅由历史观测进行因果式未来特征预测，并检验所预测的潜特征是否保留语义信息。其动因在于像素空间指标难以反映预测特征的实际可用性，需以潜空间与语义指标直接评估。

Method: 方法：以预训练V-JEPA为起点，将双向潜空间修补（bidirectional latent inpainting）目标改为无动作的因果式预测，编码器保持冻结。采用连续过去观测、与预测视野对齐的目标及组级因果注意力，单次前向预测8个未来token组（对应16帧）。评估包括：591个候选的未来检索、与持久性基线和从头训练的紧凑预测器对比、固定线性探针的语义分割（mIoU）、车辆条件检索、像素级L1与LPIPS，以及理想重建上界参照。

Result: 结果：CARLA Town12上591选1未来检索准确率69.0%，持久性基线55.3%；更高视野与变化更大的token上增益更大。优于参数量少六倍、从头训练的紧凑预测器。语义探针在发生语义类别变化的patch上mIoU为0.16，持久性为0.13。最长视野下车辆条件检索较持久性提升15个百分点。像素空间L1略有改善，LPIPS改善甚微，因此主张以潜空间与语义指标评估，并辅以oracle重建参照。

Conclusion: 结论：结果表明冻结编码器下的潜空间预测对驾驶感知具有研究价值，但下游任务的实用价值尚未验证，需进一步研究。

Abstract: We fine-tune a pre-trained V-JEPA1 predictor from bidirectional latent inpainting to action-free causal forecasting while keeping the encoder frozen. Using contiguous past observations, horizon-aligned targets, and group-level causal attention, the model forecasts eight future token groups, corresponding to sixteen frames, in one forward pass. On CARLA Town12, it retrieves the correct future among 591 candidates in 69.0% of cases, compared with 55.3% for persistence, with greater gains at longer horizons and on tokens exhibiting greater change. It also outperforms a compact predictor trained from scratch with six times fewer parameters. Semantic analysis indicates driving-relevant information in predicted features: a fixed linear probe trained on real encoder tokens achieves 0.16 mIoU on patches undergoing semantic-class changes, versus 0.13 for persistence. Vehicle-conditioned retrieval improves over persistence by 15 percentage points at the longest horizon. Pixel-space evaluation shows modest L1 gains but little improvement under LPIPS, motivating direct evaluation through latent and semantic measures alongside an oracle reconstruction reference. These findings support further investigation of latent forecasting for driving perception, while downstream utility remains unverified.

</details>


### [76] [VoluCompose: Constructing Compact Volumetric Visual Representations for Medical Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08649)
*Zanting Ye,Yunrui Lin,Shengyuan Liu,Jing Hao,Chen Hu,Yefeng Zheng,Lijun Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、模型无关的 VoluCompose 框架，将"保留什么内容"与"分配多少 token"解耦：用双曲 token 组合模块（Lorentz 距离）分组连续切片并分配/锚选/细化 token，用编码器注意力熵的容量控制模块决定预算，在 3 数据集 4 骨干 9 组设置下取得最高 quality–retention hypervolume，平均保留 22.78% token 时相对质量为 121.68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言模型处理体数据需输入数百张切片，长且冗余的视觉 token 序列带来高昂计算成本；现有压缩方法常把内容选择与预算确定耦合在一起，缺乏灵活性。作者观察到相邻切片共享视觉特征、且不同视觉编码器的注意力集中程度不同，这两点尚未被现有方法利用。

Method: 免训练、模型无关框架，将内容保留与 token 数量分配分离。(1) 双曲 token 组合模块：将连续切片聚合为有序支撑单元，用 Lorentz 距离在 token 分配、锚点选择与细化中同时考虑特征幅值与方向；(2) 容量控制模块：基于视觉编码器的注意力熵，注意力越分散则分配越多 token。

Result: 两个任务、三个数据集、四种骨干上评估；9 组校准设置中平均 quality–retention hypervolume 最高；相对未压缩参考的平均相对质量 121.68%，平均仅保留 22.78% 视觉 token；在 10% 预算下仍稳健；相对未压缩参考的最低 ROUGE-L 比率为 96.4%，而压缩基线对应最低值仅为 15.0%–70.5%。

Conclusion: 将体数据视觉 token 压缩中的"内容选择"与"预算分配"解耦，并结合切片连续性（双曲支撑单元）与编码器注意力熵，可在免训练、模型无关前提下实现高保真、强鲁棒的压缩，显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Medical vision--language models can interpret volumetric images, but processing full volumes is costly because hundreds of slices produce long, redundant visual-token sequences. Existing compression methods often couple content selection with budget determination. We observe that neighboring slices share visual features and that attention concentration varies across visual encoders. We propose VoluCompose, a training-free, model-agnostic framework that separates what visual content to retain from how many tokens to allocate. Its hyperbolic token composition module groups contiguous slices into ordered support units and uses the Lorentz distance to account for feature magnitude and direction during token allocation, anchor selection, and refinement. Its capacity control module uses visual-encoder attention entropy to allocate more tokens when attention is more dispersed. We evaluate VoluCompose on two tasks across three datasets and four backbones. Across nine calibrated settings, VoluCompose attains the highest mean quality--retention hypervolume among the compared methods. It achieves a mean relative quality of 121.68\% against the uncompressed references while retaining 22.78% of visual tokens on average. Compression remains robust even at a 10% token budget. Across settings, the lowest ROUGE-L ratio to the uncompressed reference is 96.4\% for VoluCompose. The corresponding minima for the compression baselines range from 15.0% to 70.5%. Code is available at https://github.com/yezanting/VoluCompose.

</details>


### [77] [Subliminal Learning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08538)
*Arman Hatami,Romina Aalishah,Ilya E. Monosov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将"阈下学习"(subliminal learning)从LLM扩展到视觉语言模型(VLM)：教师VLM的隐藏偏好可通过与偏好无关的几何图形任务所生成的数字，传递给在同一批图像上微调的学生VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明LLM中存在阈下学习，即教师通过与其偏好无关的训练数据向学生传递隐藏偏好，但该现象在视觉语言模型中是否成立尚不清楚；而VLM的视觉偏好与解释行为被隐性改变对训练安全具有实际意义。

Method: 以同一基座模型微调出偏好特定动物或面部属性的教师VLM，并设同源对照教师；两类教师为无关的几何图形视觉任务生成数字；两个学生模型从同一基座VLM初始化，在同一批图像上分别用偏好教师或对照教师生成的数字进行微调，覆盖2B–7B参数规模的多个VLM。

Result: 动物偏好与面部属性偏好均能通过无关任务迁移：受过偏好教师输出训练的学生选择目标类别的频率高于对照学生，动物偏好任务中差距最高约10个百分点；迁移强度随训练数据增加而增强，并随目标偏好、模型及数字生成方式变化。此外，即使三个模型选出同一图像，偏好学生的解释中与偏好教师解释共享的词汇与短语也更多。

Conclusion: 在无关数据上训练不仅可能改变VLM的图像偏好，还会改变其解释方式，且迁移可随训练样本增多而增强；这对VLM的训练实践与数据安全问题具有直接启示。

Abstract: Subliminal learning is a phenomenon in large language models (LLMs) where a teacher passes a hidden preference to a student through training data unrelated to that preference. We study whether subliminal learning also occurs in vision-language models (VLMs). We finetune a teacher VLM to prefer a specific animal or facial attribute and use a control teacher from the same base model. Both teachers generate numbers for an unrelated visual task involving geometric shapes. Two students initialized from the same base VLM are finetuned on the same images, using numbers generated by either the biased or control teacher. Across VLMs ranging from 2B to 7B parameters, we find that both animal and facial preferences can transfer through this unrelated task. Students trained on biased-teacher outputs select the target more often than control students, with a difference of up to $\sim$10 percentage points in the animal preference task. Transfer can become stronger with more training data and varies with the target preference, model, and how the numbers are generated. Beyond preferences, we show that the biased student's explanations use more of the same words and phrases as the biased teacher's explanations than those of the control student, even when all three models select the same image. These results matter for training VLMs and show that training on unrelated data may change both a VLM's image preferences and how it explains its choices, and that transfer can become stronger with more training samples.

</details>


### [78] [HMMF-Net: A Lightweight Hierarchical Multi-Frame Multimodal Fusion Network for Accurate Fish Feeding Intensity Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09224)
*Phi-Hung Hoang,Van-Manh Tran,Nam-Thuan Trinh,Thi-Thu-Hong Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级多模态网络HMMF-Net，融合多帧视觉与多轴声学信息，通过分层双向消息跨模态融合（BMCF）实现高精度、低算力的鱼类摄食强度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化鱼类摄食强度评估（FFIA）对精准水产养殖和饲料成本优化至关重要；但单模态方法易受声学噪声与视觉干扰影响，而现有多模态框架多依赖计算密集的3D卷积或Transformer，难以部署于边缘设备。

Method: 视觉分支将时间跨度较大的多帧堆叠为多通道输入，送入改造的2D EfficientNetB0以捕捉摄食行为时序变化；声学分支采用自适应多轴残差单元（AMARU）从log-mel谱图中提取时间、频率及局部时频表示；BMCF机制通过紧凑的"信使令牌"在中间表征层逐步交换两模态信息，以降低稠密跨模态注意力的计算开销。

Result: 在AV-FFIA数据集五折交叉验证下，留出测试折上达96.77%准确率、96.88% F1，仅3.987M参数与1.002 GFLOPs；相较最佳单视觉配置（95.49%）分类误差降低28.38%；在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上用TensorRT FP32部署保持相当精度，每2秒样本推理11.54 ms，模型体积18.81 MB。

Conclusion: HMMF-Net兼顾有效的多模态表征学习与计算效率，可在资源受限的边缘平台上实现准确且实用的鱼类摄食强度评估。

Abstract: Automated fish feeding intensity assessment (FFIA) is important for precision aquaculture and feed cost optimization. However, unimodal methods are vulnerable to acoustic noise and visual disturbances, while existing multimodal frameworks often rely on computationally intensive 3D convolutions or Transformers, limiting their use on edge devices. To address these limitations, we propose HMMF-Net, a lightweight multimodal architecture that combines early multi-frame visual encoding and multi-axis acoustic modeling through a hierarchical bidirectional messenger cross-modal fusion (BMCF) mechanism. The visual branch captures temporal variations in feeding behavior by stacking temporally distant frames as a multi-channel input to an adapted 2D EfficientNetB0 backbone. The acoustic branch employs adaptive multi-axis residual units (AMARU) to extract temporal, frequency, and local time-frequency representations from log-mel spectrograms. BMCF progressively exchanges information between the two modalities at intermediate representation levels through compact messenger tokens, reducing the computational overhead of dense cross-modal attention. Evaluated on the AV-FFIA dataset using five-fold cross-validation, HMMF-Net achieves 96.77% accuracy and 96.88% F1-score on the held-out test folds, with 3.987M parameters and 1.002 GFLOPs. Compared with the best-performing visual configuration (95.49% accuracy), HMMF-Net reduces the classification error by 28.38%. Deployment on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano using TensorRT with FP32 precision maintains comparable accuracy, with an inference time of 11.54 ms per 2-second sample and a model size of 18.81 MB. These results demonstrate that HMMF-Net combines effective multimodal representation learning with computational efficiency, enabling accurate and practical FFIA on resource-constrained edge platforms.

</details>


### [79] [Look Closer, Align Better: Explicit Visual Attributes for Robust EEG-to-Image Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09123)
*Ke Xiao,Banghua Yang,Wenbin Guo,Fenqi Rong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AttrEcho 框架，将 EEG 相关的显式视觉属性与预训练视觉特征进行空间对齐融合，提升细粒度 EEG-to-image 检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG-to-image 检索依赖预训练视觉编码器表征，但这类表征可能缺失区分语义相似图像所需的细粒度视觉细节。作者先验证显式视觉属性的 EEG 可解码性及相对预训练表征的互补预测价值，以寻找超越预训练表征的 EEG 相关视觉线索。

Method: 1) 先分析显式视觉属性的 EEG 可解码性与互补性；2) Region-Aligned Visual Attribute Fusion (RAVF)：通过可学习的有符号残差门（signed residual gate）将 EEG 相关局部视觉属性与空间对应的预训练特征融合；3) Feature Token Aggregation (FTA)：学习各区域上的维度特异权重，聚合精炼后的视觉特征，用于与 EEG 表征做对比对齐。

Result: 在 THINGS-EEG 上，被试内 200 类检索达 94.8% Top-1、99.7% Top-5；当候选库扩至 1600 张含同概念干扰项时仍保持 82.1% Top-1；跨被试检索为 25.3% Top-1、53.0% Top-5。

Conclusion: 实验表明 EEG 相关视觉属性及空间对齐融合对细粒度 EEG-to-image 检索至关重要；跨被试性能仍显著低于被试内，泛化仍是主要挑战。

Abstract: EEG-to-image retrieval commonly aligns neural responses with representations from pretrained vision encoders. However, these representations may underrepresent fine-grained visual details needed to distinguish semantically similar images. To address this limitation, we first investigate the EEG decodability and complementary predictive value of explicit visual attributes, identifying visual cues that capture EEG-relevant information beyond pretrained representations. Building on these findings, we propose AttrEcho, a framework that enriches pretrained visual representations with complementary, spatially aligned visual attributes. Specifically, Region-Aligned Visual Attribute Fusion (RAVF) integrates EEG-relevant local visual attributes with spatially corresponding pretrained features through a learnable signed residual gate. We further employ Feature Token Aggregation (FTA), which learns dimension-specific weights across regions to aggregate refined visual features for contrastive alignment with EEG representations. Experiments on THINGS-EEG demonstrate that AttrEcho achieves 94.8% Top-1 and 99.7% Top-5 accuracy in intra-subject 200-way retrieval, while maintaining 82.1% Top-1 accuracy as the gallery expands to 1,600 images with same-concept distractors. AttrEcho also achieves 25.3% Top-1 and 53.0% Top-5 accuracy in inter-subject retrieval. These results highlight the importance of EEG-relevant visual attributes and spatially aligned fusion for fine-grained EEG-to-image retrieval.

</details>


### [80] [UniRGBA: Unifying RGB and Alpha in Pretrained Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09069)
*Lei Xu,Zhuo Li,Yongyi Jin,Aoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniRGBA通过极简、针对性的扩展将现有RGB视频扩散模型变为原生RGBA生成模型：VAE经零初始化通道扩展+5%选择性解冻+六项渐进重建损失（含KL参考损失）升级为4通道，DiT仅用标准DoRA微调，在视觉特效RGBA基准上超越Wan-Alpha且不损失RGB保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型已能生成逼真RGB视频，但原生alpha通道视频生成仍未解决。现有方法依赖抠像（matting）式数据与非原生RGBA设计，偏向真实物体场景；面对半透明性在空间上非局部、非椭圆的视觉特效内容时严重退化。作者的核心论点是：RGBA生成并不需要专用的alpha编码器或复杂的潜空间重构。

Method: 1）UniRGBA-VAE：将因果视频VAE通过零初始化通道扩展至4通道RGBA；采用对称选择性层解冻（仅约5%可训练参数）；设计六项渐进重建损失，其中以KL参考损失为锚防止潜空间漂移；随后用两阶段调度引入轻量GAN目标。2）生成端：对预训练DiT施加标准DoRA，复用统一的4通道潜变量，不做任何alpha专用架构改动。

Result: 在视觉特效RGBA基准上，UniRGBA的RGBA重建显著优于Wan-Alpha，同时保持RGB保真度；在RGBA文本到视频生成任务上优于Wan-Alpha；在纯RGB指标上达到或超过原始预训练Wan2.1骨干。

Conclusion: 原生alpha支持可作为对RGB通路的极小扩展被集成进视频扩散模型，既不损害底层RGB先验，也保持架构通用性；专用alpha编码器与潜空间重构并非必要。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have achieved remarkable photorealistic RGB synthesis, yet generating videos with a native alpha channel remains an open challenge. Existing approaches often rely on matting-style data and non-native RGBA designs, causing them to specialize in real-object scenes and to degrade severely on visual-effect content whose semi-transparency is spatially non-local and non-elliptical. We present UniRGBA, a unified framework whose key insight is that RGBA generation does not require a dedicated alpha encoder or complex latent-space restructuring---a minimal, targeted extension of the existing RGB pathway is sufficient. UniRGBA-VAE extends a causal video VAE to 4-channel RGBA via zero-initialized channel expansion, symmetric selective layer unfreezing (5% trainable), and a six-term progressive reconstruction loss anchored by a KL reference loss that prevents latent drift, followed by a two-stage schedule that introduces a lightweight GAN objective. We then apply standard DoRA to the pretrained DiT, reusing the unified 4-channel latent without any alpha-specific architectural changes. On a visual-effect RGBA benchmark, UniRGBA substantially outperforms Wan-Alpha on RGBA reconstruction while preserving RGB fidelity. For generation, UniRGBA outperforms Wan-Alpha on RGBA text-to-video tasks and matches or surpasses the original pretrained Wan2.1 backbone on RGB-only metrics, demonstrating that native alpha support can be integrated without compromising the underlying RGB prior or the model's architectural versatility.

</details>


### [81] [ProtoCouncil: Multi-Encoder Anatomical Prototypes for Prostate Cancer Detection in Micro-Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09058)
*Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Nooshin Maghsoodi,Armin Saadat,Obed Dzikunu,Zhuoxin Guo,Hannes Cash,Ferdinand Luger,Adam Kinnaird,Brian Wodlinger,Parvin Mousavi,Purang Abolmaesumi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoCouncil 用三个基础编码器在整体腺体、针道、腺体扇区三个解剖层级学习病例级原型，并以"偏置+原型相似度精确求和"的可加性融合给出可追溯的核心级前列腺癌预测，同时用编码器分歧触发选择性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微超声（μUS）前列腺癌检测高度依赖临床经验；现有基于稀疏、噪声核心级病理训练的模型缺乏决策可解释性，且在域偏移下易失效。临床使用需要可追溯的决策过程与不确定性标记。

Method: 引入 ProtoCouncil：MedSAM、DINOv3、V-JEPA 2.1 三个基础编码器在三个解剖层级（整体腺体、针道、腺体扇区）学习基于真实训练核心的原型；原型投影到真实训练核心区域，无标签融合保证每个类别 logit = 偏置 + 原型相似度贡献的精确和；编码器分歧用于触发选择性预测（弃权）。

Result: 五折患者级交叉验证（6,588 核心、693 患者）：csPCa（GG≥3）AUROC 82.9%，比此前最佳方法高 4.3 个百分点。两个外部队列零样本评估中，全癌与 csPCa AUROC 均优于所有先前方法；最近同类原型与测试核心肿瘤累及程度的相关性优于随机同类病例。外部数据上，编码器一致时 csPCa 准确率 79%，两者反对融合结论时降至 27%；对分歧弃权使平衡准确率分别提升 7 与 15 个百分点，同时保留 58% 与 54% 的核心。

Conclusion: ProtoCouncil 将核心级预测锚定于来自真实、解剖索引训练病例的精确相似度证据，使推理可解释；在三个队列上取得最高 csPCa AUROC，并通过编码器分歧识别不可靠预测。

Abstract: Purpose: Micro-ultrasound ($μ$US) is a promising modality for prostate cancer (PCa) detection, but identifying suspicious tissue depends heavily on clinical experience. Models trained on sparse, noisy core-level pathology offer little insight into their decisions and can fail under domain shift. Clinical use needs a traceable decision process and a flag for uncertain predictions.
  Methods: We introduce ProtoCouncil, in which three foundation encoders (MedSAM, DINOv3 and V-JEPA 2.1) learn case-based prototypes at three anatomical levels: whole gland, needle track and gland sectors. Prototypes are projected onto real training-core regions, and label-free fusion keeps every class logit as a bias plus an exact sum of prototype similarity contributions. Encoder disagreement triggers selective prediction.
  Results: In five-fold patient-level cross-validation (6,588 cores, 693 patients), ProtoCouncil reaches 82.9% AUROC for clinically significant PCa (csPCa, Grade Group $\geq$3), 4.3 points above the best previously published method. In zero-shot evaluation on two external cohorts, ProtoCouncil achieves higher all-cancer and csPCa AUROC than all previous methods. Nearest same-class exemplars match test-core tumor involvement better than random same-class cases. Externally, csPCa accuracy falls from 79% when encoders agree to 27% when two oppose the fused call. Abstaining on disagreement raises balanced accuracy by 7 and 15 points, while retaining 58% and 54% of the cores.
  Conclusion: ProtoCouncil grounds core-level predictions in exact similarity-based evidence from real, anatomically indexed training cases, which makes its reasoning interpretable. It achieves the highest csPCa AUROC across three cohorts and identifies unreliable predictions through encoder disagreement.

</details>


### [82] [Grounding-Aware Judge Reward Reinforcement Learning for Out-of-Domain Face Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09047)
*Xiaoyong Yu,Xinge Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"基于grounding感知的裁判奖励"，用32B视觉语言模型作为LLM-as-judge对FAS的推理链进行过程监督，嵌入GRPO后训练阶段，提升域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAS的CoT推理缺乏可规则验证的真值：仅二分类判定可校验，推理链无法用人工规则验证。仅优化判定是稀疏且可被"捷径"（如虚假拒绝偏置）利用的信号，无法泛化。动机是在CoT监督微调后已具备良好ID性能的模型上，进一步提升OOD泛化。

Method: 采用32B视觉语言裁判模型，针对图像评估每条推理链的证据锚定（evidence anchoring）、逻辑一致性（logical coherence）与领域知识（domain knowledge），将图像本身变为验证信号；该grounding感知奖励在CoT监督微调之后的GRPO阶段施加，由裁判而非RL目标提供过程监督。

Result: 在OOD泛化上，奖励使两个物理攻击基准的ACER下降（LCC-FASD 0.288→0.273，MSU-MFSD 0.431→0.409），同时保持ID精度（CelebA-Spoof 0.033→0.035）。对比ResNet-50/101与CLIP-ViT-B/16，该MLLM在ID上占优，OOD上持平或超过最强基线。

Conclusion: grounding感知奖励是提升FAS模型OOD泛化的有效手段；据作者所知，这是首次将LLM-as-judge过程监督用于FAS。

Abstract: Face Anti-Spoofing (FAS) must distinguish genuine faces from presentation attacks. A unified Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) is an attractive solution: it detects physical attacks (print, replay, mask) in one model and produces interpretable forensic evidence via Chain-of-Thought (CoT). A central obstacle to post-training such a model with reinforcement learning is that FAS reasoning has no rule-verifiable ground truth: only the binary verdict is checkable, while the reasoning chain cannot be verified by any hand-crafted rule. Optimizing the verdict alone is therefore a sparse, hackable signal. The model can reach the correct answer through a shortcut such as a false-rejection bias, which does not generalize. We address this with a grounding-aware judge reward. A 32B vision-language judge scores each reasoning chain against the image on evidence anchoring, logical coherence, and domain knowledge, turning the image itself into the verification signal. The reward is applied in a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) stage after CoT supervised fine-tuning. It is the judge, rather than the RL objective, that supplies the process supervision a verdict-only reward cannot. Our study starts from a model that already performs well In-Domain (ID) after CoT-based supervised fine-tuning and asks how to improve its Out-of-Domain (OOD) generalization. We find that grounding-aware rewards are an effective means to that end. We validate the method on OOD generalization, where the reward reduces ACER on two physical benchmarks (LCC-FASD 0.288 to 0.273, MSU-MFSD 0.431 to 0.409) while preserving ID accuracy (CelebA-Spoof 0.033 to 0.035). Against ResNet-50/101 and CLIP-ViT-B/16 baselines, the MLLM dominates ID and matches or surpasses the strongest baseline OOD. To our knowledge this is the first use of LLM-as-judge process supervision for FAS.

</details>


### [83] [FlowMoP: Stochastic Multi-Person Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09882)
*Aadya Agrawal,Ho Kei Cheng,Alexander Schwing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出条件流匹配框架，联合生成多人多样化3D未来运动，结合B样条重参数化与双流Transformer，在CMU-Mocap(UMPM)、3DPW上超越确定性基线，并在WorldPose上建立首个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人3D运动预测本质是随机的（同一历史可有多种合理未来，取决于意图、协调与社会语境），但主流范式仍为确定性、每智能体输出固定数量结果，缺乏对预测分布的建模与交互感知的多样性生成。

Method: 1) 条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）生成式框架，联合为多智能体生成多样化未来运动；2) 同时建模全局轨迹与root-relative（根相对）姿态；3) B样条重参数化，压缩生成状态空间并强制时间平滑；4) 双流Transformer，以观测到的邻居运动为条件，实现交互感知预测。

Result: 在CMU-Mocap(UMPM)与3DPW上达到SOTA；在联合位置误差、姿态误差与末端位移误差上均优于确定性基线，同时输出完整预测分布。在WorldPose（大规模职业足球数据集）上建立首个运动预测基线，并显示社会条件化将预测不确定性集中于受交互约束的方向。

Conclusion: 生成式（流匹配）建模可在不牺牲精度的情况下替代确定性范式，获得更优的多误差指标与完整分布；B样条与双流交互条件化有效；社会条件化可解释性地塑造不确定性结构，为多人运动预测提供新基线。

Abstract: Predicting the future motion of multiple people is inherently stochastic: the same observed history admits many plausible continuations depending on intent, coordination, and social context. Yet the dominant paradigm in multi-person 3D motion prediction remains deterministic, with existing methods producing a fixed number of outputs per agent. In contrast, we present a conditional flow matching framework that jointly generates diverse future motions for multiple agents, modeling both global trajectories and root-relative poses. A B-spline reparameterization compresses the generative state space while enforcing temporal smoothness, and a dual-stream transformer conditions each agent's predictions on observed neighbor motion for interaction-aware forecasting. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on CMU-Mocap (UMPM) and 3DPW, outperforming deterministic baselines on joint position error, pose error, and final displacement error despite producing a full predictive distribution. We additionally establish the first motion prediction baseline on WorldPose, a large-scale professional soccer dataset, where social conditioning demonstrably concentrates predictive uncertainty along interaction-constrained directions.

</details>


### [84] [Zero-Shot Benchmarking of Unified Multimodal Models for Shallow-Water UAV Image Distortion Assessment and Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09740)
*Dario Scilla,Omar A. Lopez,Mattia Rosso,Kasper Johansen,Matthew F. McCabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This paper presents the first systematic zero-shot benchmark of five unified MLLMs with frozen weights for assessing water-induced distortions and restoring shallow-water UAV imagery, using 1,000 image pairs and multiple prompt variants (P0–P3, R0–R3), finding only modest assessment accuracy (best pooled balanced accuracy 0.654) and negligible restoration gains (best PSNR +0.255 dB).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shallow-water UAV imagery offers centimeter-scale benthic observations, but water-induced distortions hamper interpretation. Yet whether unified MLLMs can both diagnose such distortions and perform physical restoration in a single framework has not been systematically examined; existing restoration models also lack zero-shot transfer evidence.

Method: Zero-shot evaluation of five open-weight unified MLLMs with frozen pretrained weights, no paired references, and no seabed descriptions. Benchmark: 1,000 image pairs (600 semi-synthetic UAV scenes with synthetic water distortions applied to real shallow-water imagery; 400 fully synthetic through-water scenes), with three independent human annotations over five distortion types. Four assessment prompts (P0 direct instruction to P3 structured task decomposition) applied to all 1,000 images; four diagnosis-guided restoration prompts (R0–R3) on a common 100-image subset. Two restoration-only baselines (InstructIR, PromptFix) used for comparison.

Result: Assessment performance varies strongly by model and prompt, with systematic prediction biases; best pooled balanced accuracy for distortion presence is 0.654. Restoration: best pooled PSNR gain is +0.255 dB, no unified MLLM improves pooled LPIPS, and some outputs alter scene structure or hallucinate content. Baselines: InstructIR gains +0.040 dB pooled PSNR; PromptFix degrades all four pooled metrics under every prompt.

Conclusion: Recognizing shallow-water degradation does not imply reliable physical restoration. The benchmark offers a reproducible zero-shot baseline for future research on unified MLLM-based shallow-water image analysis and restoration.

Abstract: Shallow-water UAV imagery provides centimeter-scale observations of benthic environments, but water-induced distortions complicate interpretation. To our knowledge, we present the first systematic zero-shot benchmark of unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for both assessing water-induced distortions and restoring shallow-water imagery within the same model framework. We evaluate five open-weight unified MLLMs with frozen pretrained weights and without access to paired reference images or descriptions of the true seabed. The benchmark contains 1,000 image pairs: 600 semi-synthetic UAV scenes created by applying synthetic water distortions to real shallow-water imagery and 400 fully synthetic through-water scenes. Three independent human annotations cover five distortion types. We evaluate four assessment prompts (P0-P3), from direct instruction to structured task decomposition, on all 1,000 images and four diagnosis-guided restoration prompts (R0-R3) on a common 100-image subset. Assessment varies substantially by model and prompt, with systematic prediction biases and a best pooled balanced accuracy of 0.654 for distortion presence. The best pooled PSNR gain is +0.255 dB, no unified MLLM improves pooled LPIPS, and some outputs alter scene structure or hallucinate content. Two restoration-only baselines also show limited transfer: InstructIR achieves a pooled PSNR gain of +0.040 dB, while PromptFix degrades all four pooled metrics under every prompt. These results show that recognizing shallow-water degradation does not imply reliable physical restoration and provide a reproducible baseline for future work.

</details>


### [85] [PoseRieFlow: Riemannian Flow Matching for Whole-Body Text-to-Pose Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09692)
*Jan Spindler,Bardia Ardakanian,Patrick Stotko,Reinhard Klein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PoseRieFlow 是一个紧凑的流匹配框架，直接在 SMPL-H 关节旋转的乘积流形上生成文本条件全身 3D 人体姿态，并以 21.6M 参数实现带精细手部关节的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到姿态方法要么忽略具有关节结构的手部，要么在欧氏空间中建模本质非欧氏的旋转，导致运动学保真度不足。

Method: 在 SMPL-H 关节旋转的乘积流形上构建文本条件向量场，沿黎曼测地线将样本从各向同性 SO(3) 高斯先验输运至数据分布；采用部位分词化的 Diffusion Transformer，将 52 个关节聚合为 8 个身体部位 token，为每只手分配专用 token 与非线性投影器，并通过部位专属解码器恢复逐关节分辨率。

Result: 在 PoseScript 上，该方法在全手部生成方法中取得最低的全身运动学误差；在各实用推理预算下，黎曼表述相较其欧氏对应版本均带来一致的运动学增益，而分布与检索类指标与之相当。

Conclusion: 黎曼流形流匹配结合部位分词化建模，可在极小参数规模下实现高保真、含手部关节的文本条件全身姿态生成，运动学精度优于欧氏建模。

Abstract: We present PoseRieFlow, a compact flow-matching framework for text-conditioned generation of whole-body 3D human poses. Prior text-to-pose methods either neglect articulated hands or model rotations in Euclidean space despite their non-Euclidean geometry. PoseRieFlow instead formulates generation directly on the product manifold of SMPL-H joint rotations, learning a text-conditioned vector field that transports samples from an isotropic $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ Gaussian prior to the data distribution along Riemannian geodesics. A part-tokenized Diffusion Transformer aggregates the 52 joints into eight body-part tokens, allocating a dedicated token and nonlinear projector to each hand, and restores per-joint resolution through part-specific decoders, shortening the sequence while improving kinematic fidelity over per-joint tokenization. With only 21.6M trainable parameters, PoseRieFlow supports expressive SMPL-H generation with detailed hand articulation at a fraction of the scale of recent LLM-based generators. On PoseScript, it attains the lowest whole-body kinematic error among all hand-producing methods, with consistent kinematic gains from the Riemannian formulation over its Euclidean counterpart at every practical inference budget, while distributional and retrieval metrics remain comparable between them.

</details>


### [86] [Video2Asset: Online Construction of Persistent 3D Street-Scene Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09661)
*Zihan Zheng,Chunlei Cai,Zhenlong Wu,Linjun Li,Yichen Gong,Xiaoyun Zhang,Qiang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video2Asset：以占用为中间表示、结合历史载体引导与累积证据关联的在线多视角视频3D街景资产构建框架，实现持续更新的可编辑对象级场景，nuScenes上资产碎片化降低15.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3D街景资产构建的核心难点在于：运动与遮挡导致观测部分且间歇，跨时序累积时易发生身份碎片化（同一对象被拆分为多个身份）和跨对象证据混淆。现有方法难以在持续更新的场景中保持身份持久性与证据纯净性。

Method: 1) 采用占用（occupancy）作为中间表示，构建身份感知观测；2) 提出Historical Carrier Guidance用于实例解码，并设计身份编码器融合实例对齐的3D特征与多视角外观；3) 提出Accumulated-Evidence Association，将当前观测与资产记录中累积的身份、几何、外观证据比对，判定身份继承并更新世界坐标状态；4) 依据资产状态从资产库实例化mesh，并与占用导出的静态背景合成，支持可视化、mesh替换、对象删除与轨迹编辑。

Result: 在多个数据集上验证，跨多种占用模型的时序身份一致性与资产质量均有提升，其中在nuScenes配合SGE时资产碎片化降低15.4%。

Conclusion: 通过占用中间表示与证据累积式关联，可在在线多视角视频中维持稳定的对象身份并产出高质量可编辑3D资产；框架对底层占用模型具兼容性，并支持下游场景编辑操作。

Abstract: Online 3D street-scene asset construction turns multi-view video into a continually updated scene of independently editable objects. The central challenge is to accumulate partial, intermittent observations under motion and occlusion without fragmenting identities or mixing evidence across objects. To address this challenge, we propose Video2Asset, an online framework for constructing persistent 3D assets from multi-view video. Using occupancy as an intermediate representation, we construct identity-aware observations with Historical Carrier Guidance for instance decoding and an identity encoder fusing instance-aligned 3D features with multi-view appearance. Accumulated-Evidence Association compares current observations with identity, geometric, and appearance evidence accumulated within asset records to determine identity inheritance and update world-coordinate states. We instantiate meshes from an asset library using these states and combine them with an occupancy-derived static background to support scene visualization, mesh replacement, object deletion, and trajectory editing. Experiments on several datasets demonstrate improvements in temporal identity consistency and asset quality across multiple occupancy models, including a 15.4% reduction in asset fragmentation on nuScenes with SGE.

</details>


### [87] [HiMEM: Hippocampus-Inspired Memory Consolidation, Pruning, and Composition for Vision-Language-Action Models in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09355)
*Boran Zhao,Yanbin Hu,Jun Ye,Zhenxian Hu,Yuxin Zhai,Jin Cui,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HiMEM记忆框架，通过海马体启发的记忆巩固、情节记忆剪枝与灵活记忆组合三种机制，抑制VLA模型在3D复杂场景中的记忆噪声，提升长程任务执行与跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在复杂3D场景中的长程任务执行与跨任务泛化依赖记忆，但现有记忆机制存在"记忆噪声"：存储的情节记忆包含大量关于场景布局、物体配置与机器人自身状态的冗余细节，掩盖了可泛化的操作原理，阻碍跨任务迁移。

Method: 1）海马体启发的记忆巩固机制：将大量情节经验综合为紧凑的核心语义记忆，使模型从实际经验中提炼可泛化原理；2）情节记忆剪枝机制：删除内容已被巩固语义记忆覆盖的冗余情节记忆，提升有效记忆容量；3）灵活记忆组合机制：联合利用多个情节与语义记忆以应对复杂任务场景，增强跨场景泛化。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus上验证了HiMEM在不同VLA主干网络上的有效性；在LIBERO上将pi_0的平均成功率从91.88%提升至98.20%。

Conclusion: 通过语义巩固、情节剪枝与灵活组合的分层记忆设计，HiMEM有效抑制了3D场景下的记忆噪声，在提升长程任务执行能力的同时显著改善跨任务与跨场景泛化性能。

Abstract: Memory plays a crucial role in long-horizon task execution and cross-task generalization for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models operating in complex 3D scenes. However, existing VLA memory mechanisms suffer from memory noise, particularly in complex 3D scenes, as stored episodic memories contain extensive redundant details about scene layouts, object configurations, and robot-specific states, which obscure generalizable manipulation principles and hinder cross-task transfer. To address this challenge, we first introduce a hippocampus-inspired memory consolidation mechanism that synthesizes extensive episodic experiences into a compact set of essential semantic memories, enabling VLA models to derive generalizable principles from practical experience. Second, we propose an episodic memory pruning mechanism that removes redundant episodic memories whose contents are captured by consolidated semantic memories, thereby increasing effective memory capacity and improving long-horizon task execution. Finally, to handle complex task scenarios, we develop a flexible memory composition mechanism that jointly leverages multiple episodic and semantic memories to enhance cross-scenario generalization. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus demonstrate the effectiveness of HiMEM across different VLA backbones, improving the average success rate of pi_0 from 91.88% to 98.20% on LIBERO.

</details>


### [88] [RegNC-Mamba3: Shared Structural Feature Learning Using Non-Causal State Space Principles for Multi-Modal Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09336)
*Zhikang Li,Yan Wu,Xin Hu,Yi Dai,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出非因果状态空间原理(NC-SSP)与由粗到细配准方法RegNC-Mamba3，用于多模态图像配准，在VIS-SAR与VIS-IR上优于检测器类与半稠密方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像配准的核心挑战在于以更高精度、更低计算复杂度提取共享结构特征。Mamba-3的SSP离散化更精确、截断误差更小且保持线性复杂度，但原生SSP面向NLP，不能直接用于图像（因果性假设与图像二维非因果结构不符）。

Method: 1) 设计NC-SSP，消除积分计算对扫描路径的依赖：结合相对权重归一化与对全部环形扫描路径的聚合，构建无长程衰减的全局隐状态，获得比原始Mamba-3更宽的感受野与更高效率；2) 修改NC-SSP以增强跨模态结构特征提取：在隐状态计算与特征交互中，用带不同方向的可学习Log-Gabor核构造复值序列，并对各头进行频域调制；3) 基于NC-SSP构建由粗到细的配准框架RegNC-Mamba3。

Result: 在VIS-SAR和VIS-IR数据集上的实验表明，RegNC-Mamba3优于所对比的基于检测器的方法和半稠密多模态配准方法。

Conclusion: NC-SSP通过路径无关的全局隐状态实现线性复杂度下的宽感受野，结合Log-Gabor频域调制可有效提升跨模态共享结构感知，从而同时改善配准精度与配准质量。

Abstract: The main challenge of multi-modal image registration is shared structural feature extraction with higher accuracy and lower computational complexity. Compared with a State Space Model (SSM), the State Space Principles (SSP) formulation of Mamba-3 uses a more accurate discretization with lower truncation error while maintaining linear complexity. However, vanilla SSP is designed for natural language processing (NLP) and is not directly suitable for images. To address this issue, we propose a novel non-causal SSP (NC-SSP) with shared structure awareness and develop a coarse-to-fine registration method named RegNC-Mamba3. First, NC-SSP is designed to eliminate the dependence of the integral computation on the scanning path. By combining relative-weight normalization with aggregation over all circular scan paths, NC-SSP constructs a globally hidden state without long-range decay, which provides a wider receptive field and higher efficiency than the original Mamba-3. Second, NC-SSP is modified to strengthen the extraction of cross-modal structural features. When computing hidden states and feature interactions, we construct complex-valued sequences in NC-SSP by applying learnable Log-Gabor kernels, with different orientations to each head for frequency-domain modulation. This improves the perception of shared structural information across modalities and increases both registration accuracy and quality. Experiments on VIS-SAR and VIS-IR demonstrate that RegNC-Mamba3 outperforms the compared detector-based and semi-dense multi-modal registration methods.

</details>


### [89] [Dual-Region Slice Aggregation for CT-Based Renal Tumor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09599)
*Yuan Liang,Sourav Bhattacharjee,Abraham Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种区域感知架构用于CT图像上的ccRCC分类：从共享的冻结DINOv3特征图中分别池化肿瘤区与0–10 mm瘤周区域，经共享位置感知切片聚合器与训练集拟合的固定PCA后，用两个标量门控与影像组学特征融合；内部与外部队列AUC为0.8078±0.0154与0.9094±0.0015，较单肿瘤读出分别提升1.50与2.34个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ccRCC是成人最常见的肾癌，需要基于CT的无创分类；现有方法多仅关注肿瘤本体，忽略瘤周微环境信息，且冻结视觉基础模型特征迁移到肾脏CT时缺乏对切片维度和区域结构的适配。

Method: 在共享冻结DINOv3特征图上分别池化肿瘤区域与0–10 mm瘤周上下文；采用共享的位置感知切片聚合器，其后接训练集拟合的固定PCA（不使用测试数据）；所得表征通过两个标量特征门控与影像组学特征融合；并以早/晚PCA及重建任务作消融对照。

Result: KiTS23内部测试AUC 0.8078±0.0154，111例外部队列AUC 0.9094±0.0015；相比匹配的单肿瘤读出，平均AUC分别提升1.50与2.34个百分点；早PCA与重建对照的内部平均AUC更低，而早/晚PCA的外部AUC相近。

Conclusion: 区域感知读出与切片聚合共同作用，是使冻结视觉特征适配肾脏CT的关键；瘤周上下文带来稳定增益，PCA位置主要影响内部性能而对外部泛化影响有限。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) is the most common kidney cancer in adults. We present a region-aware architecture for CT-based ccRCC classification. Tumor and 0--10 mm peritumoral context are pooled separately from shared frozen DINOv3 feature maps. A shared position-aware slice aggregator precedes fixed training-only principal component analysis (PCA). The resulting representations are fused with radiomics using two scalar feature gates. Evaluation on KiTS23 and 111 external cases yields internal test and external AUCs of 0.8078\pm0.0154 and 0.9094\pm0.0015. Compared with matched single-tumor readout, mean AUC increases by 1.50 and 2.34 percentage points, respectively. Early-PCA and reconstruction controls yield lower internal mean AUCs, while external AUC is similar for early and late PCA. These findings highlight the joint role of regional readout and slice aggregation in adapting frozen visual features to renal CT.

</details>


### [90] [Embedding Predictive Adaptation and Refinement for Multi-Modal Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09521)
*Jaeho Cho,Jinsik Bang,Taehwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EPAR 框架，用嵌入预测式适配增强编码器判别性，并用噪声抑制网络细化粗粒度异常图，在 MVTec 3D-AD 与 Eyecandies 上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆库的多模态工业异常检测存在两点局限：(i) 冻结预训练编码器因域差距导致正常/异常特征判别性不足，而现有对比学习式适配难以捕捉精确定位所需的空间关系；(ii) 由 patch 级距离分数插值得到的像素级异常图粗糙，缺乏细粒度细节。

Method: EPAR 含两部分：一是嵌入预测适配，通过预测掩码区域嵌入捕捉空间关系，并结合 patch 级 VIC（方差-不变性-协方差）正则化适配编码器；二是噪声抑制网络，学习每个 patch 特征与其最近正常原型之间的关系性差异（而非异常外观）来细化粗异常图。

Result: 在 MVTec 3D-AD 与 Eyecandies 上 AUPRO@1% 分别提升 +1.8% 和 +1.9%；在少样本设置下表现优异，且基于合成异常学到的关系性信号对未见真实缺陷仍有效。

Conclusion: 通过嵌入预测式适配与关系性差异驱动的图细化，EPAR 同时缓解特征判别性与定位粒度问题，具备较强的实际适用性。

Abstract: Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) has evolved from 2D image-based approaches to multi-modal methods that leverage the complementary nature of RGB images and 3D point clouds. Among these, memory bank-based methods, which identify anomalies by the discrepancy between each query patch feature and its nearest normal prototype, have proven particularly effective. However, two limitations remain: (i) employing frozen pretrained encoders yields insufficient discrimination between normal and anomalous features due to the domain gap, while existing contrastive learning-based adaptation struggles to capture the spatial relationships required for precise localization; and (ii) pixel-level anomaly maps interpolated from patch-level distance scores remain coarse, lacking fine-grained detail. To address these limitations, we propose EPAR (Embedding Predictive Adaptation and Refinement). We adapt encoders by predicting the embeddings of masked regions to capture spatial relationships, together with patch-level Variance-Invariance-Covariance (VIC) regularization, resolving the poor discriminability between normal and anomalous features caused by the domain gap. We further propose a Noise Suppression Network that refines coarse maps by learning the relational discrepancy between each patch feature and its nearest normal prototype, rather than the anomaly's appearance. This relational signal remains valid for unseen real defects, despite being learned from synthetic anomalies. EPAR demonstrates strong practical applicability, excelling in few-shot settings and improving AUPRO@1% on MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies by +1.8% and +1.9%, respectively.

</details>


### [91] [V-STORM: A Structure-Aware Grounded Chain of Thought Dataset for Faithful and Fine-Grained Spatio-Temporal Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09511)
*Dian Jiao,Yuqian Yuan,Jiaqi Zhu,Mingjian Gao,Siwen Tan,Yunqi Cao,Jinhui Li,Sijing Li,Zhenxuan Fan,Haoyang Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: V-STORM 将视频转为时空场景图并生成可溯源的 GCoT 监督，构建 260K 训练集与 3K 人工校验基准；实验揭示"证据定位"与"证据组合"的能力鸿沟，微调后答案准确率与 grounding 忠实度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs 能判断视频中发生了什么，但细粒度时空推理仍薄弱：需精确界定支持证据出现的时间与位置，并将证据组合成连贯推理链。核心障碍在于缺乏可扩展的数据构建与评测管线，以提供将推理步骤与显式时空证据关联的、细粒度且可验证的监督信号。

Method: 提出 V-STORM 结构感知框架：把视频解析为时空场景图（STSG），并沿经过验证的证据路径推导 Grounded Chain of Thought（GCoT），使每个推理步骤可追溯到结构化、可验证的时空证据。基于该框架构建 260K 样本训练集与 3K 样本人工校验基准，均带 GCoT 标注。

Result: 实验显示证据定位与证据组合明显分离：现有 MLLMs 多能识别相关时空线索，却无法将其整合为正确的多步推理，该瓶颈被仅看答案的评测所掩盖；使用 V-STORM 微调可同时显著提升答案准确率与 grounding 忠实度。

Conclusion: 结构化的 STSG→GCoT 监督能有效训练忠实、以证据为凭的视频推理；数据集、基准与代码将公开。

Abstract: While MLLMs can often identify what happens in a video, they still struggle with fine-grained spatio-temporal reasoning, which requires precise grounding when and where the supporting evidence appears and composing such evidence into a coherent reasoning chain. This challenge is exacerbated by the lack of scalable data construction and evaluation pipelines that provide granular, verifiable supervision linking reasoning steps to explicit spatio-temporal evidence.To address this gap, we present V-STORM, a structure-aware framework that transforms videos into Spatio-Temporal Scene Graphs (STSGs) and derives Grounded Chain of Thought (GCoT) from validated evidence paths, making each reasoning step traceable to structured and verifiable spatio-temporal evidence.Under this framework, we construct a 260K-sample training set and a 3K-sample human-verified benchmark, both featuring GCoT annotations that link reasoning to explicit spatio-temporal evidence.Experiments reveal a clear separation between evidence localization and evidence composition: current MLLMs can often identify relevant spatio-temporal cues, yet still fail to integrate them into correct multi-step reasoning, exposing a bottleneck that answer-only evaluation largely obscures. Moreover, fine-tuning with V-STORM substantially improves both answer accuracy and grounding faithfulness, validating its effectiveness as supervision for faithful and evidence-grounded video reasoning. Our dataset, benchmark, and source code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [92] [HaNDF: Object-Conditioned Geometric Neural Hand Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08578)
*Zuhao Liu,Melih Darcan,Zhengdi Yu,Riza Alp Guler,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HaNDF：以物体为条件的神经距离场作为手部关节空间（乘积流形）上的数据驱动先验，用于建模合理的手-物交互构型，并支持位姿投影、物体条件推断、位姿精修、遮挡重建与操控合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无条件生成先验虽能建模人体姿态，但手是常与物体交互的柔性关节体，需要建模"给定交互物体下合理的手部姿态"的条件先验，而无条件先验无法刻画这种物体诱导的约束。

Method: 构建几何感知的物体条件神经距离场，将合理手部关节表示为其零水平集；借助黎曼几何工具（乘积流形上）(i) 将合理手部表示为物体诱导神经场的零水平集，(ii) 将给定位姿投影到该场；并可通过优化推断交互物体这一合理条件。

Result: 大量评测显示HaNDF为手-物交互提供了灵活而强大的先验，适用于位姿精修、遮挡下重建以及物理合理的操控合成等应用。

Conclusion: 以物体为条件的神经距离场可作为手-物交互的有效生成先验，兼具灵活性与通用性，可扩展到多种下游任务。

Abstract: We propose, HaNDF, object-conditional neural distance fields as data-driven priors for modeling the plausible hand-object configurations, in the product manifold of hand articulations. While recent unconditional generative priors showed success in modeling human pose, hands are versatile articulated bodies often found in interaction with other objects. This makes it crucial to develop a generative prior which can model the plausible hand poses conditioned on the object under interaction. To this end, our HaNDF proposes a geometry-aware, object-conditioned neural distance field, representing the plausible articulations in the zero-level set of a conditional field. We leverage tools from Riemannian geometry to (i) represent plausible hands in the zero level-set of an object-induced neural field, and (ii) project a given pose onto this field. Using HaNDF, we can also optimize for a plausible condition, determining the object under interaction. Our extensive evaluations demonstrate that our HaNDF provides a flexible and powerful prior for hand-object interaction, suitable for applications such as pose refinement, reconstruction under occlusion, and physically plausible manipulation synthesis.

</details>


### [93] [From Geometry to Function: A Multi-Task LiDAR Benchmark Dataset for Semantic and Functional Parsing of Urban Facades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08645)
*Abdiaziz Muse,Tooba Javed,Tao Pu,Jiju Poovvancheri,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HFX3D：约4.92亿点的大规模城市立面LiDAR基准，覆盖加拿大哈利法克斯26栋建筑的四季、昼夜与多天气，首个人工标注语义+实例+每实例功能属性三级标签的立面数据集，并给出点/图/卷积/Transformer四类架构的基准结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR基准止步于几何与语义分割，缺乏对建筑构件功能角色的推理支持；而城市数字孪生、遗产建模与可持续城市分析亟需“功能感知”的3D理解。

Method: 构建约4.92亿点、26栋哈利法克斯建筑立面点云，跨四季、昼夜与多天气采集；提供语义、实例、逐实例功能属性三层级标注，采用“几何引导启发式+专家校验”的半自动标注流水线；对点基、图基、卷积与Transformer架构进行统一基准评测。

Result: 在严重类别不平衡下语义分割已具挑战性；重复性结构的实例分离与基于几何的功能推理难度显著更高。数据集为据作者所知首个将几何、语义与功能标签配对的立面数据集。

Conclusion: HFX3D为功能感知的3D城市理解建立了新基准基础，并公开项目页与代码。

Abstract: Understanding building facades is central to urban digital twins, heritage modelling, and sustainable city analysis, yet existing LiDAR benchmarks stop at geometry and semantic segmentation and offer little support for reasoning about the functional role of each architectural element. We introduce HFX3D, a large-scale urban facade benchmark of approximately 492 million LiDAR points from 26 buildings in Halifax, Canada, captured across four seasons, day and night, and varied weather. Each facade is annotated at three tiers, semantic, instance, and per-instance functional attributes, making it, to our knowledge, the first facade dataset to pair geometry and semantics with functional labels. Annotations are produced by a semi-automatic pipeline that combines geometry-guided heuristics with expert validation. We benchmark representative point-based, graph-based, convolutional, and transformer architectures and find that semantic segmentation is already challenging under severe class imbalance, while instance separation of repetitive structures and functional reasoning from geometry are substantially harder. The benchmark establishes a new foundation for function-aware 3D urban understanding. Project page: https://jijup.github.io/hfx3d-benchmark/ ; code: https://github.com/jijup/hfx3d-benchmark

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [ROSE: Risk-Aware Orthogonal Subspace Navigation for Lifelong Knowledge Editing in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09770)
*Lingyun Song,Ziyao Chen,Kang Pan,Xiaolin Han,Yifei Zhang,Xiaoqi Wang,Yudai Pan,Xuequn Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ROSE框架，通过风险感知的正交子空间导航实现多模态大模型的终身知识编辑，缓解局部性侵蚀与灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs需持续更新事实知识，但终身编辑中存在严重的局部性侵蚀（Locality Erosion）与灾难性遗忘：顺序编辑会干扰预训练能力及此前的更新。

Method: （1）将参数更新限制在已编辑子空间的正交补中，以抑制遗忘；（2）引入风险引导机制，用动态计算的风险掩码保护对模型稳定性至关重要的事实参数子空间；（3）推理时采用多粒度知识整合策略，包括原型锚定的语义门控与扰动驱动的子空间融合，从而对相关查询精确激活编辑知识，同时减少对无关输入的干扰。

Result: 在两个主流多模态基准上，五项关键指标上与SOTA方法相当；在1000次顺序更新下保持出色的稳定性。

Conclusion: ROSE为MLLM的终身知识编辑提供了兼顾可塑性与稳定性的有效方案，可在大规模顺序编辑下维持模型原有能力与编辑效果。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) require continuous factual updates to stay current, yet lifelong knowledge editing remains a daunting challenge. The primary obstacle is the severe Locality Erosion and Catastrophic Forgetting, where sequential edits inevitably interfere with pre-trained capabilities and prior updates. In this paper, we propose \textbf{ROSE}, a \textbf{R}isk-aware \textbf{O}rthogonal \textbf{S}ubspace navigation framework for lifelong knowledge \textbf{E}diting. ROSE addresses forgetting by aiming to restrict parameter updates to the orthogonal complement of previously edited subspaces. Crucially, it introduces a risk-guided mechanism that utilizes a dynamically computed risk mask to safeguard specific factual parameter subspaces essential for model stability. During inference, ROSE employs a multi-granularity knowledge integration strategy, featuring prototype-anchored semantic gating and perturbation-driven subspace fusion to precisely activate edited knowledge for relevant queries while limiting interference on unrelated inputs. Comprehensive experiments on two prominent multimodal benchmarks show that ROSE is competitive with state-of-the-art approaches across five key metrics, while maintaining outstanding stability over $1,000$ sequential updates. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunlitsong/ROSE.

</details>


### [95] [Compaction, decay and reinforcement in a two-tier conversational memory: a paired evaluation against retrieval-augmented generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09807)
*Osamudiamen Edogun,Joshua Obigwe,Irfan Mehmood*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在LoCoMo基准上比较“对话压缩为原子事实”与“检索原始对话记录”两种记忆策略：压缩在四类可比任务上以更少token领先6.8点，但计入对抗集后优势消失；其得失取决于答案是否需要原文措辞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话智能体需要决定“记住什么”。RAG保留完整对话记录并检索，另一种做法是把每轮对话压缩成原子事实再检索，但两者优劣及其适用边界缺乏同条件、可统计检验的对照研究。

Method: 在LoCoMo上做双臂对照：两种方法均在同一年窗口内回答全部1,985题，使用相同存储、相同生成器与评判器，并将两方预测对齐到同一行，从而在512个预测不一致的题目上做McNemar精确检验；另按参考答案所需原文字数分组分析胜负结构，并报告四项负结果及其统计功效计算。

Result: 四类可比任务上压缩法71.4%对64.6%，领先6.8点（p=5.5e-08），且提示token少34%；纳入全部五类（含对抗集）后领先降至2.0点且不显著，因基线在对抗集上领先14.6点。领先幅度随参考答案所需原文字数下降：1–2个参考词时领先9.6点，超过8个词时落后3.1点，token重叠率同向变化，说明并非评判器所致。

Conclusion: 压缩保留事实“断言了什么”而丢弃“如何表述”，因此在答案为取值型时占优、在答案为措辞型时失利。方法选择应视查询类型而定，而非一概采用压缩式记忆。

Abstract: A conversational agent that remembers has to decide what to keep. Retrieval-augmented generation keeps the transcript and searches it; the alternative is to compact each turn into atomic facts and search those. We compare the two on LoCoMo, with both arms answering all 1,985 questions in one window, against the same stores, with the same generator and judge, and both predictions on one row, which licenses McNemar's exact test on the 512 questions where they disagree.
  On the four categories comparable work reports, compaction scores 71.4 per cent against 64.6, a lead of 6.8 points (p = 5.5e-08) at 34 per cent fewer prompt tokens. On all five, including the adversarial set comparable work drops, the lead falls to 2.0 and stops being significant, because the baseline leads adversarial by 14.6.
  That loss has a structure. Our lead falls as the reference answer demands more source wording, 9.6 points ahead at one or two reference words and 3.1 behind past eight, with token overlap moving the same way, so the judge is not the cause. Compaction keeps what a fact asserts and discards how it was said, so it wins where the answer is a value and loses where it is a phrasing. We also report four negative results with their power calculations.

</details>


### [96] [Language as Trait, Politeness as Strategy: Controlled Tests of Linguistic Effects in LLM Social-Deduction Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09768)
*Yuchen Li,Lisa Liu,Runqi Zou,Shuxuan Kuang,Qingquan Zhang,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在LURK Werewolf框架中只改写语句的语言实现（语言繁简范围与礼貌策略），检验其是否改变其他LLM智能体对说话者的信任判断与行动；4335次重放显示两种操控均不改变信任，对投票和夜间行动也无经过多重比较校正后显著的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过对话协作，同一信息可用更简单或更复杂的语言、直白或礼貌地表达。作者希望检验这种"语言实现"层面的差异是否会改变其他智能体对说话者的判断与行为，即语言表面形式在多智能体交互中是否具有因果作用。

Method: 采用LURK Werewolf框架，将每句话的言语行为谓词（speech-act predicate）与其自然语言实现分离。仅改写自然语言实现，并核验游戏内容保持不变；从保存的检查点重放每个游戏日，并与重新采样的对照组比较。自变量包括：在说话者全部消息上一致地改变语言范围（linguistic range），以及逐句改变礼貌策略。共30局游戏、4335次重放。

Result: 两种操控均未改变其他智能体对说话者的信任水平；对投票和夜间行动的影响在多重比较校正后均不显著。最大的未校正效应是：将语言范围偏离中性改述会使其他玩家约多4个百分点的投票发生改变。在自然对局中，88%的威胁面子言语（face-threatening speech）无论角色如何均采用直接公开（bald on-record）策略。

Conclusion: 在受控重放条件下，语言表面形式（繁简与礼貌）对LLM智能体的信任判断及集体决策影响很小，不足以为多智能体协调带来稳健的因果效应；同时自然对局中的礼貌策略分布高度集中于直接表达。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents coordinate through conversation, and the same message can be phrased in simpler or more complex language, bluntly or politely. We test whether such differences change how other agents judge and act, using the LURK Werewolf framework, which separates each utterance's speech-act predicate from its natural-language realization. We rewrite only the realization, check that the game content is preserved, and replay each game day from saved checkpoints against a re-sampled control. We vary linguistic range consistently across a speaker's messages and politeness strategy per utterance. Across 30 games and 4,335 replays, neither manipulation changes the trust other agents place in the speaker, and no effect on votes or night actions survives correction for multiple comparisons; the largest uncorrected effect is that shifting range away from a neutral paraphrase changes about 4 percentage points more of the other players' votes. In natural play, 88% of face-threatening speech is bald on-record regardless of role.

</details>


### [97] [Richness Is Not Evenness: Ordered Renyi Crossings under Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08892)
*Pranjal Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 递归自训练中，随训练采样温度升高，Renyi熵的变化会在不同温度处依次由"下降"转为"上升"，顺序为min-entropy→collision entropy→Shannon entropy→观测丰富度；该区间内词型数下降而最高频词主导性减弱，且该顺序可由多设置复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型崩溃研究多关注词汇缩减，但对"熵在不同信息论度量上以何种顺序、在何温度发生方向反转"缺乏系统刻画；作者希望揭示递归自训练中多样性损失并非均匀发生，并澄清熵变化与似然（质量）之间的错位关系。

Method: 在自身输出上对英文GPT-2微调九代，并改变训练数据采样温度；对每个模型用固定采样器统一评测。计算多种Renyi熵（min-entropy、collision entropy、Shannon entropy）与观测词型丰富度，定位各度量由损失转为增益的交叉温度并检验其排序。稳健性包括更细温度网格、不同测量采样器、固定词预算、五个新训练随机种子；扩展到另外四种设置（含印地语，仅在插值根处）、八种文本表示下的13种测量中的12种。另用下一词身份分析与类别模拟分离解码与采样效应，并检验人类文本微调及似然变化与熵顺序的关系。

Result: 随训练温度上升，Renyi熵变化按min-entropy→collision entropy→Shannon entropy→观测丰富度的顺序依次反转。T=1未截断训练处于中间区间：在13,000词处，第9代词型数比第0代少24%，但collision entropy高0.91比特。该排序在更细网格、不同测量采样器、固定词预算与五个新种子下均保持；在另外四种设置中（印地语仅于插值根处）及八种文本表示下13种测量中的12种中，min-entropy同样先于丰富度交叉。人类文本微调可产生相同分化而似然改善；在测试设置中，没有任何单一排序的符号能可靠指示似然变差。

Conclusion: 递归自训练导致的多样性变化具有度量依赖的分层结构：粗粒度（min-entropy）先受损/先恢复，细粒度丰富度最后。熵顺序是经验现象而非解码或采样的人为产物。熵与似然（质量）并非同向指标，故建议在明确词预算下报告熵剖面，并搭配独立的质量度量。

Abstract: Recursive self-training of language models, a setting studied in model collapse, can reduce vocabulary while making frequent words, mostly function words, less dominant. We fine-tune English GPT-2 on its own output for nine generations, vary the training-data sampling temperature, and measure every model with one fixed sampler. As training temperature rises, Renyi entropy changes turn from loss to gain at distinct temperatures: min-entropy first, then collision entropy, Shannon entropy and observed richness. Between the outer crossings, word types fall while the most-common word becomes less dominant. Untruncated training at T = 1 lies in this interval: at 13,000 words, generation 9 has 24% fewer types than generation 0 yet collision entropy is 0.91 bits higher. The ordering persists with a finer grid, different measurement samplers, a fixed word budget and five fresh training seeds. Min-entropy again crosses before richness in four further settings (in Hindi only at interpolated roots) and in 12 of 13 distinct measurements under eight text representations. Next-token identities and a categorical simulation isolate decoding and sampling effects; the crossing order remains empirical. Fine-tuning on human text can produce the same split while likelihood improves, and no single order's sign reliably identifies worse likelihood across the tested settings. We recommend reporting entropy profiles at stated budgets with an independent quality measure.

</details>


### [98] [RL-ATTACK: A Generalized Black-Box Adversarial Attack on Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08711)
*Taikun Chen,Yue Zhang,Wei Qian,Hui Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的可训练黑盒对抗攻击框架，通过从目标模型学习来生成定制化对抗变换，在NLI和生成式问答任务上实现更低攻击后准确率、更高查询效率与更好语义保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对对抗扰动存在脆弱性，可利用攻击发现脆弱点以提升安全性。但在梯度不可得的黑盒场景下，现有方法依赖外部知识猜测对抗变换，忽略了目标模型自身提供的信息，导致攻击效果与效率受限。

Method: 构建可训练的黑盒强化学习框架，通过与目标模型交互进行学习，训练攻击者生成针对性的对抗变换；并在自然语言推理与生成式问答任务上评测，同时探索多种奖励设计与训练设置。

Result: NLI任务上20次查询内将攻击后准确率降至2%；对生成式语言模型，训练后的攻击者始终优于未训练攻击者，并在6个设置中有4个胜过强优化基线；不同奖励设计与训练设置均无一致占优方案。

Conclusion: 从目标模型学习的强化学习式黑盒攻击有效且查询高效、语义保持良好，可作为提升模型安全性的手段；但奖励与训练配置缺乏普遍最优解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit vulnerabilities to adversarial perturbations that can induce unintended responses. These vulnerabilities can be identified via intentional attacks and subsequently used to improve model safety. In black-box settings where gradients are inaccessible, existing methods tend to guess adversarial transformations using external knowledge, ignoring the information provided by the target model. To mitigate this, we propose a trainable black-box framework based on reinforcement learning, which learns from the target model to generate tailored adversarial transformations. We demonstrate that our approach achieves lower post-attack accuracy, greater query efficiency and better semantic preservation on both natural language inference and generative Q&A tasks. On natural language inference, it drives post-attack accuracy to 2% within 20 queries. Against generative language models, our trained attacker consistently achieves lower post-attack accuracy than the untrained attacker and wins in 4 out of 6 settings when compared to a strong optimization-based baseline. We further experiment with multiple reward designs and training settings, and we find that no design or setting is consistently dominant. Our code is available at https://github.com/taikunudel/RL-ATTACK.

</details>


### [99] [Khmer PFGen-Bench: Building a Judge-Free Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation in a Low-Resource Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09222)
*Mengsay Loem,Kentaro Imajo,Ryota Ozaki,Masanori Hirano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Khmer PFGen-Bench，据称是首个针对高棉语开放式多句生成的无评判器（judge-free）基准，通过改编日语的 PFGen-Bench 评分器实现对低资源语言的可扩展评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式生成基准普遍依赖大语言模型作为评判器，而 LLM 评判器在低资源语言中的可靠性存疑，因此需要无需 LLM 评判的基准方案。

Method: 将 PFGen-Bench 的评分器迁移至高棉语：使用关键词规则与 LLM 生成参考文本的 n-gram 统计对多句回答打分。该方法要求参考文本兼具有效性与多样性，作者因此系统考察参考文本构建方式，对比后训练模型与其基座变体的参考质量，以及混合不同模型家族对多样性的影响。

Result: 后训练模型生成的参考文本有效性更高但多样性更低，基座变体则相反；在相同参考数量下，跨模型家族混合可提升多样性。使用来自九个家族的后训练模型参考文本时，基准给出的模型排名在不同参考集间保持稳定，并与母语者评分一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为在其他低资源语言中构建无评判器基准提供了可操作的设计经验，尤其强调了参考文本有效性与多样性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Benchmarks for open-ended generation often rely on large language model (LLM) judges, whose reliability in low-resource languages is uncertain. We introduce Khmer PFGen-Bench, to our knowledge the first judge-free benchmark for multi-sentence answers to questions composed directly in Khmer, a low-resource language. Khmer PFGen-Bench adapts the scorer of PFGen-Bench, a benchmark for Japanese that scores responses with keyword rules and n-gram statistics from LLM-generated references. This scoring requires references that are both valid and varied, which LLMs may not produce in Khmer, so we examine how to build such references. We find that post-trained models can give more valid but less varied references than their base variants, and that mixing model families increases variety for the same number of references. Using references from post-trained models in nine families, the benchmark yields model rankings that are stable across reference sets and consistent with a native speaker's ratings. These findings offer guidance for building judge-free benchmarks in other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [100] [Calibrated concept profiles: making the reading of a model's neurons a measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09196)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文把"直接读取神经元"提升为一种可重复的测量：通过声明刺激总体、生成两次抽样的提示电池，计算以神经元自身波动为单位的"校准概念画像"，并为每个数字配噪声单位、每次比较配随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究从直接读神经元转向"读第二个模型"（探针、稀疏自编码器、crosscoder），虽解决了含义混淆、阈值无单位、结论不可迁移等问题，却丧失了直接读取的独特优点：中间无训练物、神经元本身被命名、读数可重复可比较。作者希望恢复这些优点而不回到旧缺陷。

Method: 1) 将神经元激活视为刺激总体上的分布，显式声明该总体：目标构念周边的各因素按参考语料中的出现率抽样并记录；2) 由声明的分布生成提示电池并独立抽取两次；3) 由电池计算校准概念画像——每个神经元一个数，以该神经元在普通输入上的自身离散度为单位；两次抽样为每个数字赋予噪声单位；4) 在读取画像前用一组固定检查对电池在模型上做"许可"验证，并测量画像跨训练检查点可分辨的最小变化量。

Result: 在 OpenCoder-1.5B 上以 Python 实现，并沿跨越两万亿训练 token 的检查点追踪：画像给出了替代方法未报告的内容——每个数字的单位、每次比较的随机水平、以及各检查点上哪些神经元承载某一构念。

Conclusion: 直接读取可以成为严格测量；电池、代码及所有结果背后的数据均已开放，支持重复与比较。

Abstract: Interpretability has largely moved from reading a model's neurons directly to reading a second model trained on the first: a probe, a sparse autoencoder, a crosscoder. The move answered real faults -- mixed meanings, thresholds without a unit, readings that did not transfer from one study to the next -- but gave up what a direct reading alone provides: nothing trained in between, the neurons themselves named, and a reading that can be repeated and compared. Here the direct reading is made a measurement.
  A neuron's activation varies with the prompt, so its reading is a distribution over a population of stimuli. We declare that population: every factor around a target construct is drawn at its rate in a reference corpus and logged.
  From the declared distribution we generate a battery of prompts, and draw it twice. From the battery we compute the calibrated concept profile, one number per neuron, stated in units of the neuron's own spread on ordinary input; the two draws give every number a noise unit. Before the profile is read, a fixed set of checks licenses the battery on the model, and the smallest change the profile can resolve across training checkpoints is measured.
  Realised for Python on OpenCoder-1.5B and followed across checkpoints spanning two trillion training tokens, the profile gives what the alternatives do not report: a unit on every number, a chance level for every comparison, and which neurons carry a construct at every checkpoint.
  The battery, the code and the data behind every result are released.

</details>


### [101] [MARS: Training Multilingual Search Agents for Retriever-Free Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09090)
*Rui Qi,Yunlong Liang,Yufeng Chen,Jinan Xu,Fandong Meng,Kaiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 MARS：免检索器的多语言搜索智能体，通过强化学习直接与原始语料交互（DCI），用于多语言 RAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言 RAG 依赖学习式检索器与基于相似度的排序，易受跨语言检索错误与索引开销影响；转向 retriever-free 的 DCI 范式后，瓶颈变为多语言搜索动作生成——精确匹配对实体表层形式敏感，且仅有结果级监督对多轮搜索指导有限。

Method: MARS 采用强化学习训练的多语言搜索智能体：(1) 语料原生实体接地（corpus-native entity grounding），从语料证据中恢复可靠的词法锚点，缓解证据检索失败；(2) 冗余感知搜索奖励（redundancy-aware search reward），鼓励简洁有效的搜索轨迹，缓解多语言 RL 训练崩溃。

Result: 实验表明 MARS 稳定优于强检索式基线及 DCI 基线，并展现稳健的跨语言泛化能力，同时显著减少冗余的语料交互。

Conclusion: 免检索器的 DCI 多语言搜索结合 RL 与实体接地、冗余感知奖励，可有效替代传统多语言检索管线，在性能、跨语言泛化与交互效率上同时获益。

Abstract: Existing multilingual retrieval-augmented generation relies heavily on learned retrievers and similarity-based ranking from multilingual collections, which are vulnerable to cross-lingual retrieval errors and indexing overhead. In this paper, we explore retriever-free multilingual search, where the model directly interacts with raw corpora through executable shell commands. This paradigm shifts the bottleneck to multilingual search action generation, where exact search is sensitive to entity surface forms and outcome-only supervision provides limited guidance for multi-turn search. To address these challenges, we propose MARS, a multilingual search agent in direct corpus interaction (DCI) with reinforcement learning (RL). MARS introduces corpus-native entity grounding to recover reliable lexical anchors from corpus evidence, mitigating failure to retrieve evidence. Meanwhile, we present a redundancy-aware search reward to encourage concise and effective search trajectories, addressing training collapse in RL of multilingual scenarios. Experiments show that \ours~consistently outperforms strong retrieval-based and DCI baselines, while demonstrating robust cross-lingual generalization and substantially reducing redundant corpus interactions.

</details>


### [102] [Contextuality and Contradictions: Exploring Real-World Identifications of AI-Generated Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08704)
*Michelle Morgenstern,Julia Witte Zimmerman,Tabia Tanzin Prama,Kristine Harootunian,June Clark,Bryn Loftness,Calla Beauregard,Juniper Lovato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文结合定性编码与词汇变化的计算分析，考察英语维基百科编辑与学生日常识别AI生成文本的实践，发现识别依据多样且相互矛盾、动态且依赖语境，其真正触发点常是"语用失当"而非具体文本特征，识别行为因而成为维护语言社会规范的话语资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成内容已渗透几乎所有人类互动领域，正式与非正式的AI检测实践成为日常；文本的人类来源不再理所当然，这一话语景观的变化亟需考察真实世界中人们如何识别AI文本。

Method: 以语言人类学关于语言与交际的视角为理论基础，对英语维基百科编辑与学生两类群体进行定性编码，并结合针对词汇变化的计算分析，研究其AI识别实践与话语。

Result: AI识别所依据的证据类型多样：从离散的文本特征、对内容的判断，到循环式意义建构主张及对作者道德品格的推定；这些识别是矛盾的、动态的、依语境而变的。实际触发识别的往往不是某一具体文本要素，而是语用失当——即文本中出现的要素与该语境下认为恰当的要素之间 perceived mismatch。

Conclusion: 现实中的AI识别并非中立地指出"类AI"特征，而是被用作维护和界定社会语言规范（何者可说、何为恰当、何者为人）的话语资源；将"类AI性"视为文本的无语境属性的系统与工具，可能在规模化应用中继承并强制执行其训练语境的规范。

Abstract: As AI-generated content spreads into nearly every domain of human interaction, practices and discourses of AI detection, both formal and informal, have become an everyday part of how people interact with text and with each other. In this paper, we explore the real-world practices and discourses of AI identification that have emerged in response to a shifting discursive landscape where the human provenance of a text can no longer be taken for granted. We pair qualitative coding with computational analysis of lexical change, grounded in linguistic anthropological perspectives on language and communication, to investigate AI identifications among English Wikipedia editors and students. We find that these identifications draw on a diversity of evidentiary types, from discrete textual features and judgments about content to circular sense-making claims and presumptions about the author's moral character, and that they are contradictory, dynamic, and contextually dependent. In practice, what often motivates an AI identification is not the presence of any specific textual element, but pragmatic infelicity: a perceived mismatch between the elements present and those deemed appropriate within the text's particular context. Consequently, real-world identifications of AI-generated writing do not merely neutrally point out the perceived presence of "AI-like" features, but can be leveraged as a discursive resource to maintain and define sociolinguistic norms: what can be said in a given context, what is appropriate, and, ultimately, what is human. Further, the design and implementation of systems and tools that treat AI-likeness as a context-free property of text may therefore inherit, and enforce at scale, the norms of whichever contexts they were trained on.

</details>


### [103] [Using an LLM to Tackle Difficult Cases in NER for Nepali Tweets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08967)
*Claire Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在尼泊尔语推文的低资源社交媒体NER任务中，用微调PLM的token概率将测试集划分为"简单/困难"子集，并以16种prompt配置评估LLM，发现语义相似度选例与增加示例数使F1提升约10分；LLM在全集和简单集上不如PLM，但在困难集上超越PLM，二者结合后整体分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的社交媒体NER难度大。LLM在NER上通常不及预训练语言模型（PLM），但可能擅长处理PLM易失败的样本；因此需要系统考察LLM在PLM困难样本上的表现及可行的prompt优化策略。

Method: 以尼泊尔语推文数据集为对象，基于微调PLM的token概率将测试集划分为easy与hard两类；在hard子集上用LLM测试16种prompt配置（包括基于语义相似度的示例选择与不同示例数量）。

Result: 基于语义相似度选择示例并增加示例数量，可使F1较基线配置提升近10个点。LLM在完整测试集和easy子集上落后于PLM，但在hard子集上超过PLM；将两模型结合后取得更高分数。

Conclusion: PLM与LLM在NER上具有互补性：按难度划分数据可揭示LLM在困难样本上的优势，示例选择与数量是prompt设计的关键因素，模型集成可提升低资源社交媒体NER的整体性能。

Abstract: Named entity recognition (NER) is challenging for social media data in a low-resource language. LLMs often lag behind pretrained language models (PLMs) in NER performance, but they show promise in handling cases that PLMs struggle with. Using a dataset of Nepali tweets, we split the test set into "easy" and "hard" cases using token probabilities from a fine-tuned pretrained language model (PLM). We then evaluate an LLM on the hard test cases using 16 different prompt configurations. We find that selecting examples based on semantic similarity and increasing the number of examples improves F1 score by almost 10 points from the baseline configuration. Although the LLM underperforms the PLM on the full and easy test sets, it surpasses the PLM on the hard test set, resulting in an improved score when the two models are combined.

</details>


### [104] [From Procedures to Permissions: Learning Precise Subagent Authorization from Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08905)
*Wenkai Yu,Ning Ding,Wentao Zhang,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出利用Agent Skills中编码的过程性知识作为权限授权的监督来源，通过合成任务与分步流程、在交互环境中执行子代理委派，并用验证过的轨迹构建训练数据，从而提升子代理的工具与工作区资源授权精度及任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言代理处理日益复杂的任务时需多代理协同，不仅要决定子代理做什么，还要决定其可访问的工具与工作区资源。现有代理系统虽能控制工具与执行环境，但不直接规定每个被委派子任务所需资源；且仅凭任务结果与执行轨迹难以判断子代理是否获得了恰当权限，缺乏有效监督信号。

Method: 观察到Agent Skills编码了描述每步所需操作、工具与文件的过程性知识，可作为授权的天然监督来源。据此从Skills合成任务与分步流程，在交互环境中通过子代理委派执行，并利用验证过的轨迹构建用于任务执行与授权的训练数据。

Result: 在ClawArena-Team上，方法使工具权限精度提升13.4个百分点，工作区权限精度提升24.1个百分点；对Qwen3.5-35B-A3B整体任务成功率提升11.6个百分点。在Terminal-Bench 2.1与SkillsBench上与现有代理数据合成方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Agent Skills中嵌入的过程性知识可为精确的子代理编排提供监督，这是仅靠任务结果无法提供的。

Abstract: As language agents tackle increasingly complex tasks, they increasingly rely on multiple agents to coordinate specialized work, requiring decisions not only about what each subagent should do but also which tools and workspace resources it should access. Existing agent systems provide mechanisms for controlling tools and execution environments, but do not directly specify the resources required by each delegated subtask. Moreover, task outcomes and execution trajectories alone provide limited supervision for determining whether a subagent has received appropriate permissions. We observe that Agent Skills encode procedural knowledge describing the operations, tools, and files required at each step, providing a natural source of supervision for authorization. Building on this observation, we synthesize tasks and step-by-step procedures from Skills, execute them through subagent delegation in interactive environments, and use verified trajectories to construct training data for task execution and authorization. On ClawArena-Team, our method improves tool permission precision by 13.4 percentage points and workspace permission precision by 24.1 percentage points, while increasing overall task success by 11.6 percentage points for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. On Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SkillsBench, our method remains competitive with existing agent data synthesis approaches. These results show that the procedural knowledge embedded in Agent Skills provides a source of supervision for precise subagent orchestration that task outcomes alone cannot provide.

</details>


### [105] [Semantic Conditional Similarity (SCS): A Formal Mathematical Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08778)
*Mehmet Murat Albayrakoğlu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出语义条件相似度（SCS），在比较两份文档时先通过集合论运算剔除与第三份文档共享的语义成分，从而在多文档场景下分离各来源的边际语义贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多文档场景中，成对语义文本相似度（STS）输出单一标量，无法将各来源的边际语义贡献与其共享词汇区分开；且相似度形式化中默认的度量空间公理限制了角色差异化场景的表达能力。

Method: 将比较域从无序文档对扩展为有序条件三元组（目标、参考、条件文档），以集合论差集运算减去与第三文档共享的内容。提出四项设计：方向性划分（横向源间关系与纵向源到目标关系）、条件化带来的角色依赖不对称性、跨文档条件化（区别于C-STS的文档内条件化）、无需阈值即可划分共享与残余内容。该框架由n→1经纵向角色反转推广至1→n，并通过源侧与目标侧条件化的独立组合推广至m→n。

Result: 框架覆盖1→1、1→n、n→1及m→n全部文档基数情形；SCS得分按序数解释，反映边际贡献的排序而非绝对度量值。

Conclusion: 在多文档、角色差异化的场景中，相似度形式化常用的度量空间公理约束了表达力；将比较域扩展至条件三元组可恢复成对对称比较所抑制的信息。

Abstract: Semantic Textual Similarity (STS) operates on document pairs, returning a single scalar per comparison. In a multi-document setting where several sources may have contributed to one or more targets, pairwise STS cannot disentangle each source's marginal semantic contribution from their shared vocabulary. In this paper, Semantic Conditional Similarity (SCS) is introduced as a conditional form of STS that computes similarity between two documents after eliminating semantic elements shared with a third via set-theoretic operations. SCS makes four contributions: 1) it introduces directionality, partitioning pairwise relations into lateral (inter-source) and vertical (source-to-target) classes; 2) conditioning induces role-dependent asymmetry by extending the comparison domain from unordered pairs to ordered, conditioned triples of a target, a reference, and a conditioning document; 3) SCS introduces inter-document conditioning by subtracting shared content from a third document, complementing the intra-document conditioning in recent C-STS work; and 4) the set-theoretic decomposition achieves threshold-free partitioning of shared and residual content, requiring no arbitrary similarity cutoff. Although developed for the $n \to 1$ configuration, the framework extends to $1 \to n$ via vertical role reversal and to $m \to n$ via independent composition of source- and target-side conditioning. With the established $1 \to 1$ case, these span the full spectrum of document cardinality. SCS scores should be interpreted ordinally, as rankings of marginal contributions rather than absolute measurements. The broader implication is that metric space axioms commonly assumed in similarity formalizations constrain expressiveness in role-differentiated multi-document settings, and that extending the domain to conditioned triples can recover information that pairwise-symmetric comparisons suppress.

</details>


### [106] [Are These Programmers Ripe Enough to Eat? Evaluating How LLMs Handle Lexical Knowledge Conflicts in Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09785)
*Dongxu Wu,Sara Court,Micha Elsner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建受控的汉英翻译任务，将提示中给定的新词义视为上下文知识（CK），系统考察词频、句子语境和提示框架对九个模型词汇选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部词汇资源可帮助LLM翻译低资源语言与专业领域文本，但这些资源可能与模型已有的词汇先验知识冲突。作者希望隔离并量化这一冲突。

Method: 构建受控的普通话—英语翻译任务：给熟悉的源词赋予类内和跨语义类别的新含义，作为提示提供的上下文知识（CK）；系统评估词频、句子语境与提示框架的影响，覆盖九个模型，并开展三项后续实验（情态弱化、重复、推理）。

Result: 低词频源词和对给定含义支持度高的句子语境下CK依赖更强；提示框架可降低CK依赖，但九個模型中有七个在明确要求忽略给定含义时仍偏向CK；同一模型家族内大模型比小模型更倾向使用CK。后续实验显示：情态弱化降低CK依赖，重复效应取决于提示布局，推理效应因模型而异。

Conclusion: CK依赖随条件和模型系统性变化，说明需要新方法来检测与评估词汇冲突，并引导LLM翻译中的词汇选择。

Abstract: External lexical resources can help large language models (LLMs) translate texts in low-resource languages and specialized domains, but these resources may conflict with models' prior lexical knowledge. To isolate this conflict, we construct a controlled Mandarin-to-English translation task in which familiar source words are assigned new meanings within and across semantic categories. We treat these prompt-supplied meanings as contextual knowledge (CK) and systematically evaluate the effects of word frequency, sentence context, and prompt framing on the translated outputs. Across nine models, CK reliance is greater for less frequent source words and in sentence contexts that support the supplied meanings. Prompt framing can reduce CK reliance, but seven of nine models favor CK even when explicitly instructed to ignore the supplied meanings. Within model families, larger models are more likely than smaller models to utilize CK in their outputs. Three follow-up experiments further show that modal weakening lowers CK reliance, the effect of repetition depends on prompt layout, and the effect of reasoning varies across models. Overall, our controlled study reveals how CK reliance varies across conditions and models, motivating new methods to detect and evaluate lexical conflicts and guide models' lexical choices in LLM-based translation.

</details>


### [107] [Representational drift in a code model: behaviour settles, which neurons carry it does not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09204)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在OpenCoder-1.5B的预训练过程中（2万亿token、恒定学习率、169个Python语法结构），神经元的"表征漂移"普遍存在：某些结构的损失极早见底，但承载它们的神经元集合始终不固定，最终表征由"当前"神经元承担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学中的"表征漂移"指行为不变而承载表征的神经元改变。作者希望在语言模型预训练中检验其对应现象，并预先固定假设——"承载者会稳定下来"，以检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 以恒定学习率训练OpenCoder-1.5B，覆盖2万亿token、169个Python构造；采用密集检查点（间隔低至1,000步）；每次变化配噪声单元（noise unit）做基线对照；通过消融（移除承载神经元与随机神经元对比）度量依赖度，并注入人工"沉降"信号检验检测灵敏度。

Result: 169个构造中有16个（语法决定下一token者）在训练前4%内损失即达下限，但对承载神经元的依赖持续增长：移除它们比移除随机集多损失0.06/0.25/0.40 nats（对应40亿、840亿、2万亿token）。全部169个构造的承载集合在最后一个检查点仍在变化；若在840亿至1.5万亿token间出现突变式沉降，或在5000亿前渐变式沉降，99%注入案例可被检出。840亿token时的承载集合在训练末期移除已与随机集无法区分；离开集合的神经元回归率是匹配神经元的3倍，且无集合重新组装。

Conclusion: 预先固定的假设"承载者会沉降"被证伪；表现为"持久池+变化样本"的漂移结构。作者讨论了候选成因，并指出可区分这些成因的实验。

Abstract: In neuroscience, representational drift is a change in which neurons carry a representation while behaviour stays the same; we measure its analogue in OpenCoder-1.5B during pretraining, for 169 Python constructs over two trillion tokens at a constant learning rate, with a noise unit on every change, at checkpoints as close as 1,000 training steps. For 16 of the 169, those whose grammar fixes a next token, the loss at those positions reaches its floor within the first 4% of training, while the model's reliance on the neurons carrying them grows to the end: removing them costs 0.06, 0.25 and 0.40 nats more than removing a random set, at 4 billion, 84 billion and 2 trillion tokens.
