Which neurons carry a construct never settles: for all 169 the carrier set is still changing at the last checkpoint, and an abrupt settling at any date from 84 billion to 1.5 trillion tokens, or a gradual one before 500 billion, would have been detected in 99% of injected cases. The set measured at 84 billion tokens, removed at the end, cannot be separated from a random set, so the function is carried by whichever neurons are current. Up close the change is a persisting pool and a changing sample: a neuron that leaves a construct's set returns at three times the rate of a matched neuron, and no set reassembles. The hypothesis we fixed in advance, that the carriers settle, is refuted; we discuss candidate causes and name the experiment that would separate them.

</details>


### [108] [AlienBench: A Benchmark of Structural Novelty in Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09094)
*Jonas Oppenlaender,Johanna M. Silvennoinen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AlienBench，将认知心理学的"结构化想象任务"（想象外星生物）适配到语言模型，以地球动物典型特征为固定参照，用三名 LLM 评判者从描述中抽取 10 个生物特征并标注"遵循常规/偏离"，以偏离数（0–10）作为新颖性得分；评测 10 个模型，得分范围 3.28–6.55，开放权重模型整体高于专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 已广泛用于写作、设计、创意生成等任务，新颖性是创造力的标准度量之一。但现有评估依赖人类专家或 LLM 评判者主观打分，缺乏"何为新颖（即偏离熟悉范例）"的固定参照，导致评分不可比、不可复现。需要一个具有已知"熟悉默认值"的任务来衡量偏离程度。

Method: 采用认知心理学中的结构化想象任务：模型被要求描述一个外星生物。三名 LLM 评判者组成评审团，从描述中抽取 10 个生物特征（如双侧对称、以腿为运动方式等），逐个标注为"地球典型"或"偏离"。偏离特征数即为该描述的得分（0–10）。每个模型在 6 种提示条件下生成（变化任务框架与行星环境），每条件 30 条描述（共 180 条），总体得分为均值。共评测 10 个模型，覆盖前沿与开放权重模型，并与 100 名人类被试在基线提示下的结果比较。

Result: 总体得分区间为 3.28（GPT-6 Luna）至 6.55（Ministral 3 14B）。开放权重模型平均得分高于专有模型（5.40 vs. 4.58）。在基线提示下，模型在全部特征上的偏离频率平均均高于 100 名人类被试。全部提示、生成文本、评判输出与得分均已公开。

Conclusion: AlienBench 为 LLM 创造力新颖性提供了以地球动物特征为固定参照的可量化、可复现评测框架，并揭示模型的新颖性可能系统性高于人类、且开放权重模型表现更"偏离常规"，提示仅凭主观评判的新颖性评分需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for creative tasks such as writing, design, and idea generation. For these uses, it matters whether a model's ideas depart from familiar examples or stay close to them. Novelty is one standard measure of creativity, and current creativity evaluations ask human experts or LLM judges to rate how novel an output is. However, these ratings have no fixed reference for what counts as novel (or, in other terms, departure from the familiar). Measuring this departure requires a task with known familiar defaults. The structured imagination task from cognitive psychology is such a task. In this creativity test, people imagine a creature from another planet. The departure from features of Earth animals, such as bilateral symmetry and legs as mode of locomotion, is used as measure of creativity. We introduce AlienBench, a benchmark that adapts the structured imagination task to language models. Models describe alien creatures, and a panel of three LLM judges extracts ten biological features from the description and labels them as either Earth-typical or a departure. The sum of feature departures is the score of a description, from 0 to 10. Each model describes alien creatures under six prompt conditions that vary the framing of the task and the planetary environment. Each model generates 30 descriptions per condition (180 in total), and its overall score is the mean across these descriptions. We evaluate 10 language models, from frontier to open-weight models. Overall scores range from 3.28 (GPT-6 Luna) to 6.55 (Ministral 3 14B). Interestingly, open-weight models score higher than proprietary models in this task (5.40 vs. 4.58). Under the baseline prompt, the models depart from Earth defaults more often on average than 100 human participants on every feature. We release all prompts, generations, judge outputs, and scores.

</details>


### [109] [Failure Profiling and Reachable Trajectory Selection for Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09042)
*Yijun Zhu,Jianxin Wang,Chengchao Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Failure Profiling and Reachable Trajectory Selection (FPRT)，依据"学生失败在哪"与"学生实际能学会什么"联合决定训练数据：先通过重复采样刻画每个问题的错误率与错误一致性以诊断失败模式，再按模式定制提示策略生成候选修正轨迹，最后选择分歧点后最初几步最易被学生复现的正确候选，在五个基准和两个学生骨干上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用小模型学生学习大教师模型生成的推理解答时，学生易过拟合教师的输出分布；一旦推理时的自身行为偏离训练数据，泛化就差。已有引入错误数据的方法，但其修正轨迹仍体现教师的推理模式，学生无法稳定复现，分布失配基本未解决。

Method: FPRT分三步：(1) 失败画像——对学生重复采样，用错误率与错误一致性刻画每个问题上的失败行为，区分定性不同的失败模式，不同模式需要根本不同的训练信号；(2) 按诊断出的失败模式，采用相应定制的提示策略引导教师生成候选轨迹；(3) 在正确候选中，选择分歧点之后最初若干推理步最可被学生复现的那条，认为前几步纠错是主要瓶颈，后续步骤处于已纠正上下文中更易延续——区别于对整条轨迹打分、稀释关键推理转折信号的整体重排序方法。

Result: 在两个学生骨干模型、五个基准上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 有效的训练数据应由学生的失败分布与其可学习性共同决定；以学生为中心的失败诊断加"可达轨迹"选择，能缓解师生分布失配，优于传统整体重排序的蒸馏数据筛选思路。

Abstract: Training smaller language models on reasoning solutions generated by a larger teacher model is the prevailing approach for transferring mathematical reasoning capabilities. However, the student tends to overfit the output distribution of the teacher and generalizes poorly when its own behavior at inference time deviates from the training data. Recent methods attempt to bridge this gap by incorporating error data, yet the corrective trajectories still reflect the reasoning patterns of the teacher, which the student cannot reliably reproduce, leaving the distribution mismatch largely unresolved. We propose a method, Failure Profiling and Reachable Trajectory Selection (FPRT), built on the insight that effective training data should be jointly determined by what the student fails on and what it can realistically learn. Our method first profiles the failure behavior of the student on each problem through repeated sampling, characterizing it in terms of error rate and error consistency to reveal qualitatively distinct failure modes. Each mode demands a fundamentally different training signal. The diagnosed mode then guides candidate generation from the teacher via distinct prompting strategies tailored to each failure type. Among the correct candidates, our method selects the one whose initial reasoning steps after the point of divergence are most reproducible by the student. These first corrective steps constitute the primary bottleneck, while the remaining steps follow an already corrected context and can therefore be continued more readily. This contrasts with standard reranking approaches, which score the trajectory as a whole and thereby dilute the signal at the critical reasoning transition. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves the SOTA performance across the five benchmarks on both student backbones.

</details>


### [110] [Language, Not Code Abstraction: Natural-language Training Shapes Brain-aligned Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09027)
*Iñigo Parra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在相同架构与训练设置下，自然语言训练产生独特的表征几何，其与神经活动的对应显著优于代码训练；代码模型的对齐接近随机权重水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码训练语言模型可产生正的脑评分，引发"从编程语言学到的抽象结构能否替代自然语言训练"这一问题，需在同架构、同流程下系统比较代码与自然语言训练对神经对齐的影响。

Method: 训练25个GPT-2-small模型，语料分别为TinyStories、Python、C函数、x86-64汇编及对应机器码；使用共享分词器与编码流程，并在Pereira-243、Tuckute2024、Blank2014、Fedorenko2016等基准上与匹配的随机权重模型对比评分。进一步分析表征几何（种子收敛性、无拟合的神经不相似度对应ρ）、有效维度，并做仅更新嵌入矩阵的移植实验。

Result: 在Pereira-243与Tuckute2024上，TinyStories比四种代码模型均值高Δr=0.053与0.059（Holm校正p<0.001），代码训练的对齐接近随机权重；优势具基准特异性：Blank2014无校正后语言-代码差异，Fedorenko2016无语言优势。几何结果与Pereira-243编码结果一致：TinyStories种子表征最相似，其与神经不相似度的无拟合对应峰值ρ=0.14，为代码与随机权重水平的两倍；二值训练模型仅约三个有效维度，故种子一致性不必然意味着神经对应。仅用英语更新嵌入矩阵，对每个代码模型的深度平均对齐提升均大于在其自身语料上的同等更新。

Conclusion: 在该架构与训练设置内，自然语言训练产生独特的表征几何，并在Pereira-243上具有更接近的神经对应；代码训练的对齐效果接近随机权重水平。

Abstract: Code-trained language models produce positive brain scores, which raises the question of whether abstract structure learned from programming languages can substitute for natural-language training. We trained 25 GPT-2-small models on TinyStories, Python, C functions, x86-64 assembly, and the corresponding machine code, and scored them using a shared tokenizer and encoding procedure against matched random-weight models. On Pereira-243 and Tuckute2024, TinyStories exceeds the mean of the four code models by $Δr = 0.053$ and $0.059$ (Holm-corrected $p<0.001$), while code training leaves alignment near the random-weight level. The language advantage is benchmark-specific: Blank2014 shows no corrected language-code difference, and Fedorenko2016 shows no language benefit. Geometry tracks the encoding result on Pereira-243: TinyStories seeds converge on the most similar representations, and its fit-free correspondence with neural dissimilarity peaks at $ρ= 0.14$, twice the code and random-weight levels. Binary-trained models have about three effective dimensions, so seed agreement alone does not imply neural correspondence. Updating only the embedding matrix on English improves mean-over-depth alignment for every code model more than an equal update on its own corpus. Within this architecture and training setup, natural-language training produces a distinct representational geometry with closer neural correspondence on Pereira-243

</details>


### [111] [A Spiking Neural Model of the Mental Lexicon: Representations, Similarity Structure, and Associative Retrieval in Perception and Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09402)
*Bernd J. Kröger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文在神经工程框架(NEF)与语义指针架构(SPA)下构建了脉冲神经网络实现的“心理词典”，含613个词条与感知、产出两条定向加工通路，用约20万LIF神经元验证了词汇检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有词汇通达的神经模型常混淆三类要素：词汇表征结构、跨层联结关联、以及检索过程中产生的神经活动。作者希望显式区分这三者，并给出可透明复现、可检验的脉冲神经实现。

Method: 基于NEF与SPA的参数化脉冲神经元实现：概念与语音形式表征由关系图递归构造，词元(lemma)提供独立词汇身份；实现包含613个词条与两条定向通路——感知（语音形式→经词元→概念）与产出（概念→经词元→语音形式）；每条通路结合两种联想记忆变体（胜者通吃竞争选择与阈值化传递），共使用199,400个leaky integrate-and-fire神经元；并形式化定义了层内相似度、跨层联想检索与解码活动的解释。

Result: 仿真显示，在感知与产出两个方向上均能成功检索8个选定词条，且检索判决是对全部613个词条进行评估；输入分别为完整语音形式（感知）与完整概念（产出）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了词汇表征与神经检索机制的透明描述，并提供可访问的代码、词汇数据与在线仿真文档，为词汇通达的计算神经建模提供了可复现基准。

Abstract: A neural account of lexical access requires an explicit distinction between the structure of lexical representations, the associations linking representational levels, and the activity generated during retrieval. This paper specifies a spiking neural implementation of the mental lexicon within the Neural Engineering Framework and Semantic Pointer Architecture. Conceptual and phonological form representations are constructed recursively from relational graphs, whereas lemma representations provide independent lexical identities. The implementation contains 613 lexical entries and supports two directed processing pathways: perception maps a phonological form to its associated concept via a lemma, whereas production maps a concept to its associated phonological form via a lemma. Each pathway combines two associative-memory variants, winner-take-all (WTA) selection (competition) and thresholded transfer, and uses 199,400 leaky integrate-and-fire neurons. We formalize within-level similarity, cross-level associative retrieval, and the interpretation of decoded activity. Simulations show successful retrieval of eight selected words from the lexicon in both processing directions, with retrieval evaluated against all 613 lexical entries. These simulations use complete phonological forms as input for perception and complete concepts as input for production, illustrating lexical retrieval through the corresponding processing pathways. This paper provides a transparent description of lexical representations and neural retrieval mechanisms, supported by accessible code, lexical data, and online simulation documentation.

</details>


### [112] [Syn2DRE: Synergistic Dual Teacher-Student Framework for Document-level Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09342)
*Khai Phan Tran,Xue Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Syn2DRE 是一个轻量级 teacher-student 半监督框架，融合自监督学习与交叉监督以生成更可靠的伪标签，并采用统一的端到端训练提升效率，在多个 DocRE 基准上优于既有半监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级关系抽取（DocRE）需要跨文档识别实体关系，但依赖昂贵的大规模标注。伪标签虽可利用低质量数据，但现有方法存在效率低和确认偏置（confirmation bias）问题。

Method: 提出 Syn2DRE 半监督框架：在轻量级 teacher-student 架构中结合自监督学习与交叉监督（cross-supervision）以产生更可靠的伪标签；并引入统一的端到端训练策略以进一步提升框架效率。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，Syn2DRE 持续优于先前最先进的半监督 DocRE 方法。

Conclusion: 通过自监督与交叉监督结合的双重机制及端到端统一训练，可在降低标注依赖的同时缓解确认偏置并提升效率，验证了该框架在半监督 DocRE 上的有效性。

Abstract: Document-level Relation Extraction (DocRE) aims to identify relations between entities across a document but typically relies on costly large-scale annotations. While pseudo-labelling enables the use of low-quality data, existing methods often suffer from inefficiency and confirmation bias. To address these challenges, we propose Syn2DRE, a semi-supervised framework that combines self-supervised learning with cross-supervision in a lightweight teacher-student architecture to produce more reliable pseudo-labels. Additionally, we introduce a unified end-to-end training strategy to further improve efficiency of the framework. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that Syn2DRE consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art semi-supervised DocRE methods.

</details>


### [113] [Compression of Emotion in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08683)
*Laya Iyer,Sanmi Koyejo,Chen Shani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究七个大语言模型在GoEmotions（27类细粒度情绪+neutral，共28类）上的内部情绪表征组织方式，发现相较于细粒度分类体系存在一致的"过度压缩"现象：无监督聚类时最清晰的划分是把正负情绪分开的两组，粗粒度情绪族/情感极性分组用更少的簇即可恢复；但线性探针仍保留大量细粒度预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多以模型输出预测来评估LLM识别与生成情绪语言的能力，但仅凭输出预测无法揭示模型内部如何组织细粒度情绪区分；作者希望探究细粒度情绪信息在模型隐空间中的表征结构，以及它与GoEmotions细粒度标签、粗粒度情绪族与情感极性分类体系之间的对应关系。

Method: 在GoEmotions英文Reddit数据集上评测七个LLM；对比模型预测结果与"情绪提示下的最后一token表征"，并将这些表征分别与28类GoEmotions类别、更粗的情绪族分组及情感极性分组进行比较；采用KMeans在无标签条件下对隐表征进行聚类，并辅以线性探针检测细粒度预测信息的可解码性。

Result: 发现一致的"过度压缩"模式：在测试的KMeans协议下聚类隐表征时，最清晰的划分是基本区分正负情绪的两组；粗粒度情绪分组也能以远少于28类的簇数被恢复；然而线性探针仍保持可观的细粒度预测能力。

Conclusion: 细粒度情绪信息在模型内部仍然可访问（线性探针可解码），但无监督几何结构更强烈地由更宽泛的情感结构（如正负极性）所组织；即LLM内部情绪表征相对细粒度分类体系存在过度压缩，输出层面的细粒度能力与表征几何的粗粒度组织并存。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been evaluated on their ability to identify emotions expressed in text and produce fluent emotional language. However, output predictions alone do not reveal how fine-grained emotion distinctions are organized internally. We study seven LLMs on GoEmotions, an English Reddit dataset annotated with 27 fine-grained emotion categories plus neutral. We compare model predictions and emotion-prompted final-token representations with the 28 GoEmotions categories and with coarser emotion-family and sentiment groupings.
  We find a consistent pattern of $\textit{overcompression}$ relative to the fine-grained GoEmotions taxonomy. Under the tested KMeans protocol, when we cluster their hidden representations without using emotion labels, the clearest split is into two groups that largely separate positive from negative emotion. Coarser emotion groupings are also recovered with fewer clusters than the full 28-category taxonomy. Yet linear probes retain substantial fine-grained predictive information. These results suggest that fine-grained emotion information remains accessible even though broader affective structure more strongly organizes the unsupervised geometry.

</details>


### [114] [Forcing a Letter Suppresses Refusal: Mechanistic Evidence from WMDP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08639)
*Stepan Epifantsev,Anna Antipova,Oleg Chernikov,Leon Strelkov,Alexey Dontsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次从机制层面解释了大模型安全评测中多选题（MCQ）格式降低拒答率的原因：MCQ下模型快速锁定答案字母token，激活偏离拒答方向约3倍，且承载MCQ答案的注意力头与拒答机制存在竞争，故MCQ安全分数主要衡量知识能力而非安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM安全基准常用多选题格式，但已有研究记录MCQ会降低拒答率，可能显著影响模型的安全行为表现；此前缺乏对其中机制的说明，因此作者希望给出首个机制性解释，以澄清MCQ安全分数究竟测的是什么。

Method: 在WMDP（大规模杀伤性武器代理）数据集上，针对Llama-3.1-8B与Qwen3-8B两个模型：(1) 使用tuned lens技术追踪各层预测，比较MCQ与开放式格式下答案选择与拒答的形成过程；(2) 比较两种格式下激活相对“拒答方向”的距离；(3) 对承载MCQ格式答案的注意力头进行消融，观察对拒答的影响。

Result: tuned lens显示MCQ下模型很早就确定答案字母token，而开放式格式中拒答是逐步累积形成的；MCQ格式的激活到拒答方向的距离约为开放式对应样本的3倍；消融承载MCQ答案的注意力头会提升拒答率，直接表明MCQ的答案选择与拒答相互竞争。

Conclusion: MCQ安全分数主要反映模型的知识能力，而非其安全机制；基于MCQ的安全评测可能高估模型的实际安全对齐水平，需谨慎解读并考虑结合开放式格式评测。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) commonly use multiple-choice question (MCQ) format. However, prior studies documented that MCQ format reduces refusal rates, which may significantly affect the safety behavior of the model. We provide a first mechanistic explanation of this effect on the Weapons of Mass Destruction Proxy (WMDP) dataset across two models - Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-8B. First, we use the tuned lens technique to show that when processing MCQ the model commits to an answer-letter token very quickly, which is in contrast to the gradual refusal build-up in open-ended formats. Additionally, we observe that activations on the MCQ format lie roughly 3x further from the refusal direction, compared with their open-ended counterparts. Moreover, ablating the attention heads that carry the MCQ format answer increases the refusal, which is a direct evidence that the MCQ answer-selection competes with refusal. These findings indicate that MCQ safety scores primarily measure knowledge capabilities and do not assess the safety-mechanisms.

</details>


### [115] [KLEAR: Knowledge-Matched LLM Evaluation in Korean across Application Requirements via Controlled Benchmark Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09223)
*Seoyeon Kim,Jeonghyun Kang,Hamin Koo,Barom Kang,Heondeuk Lee,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出KLEAR框架，通过构造"知识匹配、应用需求不同"的对照题集，将LLM韩语评测中"缺知识"与"不会应用"两类失败解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韩语LLM基准的准确率无法区分错误源于知识缺失还是知识应用失败；且多数题目仅靠事实回忆即可作答，少数需应用能力的题目其所需知识也不同，导致应用能力难以被单独隔离测量。

Method: KLEAR在构造时先识别源题背后的知识，形成显式"锚定束（anchor bundle）"，据此引导生成所需知识相同但应用复杂度更高的题目；候选题经自动化与人工双重验证，并依据评测反馈迭代修订，最终得到知识匹配的对照题集。

Result: 实验表明所构造的知识匹配题集基本满足设定约束，可对多种LLM进行受控评测；结果显示应用需求提升会暴露出即便提供所需知识仍持续存在的失败。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种将知识与应用能力解耦的受控评测范式，揭示应用能力是独立于知识持有的瓶颈，为韩语LLM基准设计提供新思路。

Abstract: Existing Korean benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs) evaluate performance across diverse knowledge domains, but their accuracy scores cannot distinguish whether incorrect responses arise from a lack of required knowledge or a failure to apply that knowledge correctly. Our analysis of existing Korean benchmarks reveals that most questions can be answered through factual recall alone, while those demanding greater application also differ in required knowledge, making it difficult to isolate application ability. To address this, we introduce KLEAR, a framework that constructs knowledge-matched contrasts by varying application demand while preserving the required knowledge. During construction, KLEAR identifies the knowledge underlying each source question as an explicit anchor bundle to guide generation of questions requiring more complex application. Generated candidates then undergo automated and human validation, with iterative revision based on evaluation feedback. Experiments demonstrate that the resulting knowledge-matched sets largely satisfy the intended constraints, enabling controlled evaluation across diverse LLMs. These evaluations reveal that increasing application demand exposes failures that persist even when the required knowledge is provided.

</details>


### [116] [An Effective Spoken Language Understanding Approach Leveraging Query-Adaptive Intent Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09491)
*Bing-Long Kuo,Yu-Hsuan Fang,Tien-Hong Lo,Hsueh-Wei Liao,Berlin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: APEX是一个两阶段检索框架：先用意图级原型选择候选意图，再在选定意图空间内检索上下文示例，从而提升免微调SLU的意图检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型可通过上下文学习实现免微调口语理解，但直接基于查询的检索可能选出语义相似却意图无关的示例，影响意图检测效果。

Method: 提出APEX（Query-Adaptive Intent Detection via Prototype Extraction）两阶段检索框架：第一阶段利用意图级原型筛选候选意图，第二阶段在选择出的意图空间内检索上下文示例；在SLURP数据集上以Exact Match Accuracy（EMA）评估联合SLU性能，覆盖完整转写与困惑度分层条件，并在ASR转写上做检索分析。

Result: 完整转写下，APEX相对查询式检索在Qwen3 4B和Gemma-3-4B上分别提升EMA 4.34和3.97个百分点；高困惑度输入下分别提升7.66和2.13个百分点；检索分析显示原型引导的候选选择在多种输入条件下取得更高的真实意图覆盖率。

Conclusion: 原型引导的两阶段检索可有效缓解语义相似但意图无关示例的检索问题，在免微调SLU中稳定提升意图检测准确率与意图覆盖率，对高困惑度输入尤其有效。

Abstract: Large language models enable fine-tuning-free spoken language understanding (SLU) through in-context learning, but direct query-based retrieval may select semantically similar yet intent irrelevant demonstrations. This paper presents Query-Adaptive Intent Detection via Prototype Extraction (APEX), a two-stage retrieval framework that first selects candidate intents using intent-level prototypes and then retrieves contextual exemplars within the selected intent space. Experiments on the SLURP dataset evaluate joint SLU performance using Exact Match Accuracy (EMA) under full-transcript and perplexity-stratified conditions, with additional retrieval analysis on ASR-generated transcripts. Compared with query-based retrieval, APEX improves EMA by 4.34 and 3.97 percentage points with Qwen3 4B and Gemma-3-4B, respectively, on full transcripts. Under high-perplexity inputs, APEX also yields EMA gains of 7.66 and 2.13 percentage points. Retrieval analysis further indicates that prototype-guided candidate selection achieves higher ground truth intent coverage across multiple input conditions.

</details>


### [117] [How Much Conversation History Does an AI Agent Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09662)
*Danny Kim,Fernando Koch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在静控变量（检索排序与阅读器固定）下考察对话历史/段落预算对阅读器token用量与评测分数的影响：1,100道BEAM题上五个固定段落预算，均分随预算增加而升高但边际收益递减；受控任务中保留用户消息对Qwen直需求有利，但对跨轮指代不利，Mistral接近下限；评分审计使BEAM答案正确性未能定论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究使用更少对话历史（上下文预算收缩）如何同时影响阅读器的token消耗与基准分数，并检验"分数"这一度量本身是否可靠，从而为记忆/上下文预算的选择提供依据。

Method: 在1,100道BEAM问题上，固定检索排序与阅读器，设置五个固定段落预算做对照实验；另设带显式对话状态目标的受控任务，比较"保留用户消息"与token等量的近期截断策略；并对评分流程做审计。

Result: 均分随段落预算增大而上升，但高预算区间每增加token的得分增益递减；可回答问题通常随上下文增多得分更高；汇总的弃答分数在预算收缩时有时反而上升；Qwen在保留用户消息时对直接需求的完整状态匹配更频繁，但当答案依赖对早前提议的引用时匹配率下降；Mistral在各保留条件下接近下限；评分审计未能确定BEAM答案的正确性。

Conclusion: 这些实验刻画了所测记忆预算在token与分数上的后果；可靠的预算选择取决于是否保留每道题所需的证据，并需验证用于评估答案质量的度量本身。

Abstract: How does using less conversational history change reader-token use and benchmark scores? We examine this question on 1,100 BEAM questions. We held the retrieval ranking and reader constant across five fixed passage budgets, and mean assigned scores rose as the passage budget grew. The score gain per additional token became smaller at the upper tested budgets. Answerable questions generally received higher scores with more context. Pooled abstention scores sometimes rose as the passage budget shrank. We also tested message retention in a controlled task with explicit conversational-state targets and compared retained user messages with token-matched recency. Qwen matched the full state more often for direct requirements when user messages were retained. Its full-state matching declined when answers depended on references to earlier offers. Mistral produced near-floor results across the retention conditions. A scoring audit leaves the correctness of BEAM answers unresolved. These experiments characterize the token and score consequences of the tested memory budgets. Reliable budget selection depends on retaining the evidence each question requires and validating the measure used to assess answer quality.

</details>


### [118] [Beyond Truncation? Entailment-Licensed Elision and the Limits of Semantic Preservation in LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08975)
*Mahmoud Alyosify,Mirna Imbabi,Eman Elkhamisi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验"解码循环内的免训练控制器"能否在保持内容的前提下压缩LLM冗长输出，发现熵门控logits处理器节省的token实质只是截断；提出ELIDE方法（仅在NLI判定被省略内容已由前文蕴含时才省略句子片段），优于等长截断，但人工审读显示59%的省略损害答案，将许可限定到语篇单元后有害比例降至约1/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调LLM回答长度超出需求，作者想验证解码期免训练的"控制器"能否去除冗余token而保留内容，并以"模型自身答案截断到等长"作为最简基线进行严格对照。

Method: 1) 系统分析熵门控logits处理器：在MT-Bench开发集与封存测试集上测量token削减率与BERTScore非劣效性，并从理论和审计两方面验证其输出始终是原始答案的前缀；2) 调研23类免训练机制，检验是否存在无需oracle标签即可优于截断的内容选择机制；3) 提出ELIDE：在句子边界处，仅当自然语言推理模型判定某片段已被前文文本蕴含时，才对读者隐藏该片段，同时不改变模型的生成过程；4) 人工审读670个公开prompt上的936次省略，并测试将许可范围限定到语篇单元的效果。

Result: 熵门控方法在开发集削减25.5–34.9%、封存测试集削减19.9–34.2%的token，且满足预注册的BERTScore非劣效界，但其节省完全等价于截断；23类免训练机制中无一能在无oracle标签下优于截断。ELIDE在三个模型族上优于等长截断；但人工审读发现59%的省略损害答案，且无任何自动指标能检测该失败。将许可限定至语篇单元后，在两份新测试上有害省略比例降至约1/10。

Conclusion: 免训练解码期控制器在"长回答压缩"上存在根本性评估陷阱：看似有效的token削减往往只是截断，自动指标无法识别语义损害。ELIDE提供了一种有原则的、基于蕴含许可的省略框架，但需将许可范围收紧到语篇单元才能把有害率压到可接受水平，凸显人工审读在长度压缩研究中的必要性。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) answer at greater length than a request needs. We ask whether a training-free controller inside the decoding loop can remove that surplus while preserving content, judged against the simplest possible baseline: truncating the model's own answer to the same length. An entropy-gated logits processor removes 25.5-34.9% of tokens on MT-Bench development questions and 19.9-34.2% on a sealed test split within pre-registered BERTScore non-inferiority margins, yet we prove and confirm by audit that its output is always a prefix of the unmodified answer: the saving is truncation. A survey of 23 training-free mechanism families finds none that selects content better than truncation without oracle labels. We therefore introduce Entailment-Licensed In-Decoding Elision (ELIDE), which, at each sentence boundary, withholds a span from the reader only when a natural-language inference model judges it entailed by the text already shown, while leaving the model's generation unchanged. ELIDE beats matched-length truncation on three model families, but reading 936 of its elisions on 670 public prompts shows that 59% harm the answer, a failure that no automatic measure detects. Scoping the licence to its discourse unit reduces the harmful share to about one elision in ten on two fresh tests.

</details>


### [119] [On the Incompleteness of do-calculus for Universal Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08916)
*Yaroslav Kivva,Aliaksandra Novik,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究从任意观测/干预分布集合中进行因果效应识别的 Universal Identifiability (uID) 问题，形式化了 do-calculus 的完备性概念，并指出 do-calculus 在 uID 下不再完备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ID 与 gID 问题已有成熟的可识别性图准则和基于 do-calculus 的算法且被证明 sound 且 complete，但"do-calculus 完备"缺乏严格形式定义；同时更一般的 uID 问题（任意观测与干预分布集合）一直悬而未决，作者认为根源在于 do-calculus 在更一般设定下失效。

Method: 提出 do-calculus 完备性的严格形式化定义，并在此框架下重新检验已有 ID 与 gID 的完备性结论；进而分析 do-calculus 规则在 uID 这类更一般问题中的表达能力。

Result: 在形式化定义下，先前工作中 ID 与 gID 的完备性结论依然成立；但对于 uID 等更一般问题，do-calculus 规则不再完备。

Conclusion: do-calculus 的完备性依赖于问题设定；其在 uID 中的失效可能是该问题长期开放的原因，提示需要超越 do-calculus 的新识别工具或准则。

Abstract: We study the problem of causal effect identification from an arbitrary set of observational and/or interventional distributions, which we call Universal Identifiability (uID). The uID problem generalizes existing classical identifiability (ID) and general identifiability (gID) problems, which considered the identification problem from either mere observational distributions or a specific type of interventional distributions. Prior research has established graphical criteria to determine when a causal effect is identifiable in ID and gID problems. Moreover, algorithms that leverage do-calculus and basic probability manipulations have been proposed to compute these effects. Consequently, it has been shown that do-calculus is both sound and complete in these problems. Despite these well-established results, a formal definition of what it means for the do-calculus to be complete does not exist. We propose a rigorous formalization for the completeness of the do-calculus and show that the completeness results in previous work remain valid. However, for more general problems, such as uID, the rules of do-calculus are no longer complete. This is perhaps the reason why the uID problem has remained open thus far.

</details>


### [120] [FinEvolveKG: Controlled and Attributable Prompt Evolution for Financial Knowledge Graph Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08643)
*Abhinav Arun,Jayita Bhattacharyya,Fabrizio Dimino,Bhaskarjit Sarmah,Stefano Pasquali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FinEvolveKG，将知识图谱构建提示分为可复用的通用块与人物政策块，用GEPA只优化人物块，实现可机械归因的提示演化，性能优于冻结基线及无约束全提示演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构基于共享10-K构建知识图谱，但风险分析、组合管理等不同角色的分析需求各异，抽取需适配又不能割裂共享的grounding、类型与时间规则；人工persona提示与微调成本高；而无约束地演化单一整体提示会连带改写共享规则，变化难追溯、审核困难。

Method: FinEvolveKG框架将提示拆为可复用的通用块与persona政策块，把反思式提示优化器GEPA机械地限制在指定块内；适应期内通用块保持字节稳定，优化器编辑仅落在独立版本化的persona政策块。采用双目标：兼顾通用时间KG质量与persona相关事实、motif及多跳连通性。在ticker不重叠的10-K划分上评测五种变异范围，persona条件组（v3–v5）用三个种子、三种persona。

Result: 三种子均值下，两阶段块约束工作流v4较冻结提示基线v1提升19.4–30.9%，较无约束全提示工作流v5提升2.2–6.4%；两个目标分量均领先v5，九组配对种子比较全部胜出，各persona种子方差最低；未经额外优化即在所有OOD偏移上超过v5。SME审核支持生成三元组作为ground truth；盲审双SME在70.1%的对比富集判断中偏好v4。

Conclusion: 机械可归因的提示演化可与强实证性能并存；但作者强调未将增益因果归因于该边界本身。

Abstract: Financial institutions construct knowledge graphs (KGs) from shared SEC 10-Ks to serve risk analysis, portfolio management, and investment research, whose analytical needs differ. Extraction must adapt without fragmenting shared grounding, typing, and temporal rules. Hand-authored persona prompts and fine-tuning add cost. Weight-free prompt optimization offers an alternative, but unconstrained evolution of a monolithic prompt can rewrite shared rules alongside persona guidance, obscuring changes and complicating review. We introduce FinEvolveKG, a KG-construction harness separating reusable general and persona-policy blocks and mechanically confining GEPA, a reflective prompt optimizer, to the designated block. During persona adaptation, the approved general block remains byte-stable and every optimizer edit is localized to a separately versioned persona policy. A dual objective balances generic temporal-KG quality with persona-relevant facts, motifs, and multi-hop connectivity. We evaluate five mutation scopes on ticker-disjoint 10-K splits, with three seeds for the persona-conditioned arms (v3-v5) across three personas. On the three-seed mean, the complete two-stage block-constrained workflow (v4) improves over the frozen prompt baseline (v1) by 19.4-30.9% and over the tested unconstrained full-prompt workflow (v5) by 2.2-6.4%, leading v5 on both objective components and winning all nine paired-seed comparisons with the lowest seed variance in every persona. Without further optimization, v4 also exceeds v5 across all evaluated out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts. An SME audit supports using the generated triplets as ground truth; a blinded two-SME audit favors v4 over v5 in 70.1% of contrast-enriched judgments. These results show that mechanically attributable evolution can coexist with strong empirical performance, without attributing the gains causally to the boundary.

</details>


### [121] [Steerable Multi-Objective Group-Relative Preference Optimization with Jointly Trained Low-Rank Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09634)
*Constantinos Karouzos,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LambdaMix：参数高效的多目标偏好条件化策略学习方法，用组相对在线强化学习联合训练各目标对应的低秩适配器，单次训练即可在推理时通过偏好向量改变权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户偏好在变化，部署时需要在竞争目标间做不同权衡；而标准后训练只针对固定权重产出单一策略，难以灵活调整。

Method: 在冻结基模型上，为每个目标联合训练一个低秩适配器；偏好向量用于选择适配器混合以生成回复，并对分别归一化的奖励加权以更新适配器；采用组相对在线RL（GRPO式）单次训练支持推理时切换偏好。

Result: 在四个模型上测试“有用性vs无害性”和“数学求解同时遵循指令”，两个最大模型测试“改写评论保持措辞并减少个人属性线索”。前两任务上，LambdaMix在全部四个模型的超体积优于所有条件化与策略合并基线；相比一组GRPO专家策略，训练完成数更少、存储适配器参数更少。固定架构与初始化下，同时变化适配器混合与目标权重比单独变化任一项带来更宽分数范围与更高覆盖。

Conclusion: LambdaMix以单次训练实现偏好条件化的策略族，参数与数据效率优于专家策略集，且混合适配器与目标权重的联合调节能获得更优的偏好覆盖。

Abstract: Language model deployment often requires different trade-offs between competing objectives as user preferences change, yet standard post-training methods typically produce a single policy for a fixed objective weighting. We introduce LambdaMix, a new parameter efficient method for learning a preference conditioned family of policies with group-relative online reinforcement learning. LambdaMix jointly trains one low-rank adapter per objective over a frozen base model. One preference vector selects the adapter mixture that generates responses and weights the separately normalized rewards used to update the adapters. A single training run supports changing preferences at inference without further optimization. We experiment with four models on helpfulness and harmlessness and on solving math problems while following instructions. We also evaluate the two largest models on rewriting comments to preserve wording while reducing clues about personal attributes. On the first two tasks, LambdaMix achieves higher hypervolume than all evaluated conditioned and policy merging baselines across all four models. LambdaMix uses fewer training completions and stores fewer adapter parameters than a set of GRPO specialist policies. With architecture and initialization fixed, varying adapter mixtures and objective weights together gives wider score ranges and higher coverage than varying either alone.

</details>


### [122] [RuACT: Recovering Inactive Rubrics as Adaptive Critics and Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09597)
*Yijun Li,Zhengxi Lu,Zhipiao Liu,Jinzheng Yu,Xutao Weng,Jian Shao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RuACT 将基于评分细则（rubric）的 RL 中"不可达"与"饱和"两类无梯度信号细分：前者作教师提供特权信息做奖励门控的 on-policy 自蒸馏，后者作评论家分解为子 rubric 以区分当前回答，在医学与科学基准上超越 GRPO 等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在组相对优化（GRPO 类）下，若某条 rubric 被所有采样回答一致判为失败（不可达）或一致判为通过（饱和），则组内奖励无差异、梯度为零。Qwen3-4B 医学训练中 60.88% 的 query-rubric 观测处于这种失活状态。作者进一步指出两种失活并非同一问题：不可达 rubric 的失败源于信息缺失（提示中明示该 rubric 可把满足率从 1.13% 提到 91.25%），而饱和 rubric 下回答质量仍有差异，只是 rubric 区分度不足。

Method: 提出 RuACT，为每类失活 rubric 分配其所需监督角色：(1) Rubric as Teacher：把不可达 rubric 作为特权信息，进行奖励门控的 on-policy 自蒸馏；(2) Rubric as Critic：将饱和 rubric 分解为能区分当前回答的子 rubric，子 rubric 与父 rubric 共享权重。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 下于四个医学与科学基准上优于 GRPO、rubric 重加权和 rubric 引导探索方法；Qwen3-1.7B 下取得最佳平均。初始不可达 rubric 的无引导恢复率由 35.02% 升至 42.86%；子 rubric 细化使独立 rollout 上的奖励-质量对齐由 57.74% 提升到 60.77%。

Conclusion: 失活 rubric 不应被简单丢弃或重加权，而应按"缺信息"与"缺区分度"分别转化为教师信号与评论家信号，从而恢复组相对优化的有效梯度并同时提升可达性与奖励-质量对齐。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) extends RL with verifiable rewards to open-ended tasks by scoring each response against query-specific rubrics. Under group-relative optimization, however, a rubric that every sampled response fails (unreachable) or passes (saturated) gives all responses the same outcome and no learning signal; in Qwen3-4B medical training, 60.88% of query-rubric observations are inactive. We find that the two states lack different supervision: stating an unreachable rubric in the prompt raises its satisfaction from 1.13% to 91.25%, whereas responses that all pass a saturated rubric still differ in quality. We therefore propose RuACT (Rubrics as Adaptive Critics and Teachers), which assigns each inactive rubric the role that supplies what it lacks. Rubric as Teacher uses unreachable rubrics as privileged information for reward-gated on-policy self-distillation, and Rubric as Critic decomposes saturated rubrics into child rubrics that separate current responses while sharing the parent's weight. RuACT outperforms GRPO, rubric reweighting, and rubric-guided exploration on all four medical and scientific benchmarks with Qwen3-4B and achieves the best average with Qwen3-1.7B. On initially unreachable rubrics, unguided recovery rises from 35.02% to 42.86%, while child refinement improves reward-quality alignment on independent rollouts from 57.74% to 60.77%. Code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/RuACT.

</details>


### [123] [Asymmetric Correspondence Learning for Lexical and Style Embeddings: Interpreting Latent Space via Acoustic Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09313)
*Michael Whealing,Robert Gaizauskas,Thomas Hain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称对应学习（ACL），借分裂潜变量对应瓶颈将HuBERT特征蒸馏为独立的声学词嵌入（AWE）与声学风格嵌入（ASE），并揭示线性探针在验证特征解耦上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音嵌入同时编码词汇、说话人与韵律信息，造成特征难以隔离；而常规线性探针评估可能"高估"解耦成功，需澄清解耦的真实程度与诊断方法的可靠性。

Method: ACL通过split-latent correspondence bottleneck把HuBERT特征分别蒸馏为AWE与ASE；在单词发音上做探针与聚类评估，并对eGeMAPS声学特征施加组级置换特征重要性分析，以解释各潜空间的划分依据。

Result: AWE空间呈现内聚的词汇聚类（AP≈0.90，Fisher≈6.28）；ASE空间线性探针说话人准确率达97.9%（Fisher≈1.01，拓扑弥散），但仍保留大量词汇方差（Fisher≈0.86）且该信息在线性上不可恢复（0.17%）。置换重要性显示AWE按声道滤波器划分、ASE按F0动态划分。

Conclusion: 即便探针显示信息已被剥离，各潜空间中仍存在大量外源方差；标准诊断基准不足以验证特征隔离，因为可被探针完全隐藏的语音结构仍可能被显著保留。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech embeddings jointly encode lexical, speaker, and prosodic information, complicating feature isolation. We introduce Asymmetric Correspondence Learning (ACL), distilling HuBERT features into separate Acoustic Word Embeddings (AWEs) and Acoustic Style Embeddings (ASEs) via a split-latent correspondence bottleneck. Through probing and clustering evaluations on single-word utterances, we demonstrate that substantial variance from extrinsic sources remains encoded within each latent space, despite linear probing indicating the information has been stripped. Whilst the AWE space exhibits cohesive lexical clustering (AP$\approx$0.90, Fisher$\approx$6.28), linear probing on the ASE space achieves 97.9\% speaker accuracy across a diffuse topology (Fisher$\approx$1.01), yet preserves substantial lexical variance (Fisher$\approx$0.86) that remains linearly unrecoverable (0.17\%). To explain these findings, we apply group-level permutation feature importance over eGeMAPS acoustic features, finding that AWEs partition by vocal tract filters and ASEs by $F_0$ dynamics. These findings suggest that standard diagnostic benchmarks may be insufficient for verifying feature isolation, as substantial speech structure can be retained even when it remains entirely hidden from probes.

</details>


### [124] [PhoPunct: A Large-Scale Benchmark of Transformer Architectures for Vietnamese Punctuation Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09107)
*Khoa D. Nguyen,Thai Bao Huynh,Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在2,210万标注token的越南语标点恢复基准上，统一训练预算下评估17种架构，发现Bi-LSTM与CRF层在8/8对比中的7组并不稳定提升macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越南语标点恢复评测集规模较小，难以判断架构选择随数据量扩展的表现；且架构收益与超参调优收益常被混淆。

Method: 构建News与Novels受控基准（19.9M+2.2M=22.1M标注token）；4个预训练Transformer骨干，配可选Bi-LSTM与CRF层共17种架构，另加PhoBERT-large-Bi-LSTM-CRF模型PhoPunct；统一8个epoch、相同学习率、不做架构专属调参。

Result: 在8组同域同骨干的配对比较中，7组中纯Transformer分类器≥Bi-LSTM-CRF变体；PhoPunct取得News最佳macro F1 61.35，Novels最佳为XLM-R+CRF的50.18，优于PhoPunct的48.97。

Conclusion: 递归层与结构化预测头的价值取决于其优化条件，应明确区分“架构改进”与“架构专属调参带来的改进”。

Abstract: Vietnamese punctuation restoration is often evaluated on relatively small benchmarks, limiting conclusions about how architectural choices scale with data volume. We present a controlled benchmark on News and Novels corpora containing 22.1 million labeled tokens (19.9M News and 2.2M Novels). We evaluate 17 architectures formed from four pretrained Transformer backbones and optional bidirectional long short-term memory (Bi-LSTM) and conditional random field (CRF) layers, together with a PhoBERT-large-Bi-LSTM-CRF model, PhoPunct. Every model is trained for eight epochs with the same learning rate and without architecture-specific tuning. Under this fixed budget, adding Bi-LSTM and CRF layers does not reliably improve macro F1: in seven of eight matched domain-backbone comparisons, a plain Transformer classifier matches or outperforms its Bi-LSTM-CRF counterpart. PhoPunct obtains the best News result (61.35), whereas XLM-R with a CRF obtains the best Novels result (50.18), exceeding PhoPunct (48.97). These results show that the value of recurrent and structured-prediction heads depends on their optimization regime and motivate explicit separation between architectural improvements and improvements due to architecture-specific tuning. Our source code is available at: https://github.com/aisia-research-lab/PhoPunct

</details>


### [125] [The Invisible Gap: Romanized Indic Generation Fails Where Automatic Metrics Succeed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09085)
*Madhu Sudhan Rao Beesetty,Surbhi Sharma,Devesh Maheshwari,Lokesh Meesala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出罗马化印度语（如"aap kaise hain"）生成完全缺席现有NLP评测体系，且BGE-M3、BERTScore等自动指标会系统性误判其质量，提出"失效感知"的捆绑式评测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数亿印度用户以拉丁字母罗马化方式与数字系统交互，但罗马化印度语生成未被纳入基准与评测框架；同时自动语义指标可能系统性掩盖该类生成的真实质量差异与失效模式。

Method: 基于142组OpenAssistant问答对，评测4个LLM（Claude Opus 4.8、GPT-5.5、Gemini 2.5-flash、Qwen3.5-35B）在罗马化印地语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语上的生成；选取72条回答子集做母语者12分制人工评分，并与全量数据集BGE-M3均值对照；引入失效感知评测策略，对空输出、脚本污染与指令崩坏赋零分。

Result: 在72条诊断子集上，母语者对GPT-5.5的评分比Claude Opus 4.8高1.43分（12分制），而两者全量BGE-M3均值仅差0.004，表明自动指标无法区分真实质量差距。识别出两种模型特异失效模式：Gemini出现脚本溢出（16.9%泰卢固语回答混入原生文字），Qwen退化为纯英文输出（16.2%卡纳达语回答）。

Conclusion: 罗马化印度语生成的评测需采用协同捆绑方案：语义相似度、脚本合规性、失效覆盖度与母语者可接受度四者缺一不可，仅靠自动指标会漏检关键失效。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of Indians communicate with digital systems in romanized Latin characters -- typing "aap kaise hain" rather than native script -- yet romanized Indic generation is absent from NLP benchmarks and evaluation frameworks. We demonstrate that automatic metrics (BGE-M3, BERTScore) systematically misrepresent the quality of romanized Hindi, Telugu, and Kannada generation. We evaluate four LLMs (Claude Opus 4.8, GPT-5.5, Gemini 2.5-flash, Qwen3.5-35B) on 142 OpenAssistant query-response pairs. On a 72-response diagnostic subset, native speakers rate GPT-5.5 1.43 points higher than Claude Opus 4.8 on a 12-point rubric, whereas their full-dataset BGE-M3 means differ by only 0.004. Two distinct model-specific failure modes emerge: Gemini produces script spillage (16.9% of Telugu responses leak native script), while Qwen collapses to English-only output (16.2% of Kannada responses). We introduce a failure-aware evaluation policy that assigns zero to empty outputs, script-corrupted responses, and instruction collapses -- revealing failures metrics alone cannot detect. Our results show that evaluating romanized Indic generation requires a coordinated bundle: semantic similarity, script compliance, failure coverage, and native-speaker acceptability.

</details>


### [126] [Multilingual Artificial Datasets for AI-Driven Cardiology: the CliTe and ParaCliTe corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09296)
*Lorenzo Pratesi,Eulalia Farre-Maduell,Machteld Boonstra,Michele Miranda,Jonas Mack,Eduard Rodenas-Alesina,Jonathan Kossoff,Corinne Allaart,Sébastien Bratières,Manuela-Daniela Danu,Isabella Fumarulo,Milou Gamage,Antonio Iaconelli,Pavel Leinveber,Felix Lindberg,Pavel Macháč,Vladimíra Macíková,Gianluigi Savarese,Raffaele Scorza,Bram van Es,Emma Weiss,Andrea Guala,Martin Krallinger,the DataTools4Heart NLP group*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出人工合成心脏病学语料库的构建指南，并发布三个多语言语料库 CliTe、ParaCliTe 与 ParaCliTe Silver，以缓解隐私限制下心脏病学 NLP 资源匮乏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏病学 NLP 需要可比、多语言且经过验证的语料库，但隐私顾虑导致此类资源极度稀缺。本文旨在填补这一空白：给出人工语料的构建指南，并公开多语言语料。

Method: 依据专门编写的公开语料库构建指南，由 7 家欧洲医院的心脏科医师用捷克语、荷兰语、英语、意大利语、罗马尼亚语、西班牙语和瑞典语撰写人工临床记录（强调临床多样性与性别平衡），形成 CliTe；其子集经多阶段人工翻译为其余 6 种语言，形成句级对齐的平行语料 ParaCliTe；其余报告采用机器翻译，构成 ParaCliTe Silver。

Result: CliTe 含 699 份人工心脏病学报告，以门诊记录（340 份）、出院报告（263 份）和住院记录（89 份）为主，涉及虚构的缺血性心脏病（199 例）、心力衰竭（188 例）、瓣膜病（79 例）等患者。ParaCliTe 含 94 名虚构患者的报告，每种语言均具备句级对齐，共 658 份文档，主要为出院报告（322 份）、门诊（196 份）与住院记录（140 份），涵盖心力衰竭（399 份文档）、缺血性心脏病（287 份）和瓣膜病（238 份）。实证结果显示 ParaCliTe 具有实用价值，同时揭示了现有 SOTA NLP 模型在心脏病学领域适配上的局限。

Conclusion: 在详细指南支持下，构建多语言、公平且贴近真实的人工患者语料库以提升心脏病学 NLP 性能是可行的。

Abstract: Background and Aims. Comparable, multilingual, validated cardiology corpora are critical to advance trustworthy natural language processing (NLP) models for cardiology but such resources are scarce due to privacy concerns. This study aimed to cover this gap by presenting suitable artificial corpus creation guidelines and by releasing 3 multilanguage corpora. Methods. Following purpose-specific publicly-available corpus construction guidelines, cardiologists from 7 European hospitals generated artificial clinical records in Czech, Dutch, English, Italian, Romanian, Spanish and Swedish, ensuring clinical diversity and sex balance, resulting in the CliTe corpus. A CliTe subset underwent a multi-stage manual translation into the other 6 languages, resulting in the parallel ParaCliTe corpus, while the remaining reports were automatically translated, constituting ParaCliTe Silver. Results. CliTe is a collection of 699 artificial cardiology reports, mostly outpatient notes (340 documents), discharge reports (263), and inpatient notes (89), describing fictional patients with ischaemic heart disease (199 patients), heart failure (188), and valvular disease (79), among others. ParaCliTe contains reports from 94 fictitious patients, each available in seven languages with sentence-level alignment, resulting in 658 documents. The corpus mainly comprises discharge reports (322 documents), and outpatient (196) and inpatient (140) notes, describing patients with heart failure (399 documents), ischaemic heart disease (287), and valvular disease (238). Empirical results illustrate the utility of ParaCliTe and highlight the limitations of state-of-the-art NLP models adapted to the cardiology domain. Conclusions. The creation of multilingual, equitable and realistic corpora of artificial patients capable of strengthening NLP performance for cardiology is feasible when supported by detailed guidelines.

</details>


### [127] [Guardrail Erosion in Domain Fine-Tuned Large Language Models: A Paired Audit of Knowledge-Centric Abstention in Biomedical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08780)
*Stephen Peng,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair,Noah Broestl,Anne Friederike Kleppe,Steven Mills,Adi Zolotov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了25个生物医学领域微调大模型与11个配对基座检查点，发现"知识型弃答"（认识自身知识边界而拒答）能力在基座模型本就偏低（MedAbstain中位数8.3%），微调后进一步下降（中位数4.2%，25对中21对下降），且变化幅度与方向在配对间异质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域微调模型数量激增，成本低但可能带来非预期行为变化。已有研究表明微调会侵蚀安全护栏，但相关大规模研究在模型架构、数据混合与微调方法维度上仍然有限；且既有工作多关注价值导向的拒绝回答，缺乏对知识型弃答的系统考察。

Method: 构建配对审计设计：25个生物医学领域微调LLM对11个基座检查点，在MedAbstain与ClinDet-Bench两个知识型弃答基准上评测微调对弃答行为的影响，并分析配对间的变化幅度与方向。

Result: 基座模型的适当弃答率已偏低（MedAbstain中位数8.3%）；微调后中位数降至4.2%，MedAbstain上25对中有21对下降；变化幅度与方向在不同配对间呈异质性。

Conclusion: 领域微调会系统性削弱知识型弃答能力，亟需既能提升领域专用能力又能保持适当审慎的微调方法。

Abstract: The number of domain fine-tuned models, particularly in the biomedical domain, is rapidly expanding. Fine-tuning promises high-performing models at a fraction of the cost of training a base model, but it can also produce unintended behavioral changes. Multiple studies have shown that safety guardrails can erode with fine-tuning, yet large-scale studies across model architectures, data mixtures, and fine-tuning methodologies remain limited. Where prior work in this space has studied value-centric refusal, this work focuses on knowledge-centric abstention. We audit 25 biomedical domain fine-tuned large language models against 11 paired base checkpoints on MedAbstain and ClinDet-Bench to evaluate the impact of fine-tuning on knowledge-centric abstention. We find that defined appropriate abstention measured by these benchmarks, already low in base models (median 8.3% on MedAbstain), falls further after fine-tuning (median 4.2%, decreasing in 21 of 25 pairs on MedAbstain), and that the magnitude and direction of change are heterogeneous across pairs. These results motivate fine-tuning methods that improve domain-specific capability while preserving appropriate caution.

</details>


### [128] [Structuring Survey Histories for LLM Digital Twins: A Controlled Study of Background, Decision Procedure, and Evaluation Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08730)
*Iris Ye,Tianze Deng,Ozan Candogan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出并验证固定三元结构表示 BDE（Background–Decision procedure–Evaluation），在 Twin-2K-500 上比原始问卷转录与等长自由文本更能提升 LLM 数字孪生的回答预测准确率，其增益主要来自画像要素构成而非压缩或更深的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据显示问卷历史压缩未必损失预测精度，提示"表示结构"可能而非"信息量"是关键设计变量；但缺少对结构本身的受控检验，尤其是何种结构成分带来增益尚不清楚。

Method: 在 Twin-2K-500 上以 100 名受访者做配对评估，预测其在 17 项任务上的留出答案。比较对象包括：原始问卷转录、完全推断的 BDE 画像、同提取器基于同一转录生成的等长自由文本画像；并做三项分析：模式（schema）对比、提取深度（更深推断）、成分消融（Background–Decision procedure 是否加入 Evaluation）。模拟器为 gpt-5.4-nano 与 gpt-5.4-mini。

Result: BDE 画像总体准确率较原始转录提升 1.27pp（nano，p<0.01）与 1.19pp（mini，p<0.001）。对比等长自由文本：定价任务提升 2.15pp（nano，p<0.01）与 1.38pp（mini，p<0.05），总体仅在 nano 显著（1.05pp，p<0.05）。加深提取推断无显著增益。在 B–D 基础上加入 Evaluation 使总体准确率提升 1.44pp（nano，p<0.001）与 1.22pp（mini，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 相对原始转录的优势不能用压缩或长度解释；增益主要归因于画像成分构成（尤其 Evaluation 要素），而非更深的提取推断。结构设计应作为数字孪生表示的核心变量。

Abstract: LLM-based digital twins aim to predict how individuals would respond in new settings from representations of their prior responses. Existing evidence that compression of survey histories need not reduce predictive accuracy suggests that representation structure, rather than information volume alone, may be an important design choice. We study BDE, a fixed representation that organizes each respondent's survey history into Background, Decision procedure, and Evaluation. We evaluate it on Twin-2K-500 (Toubia et al., 2025) by predicting each respondent's held-out answers on 17 tasks. In a paired evaluation with 100 respondents, a fully inferred BDE profile improves overall accuracy over the raw survey transcript by 1.27 percentage points (pp) on gpt-5.4-nano (p < 0.01) and 1.19 pp on gpt-5.4-mini (p < 0.001). Three analyses then examine the schema, extraction depth, and component composition. Against a length-matched free-form profile written from the same transcript by the same extractor, BDE improves pricing accuracy on both simulators (2.15 pp on nano, p < 0.01; 1.38 pp on mini, p < 0.05), while its overall advantage is significant only on nano (1.05 pp, p < 0.05). Allowing deeper inference during extraction yields no statistically significant gain on either simulator. By contrast, adding Evaluation to a Background--Decision procedure profile improves overall accuracy by 1.44 pp on nano (p < 0.001) and 1.22 pp on mini (p < 0.001). These results indicate that the advantage over raw transcripts is not explained by compression or length alone, and attribute it primarily to profile composition rather than deeper extraction.

</details>


### [129] [Does Reasoning Improve Psychological Depth in Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09061)
*Ruichen Zheng,Yihe Wang,Fabrice Harel-Canada,Sara Khosravi,Zeynep Senahan Yildiz,Amit Sahai,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过60组盲评对比推理增强模型（DeepSeek-R1、GPT-5）与对应基线（DeepSeek-V3、GPT-4o）在短篇小说心理深度上的表现，发现推理优势并非普适，且LLM评审存在强烈的"亲推理"偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试中推理增强LLM的分数提升使人们默认其在复杂生成任务上普遍更强，但在受读者体验细微差异影响的开放式创意写作领域，这一假设缺乏检验。

Method: 以短篇小说的"心理深度"为切入点，构建60组提示匹配的成对故事输出，由7名人工标注者与一个LLM-as-a-Judge集成（5种配置）进行盲评；对比DeepSeek-R1 vs. DeepSeek-V3、GPT-5 vs. GPT-4o（GPT-5含两档推理强度）。

Result: 人工评价无一致的推理优势：GPT-5略优于GPT-4o（两档推理强度下偏好率60.0%–62.9%），而DeepSeek-R1落后于DeepSeek-V3（42.9%）。标注者间一致性很低（Krippendorff's α=0.070），但标注质量核查表明这源于评价本身的主观性（不同的决策方案）而非标注失误。相比之下，LLM-as-a-Judge表现出强烈的亲推理偏置（在98.3%的配对中选推理模型，而人类投票仅55.2%），该偏置在5种配置下一致，并部分与句子长度相关。

Conclusion: 在前沿模型水平上，推理相关的能力差异小到被读者主观性所主导；LLM-as-a-Judge评审会掩盖这种主观性，在细腻的创意生成任务中可能产生误导性结论。

Abstract: Benchmark gains from reasoning-enhanced large language models encourage the assumption that these models should broadly perform better on complex generative tasks, but this remains underexplored in open-ended, creative domains shaped by nuances of reader experience. We tested this assumption through the lens of psychological depth in short stories. We compared DeepSeek-R1 vs. DeepSeek-V3, our closest available contrast for reasoning-oriented post-training, and GPT-5 vs. GPT-4o, through 60 blinded pairwise evaluations in which seven human annotators and an ensemble LLM-as-a-Judge rated prompt-matched story outputs. Human evaluations showed no universal reasoning advantage: GPT-5 was slightly preferred over GPT-4o (60.0-62.9% across two reasoning-effort settings), whereas DeepSeek-R1 fell behind DeepSeek-V3 (42.9%). Although this pattern was marked by low inter-annotator agreement (Krippendorff's $α= 0.070$), our annotation-quality checks suggested genuine evaluative subjectivity, plausibly reflecting distinct annotator decision schemes rather than annotation failure. In contrast, the LLM-as-a-Judge showed a strong pro-reasoning bias (98.3% of pairs vs. 55.2% of human votes), consistent across its five configurations and partly tracking sentence length. These findings suggest that, at the frontier, reasoning-related quality differences are small enough to be dominated by reader subjectivity, and that LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators can obscure this subjectivity in nuanced creative-generation tasks.

</details>


### [130] [CodeQuant2: Enhanced Outlier Mitigation and Adaptive Clustering for Low-Precision LRMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09868)
*Xingyu Liu,Xiangyang Yin,Tianhua Xia,Haiyu Wang,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CodeQuant2 是一个面向低精度大型推理模型（LRM）的基于聚类的查找表（LUT）量化框架，通过旋转缩放抑制激活异常值、聚类拟合非均匀权重、按层自适应码本大小，并改进校准样本选择以匹配推理期解码统计，配合定制 LUT CUDA 内核，在推理基准上大幅提升精度并较 BF16 提速最高 2.85 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型依赖更长的中间推理轨迹提升性能，但显著增加推理成本，使低精度量化愈发重要；而激进量化模型在推理任务上往往性能下降。作者观察到推理首个解码步存在更大的激活波动，忽略这些早期状态的校准会遗漏其激活波动，这是推理特有的量化难点。

Method: 提出 CodeQuant2：1）用旋转（rotation）与缩放（scaling）缓解激活异常值；2）用聚类表示非均匀权重分布，构成基于 LUT 的量化；3）按各层量化难度自适应调整码本大小；4）基于对推理期激活行为的观察，改进校准样本选择以更贴合实际解码统计；5）设计定制化 LUT CUDA 内核以实现加速。

Result: 在推理基准上取得显著的精度提升，同时相对 BF16 实现最高 2.85 倍加速。

Conclusion: 针对 LRM 推理期激活波动的量化难题，CodeQuant2 通过旋转缩放、聚类码本、层自适应码本与校准样本优化的组合设计，在保持低精度效率的同时有效恢复推理精度，验证了推理感知校准与 LUT 量化结合的有效性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve task performance by generating longer intermediate reasoning traces, but this substantially increases inference costs and makes low-precision quantization increasingly important. However, aggressively quantized models often perform poorly on reasoning tasks. We observe larger activation fluctuations during the first decoding steps of reasoning. Calibration that omits these early states may miss their activation fluctuations. To address these reasoning-specific quantization challenges, we propose \textbf{CodeQuant2}, a clustering-based LUT quantization framework for low-precision large reasoning models. CodeQuant2 mitigates activation outliers with rotation and scaling, uses clustering to represent non-uniform weight distributions, and adapts codebook sizes to the quantization difficulty of each layer. Guided by our observation of reasoning-time activation behavior, CodeQuant2 further improves calibration sample selection to better match actual decoding statistics. Combined with our customized LUT CUDA kernel, CodeQuant2 delivers substantial accuracy improvements on reasoning benchmarks while achieving up to $2.85\times$ speedup over BF16.

</details>


### [131] [DUET: Dual-Sparse Inference for Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09239)
*Siyuan Yan,Shen Gu,Xiaoxing Ma,Chun Cao,Jingwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Duet：面向扩散语言模型的免训练双重稀疏推理框架，通过前缀KV稀疏与早期查询稀疏分别降低历史注意力开销与当前块冗余计算，在32K上下文下实现1.34×–2.41×端到端、2.83×–4.72×单步加速且精度保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）虽通过迭代去噪实现并行token生成，但推理成本高：一是历史前缀随生成推进不断增长，重复注意力开销日益增大；二是每个去噪步都将整个当前生成块的所有位置送入Transformer，而每步通常仅有少部分位置被确定。这两类冗余计算导致推理效率受限。

Method: 提出免训练的Duet推理框架，包含两部分：(1) 前缀KV稀疏——利用每个生成块内历史注意力分布的集中性与跨去噪步的稳定性，构建紧凑的历史KV子集并跨去噪步复用；(2) 早期查询稀疏——从浅层表示预测本步可能更新的位置，仅将这些位置的query传播至后缀Transformer层，避免对大概率不更新的位置进行深层计算。

Result: 在LLaDA2.1-mini上取得1.34×端到端/2.83×单步加速，在SDAR-8B-Chat-b32上取得2.41×端到端/4.72×单步加速（32K上下文长度），同时保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 历史注意力的集中性/跨步稳定性与块内更新的局部性为DLM推理提供了可利用的稀疏结构；Duet以免训练方式同时削减前缀注意力与块内深层计算，在长上下文场景下显著提升推理效率且不显著损失精度。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) enable parallel token generation through iterative denoising, yet their inference remains costly due to repeated attention over a growing historical prefix and repeated processing of the entire current generation block. Historical attention becomes increasingly expensive as generation progresses, while all current-block positions are propagated through the Transformer at every denoising step even though only a small fraction are typically resolved at that step. In this paper, we introduce Duet, a training-free dual-sparse inference framework that addresses these challenges through prefix Key-Value (KV) sparsity and early query sparsity, respectively. Specifically, Duet exploits the concentration and cross-step stability of historical attention within each generation block to construct a compact historical KV subset that is reused across denoising steps. For query sparsity, Duet predicts update positions from shallow representations and propagates only the corresponding queries through the suffix Transformer layers, avoiding deep computation at positions unlikely to be updated in the current step. Experiments show that Duet maintains competitive accuracy while achieving 1.34$\times$/2.83$\times$ end-to-end/per-step speedups on LLaDA2.1-mini and 2.41$\times$/4.72$\times$ on SDAR-8B-Chat-b32 at a 32K context length.

</details>


### [132] [Query-Guided RoPE Modulation for Lost-in-the-Middle Mitigation in Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09554)
*Peiyu Zhang,Heng Ping,Shinibali Bhattacharyya,Anwesan Pal,Nikos Kanakaris,Kevin Cheang,Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的QGPM方法，利用模型自身注意力头的查询相关性重塑RoPE位置，缓解长上下文"lost-in-the-middle"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文模型常忽略输入中段信息（lost-in-the-middle），部分原因在于RoPE的远程衰减削弱了远距离token间的位置信号。现有输入压缩会删除低重要度token，注意力重加权只能在RoPE衰减之后起作用，而RoPE层面的方法对所有query施加相同的位置重映射，缺乏查询自适应性。

Method: QGPM（Query-Guided Positional Modulation）为免训练方法：利用模型自身注意力头计算查询相关性，构建相关性图——低层用宽泛（broad）图、高层用尖锐（sharp）图；在快旋转平面上令低相关token前进不足一个位置，从而把相关证据拉向查询；慢旋转平面保持原始布局、token位置不变。

Result: 在NoLiMa与RULER多个上下文长度上，QGPM优于基座模型与强基线，检索类任务增益最大，并在多种骨干模型上具备泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过查询引导、分层差异化的RoPE位置调制，可在无需训练的情况下有效缓解中段信息丢失，验证了位置编码层面查询自适应调制的可行性与通用性。

Abstract: Long-context language models often neglect information in the middle of the input, a phenomenon known as "lost-in-the-middle". Part of this gap may stem from the long-range decay of rotary position embedding (RoPE), which weakens the positional signal between distant tokens. Existing input compression deletes low-importance tokens, and attention reweighting acts only after RoPE has weakened this signal. RoPE-level methods modify positions directly but apply the same remapping to every query. To address these limitations, we propose QGPM (Query-Guided Positional Modulation), a training-free method that reshapes RoPE positions using query relevance from the model's own attention heads. QGPM builds a broad relevance map for lower layers and a sharp one for upper layers. Low-relevance tokens advance by less than one position on fast-rotating planes, pulling relevant evidence toward the query. Slow-rotating planes keep the original layout, with every token in place. Experiments on NoLiMa and RULER with multiple context lengths show that QGPM improves over base models and strong baselines, with the largest gains on retrieval tasks, and generalizes across multiple backbones. Our code is available at https://github.com/iZHpy/QGPM.

</details>


### [133] [Benchmarking Speaker Embedders in the Sub-Second Regime for Real-Time Voice AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09516)
*Abhishek Gautam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在严格亚秒延迟预算下，系统评测了TitaNet-Large/Small、ECAPA-TDNN、CAM++、SpeakerNet五种说话人嵌入模型对"背景人声"的鲁棒性，得出三条部署要点：嵌入前去噪有害、裁剪语音块有害、TitaNet约一半嵌入维度冗余，并据此给出原始音频+完整VAD块、阈值偏精确率、截断嵌入降检索成本的工程建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时语音AI代理需区分目标说话人与可懂背景人声（电视、家人、同事）。生产级降噪器能去除非平稳环境噪声，但背景人声与目标说话人频谱特性相近，会穿透降噪，导致下游说话人验证（SV）与ASR误接受。论文旨在弄清在实时约束下现有说话人嵌入模型对背景人声的处理特性，并给出可落地的部署指导。

Method: 基准测试五种开源说话人嵌入模型（TitaNet-Large、TitaNet-Small、ECAPA-TDNN、CAM++、SpeakerNet），均在亚秒级延迟预算内运行。评估由两部分组成：(1) 受控基准——基于真实通话，在静音区间以调制幅度注入第二个真实说话人；(2) 加权的人工标注生产数据集。指标以等错误率（EER）为主，并分析嵌入维度对"人口属性"（语种、性别）的编码情况。

Result: 三项发现：① 嵌入前先去噪通常有害，跨架构普遍抬升EER；② 将音频截断至短于完整语音活动（VAD）片段会降低所有模型性能；③ TitaNet嵌入约有一半维度冗余，尾部维度主要编码语种、性别等群体属性，在人口统计学同质的用户群中判别价值低。

Conclusion: 部署建议：说话人验证应使用原始音频与完整VAD语音块，避免前置降噪与截断；阈值设定应偏向精确率（precision）而非F1；采用截断后的嵌入可降低相似度检索成本。

Abstract: Real-time Voice AI agents must separate the active user from intelligible background speech that survives conventional noise suppression. Background sources include television audio, family members, and colleagues. Production denoisers remove non-stationary environmental noise effectively. Background speech shares the spectral characteristics of the target and passes through, causing false acceptances in downstream speaker verification (SV) and automatic speech recognition (ASR). We benchmark open-source speaker embedders TitaNet-Large, TitaNet-Small, ECAPA-TDNN, CAM++, and SpeakerNet under a strict sub-second latency budget. Evaluation combines a controlled benchmark with a weighted human-annotated production set. Our controlled benchmark is built from real calls in which a second real speaker is injected into silence intervals at modulated amplitude. We report three findings. First, denoising before embedding is generally harmful and raises equal error rate (EER) across architectures. Second, truncating audio below the full voice-activity segment degrades every model. Third, roughly half of the TitaNet embedding is redundant for this task. Trailing dimensions encode population attributes such as language and gender. Such attributes carry little discriminative value in a demographically homogeneous user base. We distill these results into deployment guidance. Verification should run on raw audio with full voice-activity chunks. Thresholds should favor precision over F1. Truncated embeddings reduce the cost of similarity search.

</details>


### [134] [On the Effectiveness and Limitations of Open-Weight LLMs for Low-Resource Offensive Language Detection in Danish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09506)
*Elnur Adl Zarabi,Amit Kumar,Adi Singh,Sierra Nelson,Megan Stubbs-Richardson,Taoran Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评测开放权重LLM在丹麦语冒犯性语言检测（低资源任务）上的效果与鲁棒性：四类模型家族×十一种提示策略，并与BERT族基线比较；LLM零样本即可超越微调BERT，轻量微调进一步提升；语义型提示迁移更稳健，类别不平衡仍是核心难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丹麦语冒犯性语言检测属低资源且研究不足的任务；现有工作对开放权重LLM在该任务上的有效性、提示设计敏感性、正字法扰动及类别不平衡鲁棒性缺乏系统认识。

Method: 评测Llama、Mistral、Gemma、Qwen四个模型家族，覆盖五大设计类别的十一种提示策略，考察跨提示泛化、丹麦语正字法变体（æ/ø/å的ASCII转写）以及平衡与自然不平衡类分布下的鲁棒性；对照三类预训练策略不同的BERT族基线，采用MLM提示与有监督微调两种方式评估。

Result: 无需任务微调的LLM即可超过三个微调BERT基线，轻量微调带来显著增益；无单一模型或提示类别占优，提示与预训练的交互难以先验预测；语义型提示迁移更稳健，格式约束型提示普遍泛化失败；ASCII转写通常导致性能下降，说明丹麦语特殊字符携带有效信号；平衡测试集最佳F1为0.84，自然偏斜分布下为0.62（偏斜数据训练）与0.70（平衡数据训练），相对下降26.2%与16.7%。

Conclusion: 开放权重LLM在低资源丹麦语冒犯性语言检测中具备强竞争力，轻量微调性价比高；提示设计需与预训练特性匹配且不可先验预测；正字法细节不可忽略；类别不平衡仍是关键未解挑战，需在训练与评测协议中专门处理。

Abstract: We study the effectiveness and robustness of open-weight large language models (LLMs) for Danish offensive language detection, an underexplored low-resource task. We evaluate four model families (Llama, Mistral, Gemma, and Qwen) across eleven prompting strategies spanning five design categories, cross-prompt generalization, Danish orthographic variation, and robustness under balanced and naturally imbalanced class distributions. We compare against three BERT-family baselines with distinct pre-training strategies, evaluated via both masked language model prompting and supervised fine-tuning. Even without task-specific fine-tuning, LLMs can surpass all three fine-tuned BERT baselines, and lightweight finetuning yields substantial further gains. No single model or prompt category dominates, underscoring that prompt design interacts with model pre-training in ways that cannot be predicted a priori. Semantically grounded prompts transfer more robustly, while format-constrained patterns consistently fail to generalize. ASCII transliteration of Danish-specific characters (æ, ø, å) generally degrades performance, indicating these characters encode meaningful signal. At the same time, the gap between a balanced test set (best F1 of 0.84) and the corpus's natural class skew (0.62 trained on skewed data, 0.70 trained on balanced data) -- relative drops of 26.2\% and 16.7\% -- shows that class imbalance remains a critical open challenge. Our code and configurations are available at https://github.com/PASHAADC/Danish-LLM-HateSpeech.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Flex-RSI: Benchmarking Recursive Self-Improvement of Frontier Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08946)
*Jingdong Zhang,Jionghao Wang,Zhengming Yu,Yancheng Cai,Wei Chow,Chris Choy,Zhiyang Dou,Hang Hua,Jiachen Li,Zhixuan Liu,Chen Wang,Songlin Wei,Siyuan Yang,Yu Yuan,Xiaohang Zhan,Yucheng Zhang,Shuhong Zheng,Xin Li,Lingjie Liu,Wojciech Matusik,Zhengzhong Tu,Wenping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Flex-RSI，一个在固定预算下评测智能体在视觉与机器人任务上递归自我改进能力的基准；智能体不仅改进任务解，还通过反馈修改自身 harness（提示、工具、记忆、控制循环），最终解在隐藏测试数据上评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常固定智能体的 harness 只评测其产出解，或聚焦大规模模型训练，无法回答"智能体能否利用任务反馈改进自身工作方式而非仅改进答案"这一问题，因此需要一个评测智能体递归自我改进（RSI）能力的基准。

Method: 构建 Flex-RSI 基准：每轮中执行器（executor）改进任务解，仅在留出验证集上得分更高时保留；随后改进器（improver）修订 harness，包括执行器与改进器自身。所有运行从同一通用 harness 出发，最终解在隐藏测试集上评分；任务覆盖感知、推理、机器人动作、3D 重建、视觉创作与基础设施，评测闭源与开放权重模型。

Result: 实验发现：当智能体自行构建解决方案时自我改进收益最大；仅记录成功/失败的反馈会限制开放权重模型的表现；开放权重模型在输出需通过物理稳定性或精确正确性严格检查的任务上落后最多；配对对比实验进一步表明，对 harness 本身的编辑对性能提升有实际贡献。

Conclusion: 智能体的递归自我改进在固定预算下是可评测且有效的，harness 的自我编辑是增益的重要来源；反馈信息的丰富程度与任务对严格校验的要求是区分模型能力的关键因素。

Abstract: Frontier language models are increasingly deployed as agents, and how well an agent performs depends not only on its model but also on its harness: the prompts, tools, memory, and control loop through which the model acts. When an agent is applied to a new problem, a natural question is whether it can use the feedback of the task to improve the way it works, and not merely its answer. Most existing benchmarks keep the agent's harness fixed and score the solution it produces, or target large-scale model training. We introduce Flex-RSI, a benchmark for the recursive self-improvement of agents on vision and robotics tasks under a fixed budget. In each round, an executor improves the task solution, which is kept only if it scores higher on held-out validation data, and an improver then revises the harness, including the executor and itself. Every run starts from the same general-purpose harness, and the final solution is scored on hidden test data. Evaluating closed-source and open-weight models on tasks spanning perception, reasoning, robot action, 3D reconstruction, visual creation, and infrastructure, we find that self-improvement helps most when the agent builds its own solution, that feedback recording only success or failure holds back open-weight models, and that open-weight models fall furthest behind where outputs must pass strict checks of physical stability or exact correctness. Paired runs further show that editing the harness itself contributes to the gains.

</details>


### [136] [Flow-Guided Target-Space Alignment via Path Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09863)
*Ruizhi Yuan,Zeqiu Yu,Wei Gao,Wei Chen,Xiwei Tang,Lu Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowAlign 将条件流匹配作为训练期的对齐信号（而非推理期的传输模型），在保留目标空间全局结构的同时提升实例级配对对应，并在合成数据、单细胞 multiome 与图文对齐实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对齐需把源模态映射到由目标模态定义的固定空间，且该空间直接用于下游分析。此时理想对齐应同时保持目标分布的全局结构与配对训练数据中的实例级对应。现有目标函数各偏一端：对比损失只约束相对相似性，分布匹配损失改善边缘一致性，逐点回归仅拟合配对目标而忽略目标空间的路径几何。

Method: 提出 FlowAlign：把条件流匹配改造为训练期对齐正则，利用学习到的流场引导源侧表征向固定目标空间对齐，在保持配对对应的同时与目标空间全局结构相容；并给出理论分析，说明其通过控制端点精度、捕捉局部目标空间几何来改善配对对应。

Result: 在合成数据、配对单细胞多组学（multiome）基准和图文对齐任务上，FlowAlign 在不同模态设置下均提升了实例级对应，同时保持目标空间结构。

Conclusion: 流匹配可作为有效的训练期对齐正则，兼顾实例级配对精度与目标空间的全局/局部几何一致性，适用于目标空间直接用于下游分析的对齐场景。

Abstract: Many multimodal alignment problems require mapping a source modality into a fixed space defined by a target modality, where the target space is not merely an intermediate embedding but is used directly for downstream analysis. In this setting, successful alignment should preserve both the global structure of the target distribution and the instance-level correspondence observed in paired source--target training data. Existing objectives typically address only part of this goal: contrastive losses encourage relative similarity, distribution-matching losses improve marginal agreement, and pointwise regression fits each source sample to its paired target without explicitly modeling target-space path geometry. We propose FlowAlign, a flow-guided target-space alignment method that repurposes conditional flow matching as a training-time alignment signal rather than an inference-time transport model. FlowAlign uses the learned flow field to guide the alignment of source-side representations with the fixed target space, encouraging them to preserve paired correspondence while remaining compatible with the global target-space structure. We further provide theoretical analysis showing how FlowAlign helps improve paired correspondence by controlling endpoint accuracy and capturing local target-space geometry. Experiments on synthetic data, paired single-cell multiome benchmarks, and image--text alignment demonstrate that FlowAlign improves instance level correspondence while preserving target-space structure across different modality settings.

</details>


### [137] [Memory-Aware Multi-Agent Architecture with LLM-based Orchestration for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08718)
*Emilly Pitman de Castro,Vanessa Silva,Gustavo Kanno,Marcus Santos,Bruno Ferraz do Amaral,Marcelo Nogueira,Lucas Francisco Pellicer,Lucas Lanaro,Pedro Augusto Luiz,João Renato Ribeiro Manesco,Giovanni Carbinatti,Isabela de Lima Santos,Lucas Oliveira Jeronymo,Jeison Fappi,Raul Rotilli Aguirre,Fabio André Frantz,Renata De Paris,Fabiana Marucci,Lohan Silva,Vinicius Diniz Reolon,Wandemberg Gibaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出记忆感知的多智能体对话推荐架构，将LLM作为编排层，把长期记忆作为一等组件整合进路由与语义查询构建；在Amazon Reviews上实验表明记忆机制可弥补最高63%与显式查询场景的差距，监督式编排使用户接受度提升28.9个百分点，并提出语义一致性分数(SCS)补充评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话推荐系统通常将用户会话孤立处理，难以刻画用户意图随时间的演化；LLM多仅作为孤立组件提升语义理解，未在系统层面整合记忆、检索与决策。论文因此关注系统级设计（记忆、编排、评估）而非单一模块改进。

Method: 构建记忆感知的多智能体架构：LLM作为编排层，协调意图细化、查询构建与交互流程；长期记忆作为一等组件参与路由决策与语义查询形成；并设计监督者(supervisor)驱动的结构化编排。评估采用跨会话记忆实验、多轮对话模拟及监督交互研究，在Amazon Reviews数据集上进行；同时提出语义一致性分数(SCS)作为补充指标。

Result: 记忆启用的交互可恢复至多63%的与显式查询场景之间的差距，并防止长对话中的语义退化；监督式编排使用户接受度提升28.9个百分点；观察到检索候选与展示推荐之间存在“曝光瓶颈”(exposure bottleneck)；LLM重排序不提升Hit@K，但持续改善语义一致性与多样性。

Conclusion: 有效的对话推荐依赖系统级设计：需要显式记忆整合、结构化编排，以及超越传统排序指标（如SCS）的评估体系；仅优化单一LLM组件不足以捕获意图演化与交互质量问题。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems often treat user sessions in isolation, limiting their ability to capture how user intent evolves over time. While large language models (LLMs) have improved semantic understanding, they are typically used as isolated components, without addressing how memory, retrieval, and decision-making should be integrated at the system level. In this work, we propose a memory-aware multi-agent architecture for conversational recommendation, in which LLMs act as orchestration layers that coordinate intent refinement, query construction, and interaction flow. A key design principle is the integration of long-term memory as a first-class component, influencing both routing decisions and semantic query formation. We evaluate the system through cross-session memory experiments, multi-turn conversational simulations, and supervisor-driven interaction studies on the Amazon Reviews dataset. Results show that memory-enabled interactions recover up to 63\% of the gap toward explicit-query scenarios and prevent semantic degradation in extended dialogues. In addition, supervisory orchestration increases user acceptance by +28.9 percentage points, highlighting the importance of structured orchestration in guiding interaction and output quality. We also observe an exposure bottleneck between retrieved candidates and displayed recommendations. While LLM-reranking does not improve Hit@K, it consistently enhances semantic consistency and diversity. To capture these effects, we introduce the Semantic Consistency Score (SCS), a complementary evaluation measure. Overall, the results emphasize the importance of system-level design choices, suggesting that effective conversational recommendation benefits from explicit memory integration, orchestration, and evaluation beyond traditional ranking metrics.

</details>


### [138] [ChainFlow: Few-Step Latent Flow Matching for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08669)
*Selim Aktas,Yusuf Erdem Erdem,Ahmed Zeer,Himmet Toprak Kesgin,Muzaffer Kaan Yuce,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChainFlow：首个以目标模型隐状态为条件的流匹配投机解码草稿器，用1–2步流输运替代多步去噪，配合VAE压缩隐状态、轻量反馈头恢复token间依赖与树状草稿提升接受率，在Qwen3.5上于同规模同数据条件下较EAGLE-3与DFlash分别快39%和11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有草稿器存在速度—质量权衡：自回归草稿器逐token生成，草稿开销随草稿长度线性增长；扩散草稿器虽并行生成，但各位置独立预测、忽略token间依赖，二者均限制了投机解码的加速比。

Method: 将草稿过程建模为以目标模型隐状态为条件的短程流匹配输运，仅需1–2个流步而非从噪声出发的多次去噪；用变分自编码器（VAE）压缩隐状态，使草稿开销几乎不随目标模型规模增长；以轻量反馈头恢复token间依赖；结合树状草稿（tree drafting）提高接受率。

Result: 在Qwen3.5系列模型上，草稿器规模与训练数据一致的前提下，vLLM中相比EAGLE-3提升39%、相比DFlash提升11%；当草稿器顺序适配新语言时增益进一步扩大；消融实验覆盖草稿器设计与并发服务场景，并开源代码与草稿模型。

Conclusion: 链式流匹配（ChainFlow）作为一种新的投机解码范式，通过隐状态条件化与短程流输运，在保持并行生成效率的同时建模token间依赖，实现了优于现有自回归与扩散草稿器的加速效果，并具备跨语言顺序适配的良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has become a standard way to accelerate LLM inference: a lightweight drafter proposes tokens that the target verifies in one parallel pass. Existing drafters face trade-offs that limits their speedups: autoregressive drafters generate tokens one at a time, so drafting cost grows with draft length, while diffusion drafters generate tokens in parallel but predict each position independently, ignoring inter-token dependencies. We introduce ChainFlow, a new speculative decoding paradigm and the first flow-matching drafter conditioned on the target's hidden states. ChainFlow treats drafting as a short transport over hidden states, needing one or two flow steps instead of the many denoising steps of generation from noise. A variational autoencoder compresses these states so drafting cost barely grows with target size, lightweight feedback heads restore inter-token dependencies, and tree drafting raises acceptance. On Qwen3.5 models, at matched drafter size and training data, ChainFlow outperforms EAGLE-3 and DFlash in vLLM by 39% and 11%, respectively. These gains further widen as drafters are adapted to new languages sequentially. Our ablations cover drafter design and concurrent serving, and we open-source our code and drafter models.

</details>


### [139] [Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039)
*Artin Yousefi,Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Lorien C Abroms,David A Broniatowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文审计六个主流LLM在戒烟建议上的"人设一致性"：相同四问对十二类用户人设共2,880条回复，发现人设显著影响事实覆盖（对青少年更少提及处方戒烟药，差距32–67个百分点），并提出"事实底线"系统提示作为可部署干预，但干预效果因模型而异、不可迁移。作者同时强调事实底线须年龄感知，且报告的是场景级差异而非单一属性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数亿用户向LLM聊天机器人寻求健康建议，但"不同用户是否得到相同建议"这一公平性问题缺乏系统实证；戒烟领域具备成文临床指南与显著公共卫生意义，适合作为审计场景。

Method: 审计四个提供方的六个广泛部署LLM；以戒烟四项问题为固定输入，对十二个在生命阶段、自述专业程度、吸烟史上不同的人设进行提问，共收集2,880条回复并编码事实覆盖；随后评估一种可部署干预——短系统提示设定"事实底线"（用户相关时必须获得的核心医学事实集合），但不限制语气、措辞与框架。

Result: 所有模型对青少年人设提及FDA批准处方戒烟药物的频率低于成人人设，差距32–67个百分点；六个模型中有五个在"呈现医疗专业背景"的人设上给出的事实数量少于"无专业背景"人设。系统提示在全部模型中缩小了人设差异，但对总体事实覆盖的影响在幅度与方向上因模型而异，说明单一系统上验证的提示不可假定可迁移。

Conclusion: LLM健康建议存在基于用户表象的系统性事实覆盖差异，需加以审计与干预。但等量提及率并非总是正确临床目标（处方戒烟药物不推荐用于未成年人），事实底线应年龄感知而非统一；因人设多属性同时变化，结论应作场景级差异解读而非年龄或专业度的孤立效应。作者公开全部2,880条回复与分析代码。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people now ask large language model (LLM) chatbots for health advice, which raises a question that is simple to pose and hard to answer: does everyone get the same advice? We audit six widely deployed LLMs from four providers on smoking cessation, a domain with codified clini- cal guidance and substantial public-health stakes, by putting the same four cessation questions to twelve user personas that differ in life stage, self-described expertise, and smoking history, for a total of 2,880 responses. What the models say depends on who appears to be asking. Every model named FDA-approved prescription cessation medications less often to teen personas than to adult personas, by 32 to 67 percentage points, and in five of six models a persona presenting health- care expertise received fewer facts than a persona presenting none. We then evaluate one deployable intervention: a short system prompt specifying a factual floor, the set of core med- ical facts every user should receive when they are relevant, while leaving tone, vocabulary, and framing free to adapt. The prompt narrows persona differences in every model, but its effect on overall fact coverage varies in both magnitude and direction across models, so a prompt validated on one system cannot be assumed to transfer to another. Two cautions frame these results. Equal mention rates are not always the correct clinical target, because prescription cessation pharmacother- apy is not recommended for minors, so a defensible factual floor must be age-aware rather than uniform. And because our personas vary on several attributes at once, we report scenario- level differences rather than isolated effects of age or expertise. We release all 2,880 responses and the analysis code.

</details>


### [140] [Enabling Privacy-Preserving Natural Language Access to Industrial Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09372)
*Carlos Martín-Abellán,Mark Kröll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OpenMeter——一个模式无关的多智能体系统（MAS），将自然语言查询翻译为可执行代码与人类可读洞察，用于智能电表数据；多场景消融显示全多智能体编排在资源受限的本地硬件上收益最大（EX-R 较最强单智能体配置提升 6.1 个百分点），而在前沿规模下各组件应选择性启用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源管理方与电网运营商依赖智能电表数据进行异常检测与决策，但从大规模异构遥测库提取洞察需高级 SQL 与数据工程能力，形成非技术人员的使用瓶颈；同时关键能源基础设施的严格隐私法规与安全约束禁止敏感数据经云端 LLM 处理。

Method: 设计模式无关的多智能体系统 OpenMeter，采用多智能体编排将自然语言问题转化为可执行代码并生成人类可读洞察，支持通过自然语言对话与复杂数据库交互；在本地硬件与前沿规模两种条件下开展多场景消融实验，比较全编排与单智能体配置及各组件贡献。

Result: 全多智能体编排在资源受限本地硬件上取得最大精度增益，执行准确率（EX-R）较最强单智能体配置提升 6.1 个百分点；在前沿规模下，各组件仍具价值但应选择性启用。

Conclusion: 全编排的额外成本仅在因隐私要求必须本地执行的场景下才值得；OpenMeter 降低了电网日常运营的技术门槛，使非专家人员能将原始遥测数据转化为常规工作流与战略决策的有力工具。

Abstract: Energy management officers and grid operators increasingly rely on smart meter data for anomaly detection, performance monitoring, and strategic decision-making. However, extracting actionable insights from large-scale, heterogeneous telemetry databases requires advanced SQL and data engineering expertise, creating a critical bottleneck for non-technical personnel. Furthermore, the strict data privacy regulations and security constraints inherent to critical energy infrastructure often prohibit the routing of sensitive consumer data through cloud-based Large Language Models (LLMs). To address these challenges, we present OpenMeter, a schema-agnostic Multi-Agent System (MAS) designed to translate natural language queries into executable code and human-readable insights. This architecture lowers the technical barrier for day-to-day utility operations by empowering non-expert personnel to interact with complex databases through intuitive natural language dialogue, transforming raw telemetry into a highly valuable tool for routine grid workflows and strategic decisions. Multi-scenario ablation studies over this environment demonstrate that full multi-agent orchestration shows its largest observed accuracy gains on resource-constrained local hardware (+6.1 pp EX-R over the strongest single-agent configuration); at frontier scale, individual components retain value but should be enabled selectively, indicating that the cost of full orchestration is justified specifically where privacy demands local execution.

</details>


### [141] [BMMS: Policy-Conditioned Measurement of Behavioral Compliance in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09147)
*Anthony Rhodes,Gadi Singer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出行为失配度量套件 BMMS，以政策语料为输入、输出合规画像，核心量 CAS/CRM 加 BIV/BDI，维度须通过"归因性"准入检验；在 1003 条义务上验证抽取与判定可靠性，并发现 GPT-4o 在其自身 Model Spec 硬约束上失败率 70.7%，而两个 Claude 模型分别 0.0% 与 9.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型行为评测预设固定维度（幻觉、谄媚、毒性、过度拒答），无法回答受监管行业操作者的实际问题：系统是否满足某一特定规则手册的要求。需要一种以政策为条件、可适配任意已发布政策和任意自然语言应答系统的合规度量框架。
