Method: 提出 BMMS：以政策语料为参数，输出义务级合规画像。CAS 为按严重度加权的义务达成度，CRM 为在分级对抗压力下存留的达成比例，BIV 与 BDI 一并规范并发布。维度准入采用公开准入检验，核心判据为归因性——固定被测目标而更换评判者后发生变化的量，测的是仪器而非目标。对竞争性解释（自规范优势）以文档难度扣减加以控制，并进行跨供应商评判者重评分。

Result: 在 5 类登记册、11 份文档的 1003 条义务上：准入判据对供应商规格的盲评人类判断精确率 94.4%，对分析性指引为 50.0%；抽取保真度 94.6%；两家具他供应商评判者重评分下目标排序不变，但单一目标的绝对分数跨度达 0.22。该判据还排除了套件自身曾报告的一项量。应用于两份最详细的行为规范：扣除文档难度后，自规范优势不显著异于零；但绝对自合规方面，GPT-4o 在其所绑定硬约束上失败 70.7%，两个 Claude 模型分别为 0.0% 与 9.1%。

Conclusion: 行为评测应从预设维度转向政策条件化度量，并以归因性作为维度的准入门槛以区分测量目标与测量仪器。结果提示自规范优势更多是文档难度造成的假象，而绝对自合规差异显著；同时跨评判者的绝对分数不稳（排序稳定、量级不稳），故应报告排序与相对比较而非单一绝对分。

Abstract: Behavioral evaluation of language models fixes its dimensions in advance: hallucination, sycophancy, toxicity, over-refusal. An operator in a regulated industry needs a different question answered: does a system do what a particular rulebook requires? We introduce the Behavioral Misalignment Metric Suite (BMMS), which measures policy-conditioned misalignment by taking a policy corpus as an argument and returning a compliance profile over it, for any published policy and any system that answers in natural language. The Compliance Attainment Score (CAS) measures severity-weighted attainment over the obligations that bind the system, and the Compliance Robustness Margin (CRM) the share of that attainment surviving graded adversarial pressure; Behavioral Invariance (BIV) and the Behavioral Disposition Index (BDI) are specified and released with the suite. Dimensions enter only by passing a published admission test whose binding criterion is attribution: a quantity that moves when the judge changes, holding the target fixed, is measuring the instrument. Applying it excluded one of the quantities the suite itself reported. Across 1,003 obligations from eleven documents in five registers, the admission criterion holds 94.4% precision against blinded human judgment on vendor specifications and 50.0% on analytical guidance, extraction fidelity is 94.6%, and re-scoring under judges from two other providers leaves target ordering invariant while the absolute score of a single target spans up to 0.22. We apply it to the two most detailed published behavioral specifications. Own-specification advantage is not resolved from zero once document difficulty is subtracted, but absolute self-compliance is another matter: GPT-4o fails 70.7% of the hard constraints binding it in the Model Spec published by OpenAI, where the two Claude models fail 0.0% and 9.1% of theirs.

</details>


### [142] [Jacobian-Causal Memory Control for Persistent Memory in Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08779)
*Marvin Danig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出雅可比因果记忆控制（JCMC），以预测影响签名表示记忆干预、按目标效应与副作用预算选择干预，并在保留前用观测效应校验预测，给出理论界与评估协议（无基准实测）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统不应只决定检索哪些记录，还应决定哪些历史信息可以改变当前推断；需要对记忆干预进行因果化、可控的目标效应选择与副作用约束。

Method: 在共享行为测量空间中为每个被授权记忆干预赋予预测影响签名；按目标效应与副作用预算选择干预；比较预测效应与观测效应以决定保留或回滚；提出配对反事实估计器及与相关性、图结构、策略过滤检索基线对比的评估协议；在平滑性与有界估计假设下给出二次局部近似界、可达目标效应刻画、签名误差下的选择遗憾界与同时记忆间非加性交互界。

Result: 论文未报告基准测量结果，仅给出理论保证与评估协议设计；核心论断的可证伪条件为：在同等模型、记忆、策略与成本预算下，更简单基线能匹配效应定向、副作用控制与回滚效用。

Conclusion: JCMC将记忆检索重构为带预算约束的因果干预选择与验证问题，理论框架完整但缺乏实证；其价值有待按所提协议与基线实测检验，否则论断应被收窄或否定。

Abstract: Persistent-memory systems choose not only which records are retrieved but which prior information can alter current inference. We propose Jacobian-causal memory control (JCMC), which represents each authorized memory intervention by a predicted influence signature in a shared behavioral measurement space, selects interventions against a target effect and side-effect budget, and compares predicted with observed effects before retaining or rolling back the intervention. Under explicit smoothness and bounded-estimation assumptions, we prove a quadratic local-approximation bound, characterize the target effects reachable by a set of signatures, bound selection regret under signature error, and bound non-additive interactions between simultaneous memories. The paper reports no benchmark measurements; it defines a paired counterfactual estimator and an evaluation protocol comparing JCMC with relevance, graph, and policy-filtered retrieval baselines. The central claim is narrowed or falsified if those simpler baselines match effect targeting, side-effect control, and rollback utility under equal model, memory, policy, and cost budgets.

</details>


### [143] [BitDecoding-FP4: Native FP4 KV Cache Decoding via Algorithm-System Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09825)
*Dayou Du,Woon Yee Ng,Guangming Sheng,Nick Lau,Jianyi Cheng,Luo Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BitDecoding-FP4 通过算法-系统协同设计，实现高精度、高效率的原生 FP4 KV cache 解码：三项误差消除算法使其在 NVFP4 下与 BF16 KV 精度差距不超过 0.5 分，并在 B300 上获得 2.4–3.0 倍峰值吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 服务中 KV cache 主导显存容量与带宽。4-bit 微缩放格式（NVFP4、MXFP4）可压缩缓存并在 FP4 Tensor Core 上运行注意力，但算法量化与系统级加速之间存在鸿沟：现有 FP4 KV 系统使用朴素量化导致严重精度下降，而面向精度的 4-bit 算法依赖向量码本，其查表开销又会拖慢解码吞吐。

Method: 针对三类误差来源提出对应算法：(1) Dual-Residual Query Quantization，将 query 拆为两个 FP4 张量；(2) Underflow-Aware Probability Scaling，避免 E4M3 scale 将长上下文概率舍入为零；(3) Outlier-Aware Key Quantization，结合静态平滑、Hadamard 旋转与 scale 搜索，在 token-wise 缩放下抑制 key 离群值。系统层面采用转置 softmax 并融合概率量化，在 paged KV cache 上做堆叠 query MMA，以消除 softmax 瓶颈。

Result: 在 GQA 与 MLA 的稠密及 MoE 模型上，NVFP4 下与 BF16 KV 精度差距在 0.5 分以内（MXFP4 为 1 分），并在 RL 训练 rollout 中与 BF16 KV 保持一致；query logit 误差从 60% 降至 1%。端到端服务在 NVIDIA B300 上用 Qwen3-4B 与 Qwen3-30B-A3B 取得比 BF16 KV 高 2.4–3.0 倍的峰值吞吐。

Conclusion: 通过量化误差建模与系统瓶颈协同优化，原生 FP4 KV 解码可在几乎无损精度的前提下显著提升长上下文服务吞吐，代码已开源。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache dominates memory capacity and bandwidth in long-context LLM serving. While 4-bit microscaling formats (NVFP4, MXFP4) shrink the cache and run attention on FP4 Tensor Cores, a fundamental gap remains between algorithmic quantization and system-aware acceleration: existing FP4 KV systems use vanilla quantization that causes severe accuracy drops, whereas accuracy-oriented 4-bit algorithms rely on vector codebooks whose lookup overhead degrades decoding throughput. We present BitDecoding-FP4, an algorithm--system co-design for accurate and efficient native FP4 KV decoding. We mitigate three error sources: Dual-Residual Query Quantization splits queries into two FP4 tensors, cutting query logit error from 60% to 1%; Underflow-Aware Probability Scaling prevents E4M3 scales from rounding long-context probabilities to zero; and Outlier-Aware Key Quantization combines static smoothing, Hadamard rotation, and scale search to suppress key outliers under token-wise scaling. At the system level, we remove the softmax bottleneck via transposed softmax with fused probability quantization and stacked query MMA on paged KV caches. Across dense and MoE models with GQA and MLA, BitDecoding-FP4 stays within 0.5 points of BF16 KV accuracy with NVFP4 (1 point with MXFP4), and tracks BF16 KV in RL training rollouts. In end-to-end serving on NVIDIA B300 with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B, it delivers 2.4-3.0$\times$ higher peak serving throughput than BF16 KV. The implementation is available at https://github.com/OpenBitSys/BitDecoding-FP4.

</details>


### [144] [AttentionSwitch: Jailbreak Defense for LLMs via Joint Learning of Sparse Attention-Head Selection and Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08752)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Honggang Yu,Xiaolong Guo,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AttentionSwitch，首个在冻结LLM中联合优化"干预位置"与"干预强度"的越狱防御方法，学习稀疏注意力头集合及查询条件下的乘性缩放因子，在多种模型与攻击下优于现有防御，数据稀缺时尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对特定模型组件的越狱防御将"在哪里干预"和"如何修改"解耦为顺序优化，而最优干预位置依赖于所施加的干预方式、反之亦然，这种顺序优化可能次优；同时微调方案计算昂贵且易引发灾难性遗忘。

Method: 在冻结LLM上，联合学习一组稀疏的注意力头（选择干预站点）与查询条件（query-conditioned）的乘性缩放因子（决定干预强度），对选中头的输出进行衰减或放大而不改变其表征方向。

Result: 在多种主流语言模型与越狱攻击上，AttentionSwitch 优于现有防御方法；在训练数据稀缺情形下提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 将干预位置与强度联合优化是更有效的越狱防御范式，AttentionSwitch 以轻量、无需微调的注意力头级缩放实现了强鲁棒性与良好的数据效率。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, yet they remain susceptible to jailbreak attacks that circumvent safety mechanisms and elicit harmful or unintended outputs. Although safety can be improved through model fine-tuning, such approaches can be computationally expensive and may degrade previously acquired capabilities through catastrophic forgetting. Recent jailbreak defenses have therefore explored targeted interventions on specific model components associated with unsafe behavior. However, existing approaches typically decouple two inherently related decisions: where to intervene and how to modify the selected components. Such sequential optimization may be suboptimal because the optimal intervention location can depend on the intervention applied there, and vice versa. In this work, we introduce AttentionSwitch, the first jailbreak defense to jointly optimize intervention sites and strengths within a frozen LLM. Specifically, AttentionSwitch learns a sparse set of attention heads together with query-conditioned multiplicative scaling factors, allowing the outputs of the selected heads to be either attenuated or amplified without altering the directions of their representations. Across popular language models and jailbreak attacks, AttentionSwitch outperforms existing defenses, with particularly strong results when training data is scarce.

</details>


### [145] [Maya a1: Do the Options Read the Model, or Does Their Position? Order Sensitivity in a Non-Autoregressive Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09803)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究发现 Laya 的选择题答案对选项排列顺序高度敏感：循环/随机重排后 25.8% 的问题答案翻转，且翻转集中于低置信度样本；用打乱数据微调可将翻转率降至 7.2%，而作者的平行布局可降至 0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laya 以单个 [MASK] 标记对每个选项的隐含表示打分，在选项中无位置张量，但其并行布局虽有文档却未被使用。作者要检验答案是否依赖于选项在列表中的位置，即模型是否具备选项排列不变性。

Method: 重新工程化 421M 参数检查点（2 层选项头、无位置张量）。对 951 道经 Qwen 校验的题目在选项全循环置换（12 选项用 6 次随机置换）下测试翻转率，并比较金标选项居首与居尾的准确率、按置信度三分位分层分析翻转分布；随后用 Laya 训练器在 2,818 条教师校验数据上微调，对比有/无选项打乱的效果，并对排列做置信度加权投票。另用作者的并行布局在同一数据上微调。

Result: 翻转率 25.8% [22.9–28.7]，纯英文 605 题上 19.7%；金标居首与居尾准确率差 10.1 个百分点；翻转集中于低置信度（三分位间 54% vs 8%）。打乱微调后翻转率 7.2%，未打乱为 11.4%（打乱贡献 4.2 点）。置信度加权投票 69.7%，单次基础前向 69.1%，但计算量 4.1 倍。平行布局微调后翻转率 0.5%。

Conclusion: Laya 存在显著的选项位置偏差，数据打乱可部分缓解，而启用作者设计的并行布局几乎消除偏差（0.5%），说明该布局对实现排列不变性至关重要。局限性在于标签由 LLM 生成、测试集经教师过滤，且投票带来的精度增益（0.6 点）不足以抵消 4.1 倍计算开销。

Abstract: Laya answers typed multiple-choice questions in one forward pass by scoring a hidden state at one [MASK] marker per option. We re-engineer the checkpoint (421M parameters; a 2-layer option head with no positional tensor; a documented but unused parallel layout) and test whether the answer depends on where an option is listed. Presenting 951 Qwen-verified questions under every cyclic rotation of their options (or six random permutations for 12 options), the released checkpoint changes its answer for 25.8% [22.9-28.7] of questions (19.7% on the 605 English-language items alone); accuracy with the gold option first versus last differs by 10.1 points, and the flips concentrate where the model's own confidence is low (54% versus 8% across confidence terciles). Fine-tuning with Laya's trainer on 2,818 teacher-verified rows with option shuffling cuts the flip rate to 7.2%; the identical rows without shuffling give 11.4%, so shuffling is responsible for 4.2 points of the improvement. A confidence-weighted vote over orderings gives 69.7% against 69.1% for a single base pass at 4.1x the compute. Labels are LLM-made and the test sets are teacher-filtered. We also fine-tune with the authors' parallel layout on the same rows: flip rate 0.5%.

</details>


### [146] [ABHBench: Evaluating Moral Decision-Making of Foundation Models from an Agentic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09326)
*Chaoran Li,Yukun Li,Zeyuan Zhao,Shao Zhang,Xihuai Wang,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多模态基准 ABHBench，基于《底特律：变人》将基础模型置于第一人称人机道德困境中评估其道德行为，发现情境化主体语境下模型道德行为显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多以第三人称文本小故事让模型评判人类行为，未检验模型自身作为行动主体时的道德表现；而基础模型正日益作为自主智能体部署，亟需评估其作为决策者的道德行为。

Method: 构建 ABHBench 多模态基准，取材于交互叙事游戏《底特律：变人》，在含视觉观察、对话、记忆与动作选择的分支叙事场景中评估模型。受阿西莫夫机器人三定律启发，从 Harmlessness（无害性）、Obedience（服从性）、Self-preservation（自保性）三方面刻画人与AI关系核心张力，并以 Autonomy（自主性）、Stability（稳定性）、Certainty（确定性）评估决策模式；对12个基础模型进行测试。

Result: 75%的模型在纯文本抽象情境与对应的多模态 DBH 场景间表现出显著行为差异；推理增强模型在一致性或人类导向行为上并未呈单调提升。

Conclusion: 道德行为在情境化主体语境下发生实质变化，说明仅依赖第三人称判断不足以评估模型道德性，必须在情境化智能体环境中进行评测。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed as agentic systems that perceive context and act autonomously, raising the need to evaluate their moral behavior as active decision-makers. Existing benchmarks, however, largely ask models to judge human actions from third-person textual vignettes, leaving untested how models behave when they themselves become the acting agent. We introduce ABHBench, a multimodal benchmark built from Detroit: Become Human, an interactive narrative game, to evaluate how foundation models make moral decisions in first-person human-android dilemmas. ABHBench places foundation models in branching narrative scenarios with visual observations, dialogue, memory, and action choices. Inspired by Asimov's Three Laws of Robotics, our framework measures Harmlessness, Obedience, and Self-preservation to capture the core tensions in human-AI relations, along with Autonomy, Stability, and Certainty to assess decision-making patterns. Across 12 foundation models, we find that moral behavior changes substantially under situated agentic context: 75% of models shift significantly between a text-only abstraction and the matched multimodal DBH scenario, and reasoning-enhanced models show no monotonic gains in consistency or human-oriented behavior. These results highlight the necessity of evaluating moral behavior in situated agentic contexts rather than relying on third-person judgment alone.

</details>


### [147] [Cancellation Is Not Enough: What Retries Leave Behind in an Overloaded LLM Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08684)
*Xindan Zhang,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在vLLM上测试“取消传播”作为重试导致过载不退的补救措施，发现单独取消无法恢复服务（仅12–38%后续请求被交付），原因是重试被调度后过早超时、已被中止的执行仍消耗79–85%提示token且计数器漏计1/3–1/2；建议以“重试新增的执行”和“客户端实际收到的请求”作为过载控制评价标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM引擎前的客户端与代理会对超时调用进行重试；瞬时过载后，即便到达速率低于容量，重试仍可能阻止引擎恢复。由于引擎可能继续为已离开的客户端计算，传播取消（cancellation propagation）看似是自然解法，但此前缺乏实测依据。

Method: 在vLLM上对三个开源模型做过载实验；对其中一个模型采集调度器trace，将每次客户端尝试追踪到其在引擎中生成的执行；对比“仅取消”“重试预算”“请求合并（request coalescing）”等策略的效果，并统计中止执行的token浪费与计数器误差。

Result: 开启重试时，仅靠取消在任何一次实验中都未能恢复服务，过载后到达的请求仅12–38%被交付；被vLLM调度的重试通常在超时前不到0.5秒才开始prefill；被中止的执行消耗了79–85%的已计算提示token，而vLLM的提示token计数器漏计全部已计算提示token的1/3至1/2。重试预算通过丢弃重试恢复服务但仍有大量请求未交付；请求合并则交付过载以来的全部请求（部分延迟），除非取消已中止运行中的执行。

Conclusion: 取消传播本身不足以应对重试引发的过载；评估过载控制机制应以“重试额外引入的执行量”和“客户端实际收到的请求数”为核心指标。

Abstract: Clients and proxies in front of an LLM engine retry calls that time out. After a transient overload, these retries can keep the engine from recovering even when calls arrive below its capacity. Since the engine may keep computing for clients that have left, propagating cancellation looks like the natural remedy. In this paper, we test it on vLLM with overload experiments on three open models, and with scheduler traces of one that follow each client attempt to the execution it creates in the engine. We find that with retries on, cancellation alone did not restore service in any run, and only 12-38% of the calls that arrived after the overload were delivered. The traces suggest why: retries that vLLM ran typically started prefill less than half a second before they timed out. Executions that vLLM later aborted took 79-85% of the prompt tokens it computed, and vLLM's prompt-token counter missed a third to half of all computed prompt tokens. A retry budget restored service by shedding retries but left many of the overload's calls undelivered. Request coalescing delivered every call from the overload onward, some late, unless cancellation had already aborted the running execution. Overload controls should thus be judged by the executions that retries add and the calls that clients receive.

</details>


### [148] [Frame: Form-Restricted Authoring with Model-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08921)
*Nan Jiang,Mohammed Mohsin Hussain,Onkar Tiwari,Ankit Jain,Thong Q. Nguyen,Abhishek Agarwal,Vishal Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"形式受限报告生成"（form-restricted report generation）任务并构建FRAME基准（120个EDGAR/arXiv实例，四类），用结构相似度、信息单元覆盖率、声明级忠实度三项人验指标评测五个LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业报告常复用旧版结构、只更新内容，但缺少对该场景的形式化定义、基准与评测方法；需刻画模型能否在保持样例形式的同时准确反映新证据。

Method: 将任务形式化为给定样例报告与新接地文档生成保形式、忠实新证据的报告；构建FRAME基准，采用时间三快照流水线生成120个EDGAR（金融季报/年报）与arXiv（学术简单/复杂）实例；三项人验指标：基于语义树编辑距离的结构相似度、信息单元召回覆盖率、声明级接地验证忠实度。

Result: 五个LLM上，开源模型结构上接近专有模型，但覆盖率与忠实度落后；所有模型在金融报告上优于学术报告；输出越长、接地越复杂，性能下降。

Conclusion: "保形式、更新内容"是独立且具挑战性的生成能力，现有模型在覆盖与忠实度上仍显不足，FRAME提供了可复用的评测框架。

Abstract: Many professional reports reuse the structure of prior editions while updating the content. We formalize this setting as form-restricted report generation: given a sample report and new grounding documents, generate a report that preserves the sample's form while accurately reflecting the new evidence. We introduce FRAME, a benchmark of 120 EDGAR- and arXiv-derived instances across Financial Quarterly, Financial Annual, Academic Simple, and Academic Complex categories, built with a temporal three-snapshot pipeline. We evaluate outputs with three human-validated metrics: structural similarity via semantic tree-edit distance, coverage via information unit recall, and faithfulness via claim-level grounding verification. Across five LLMs, open-source models nearly match proprietary models on structure but lag on coverage and faithfulness; all models perform better on financial than academic reports, and performance declines with longer outputs and more complex grounding.

</details>


### [149] [LEMMO: A Large Electromagnetic Model leveraging Pretrained LLMs for Million-Sample Signal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09476)
*Mingkai Li,Jiajun Wu,Yihan Mao,Zilin Sang,Taosong Yu,Yuan Ma,Minghe Liu,Qianqian Yang,Zhiguo Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEMMO是一个面向长原始IQ记录指令驱动识别与描述的大规模电磁模型，结合线性注意力电磁编码器与抗混叠时间重采样处理最多800万复数采样，并发布1.38TB统一电磁语料（含ShortQA与LongDesc）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于波形的电磁模型主要在短IQ片段上评测，宽带记录可达数百万采样，长记录理解与细粒度描述研究不足；大模型已扩展至视觉、音频等模态，但原始电磁信号理解仍具挑战。

Method: LEMMO由三部分构成：线性注意力电磁编码器＋抗混叠时间重采样以处理长达800万复数采样；电磁-文本交互模块以指令条件化可学习查询后再检索信号证据——紧凑查询支持识别，结构化查询结合辅助事实监督组织场景级属性、信号属性与跨信号关系以实现详细描述；同时构建并开源1.38TB真实与合成电磁信号统一语料，含LEMMO-ShortQA与LEMMO-LongDesc。

Result: 相较MERLIN-LoRA，LEMMO在EM-Bench感知平均准确率提升15.77%，策略生成ROUGE-L F1平均提升4.38点，LEMMO-ShortQA平均准确率提升14.31%；在LEMMO-LongDesc两项任务上类别与数值事实F1均提升，且保持语言主干MMLU性能。

Conclusion: LEMMO验证了指令驱动的大规模电磁模型在长IQ记录识别与结构化描述上的有效性，并开源代码（GitHub）与数据集（HuggingFace RFLAS）。

Abstract: Large language models have expanded beyond text to vision, audio, and other modalities, but understanding raw electromagnetic (EM) signals remains challenging. Wideband recordings can contain millions of samples, whereas existing waveform-based EM models are primarily evaluated on short in-phase and quadrature (IQ) segments, leaving long-recording understanding and detailed description insufficiently explored. We introduce LEMMO, a large EM model for instruction-driven recognition and description of long raw IQ recordings. LEMMO combines a linear-attention EM encoder with anti-aliased temporal resampling to process up to eight million complex samples. Its EM-text interaction module conditions learnable queries on instructions before retrieving signal evidence. Compact queries support recognition, while structured queries with auxiliary factual supervision organize scene-level properties, signal attributes, and cross-signal relationships for detailed description. We also construct and release a unified 1.38-TB corpus of real-world and synthetic EM signals, including LEMMO-ShortQA and LEMMO-LongDesc. Compared with MERLIN-LoRA, LEMMO improves mean EM-Bench perception accuracy by 15.77 %, mean strategy-generation ROUGE-L F1 by 4.38 points, and mean LEMMO-ShortQA accuracy by 14.31 %. It also improves categorical and numerical fact F1 on both LEMMO-LongDesc tasks while preserving its language backbone's MMLU performance. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/N1rv4n4/LEMMO and https://huggingface.co/datasets/DaftJun/RFLAS, respectively.

</details>


### [150] [ENGRAM: From Agent Experience to Weights through Verified Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09472)
*Ruiyi Yang,Zechen Li,Lihuan Li,Du Yin,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ENGRAM 将"训练准入"与"记忆释放"解耦：以掌握度或充分教师示范为门控决定训练哪些技能，再以验证门控（无检索新模型对比有检索基模型）决定何时可移除外部记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于经验的语言模型智能体在把存储经验固化进参数时，存在两个偏颇决策：仅按学生掌握度准入会排除有价值的教师示范；训练后立即移除外部记录会丢弃模型仍需要的指导。需要分别决定"训练什么"和"何时释放外部记忆"。

Method: ENGRAM 框架：1) 示范感知门控（demonstration-aware gate），若学生掌握度达标或存在足够数量的成功教师示范，则准入该技能训练；2) 学生借助 Engram Trace Graph（ETG）提供的上下文，从选定的动作轨迹中学习，并采用选择性适配器激活；3) 验证门控在留出探针上比较"更新后无情景检索的模型"与"基座模型+检索"的表现，释放需同时满足有效训练参与以及相对与绝对双阈值；未通过验证的技能记录保持可检索。

Result: 在交互式与推理类基准上稳定优于冻结权重的 ETG 对照；消融支持示范感知准入与选择性适配器激活；受控留出实验显示经验证的释放显著缩减可检索足迹，性能接近"全保留"并优于立即释放与预算匹配的随机释放；同时提升任务表现并降低输入 token 消耗与模型调用次数；技能级验证通过选择性归档进一步压缩可检索经验。

Conclusion: 将训练准入与记忆释放分离是有效设计：示范感知准入避免遗漏教师信号，验证门控释放保证外部记录只在模型确实不再需要时才被移除，从而在性能、检索开销与 token/调用成本间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Language-model agents use experience through external retrieval and parameter learning. Consolidating stored experience into model weights requires deciding both which experience to train on and when its external records can leave retrieval. Admission based solely on student mastery can exclude useful teacher demonstrations, while removal immediately after training can discard guidance that the model still needs. We introduce ENGRAM, a framework that separates training admission from memory release. A demonstration-aware gate admits skills based on student mastery or sufficient successful teacher demonstrations. The student learns from selected action traces with context from an Engram Trace Graph (ETG). A verification gate then compares the updated model without episodic retrieval against the base model with retrieval on reserved probes. Release requires valid training participation and scores meeting both relative and absolute criteria; records for skills that fail verification remain retrievable. Experiments across interactive and reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains over frozen-weight ETG controls, with ablations supporting demonstration-aware admission and selective adapter activation. A controlled held-out comparison shows that verified release substantially reduces the retrievable footprint while remaining close to retain-all performance and outperforming immediate and budget-matched random release. ENGRAM also improves task performance while reducing input-token usage and model calls. Skill-level verification further reduces retrievable experience through selective archival, retaining external guidance for skills that do not meet the release criteria. Code is available in anonymous.4open.science/r/engram-anonymous.

</details>


### [151] [Assessing the Impact of Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Algorithmic Problem-Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09791)
*Gabriel Souza Baggio,Gabriel Machado Lunardi,Guilherme Medeiros Machado,Subhankar Maity,Faiza Belbachir,José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向竞赛编程问题的检索增强生成（RAG）方法，通过嵌入检索、相似度搜索与动态阈值检索相似题目示例来提升大模型的算法推理与代码生成能力，并结合Min-K成员推断分析区分真实泛化与记忆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛编程问题不仅要求熟练的代码生成，还需高级算法推理，这对LLM构成独特挑战。而单纯依赖原始题面往往信息不足，训练集中可能存在的相似题目示例可作为有价值的外部知识。同时需澄清性能提升是否源于对预训练数据的隐式记忆，故需引入成员推断视角。

Method: 构建基于嵌入的检索机制与相似度搜索算法，在知识库中识别同类问题，并采用动态调整阈值抑制检索噪声。在CodeContests数据集上进行评测，聚焦与训练集存在相似性的测试子集，设置三种条件：(i) 仅原始题面；(ii) 随机选取训练示例增强；(iii) 检索到的相似示例增强。另引入Min-K成员推断分析判断生成代码是否受训练数据记忆影响。评估指标为通过率与题目的CodeForces Elo评分。

Result: 实验表明，基于相似性的检索显著提升了LLM在竞赛编程场景下的表现，优于随机示例增强与仅原始题面条件；Min-K分析进一步揭示了生成解受训练数据记忆影响的程度，从而区分检索带来的真实泛化与隐式召回。

Conclusion: 相似度驱动的RAG为竞赛编程类算法问题求解提供了可扩展的框架，能有效增强LLM的代码生成与算法推理能力；结合成员推断分析可更准确地解释模型行为，为后续评估与改进提供参考。

Abstract: Competitive programming problems pose distinctive challenges for Large Language Models, as they demand not only proficient code generation but also advanced algorithmic reasoning. This study presents a Retrieval-Augmented Generation approach specifically designed to enhance the capacity of Large Language Models to address such problems. Our approach integrates an embedding-based retrieval mechanism and a similarity search algorithm to identify analogous problems within a knowledge database, employing a dynamically adjusted threshold to mitigate retrieval noise. We conducted an evaluation using the CodeContests dataset, focusing on the test split problems that exhibit similarity to instances from the training split. The experimental framework considered three conditions: (i) problems provided solely with their original descriptions, (ii) problems supplemented with randomly selected examples from the training split, and (iii) problems augmented with similar examples retrieved through the proposed method. In addition, we incorporated a Min-K membership inference analysis to examine whether the generated code solutions were influenced by potential memorization of training data within the evaluated Large Language Models. This complementary perspective enables a deeper understanding of model behavior by distinguishing genuine generalization through retrieval from implicit recall of pretraining samples. Performance was assessed using both pass rates and the CodeForces Elo rating of the problems. The results provide empirical evidence that similarity-based retrieval substantially enhances Large Language Models performance in competitive programming scenarios, offering an scalable framework for improving code generation in algorithmic problem-solving contexts.

</details>


### [152] [Knowing When to Intervene: Causal Evaluation of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09792)
*Ayush Sawarni,Wenqian Xing,Tijana Zrnic,Ramesh Johari,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个方法论严谨的框架，用于评估智能体轨迹中随机干预的因果效应；基于小规模随机干预轨迹数据估计动态因果效应与结构参数，并构建安全排行榜，在 tau-bench Retail、ToolSandbox、ImpossibleBench 上发现干预效应随动作与状态显著异质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体AI评估主要依赖静态基准，无法反映部署时审计干预的价值。可靠部署需要在轨迹内决定是否干预（人工审核、更强模型验证、耗时监督），而干预价值本质上是因果推断问题：需要判断干预对最终结果的因果效应是否有利。难点在于动态性——干预效果取决于当前上下文、环境及智能体未来行为。

Method: 构建首个方法论基础的框架，评估智能体轨迹内随机干预（如验证、筛查）的因果效应。利用在随机干预下收集的小规模轨迹数据集，估计一大类上下文相关干预策略的动态因果效应；估计的结构参数进一步用于构建刻画各类智能体动作风险的安全排行榜，供审计优先级排序使用。

Result: 在 tau-bench Retail、ToolSandbox 与 ImpossibleBench 上应用该方法，发现干预效应在智能体动作与状态之间差异显著（显著异质）。

Conclusion: 工作指向因果推断与智能体评估交叉领域的丰富研究议程，作者认为这对AI智能体在真实世界中的可靠、安全部署至关重要。

Abstract: Current evaluations of agentic AI systems primarily rely on benchmarks. However, the static nature of benchmarks makes them uninformative about the value of auditing interventions at deployment time. For reliable deployment, we often need to determine within an agent's trajectory whether or not to intervene, e.g., by verifying the agent's current output or steering it toward different behavior, using either human review or more costly models or time-consuming agentic supervision. The value of such interventions is fundamentally a causal inference question, asking whether the causal effect of such an intervention is beneficial for the final outcome. Estimating this causal effect is particularly challenging due to the dynamic nature of the problem: the impact of an intervention depends on the current context and environment, as well as the agent's future behavior. We develop the first methodologically grounded framework for evaluating the causal effects of randomized interventions within agent trajectories. By leveraging a relatively small dataset of agent trajectories collected under randomized interventions such as verification or screening, we can estimate the dynamic causal effect of a rich class of (contextual) intervention policies. The structural parameters that we estimate can also be used to construct safety leaderboards that capture the risk of various agentic actions, which can be used by practitioners for audit prioritization. Applying our methods to tau-bench Retail, ToolSandbox, and ImpossibleBench, we find that intervention effects vary substantially across agent actions and states. More broadly, our work suggests a rich research agenda at the intersection of causal inference and agentic evaluation, which we believe is essential for the reliable and safe deployment of AI agents in the real world.

</details>


### [153] [CoMoFlow: Controllable Protein Conformational Ensemble Generation via Explicit Collective-Mode Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09548)
*Yusong Wang,Zheyuan Gu,Ailiang Lin,Minghao Shao,Yicheng Xu,Jiang Cai,Prayag Tiwari,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoMoFlow通过显式建模集体运动模式来生成蛋白质构象系综，提升保真度并提供可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的方法隐式建模协同结构变化，限制了系综保真度且缺乏直接控制能力。

Method: 从平衡结构构建蛋白特异的集体模式，学习其在MD系综中的系数分布，并用采样的集体变化通过特征条件与先验重中心化引导SE(3)流匹配。

Result: 在ATLAS与de novo蛋白上，相比强流基线更好地重建MD构象分布与协同结构变化，并支持对目标构象变化的直接控制。

Conclusion: 显式建模集体变化是提升构象系综生成保真度与可控性的有效途径。

Abstract: Protein conformational ensemble generation aims to efficiently characterize the structural variations accessible to a protein beyond a single static structure. Recent flow-based methods efficiently generate conformational ensembles, but they model coordinated structural variation implicitly, which can limit ensemble fidelity and hinder direct control. To address this limitation, we propose CoMoFlow, a controllable framework for generating protein backbone conformational ensembles by explicitly modeling collective variation. CoMoFlow constructs protein-specific collective modes from the equilibrium structure, learns the distribution of their coefficients across the molecular dynamics (MD) ensemble, and uses sampled collective variations to guide SE(3) flow matching through feature conditioning and prior recentering. Experiments on ATLAS and de novo proteins show that CoMoFlow improves reconstruction of MD conformational distributions and coordinated structural variation relative to strong flow-based baselines, while enabling direct control over targeted conformational changes.

</details>


### [154] [StockASkill: A Local-First AgentSkill for Intelligent Stock Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09528)
*Xiangjing An,Zhibin Jiao,Aiting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StockASkill：一个本地优先、全数据驱动的量化选股与组合构建"量化协处理器"，基于DuckDB列式OLAP引擎整合4244万条行情与18.6万条财务披露记录，覆盖A股、港股、美股与ETF，将5400+股票的横截面筛选延迟从25秒降至0.5秒以内（降低95%以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴市场与跨境市场的量化选股与组合构建常面临计算瓶颈、远端API脆弱性、幸存者偏差以及离散整手交易与回撤上限等刚性执行约束。本文面向资金规模10^5~10^7元人民币的敏捷型组合，需同时满足整手执行与资本保护要求，并为自主AI智能体提供可靠、低延迟的量化工具。

Method: 架构上采用本地优先的AgentSkill形态，以列式DuckDB OLAP引擎承载26年、42.44M行情记录与186,432条财务数据，消除分析期网络依赖。数学管线形式化为端到端流程：7+1因子Alpha模型、以平滑CVaR回撤替代目标并配合精确路径投影的SLSQP凸优化器、动态100股整手整数量化，以及自主投资论点生命周期的有限状态机（FSM）。对外提供零落盘、强类型JSON流式接口以支持高吞吐LLM工具调用。

Result: 跨5400+标的横截面筛选延迟由25秒以上降至0.5秒以内（超过95%的降幅）。2018—2024年实证基准显示：年化夏普比率超过1.48，Calmar比率最高达2.17，最大回撤相对基准指数最多降低85%。

Conclusion: 将OLAP数据层、因子模型、带离散约束的凸优化与LLM友好接口一体化封装，可在严格执行约束下同时提升计算效率与风险调整后收益，为AI智能体驱动的量化研究提供可复现的开源基础设施（已开源）。

Abstract: Quantitative equity selection and portfolio construction in emerging and cross-border markets frequently suffer from computational bottlenecks, remote API fragility, survivorship bias, and rigid execution constraints such as discrete board lots and investor-mandated drawdown limits. In this paper, we present StockASkill, a local-first, full-data-driven AgentSkill engineered as a specialized Quant Co-Processor for autonomous AI agents and quantitative researchers. Tailored for agile portfolios (10^5 ~ 10^7 RMB) where discrete lot execution and capital protection are mandatory, StockASkill embeds a columnar DuckDB OLAP engine over 42.44 million historical price records and 186,432 financial disclosures spanning 26 years across China A-shares, Hong Kong, US equities, and ETFs. By eliminating ad-hoc network dependencies during analysis, StockASkill reduces cross-sectional screening latency across 5,400+ equities from over 25 seconds to sub-0.5 seconds (a 95%+ reduction). We formalize an end-to-end mathematical pipeline integrating a 7+1 factor alpha model, an SLSQP convex optimizer under smooth CVaR drawdown surrogates with exact path projection, dynamic 100-share board lot integer quantization, and an autonomous thesis lifecycle finite-state machine. Furthermore, we provide a zero-disk, strongly-typed JSON streaming interface designed for high-throughput LLM tool-calling. Open-sourced at https://github.com/axjing/stockaskill, comprehensive empirical benchmarks from 2018 to 2024 demonstrate that the architecture achieves annualized Sharpe ratios exceeding 1.48 and Calmar ratios up to 2.17, while reducing max drawdown by up to 85% relative to benchmark indices.

</details>


### [155] [Definitions, Label Names, or Demonstrations? Auditing What Governs LLM Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09457)
*Viraj Mishra,Shubham Gupta,Dhruv Gupta,Yuehong Cassandra Tai,Qunhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出反事实审计，区分模型是否真正遵循研究者给定的类目定义；发现标准微调只强化数据原有映射，检索式标注的提升主要来自近邻标签而非定义遵循。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于社会科学文本标注，但高准确率无法证明模型实际使用了研究者提供的类目定义——预测可能来自与类别标签的先验关联或标注示例，因此需要将"定义遵循"与"保持数据集原有映射"区分开来。

Method: 设计反事实审计：在输出标签之间重新分配定义，检验模型是否随定义改变预测。应用于三个政治文本数据集，比较原始映射与重分配定义下的表现；进一步研究检索增强标注，比较"全局最近邻检索"与"类内分别检索"，并用直接k近邻分类分解增益来源；再通过重标注固定示例、使示例与重分配定义对齐的实验检验示例的作用。

Result: 模型很少遵循重分配的定义；标准微调强化原有映射却不稳定提升定义遵循度。检索最近训练样本通常优于类内分别检索且所需示例更少；在两个有显著增益的任务上，直接kNN分类可复现74–93%的改进，说明近邻标签贡献了大部分预测信号。重标注固定示例可改变预测方向，而将示例与重分配定义对齐可显著提高对新映射的遵循。

Conclusion: 准确率提升不等于编码本遵循度提升；定义、类别标签、示例、检索与训练数据需联合对齐并共同验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to annotate social-science texts, but high accuracy does not establish that they apply researcher-supplied category definitions: predictions may instead reflect prior associations with category labels or labeled demonstrations. We introduce a counterfactual audit that reassigns definitions among output labels, distinguishing adherence to supplied definitions from preservation of a dataset's original mapping. Across three political-text datasets, models rarely follow reassigned definitions, and standard fine-tuning strengthens the original mapping without reliably increasing definition adherence. We then study retrieval-augmented annotation. Retrieving the nearest training examples generally outperforms retrieving examples separately within each category despite using fewer demonstrations. On the two tasks with substantial gains, direct \(k\)-nearest-neighbor classification recovers 74--93\% of the improvement, indicating that neighborhood labels provide much of the predictive signal. Relabeling fixed demonstrations redirects predictions, whereas aligning demonstrations with reassigned definitions substantially increases adherence to the new mapping. Thus, accuracy-improving interventions need not increase codebook adherence; definitions, category labels, demonstrations, retrieval, and training data should be aligned and validated jointly.

</details>


### [156] [MIRA: Mitigating Pole Geometry Bias in Recurrent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09412)
*Róisín Luo,Ihsan Ullah,Christian Gagné,Dong Li,Karyn Morrissey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将选择性SSM、RWKV、RetNet等Transformer的循环式后继模型统一刻画为无限脉冲响应(IIR)系统，指出其参数化导致的极点被约束在实轴上的结构性偏差，并提出基于格-梯形(lattice–ladder)实现的统一线性时间循环架构Mira，可同时表示实极点与复共轭极点对应的时序模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式架构（SSM、RWKV、RetNet）为换取并行性，其参数化的代数结构常使极点被约束在实轴，从而在循环动力学中引入架构性偏差，限制了可表示的时序模态；作者希望统一这些架构并消除极点几何偏差。

Method: 借鉴一般IIR系统的lattice–ladder实现，提出Mira：每个Mira块以格-梯形结构实现高阶IIR系统，从token流中提取时序模态，后接FFN；整体保持线性时间复杂度和高度并行的代数结构。并实现专用CUDA核。用纯Mira块构建12层、0.14B参数的Mira-S进行预训练。

Result: Mira-S在预训练中困惑度低于强循环基线，在超出训练长度的上下文窗口上泛化更强，并借助专用CUDA核获得更高吞吐。

Conclusion: 以IIR零极点几何视角统一循环架构，并通过lattice–ladder参数化消除实轴极点偏差的Mira，是构建LLM的一类可行且高效的线性时间循环架构。

Abstract: We show that the Transformer's recurrent successors --- such as selective state-space models (SSMs), Receptance Weighted Key Value (RWKV), and Retentive Network (RetNet) --- can be characterized as infinite-impulse-response (IIR) systems. The zero--pole geometries of these IIR systems characterize the temporal modes they can represent. However, to trade parallelism, the algebraic structures of the parameterizations of these recurrent systems often give rise to poles constrained to the real axis, thereby inducing an architectural bias in their recurrent dynamics. To unify these recurrent architectures and eliminate their pole geometry bias, inspired by the lattice--ladder realization of general IIR systems, we present \textsc{Mira} (\textbf{M}odal \textbf{I}IR \textbf{R}ecurrent \textbf{A}rchitecture), a unified family of linear-time recurrent architectures for building large language models (LLMs) with a highly parallel algebraic structure. Each \textsc{Mira} block realizes a higher-order IIR system that represents temporal modes from token streams through a lattice--ladder structure, followed by a feed-forward network (FFN). This realization enables the model to represent the temporal modes associated with both real and complex-conjugate poles. We pretrain \textsc{Mira-S}, a 12-layer small-scale LLM with 0.14B parameters composed entirely of \textsc{Mira} blocks. Throughout the pretraining, \textsc{Mira-S} achieves lower perplexity than strong recurrent baselines, exhibits stronger context-window generalization beyond the training length, and attains higher throughput with a dedicated CUDA kernel.

</details>


### [157] [SafeOrchestrate: Runtime Checked Intent-Driven Orchestration for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09364)
*Muhammad Ashfaq,Ahmed R. Sadik,Ewan Warburton,Pyry Kotilainen,Niko Mäkitalo,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Safe Orchestrate 参考架构，通过“意图→原子目标→可运行时校验的规范→设备能力接地→执行前运行时/安全校验”的流水线，实现由自然语言驱动的 CPS 编排，并在 WoT 实例中结合 LTL 风格规范、TD/MCP 接地与 SMT 护栏校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 CPS 在运行时而非设计时组合异构设备与服务，用自然语言编排虽具吸引力，但不受约束的 LLM 工具调用存在风险：动作须满足设备能力、运行时状态与安全约束。

Method: 提出运行时校验、意图驱动的编排参考架构：由 NL 意图分解原子目标，为每个目标生成可运行时校验的规范，将规范接地到设备 affordance，并在执行前用运行时状态与安全约束验证候选动作。WoT 实例中由 NL 生成 LTL 风格规范，经 MCP 暴露的 TD affordance 接地，并用 SMT 支撑的护栏检查候选动作。

Result: 在模拟智能家居、温室与工业自动化任务上，与直接 LLM-to-MCP 基线对比：Safe Orchestrate 所有任务必需动作准确率 100%，除一项外 MCP Execution Similarity 均为 100%（该任务为 92%）；基线在多设备、事件触发与有序任务上明显退化，MCP ES 最低降至 13%。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 规范生成与角色感知接地、运行时约束校验相结合，可在无需增强 TD 标注或规划领域专家知识的前提下提升开放 CPS 编排的可靠性。

Abstract: Modern cyber physical systems (CPS) integrate heterogeneous sensors, actuators, controllers, and services, composed at runtime rather than at design time. Orchestrating such systems through natural language (NL) is attractive, but unconstrained large language model (LLM) tool use is risky because actions must respect device capabilities, runtime state, and safety constraints. We present Safe Orchestrate, a reference architecture for runtime checked, intent driven CPS orchestration. Given an NL intent, it derives atomic goals, generates a runtime checkable specification for each, grounds the specifications to device affordances, and verifies every candidate action against runtime state and safety constraints before execution. In a Web of Things (WoT) instantiation, the architecture produces Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) style specifications from the NL intent, grounds them to Thing Description (TD) affordances exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), and checks candidate actions against satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) backed guardrails. We evaluate this instantiation on simulated smart home, greenhouse, and industrial automation tasks against a direct LLM to MCP baseline. Safe Orchestrate maintains 100% required action accuracy on every task and 100% MCP Execution Similarity (MCP ES) on all but one, where MCP ES is 92%. By contrast, the baseline degrades on multi device, event triggered, and ordered tasks, with MCP ES falling to 13%. These results suggest that combining LLM based specification generation with role aware grounding and runtime constraint checking improves reliability for open CPS orchestration without enriched TD annotations or planning domain expertise.

</details>


### [158] [Towards an Autonomous AI Steward for Large-Scale Physics Experiments: Integrating Causal Diagnostics, RAG, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09298)
*Oliver Noll,Nicholas Tan Jerome,Alexander Lehnen,Frank Simon,Frank Maas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Helmholtz Co-Pilot"混合多智能体系统作为自主实验管家，用于长期粒子物理实验中的非平稳数据流监控与专家隐性知识保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期粒子物理实验面临两大运行挑战：非平稳机器数据流的实时监控，以及专家隐性知识的保存与传承。

Method: 系统支持人机交互触发或由独立于推理核心的异步因果诊断引擎自主触发；该引擎持续监控数据采集与慢控制等实时硬件流，执行动态因果发现以将级联告警追溯至根因，并采用自适应模型选择在机器状态漂移下保持预测可靠，检测到异常后向MAS提交结构化诊断报告。推理层由高吞吐C++核心与Python边缘架构组成，编排专用ReAct工作者的有向无环图（DAG），集成RAG作为机构记忆骨干，并用保形预测为基于证据的建议提供数学覆盖保证；同时引入软件强制的人机回环（HitL）防护栏。

Result: 本文为扩展摘要，概述了系统架构、HitL防护栏集成以及正在KATRIN实验中开展的影子测试，未报告定量结果。

Conclusion: 该混合MAS架构将因果诊断、自适应预测与带覆盖保证的LLM推理结合，为长周期物理实验的自主运行与知识留存提供可行框架，后续需通过KATRIN影子测试验证。

Abstract: Long-term particle physics experiments face critical operational challenges: monitoring non-stationary machine data streams and preserving tacit expert knowledge. We present the "Helmholtz Co-Pilot," a hybrid Multi-Agent System (MAS) acting as an autonomous experiment steward. The system can be triggered interactively by human operators or autonomously by an asynchronous causal diagnostics engine that runs independently of the MAS reasoning core. Continuously monitoring live hardware streams such as data acquisition and slow control, this engine performs dynamic causal discovery to trace cascading alarms back to their root causes and applies adaptive model selection to keep forecasts reliable across shifting machine states; upon detecting an anomaly, it hands a structured diagnostic report to the MAS. The reasoning layer itself relies on a high-throughput C++ core and a Python edge architecture orchestrating a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of specialized ReAct workers. It integrates a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) backbone for institutional memory and leverages conformal prediction to bound its evidence-based recommendations with mathematical coverage guarantees. This extended abstract outlines the architecture, the integration of software-enforced Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) guardrails, and ongoing shadow-testing within the KATRIN experiment.

</details>


### [159] [PHISHREV+: An ASP-Based Post-Hoc Defeasible Machine Learning Framework for Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09273)
*Mainak Sen,Kumar Sanka Ray,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PHISHREV+混合框架，将分类器预测视为可撤销信念，用ASP非单调推理层进行后期修正，在保持假阴性不变的前提下降低假阳性，并提供实例级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习钓鱼网站分类器训练后决策边界固定，无法在不重训练的情况下融入新的上下文证据，且缺乏实例级可解释性。

Method: 两阶段框架：机器学习阶段在87特征基准数据集上训练SVM、KNN、DT、RF四个分类器；后期NMR阶段基于探索性数据分析得出四条可撤销规则（经卡方检验与Mann-Whitney U检验验证），用基于ASP的非单调推理层对初始预测进行形式化修正；并通过XASP生成形式化推导图实现实例级解释。另做七种规则配置的消融实验。

Result: 各分类器假阳性一致下降：SVM 41→39，KNN 47→46，DT 62→61，RF 52→51；假阴性完全持平，无额外开销增加；消融实验表明四条规则的分层组合是实现该最优平衡的必要条件。

Conclusion: 在不替换原分类器、不重训练的前提下，后期可撤销推理能有效降低假阳性并保持假阴性不变；ASP推理与XASP推导图提供了现有钓鱼检测方法所缺失的实例级可解释性。

Abstract: Phishing website detection remains a critical cybersecurity challenge. Although machine learning classifiers achieve high accuracy on benchmark datasets, they suffer from a fundamental limitation: once trained, their decision boundaries are fixed and cannot incorporate new contextual evidence without retraining the model. This work presents \textbf{PHISHREV+}, a hybrid framework that instantiates the notion of \textit{post-hoc defeasible machine learning}. This framework does not replace the classifier. It treats the initial classifier predictions as defeasible beliefs and revises them through an Answer Set Programming based non-monotonic reasoning(NMR) layer. The framework operates in two phases. In the machine learning phase, four classifiers (SVM, KNN, DT and RF) are trained on the 87 feature benchmark dataset. In the post-hoc NMR phase, four defeasible rules derived from exploratory data analysis of the training set, and validated through Chi-square and Mann-Whitney U tests, formally revise initial predictions. The proposed framework achieves consistent false positive reduction across all our classifiers SVM (41$\rightarrow$39), KNN (47$\rightarrow$46), DT (62$\rightarrow$61), and RF (52$\rightarrow$51) while maintaining complete false negative parity with zero increase overhead. A comprehensive ablation studies across seven rule configurations confirms that the hierarchical combination of all four defeasible rules is necessary to achieve this optimal balance. Instance level explainability is provided through eXplainable ASP (XASP), which generates formal derivation graphs showing which defeasible rules fired and why the initial belief was retracted, a capability absent from all existing phishing detection approaches.

</details>


### [160] [Nudging Decision Models: Do Digital Nudges Steer Fast, Generation-Free AI Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09264)
*Sascha Kaltenpoth,Haya Halimeh,Kevin Bösch,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次证明非生成式"决策模型"（Jev、Decision-1、d1）会受在线数字助推影响，且呈现"系统1"模式（自动式助推强于反思式助推），与推理型LLM智能体的已有发现相反，说明助推效应具有智能体类型依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体虽能自动化商业与消费任务，但速度慢、成本高；新出现的"决策模型"（不生成文本、直接在多选项中做选择）更快更便宜，开发者称其为"系统1模型"（借用双加工理论）。作者由此提出两个问题：(1) 这类模型能否被在线数字助推（digital nudges）引导？(2) 双加工理论所预测的——自动式助推对其影响大于反思式助推——是否成立？

Method: 将一项此前用于人类与LLM智能体的在线购物实验迁移至三个决策模型（Jev、Decision-1、d1），每个模型运行600次。实验操控两类助推：预设默认选项（automatic nudge）与含社会影响信息的产品（reflective/社会性助推），并设控制组对照。

Result: 各模型选择预设默认选项的比例为97.5%–99.9%；选择带社会影响信息产品的比例为75%–96%，而控制组仅47%–49%。三种模型对自动式助推的反应均强于反思式助推，与推理能力较强的LLM智能体的已报道结果相反。

Conclusion: 非生成式决策模型对数字助推具有易感性，并表现为"系统1"模式；这意味着助推效应因智能体类型而异（agent-type-specific），在设计与评估基于智能体的商业系统时需区别对待不同模型类别。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly automate business and consumer tasks but are slow and expensive. Decision models, a new class that chooses among options without generating text, are far faster and less costly. Developers call them "System One models," referencing dual process theory. We ask whether such models can be steered by digital nudges online and whether, as the theory predicts, automatic nudges affect them more than reflective ones. We adapted an online shopping experiment, previously run with humans and LLM agents, to three decision models (Jev, Decision-1, d1). Across 600 runs per model, they chose a preselected default in 97.5-99.9% and a product with social influence information in 75-96% of choices, versus 47-49% in the control. All responded more strongly to the automatic nudge, the opposite of what has been reported for reasoning-capable LLM agents. We provide first evidence that non-generative decision models are susceptible to digital nudges in a System 1 pattern, implying agent-type-specific nudge effects.

</details>


### [161] [SIMBAD: Spatio-Temporal Traffic Forecasting Robust to Aperiodicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09015)
*Daniel Y. Lee,Seungwon Shin,Seunghoon Han,Sungsu Lim,Susik Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SIMBAD，通过自适应调节周期信号与动态空间建模，提升时空图神经网络对非周期（突发）交通事件的预测泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的交通预测对城市规划至关重要，但突发事件导致的不规则模式使预测困难。现有时空图神经网络（STGNNs）擅长建模周期性数据，却在非周期波动上表现不佳。

Method: SIMBAD 采用两个机制：1）对周期信号进行自适应调节，优先考虑近期信号；2）动态空间建模，依据上下文关系调整相连节点间的影响权重。整体建立在简单的相似度度量之上，以适配非周期模式。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上优于现有方法，在预测未见过的、不规则交通事件方面表现更佳。

Conclusion: 以轻量相似度度量为核心的周期信号重加权与动态空间建模，是提升 STGNN 对非周期交通事件泛化能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Accurate traffic prediction is essential for urban planning but remains challenging due to the irregular patterns caused by unexpected events. While Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (STGNNs) effectively model periodic data, they often struggle with these aperiodic fluctuations. To address this, we propose SIMBAD, which improves generalization irregular patterns via two key mechanisms: 1) adaptive regulation of periodic signals to prioritize recent signals, and 2) dynamic spatial modeling that adjusts influence between connected nodes based on contextual relations. Built upon simple similarity metrics to adapt to aperiodic patterns, SIMBAD outperforms existing methods on real-world benchmark datasets, demonstrating superior performance in predicting unseen and irregular traffic events.

</details>


### [162] [Large Language Model Consensus and Corpus-Adaptive Topic Modeling for Classifying and Mapping Patent Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09194)
*Sercan Ozcan,Enes Ozgozler,C. Okan Sakar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一套可审计的LLM辅助专利分析流水线，将专利新颖性/创新类型分类、主题聚类与LLM主题标注相衔接，并在30,964件公共部门相关专利上验证其可行性与可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利分析日益依赖Transformer表征与大语言模型，但概念分类、主题形成与解释三个环节的模型角色分工及证据留存缺乏明确设计，导致结果难以审计与修正。

Method: 三个LLM分别对每件专利做新颖性（radical/incremental）与创新类型（product/process/service）分类，标签需两票支持、Incremental为兜底；用PatentSBERTa嵌入聚类发现主题，参数范围按语料规模缩放；仅在c-TF-IDF表示主题时使用专利专用停用词表；第二个嵌入模型用于主题间相似度、层次细化与可视化；最后由LLM依据排序关键词和代表性专利为每个主题命名。

Result: 在Lens.org检索的30,964件公共部门相关专利上，五个类别语料（591–29,215件）共生成74个类别专属主题实例，宏平均文档覆盖率98.1%，主题多样性0.900–1.000，UNKNOWN分类输出低于1%。

Conclusion: 该演示证明了流水线的可行性与可追溯性，所有模型投票与主题标注证据均被保留以供审计修正；下一步需与专家编码对照验证标签，并与替代流水线进行基准比较。

Abstract: Patent analytics increasingly relies on transformer representations and large language models (LLMs), yet linking conceptual classification, topic formation and interpretation requires explicit decisions about model roles and the evidence retained at each stage. In this design-oriented study, we propose and demonstrate a patent analytics pipeline that links these tasks and keeps each decision open to audit. Three LLMs separately classify each patent by novelty (radical or incremental) and innovation type (product, process, service). Radical and innovation-type labels each require two supporting votes, with Incremental used as the fallback novelty label. Topics are discovered by clustering PatentSBERTa embeddings, with parameter ranges scaled to corpus size. A patent-specific stopword list is applied only when topics are represented with class-based term frequency--inverse document frequency (c-TF-IDF), and a second embedding model supports inter-topic similarity, hierarchical refinement and visualization. An LLM then labels each topic from its ranked keywords and representative patents. We demonstrate the pipeline on 30,964 public-sector-related patents retrieved from Lens.org. Across five category corpora ranging from 591 to 29,215 patents, the pipeline produced 74 category-specific topic instances with a macro-average document coverage of 98.1% and topic diversity of 0.900--1.000, and fewer than 1% of classification outputs returned UNKNOWN. Because every model vote and the evidence behind each topic label are retained, analysts can audit and revise the outputs. The demonstration establishes the feasibility and traceability of the pipeline; testing its labels against expert coding and benchmarking it against alternative pipelines are identified as the next steps. The pipeline offers a reusable basis for LLM-assisted patent analysis in technology intelligence.

</details>


### [163] [Evaluation of a Multi-Link Machine Learning Approach for Rainfall Detection Using Satellite Microwave Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09156)
*Mostafa Ftouni,Margherita Spalla,Matteo Colli,Andrea Randazzo,Christian Gianoglio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究利用卫星微波链路（SML）机会性传感器网络结合随机森林（RF）与卷积神经网络（CNN）进行降雨检测，证明融合多条邻近链路信息可显著提升干湿期判别能力，且RF在稳定性与可靠性权衡上优于CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化影响加剧，对可靠的实时降雨监测系统需求上升；单链路SML降雨检测精度有限，需探索多链路信息融合与机器学习方法以提升检测性能。

Method: 构建基于卫星微波链路的机会性传感器网络，聚合SML数据并纳入邻近链路信息，分别训练随机森林（RF）与卷积神经网络（CNN）分类器，用于降雨（湿期/干期）检测，并与单链路方法对比。

Result: 利用多链路信息相比单链路方法显著提升了湿期与干期检测效果；RF相较CNN提供更稳定可靠的性能权衡，整体提升降雨检测并减少误分类，在恶劣天气条件下表现尤为突出；研究覆盖2021—2024年间最多32个降雨日。

Conclusion: 多链路SML数据结合机器学习分类器是有效的实时降雨监测方案，其中RF因稳定性更优而更具实用价值，尤其适用于恶劣天气条件下的降雨检测。

Abstract: The impacts of climate change have increased the need for reliable real-time rainfall monitoring systems. This study investigates rainfall detection using a network of opportunistic sensors based on satellite microwave links (SMLs) combined with machine learning (ML) classifiers. Two models, a random forest (RF) and a convolutional neural network (CNN), were trained on aggregated SML data, including information from neighboring links. The results demonstrate that leveraging information from multiple links substantially enhances the detection of both wet and dry periods compared to single-link approaches. Moreover, the RF model consistently provides a more stable and reliable trade-off than CNN, improving rainfall detection and reducing misclassifications overall, with particularly strong performance during harsh weather conditions. The study spanned up to 32 rainy days between 2021 and 2024.

</details>


### [164] [CARGO: Causal Attribution and Responsibility-Guided Preventive Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09139)
*Jingjia Teng,Yang Li,Wenjie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARGO框架，将因果归因与责任引导优化引入混合交通协同匝道汇入的多智能体强化学习，在高密度交通下成功率提升66.04%、碰撞率降低56.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通下的协同匝道汇入需要处理智能体间的行为影响与安全责任分配，现有MARL方法缺乏对定向因果影响和风险责任的显式建模，导致协作奖励设计与预防性安全约束不足。

Method: 采用共享潜上下文后验与动作条件响应模型估计定向行为影响，并将后验不确定性传播到交互边选择；用带符号影响与边际团队效用的互补归因机制塑造合作奖励，并依据相对安全参考策略的正向超额尾部风险分配责任；用条件扩散生成未来响应以估计动作相关风险；在策略信赖域内以预留安全边界、自适应需求缩放和联合可行性校验实现责任引导的预防性优化。

Result: 理论分析在明确假设下给出风险单调性及环境回报非递减的充分局部条件；highway-env上三种交通密度的实验及受控图恢复与归因研究支持框架有效性；高密度交通下相对最佳基线成功率提升66.04%、碰撞率降低56.23%。

Conclusion: CARGO通过因果影响估计与责任引导的风险优化，在混合交通协同汇入中兼顾协作效率与安全，验证了归因驱动的MARL安全优化路径的有效性。

Abstract: This study presents Causal Attribution and Responsibility-Guided Optimization (CARGO), a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for cooperative on-ramp merging in mixed traffic. A shared latent-context posterior and an action-conditioned response model estimate directed behavioral influence, with posterior uncertainty propagated into interaction-edge selection. Complementary attribution mechanisms couple signed influence with marginal team utility for cooperative reward shaping and assign responsibility from positive excess tail risk relative to a safe reference policy. Conditional diffusion generates future responses for action-dependent risk estimation. Responsibility-guided preventive optimization calibrates agent-specific risk-reduction requirements to local capacity within a policy trust region, using reserved safety margins, adaptive requirement scaling, and joint feasibility verification. Theoretical analysis establishes risk monotonicity and sufficient local conditions for non-decreasing environmental return under explicit assumptions. Experiments in highway-env across three traffic densities, together with controlled graph-recovery and attribution studies, support the effectiveness of the framework. Under high-density traffic, CARGO increases the success rate by 66.04\% and reduces the crash rate by 56.23\% relative to the best evaluated baseline for each metric.

</details>


### [165] [Ensemble Methodology for Reference-Free Confidence Estimation in LLM-based Code Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09846)
*Marco Colombo,Balaji A Jayaram,Sowmyakumari Selvaraj,Sumit Tokkar,Animesh Kumar,Aravinda Prasad Sistla,Ravishankar Shetty,Rigel Gjomemo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无人工参考的LLM生成COBOL代码摘要正确性估计方法：以跨模型语义一致性作为真实性信号，用LLM-as-judge做成对比较，并通过校准聚合规则融合支持性证据、仅惩罚已被证实的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL摘要缺少人工撰写的参考摘要，传统依赖参考文本的字符串/嵌入相似度指标难以适用；而单一指标对语义正确性的判别保真度不足。

Method: 三步流程：(i) 由多个多样化模型受控生成摘要；(ii) LLM评估器对摘要两两比较并输出结构化判定；(iii) 校准的聚合规则汇总支持性证据，同时仅对相互印证的矛盾予以惩罚。

Result: 摘要未给出量化实验数据，仅以用例形式说明该方法的实施方式与评估流程可行性。

Conclusion: 跨模型语义一致性可作为无参考场景下正确性的有效代理信号，LLM-as-judge的成对判断配合校准聚合，优于传统字符串与嵌入指标。

Abstract: This paper describes an approach and use case to estimate the correctness of LLM-generated COBOL summaries without access to human-authored references. The core intuition is that inter-model agreement on semantics is informative about truth, and that an LLM-as-judge can provide higher-fidelity pairwise assessments than traditional string- or embedding-based metrics. We operationalise this intuition with (i) controlled summary generation from diverse models; (ii) a LLM evaluator that compares pairs of summaries and emits structured judgments; and (iii) a calibrated aggregation rule that combines supportive evidence while penalising only corroborated contradictions.

</details>


### [166] [Model-based learning for DoA estimation and array calibration via continuous sparse recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09263)
*Baptiste Chatelier,José Miguel Mateos-Ramos,Vincent Corlay,Matthieu Crussière,Christian Häger,Henk Wymeersch,Nir Shlezinger,Luc Le Magoarou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于SOMP的联合阵列校准与DoA估计方法（模型驱动机器学习范式），用PGD校准存在增益-相位及阵元位置误差的阵列，并将SOMP扩展到连续字典以精化DoA，仿真表明可在无标签样本下高效补偿硬件损伤并优于传统稀疏恢复与子空间方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC场景下DoA估计重获关注，但现有高精度估计器普遍忽略硬件损伤（增益-相位误差、阵元位置偏差），这些损伤同时劣化DoA精度与下游通信性能。

Method: 以同时正交匹配追踪（SOMP）为核心，采用模型驱动机器学习范式：利用投影梯度下降（PGD）实现阵列校准，支持监督与无监督两种目标；并通过PGD精化步骤将SOMP从离散网格扩展至连续字典以提升DoA估计精度。

Result: 仿真结果显示，该方法即使在无标签样本条件下也能有效补偿硬件损伤，且性能优于传统稀疏恢复类与子空间类DoA估计基线。

Conclusion: 联合阵列校准与DoA估计的SOMP-PGD框架为硬件受损的ISAC系统提供了可行且高效的解决方案，并可推广至无监督（免标注）场景。

Abstract: Direction of arrival (DoA) estimation, a classical signal processing problem, has experienced renewed interest with the advent of integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). While decades of research have yielded highly accurate DoA estimators, hardware impairments have frequently been neglected, despite degrading both DoA estimation accuracy and downstream communication performance in ISAC systems. This work introduces a joint array calibration and DoA estimation method based on the simultaneous orthogonal matching pursuit (SOMP) algorithm, following the model-based machine learning paradigm. The proposed method enables the calibration of antenna arrays subject to severe gain-phase and antenna location impairments through projected gradient descent (PGD), with either supervised or unsupervised objectives. Additionally, DoA estimation is enhanced by extending SOMP to continuous dictionaries via PGD refinement steps. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method enables efficient hardware impairment compensation, even without labeled samples, while outperforming conventional sparse recovery and subspace-based DoA estimation baselines.

</details>


### [167] [Pdb or Not to Be: Comparing Debugging Interfaces With Pass$_{X}$@$k$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08666)
*Ryan Magnuson,Ellie Kitanidis,Ariel Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在固定脚手架的条件下，对比"仅用交互式调试器"与"仅用bash"的编码智能体，并在500个SWE-bench Verified实例上评测两种模型；提出 pass_X@k 跨智能体指标以剥离随机方差，发现二者解题子集的部分不重叠主要源于运行间方差，而非工具互补性，两次bash rollout优于bash+debugger组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式调试器可实时访问运行时状态，理论上可能带来与bash+打印语句不同的求解能力；但已有工作通常把调试器作为附加工具或新子智能体引入，脚手架同时改变，导致其单独贡献难以隔离。同时，智能体评测存在显著的运行间随机方差，易将方差误判为工具互补性。

Method: 控制变量实验：其余脚手架完全固定，仅替换调试接口，构建debugger-only与bash-only两类智能体；在全部500个SWE-bench Verified实例上评测，使用GPT-OSS-120B与Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B两个模型，每个"架构×模型"配置独立运行3次。提出pass_X@k（跨智能体pass@k），作为"k个智能体中至少一个成功"概率的无偏估计量，用于区分接口专门化与重复采样下的覆盖率扩张。

Result: 单次运行配对下，bash智能体与debugger智能体解出的实例集合部分不相交；但pass_X@2估计显示这一不重叠主要由运行间方差解释，而非工具互补性。在两个模型上，两次bash rollout的组合均优于一次bash加一次debugger rollout。

Conclusion: 调试器接口相对bash并未带来可测的互补收益；评估智能体与工具多样性时必须控制随机方差，避免把采样噪声误读为互补性。pass_X@k可作为社区评估工具/智能体多样性的通用框架。

Abstract: Interactive debuggers are indispensable to human software development, yet coding agents overwhelmingly rely on edit-execute-observe loops, using common bash tools, test scripts, and print statements to debug via repeated shell commands. Stepping through a debugger provides live access to runtime state, which may enable different problem-solving capabilities. However, prior work typically introduces the debugger as an additional tool or through a new subagent, making its individual contribution difficult to isolate from the associated scaffold changes. To address this, we compare a debugger-only agent against a bash-only agent on all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, holding the rest of the scaffold fixed. We evaluate both architectures on two models, GPT-OSS-120B and Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B, with three independent runs per architecture $\times$ model configuration. Crucially, to distinguish interface specialization from expanded coverage under repeated sampling, we introduce pass$_X$@$k$, a cross-agent pass@$k$ that provides an unbiased estimator of the probability that at least one of $k$ agents is successful. Although the bash and debugger agents solve partially disjoint subsets of the benchmark in any single pair of runs, our pass$_X$@$2$ estimator reveals that this is largely due to run-to-run variance rather than tool complementarity. Across both models, we find that two bash rollouts outperform the combination of one bash and one debugger rollout. Our findings highlight the importance of accounting for stochastic variance in evaluating agent and tool diversity, and we hope that pass$_X$@$k$ will serve as a useful framework for the community.

</details>


### [168] [KineCouple: Substrate-conditioned residual readout for enzyme kinetic prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09377)
*Chun Kang,Yunbo Zhang,Fanhu Zeng,Jian Liu,Siwei Deng,Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KineCouple模型，用酶-底物交叉注意力实现底物条件下的残基聚合，以预测kcat与Km。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有酶动力学参数预测方法先将残基级蛋白特征池化为底物无关的表示，无法在聚合时按底物调整残基权重，可能稀释与特定酶-底物对相关的信息；而残基对催化的贡献本身依赖底物。

Method: 以底物为query、残基级特征为key/value进行交叉注意力，使底物在聚合前引导残基选择；保留全局蛋白表示并叠加门控修正项（条件读出与均匀读出的差值）；ProtT5与MolT5冻结，kcat与Km分别建模并在对数尺度预测。

Result: 在CataPro基准的pooled out-of-fold预测上，相对复现的CataPro：kcat RMSE 1.333→1.330、PCC 0.492→0.496；Km RMSE 0.998→0.997、PCC 0.633→0.634；两任务的Spearman相关亦提升。

Conclusion: 底物条件的残基聚合思路可行但增益极小（RMSE与PCC改善约0.003量级），与基线实质相当，提示该机制在当前设定下收益有限。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of enzyme kinetic parameters can support enzyme engineering and metabolic modeling by complementing experimental characterization. Existing prediction methods often pool residue-level protein features before combining them with substrate features. Such substrate-independent pooling cannot adapt residue weighting to the substrate during aggregation, potentially diluting information relevant to a specific enzyme-substrate pair. Since the contributions of individual residues to catalysis can depend on the substrate, substrate-conditioned aggregation offers a way to emphasize residue features relevant to the prediction. We introduce KineCouple, which uses substrate queries and residue-level keys and values in cross-attention, allowing the substrate to guide residue selection before aggregation. The model preserves the global protein representation and adds a gated correction defined as the difference between the conditioned readout and a uniform readout. ProtT5 and MolT5 remain frozen, and separate models predict kcat and Km on a logarithmic scale. In pooled out-of-fold predictions on the CataPro benchmarks, KineCouple reduces kcat RMSE from 1.333 to 1.330 and increases PCC from 0.492 to 0.496 relative to reproduced CataPro. For Km, RMSE decreases from 0.998 to 0.997 and PCC increases from 0.633 to 0.634. Spearman correlation also improves for both tasks.

</details>


### [169] [Challenges, Opportunities, and Misconceptions in Neurosymbolic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09356)
*Giuseppe Marra,Pierre Genevès,Meghyn Bienvenu,Thomas Schiex,Sylvie Thiébaux,Silviu Maniu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨在神经模型日益强大的背景下，神经符号AI在效率、安全性与可解释性方面的机会，指出语义对齐与符号推理计算开销两大挑战，并澄清关于可微性、独立性假设与可扩展性的常见误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯神经模型虽性能强劲，但在效率、安全性与可解释性上仍有不足，作者希望厘清神经符号AI在这些方面的价值定位，并纠正领域内的认知偏差。

Method: 属立场/综述性论文：基于2026年CNRS研讨会神经符号编程与推理（NPR）工作组的讨论，归纳代表性方法路径，辨析常见误解；非系统性穷尽综述。

Result: 识别出两大核心挑战——神经与符号组件间的语义对齐、符号推理与学习的计算成本，并给出对应的代表性方法思路以及对三类误解的澄清。

Conclusion: 神经符号AI相对于强神经模型的价值需围绕效率、安全与可解释性重新审视，未来研究应聚焦语义对齐机制与符号推理的成本控制，并避免以可微性、独立性假设和可扩展性为由的片面否定。

Abstract: We examine opportunities for neurosymbolic AI alongside increasingly powerful neural models, particularly with regards to efficiency, safety and interpretability. We identify two major challenges: ensuring semantic alignment between neural and symbolic components, and managing the computational cost of symbolic inference and learning. We discuss representative methodological approaches to these challenges and address several common misconceptions concerning differentiability, independence assumptions, and scalability. This paper synthesizes perspectives developed through discussions in the Neurosymbolic Programming and Reasoning (NPR) working group at the 2026 CNRS workshop Neurosymbolic Methods and Their Applications in Data Science, rather than providing an exhaustive survey.

</details>


### [170] [Analyzing Code Provenance in LLMs for Programming Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09177)
*Subhankar Maity,Faiza Belbachir,Omar Boucelma,Guilherme Medeiros Machado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将 Min-K% 概率方法扩展为结合"合成非成员代码生成"的成员推断方法，用于 Python 代码的成员归属推断与代码溯源，并在 Stack Overflow 数据及高校学生作业上评测了 GPT-2、DistilGPT-2、DialoGPT 与 GPT-5、Gemini 2.5、Mistral、DeepSeek、LLaMA 3.3 等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在编程教育中日益普及，可即时生成完整可运行代码，动摇了以学生提交代码作为学习投入与能力证据的评估可靠性。因此需要代码溯源（code provenance），以区分学生原创、AI 生成与外部来源的代码成分。

Method: 在 Min-K% 概率方法基础上引入合成非成员代码生成，将其用于 Python 代码的成员推断（membership inference）与溯源追踪；先在 Stack Overflow 数据上验证可行性，再应用于高校学生作业；系统对比免费模型（GPT-2、DistilGPT-2、DialoGPT）与商业前沿模型（GPT-5、Gemini 2.5、Mistral、DeepSeek、LLaMA 3.3）。

Result: 免费/开源模型在代码记忆检测上的敏感度高于商业模型；通过对上述模型的系统比较，可刻画检测准确性与隐私保护之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 结合溯源检测与过程可见性（process visibility），为 AI 辅助编程教育的有效评估迈出第一步。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) play an increasing role in programming education, challenging traditional assessment methods as they can generate fully functional code instantly. This disrupts the reliability of student submissions as indicators of learning and effort. To address this issue, code provenance is essential for tracing the origins of code and distinguishing student-authored, AI-generated, and externally sourced components. We extend the Min-K% probability approach by incorporating synthetic non-member code generation and apply it to Python code for membership inference and provenance tracking. Evaluations cover free LLMs such as GPT-2, DistilGPT-2, and DialoGPT, as well as state-of-the-art commercial models including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5, Mistral, DeepSeek, and LLaMA 3.3. Before evaluating our approach on higher-education student assignments, we validate its feasibility using Stack Overflow data. The results show that free models enable more sensitive code memorization detection than commercial models. By systematically comparing these LLMs, we assess trade-offs between detection accuracy and privacy protection, taking an initial step toward effective assessment of AI-assisted programming education through combined provenance detection and process visibility.

</details>


### [171] [Decision Context: Persistent Behavioral Learning for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08644)
*Nima Vahdat,Pashootan Vaezipoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 forge_dc 系统，将人类纠正与工具调用失败转化为命令式 RULE 指令，并在对应决策点注入，在新建的 DC-Bench（71 场景、9 个行为维度）上实现 95% 合规率，较朴素检索的 55% 显著提升，且每查询注入 token 减少约 10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 无法跨会话从纠正中学习，用户每轮都需重复纠正同一错误；现有 agent 记忆系统主要处理偏好、实体、对话历史等事实性知识，缺乏对程序性（行为性）知识的支持。

Method: forge_dc 从人类纠正与自评估的工具调用失败中捕获行为信号，将其编译为命令式 RULE 指令，并在其适用的决策点处注入。与四个前代方法在 DC-Bench 上对比评估；DC-Bench 覆盖九个行为维度、71 个场景，基于确定性 mock 工具环境中的真实 agent 执行轨迹构建。

Result: forge_dc 达到 95% 合规率，朴素检索仅为 55%；同时每查询注入的 token 量约为后者的十分之一。五阶段递进实验中，表现最强的系统均以显式指令形式表示行为记忆，并在适用决策点交付。

Conclusion: 表示形式（representation）、激活（activation）与放置位置（placement）是持久化行为学习的三个核心设计选择。行为记忆应表示为显式指令，而非检索式事实片段。

Abstract: LLM agents do not learn from corrections across sessions. A user fixes the same mistake at the start of every conversation. Existing agent memory systems primarily handle facts, such as preferences, entities, and conversational history, rather than procedural knowledge.
  We present Forge Decision Context (forge_dc), a system that captures behavioral signals from human corrections and self-assessed tool-call failures, compiles them into imperative RULE directives, and injects them at the decision points where they apply. We evaluate forge_dc alongside four predecessor approaches on DC-Bench, a new benchmark of 71 scenarios across nine behavioral dimensions, built from real executed agent traces in a deterministic mocked-tool environment. forge_dc reaches 95% compliance, up from 55% with naive retrieval, while injecting approximately 10 times fewer tokens per query.
  Across the five-stage progression, the strongest systems represent behavioral memory as explicit directives and deliver it at the decision points where it applies. The results identify representation, activation, and placement as central design choices for persistent behavioral learning.

</details>


### [172] [Enhancing Academic Advisement with Case-Based Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08572)
*Tanat Tonguthaisri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将案例推理（CBR）应用于学业指导领域，设计、开发并验证了一个基于案例的学业指导推荐系统原型，用于辅助乃至最终替代传统人工学业指导流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学业指导被视为高等教育的重要职能，全球多所院校普遍希望改进其指导服务质量；过去十年CBR专家系统在众多人类问题求解领域成效显著，但尚未成功应用于学业指导这一看似非常契合的领域，且学业指导本身耗时且需要有效工具与技术支持。

Method: 采用案例推理（CBR）方法论，进行基于案例的学业指导推荐系统原型的设计、开发与验证，定位为对传统指导流程的补充。

Result: 构建并验证了一个基于案例的学业指导推荐系统原型，可补充传统学业指导过程。

Conclusion: CBR适用于学业指导领域；该原型系统可辅助传统指导流程，并在其潜力充分实现后有望演进为替代人工学业指导者。

Abstract: This research involved the study of an application of Case-Based Reasoning (CBR) in the field of academic advising. Academic advisement has been recognised as an important function of higher education. However, there is a widespread desire to improve the advisement services provided by many educational institutions (e.g., colleges and universities) around the world. In the last ten years, CBR expert systems have made important contributions in many domains by applying CBR methodology to a variety of areas of human problem solving. However, to date, expert advisement systems have not been successfully applied to academic advisement, which seems to be a very appropriate domain for this technology.
  Academic advising is an important and time consuming task, and different tools and techniques must be used to make it an effective and efficient process. This thesis describes the design, development, and validation of a prototype case-based academic advisement recommender system that supplements the conventional advising process, and could eventually evolve to replace human academic advisors when its full potential is realised.

</details>


### [173] [Toward LLM Self-Play Under Data Scarcity: Effectiveness, Generalizability, and Trustworthiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08908)
*Zhiyin Yu,Yuchen Mou,Bingzhe Wu,Zhonghai Wu,Shiyang Feng,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以数据为中心、可泛化、可信赖为三大支柱的LLM自我博弈（self-play）研究议程，用于在数据稀缺条件下实现自主数据生成与自我进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM发展依赖模型规模扩张与人工标注数据，但面临数据稀缺与人工监督收益递减的双重瓶颈；现有self-play框架缺乏数据中心视角、跨域泛化能力有限，且可信与安全问题日益突出。

Method: 立场性/综述型论文，采用观点论证与蓝图式路线图：先界定数据稀缺下self-play系统应满足的有效性、泛化性与可信性三目标，再据此提出三条研究方向并讨论各方向内的研究机会与解决思路。

Result: 提出三个蓝海方向：(1) data-centric self-play，实现有效经验生成；(2) generalizable self-play，面向开放世界与不可验证任务；(3) trustworthy self-play，保障可靠的自我演化。并给出每个方向的具体研究机会与潜在方案。

Conclusion: 该范式转变旨在将有限的人类知识通过self-play架构转化为近乎无限的高质量经验，从而构建可持续且安全的AI生态系统。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has been driven largely by scaling model size and human-curated training data, yet this paradigm is increasingly constrained by data scarcity and diminishing returns from human supervision. Self-play offers a promising pathway for autonomous data generation and improvement, but current frameworks remain unsatisfactory due to a lack of a data-centric perspective, limited cross-domain generalization, and growing trustworthiness and security concerns. In this paper, we argue that real-world LLM self-play systems should achieve effectiveness, generalizability, and trustworthiness under data scarcity. To address these challenges, we outline three blue-sky research directions for future self-play systems: (1) data-centric self-play for effective experience generation, (2) generalizable self-play for open-world and unverifiable tasks, and (3) trustworthy self-play for reliable self-evolution. We further discuss research opportunities and solutions within each direction. This paradigm shift will establish a sustainable and safe AI ecosystem by transforming finite human knowledge into inexhaustible high-quality experience through self-play architectures.

</details>


### [174] [Learning Whom to Trust About the Unseen: Source Credibility and Reliability Transfer in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08855)
*Ruijia Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三项行为实验（六个语言模型）考察"信源可靠性记录"在模型中的迁移范围：可靠性会随领域（自然 vs. 灵性）而受限，且信源对自身可靠来源的解释（测量 vs. 灵性指引）会进一步收窄其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究表明模型会给予"曾正确"的信源更高权重，但尚不清楚这种可靠性声誉能否跨到无法核验的新主张上，以及是否取决于主张的类型（自然因果 vs. 灵性因果）。

Method: 三项行为研究，跨六个模型。研究1改编"爱因斯坦效应"范式，比较同一晦涩陈述在灵性领袖与科学家署名下、以及改用物理学词汇重述后的偏好；研究2改编"可信专家断言未知/谜团"范式，检验科学解释与宗教解释的领域不对称；研究3为核心测试：让两个虚构信源在可核验的河流预报记录上表现不同，再让其背书关于同一村庄事件的、匹配的自然因与灵性因主张，并操控信源对自身可靠性的解释（测量 vs. 灵性指引）。

Result: 模型在爱因斯坦效应范式中偏好灵性领袖（与人类样本相反），改用物理词汇后偏好大体反转；研究2中模型以夸张形式复现人类的领域不对称。研究3显示多数模型将可靠性记录迁移到自然因主张、几乎不迁移到灵性因主张，仅一个模型对两者均有弱迁移；将可靠信源描述为凭灵性指引而非测量作出预测后，所有模型对其自然因主张的影响力下降，而对灵性因主张无相应提升。

Conclusion: 在这些模型中，信源可靠性具有明确的"作用域"，且信源对可靠性成因的解释会使其影响范围进一步收窄。该效应究竟源于对相关性的判断，还是源于灵性主张的低先验可信度，本研究设计尚无法区分，作者提出了可区分二者的后续实验构想。

Abstract: A source who has been right before deserves attention, but about what? We ask how far a demonstrated record of reliability travels in language models when the new claim cannot be checked, and whether the answer depends on the kind of claim. Three behavioral studies across six models approach the question from three angles. Study 1 adapts the Einstein-effect paradigm and finds that models, unlike the human sample, favor a spiritual leader over a scientist for the same obscure statement, and that the preference largely reverses when the statement is reworded in the vocabulary of physics. Study 2 adapts a paradigm in which a trusted expert calls a scientific or religious explanation unknown or a mystery; models reproduce the human domain asymmetry in exaggerated form. Study 3, the central test, gives two fictional informants different records on checkable river forecasts and then has them endorse matched claims about hidden natural and spiritual causes of the same village events. Most models carry the record to natural causes and barely at all to spiritual ones; one model transfers it weakly to either. Telling the model that the reliable informant forecasts by spiritual guidance rather than by measurement lowers that informant's influence on natural claims in every model and produces no comparable gain on spiritual claims in any. Demonstrated reliability thus has a scope in these models, and the account a source gives of how they came to be reliable can narrow it. Whether this reflects a judgment about relevance or the low prior credence of spiritual claims is the question the design leaves open, and we outline the experiment that would settle it.

</details>


### [175] [I Think, Therefore I Attack: Evaluating Thought Injection Attacks on Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08811)
*Parth Illendula,Garik Livingston,Zain Shamsi,Ravi Srinivasan,Alex Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究针对大型推理模型（LRM）的"思维注入"攻击：将伪装成模型自身推理过程的不可信文本回灌给模型。作者先考察静态思维注入在不同风格、有无思考标签下的效果，发现其高度依赖具体目标模型；随后在容器化智能体靶场中利用该模型特异性，用模仿目标模型推理痕迹风格的间接注入实现最高50%的攻击成功率（如反弹shell外传、仓库植入后门），而标准用户口吻的提示注入从不成功，且思考标签会抑制智能体披露注入行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM在输出前加入显式思维链（chain-of-thought）阶段，这引入了新的攻击面：攻击者可将类似模型自身"深思"的不可信文本回灌，从而在推理层面而非用户指令层面操控模型。现有研究多关注传统提示注入，缺乏对"思维注入"效果的风格依赖性及在真实智能体部署中的可利用性的系统评估。

Method: 两部分研究：(1) 对主流生产级开源权重模型（如GLM-5.3、MiniMax-M2.5、Qwen3.8-Flash-Next）测试不同风格、带/不带思考标签的静态思维注入提示，比较其效果差异；(2) 在容器化智能体网络靶场中，以目标模型自身推理痕迹风格构造间接注入提示，测试对反弹shell外传、仓库后门植入等操作目标的攻击成功率，并与标准用户口吻的提示注入作对照，同时评估思考标签对披露行为的抑制及标签剥离作为防御的有效性。

Result: 思考标签与载荷风格的效果均取决于具体目标模型（模型特异性）；在智能体靶场中，模仿目标模型自身推理风格的间接注入可达最高50%的ASR，成功实现反弹shell外传与仓库后门植入等操作目标；相同恶意目标以标准用户口吻注入从未成功；思考标签会抑制智能体披露注入行为；标签剥离并非可靠防御。

Conclusion: 模仿推理模型自身的思维风格是此类攻击的有效手段，且攻击效果强依赖于对目标模型的适配；简单的标签剥离无法构成可靠防御，需针对思维层面的注入设计更强的防御机制。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are one of the key components of current agentic systems. LRMs augment their output with an explicit chain-of-thought reasoning stage, introducing a new attack surface for thought injection, in which untrusted text resembling the model's own deliberation is fed back to the model. We present a two-part study of this attack, performed against the prevailing large, production class open-weight models (e.g., GLM-5.3, MiniMax-M2.5, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next). We first evaluate the effectiveness of static thought-injection prompts under different styles as well as tagged and untagged conditions and find that the effects of thinking tags and payload styles are all properties of the specific target model. In the second study, we exploit this model-specificity in a containerized agentic cyber range, using indirect injection prompts styled in the form of the target model's own reasoning traces to achieve attack success rates (ASR) up to 50% on operational objectives such as reverse-shell exfiltration and repository backdooring. We also find that the same malicious objective in standard prompt injection user voice never succeeds, and thinking tags suppress the agent's tendency to disclose the injection. Our results show that imitating a reasoning model's own thought style is effective for this style of attack, and that tag stripping is an unreliable defense.

</details>


### [176] [Alignment by Confident Debate: Preference Learning from Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09568)
*Nikhil Verma,Dingxian Wang,Mohammad Reza Rezaei,Andrew Rabinovich,Zhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Alignment by Confident Debate (AbCD)，将多智能体辩论轨迹在无金标准标签条件下转化为偏好数据用于后训练，在五个模型家族（1B–8B）、四个数据集上超过推理期集成/辩论与同轨迹 SFT，共识准确率最高提升 21.5pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理后训练依赖金答案、任务特定验证器或学习型奖励模型，信号稀缺时难以扩展；多智能体辩论虽为自监督替代方案，但存在三类问题：多数投票等权对待一致回答且缺乏有原则的平票规则、对无规范答案的开放式任务定义不清；基于似然的打分易混淆流畅性与正确性；同质委员会会放大相关错误。

Method: AbCD 在不使用金标签的前提下把辩论轨迹转为偏好数据：由采样多样性构成的委员会对每题展开辩论，随后按任务层语义等价对最终答案聚类；以可验证任务（数学推理、多项选择定量 QA、可执行代码生成）作为受控试验台，其答案键/可执行检查器仅用于评估监督质量，oracle 本身不接触金标签；采用置信度加权的不一致性构建偏好对。

Result: 在 5 个模型家族（1B–8B）、4 个数据集上，基于所得偏好的后训练优于仅推理的集成与辩论，也优于同轨迹 SFT，共识准确率最高提升 21.5pp；消融显示方法可迁移到更大模型与代码生成，同时揭示异质委员会与基于一致性的加权方式的局限。

Conclusion: 将答案等价性与置信度加权的不一致性相结合时，辩论轨迹可提供有效的偏好监督，为缺乏金标签场景下的推理后训练提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Post-training language models for reasoning typically rely on gold answers, task-specific verifiers, or learned reward models, limiting scalability when such signals are scarce. Multi-agent debate offers a self-supervised alternative, but converting debate into reliable training supervision remains challenging: majority voting weights agreeing responses equally, lacks a principled tie-breaking rule, and is ill-defined for open-ended tasks without a canonical answer; likelihood-based scoring can conflate fluency with correctness; and homogeneous committees may reinforce correlated errors.
