We introduce Alignment by Confident Debate (AbCD), which converts debate traces into preference data without gold answer labels. A sampling-diverse committee debates each problem, after which final answers are clustered by task-level semantic equivalence. We use verifiable tasks (mathematical reasoning, multiple-choice quantitative QA, and executable code-generation) as a controlled test bed: their answer keys or executable checkers let us evaluate the quality of debate-derived supervision against ground truth, even though the oracle itself never uses gold labels. Post-training on the resulting preferences outperforms inference-only ensembling and debate, as well as SFT on the same traces, across five model families spanning 1B to 8B parameters and four datasets, improving consensus accuracy, with a maximum gain of $21.5_{pp}$. Ablations show that the approach transfers to a larger model and to code generation, while also revealing limitations of heterogeneous committees and agreement-based weighting. Overall, the results suggest that debate traces can yield useful preference supervision when answer equivalence is combined with confidence-weighted disagreement.

</details>


### [177] [Test-Time Compute Scales Better with Parallel Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09798)
*Junlin Wang,Haozhe Chen,Ang Li,Rahul Chalamala,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“并行智能体团队”（parallel agent teams）：并行采样多个小型协作团队、保留最佳团队结果，在长程任务上比Best-of-N和多智能体大团队更有效地扩展测试时计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种测试时扩展方式各有瓶颈：Best-of-N并行独立智能体虽可让验证器打分择优，但性能很快饱和；单个大型协作团队虽能得更高分，却随规模增长停止提升。作者希望兼得两者优势。

Method: 方法为并行采样多个小型团队（每组内部协作），再从各团队结果中择优保留。实验覆盖12个长程任务（GPU内核、形式化证明、漏洞利用开发），使用27B模型、100个智能体预算；并将10人×10组并行团队与100个并行独立智能体、单个100人大团队对比，同时做等美元成本对比与统计分析。

Result: 10人并行团队在12个任务中10个优于并行独立智能体；在运行超大规模团队的4个任务上全部优于单支100人团队。在等美元成本下，同配置团队在一半任务上超过前沿模型。统计模型显示：独立智能体因大量样本均值偏低而失败，单一大团队因单样本无法获得方差增益而失败，多个小团队并行则在较高均值与方差增益间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 并行智能体团队是更有效的测试时计算扩展方式，兼顾高均值与方差带来的增益，优于Best-of-N与单一大团队范式。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves the performance of a fixed model by spending more computation at inference. There are two common ways to spend it on agents when a verifier can score every attempt. Best-of-N sampling runs many independent agents and keeps the best result but saturates quickly. One large team of collaborating agents can score higher but stops improving as it grows. Can we get the best of both? Our method, parallel agent teams, does so by sampling many small teams in parallel and keeping the best team's result. We test it on 12 long-horizon tasks from GPU kernels and formal proofs to exploit development. With a 27B model and a budget of 100 agents, parallel teams of ten beat parallel agents on 10 of 12 tasks and beat one team of 100 on all four tasks where we ran such large teams. At equal dollar cost the same parallel teams beat a frontier model on half the tasks. A simple statistical model shows that individual agents lose because their many samples have a low mean and that one large team loses because a single sample gets no gain from variance. Sampling many small teams in parallel balances a high mean with the gain from variance. Parallel agent teams thus offer a more effective way to scale test-time compute.

</details>


### [178] [FOSCAR: Accelerating Decode Attention over 2- and 4-Bit KV Caches through Affine Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09722)
*Donglin Zhuang,Pragaash Ponnusamy,Xiaoxia Wu,Shuaiwen Leon Song,Zhongzhu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FOSCAR 通过仿射折叠与位操作，将 KV cache 量化中逐元素的 scale/offset 运算转移或消去，实现 H100/B200 上高效的 2-bit/4-bit decode-attention 内核，显著提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 解码阶段需为每个生成 token 流式读取 KV cache，将其压到 2/4-bit 可降低内存流量，但现有 2-bit 融合内核实测仅为 H100/B200 峰值 HBM 吞吐的 31%/15%，原因是逐元素反量化（每个值先转码再乘 scale 加 offset）受限于稀缺的指令发射吞吐，成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出仿射折叠（affine folding）：不再对每个 KV 值施加 scale/offset，而是按量化方案（token-wise 或 channel-wise）将缩放与偏移转移到另一操作数或 decode-attention 矩阵乘积的输出上，在精确算术下保持反量化后的 attention 输出不变，并将 scale/offset 操作减少最多两个数量级；同时用位操作高效将 KV 操作数送入 Tensor Core（INT2 每元素仅需 1 条与 0.19 条 ALU 指令，分别对应 H100/B200）；并基于 wgmma 与 tcgen05 构建面向 Hopper 和 Blackwell 的 warp-specialized GPU 流水线。

Result: H100 上 INT2/INT4 内核相对 FlashInfer BF16 几何平均加速 3.36×/2.83×，相对 TensorRT-LLM FP8 为 2.03×/1.71×；B200 上相对 FlashInfer BF16 为 2.67×/2.30×，相对 TensorRT-LLM FP8 为 1.48×/1.28×，相对 TensorRT-LLM NVFP4 为 1.16×/1.00×。集成到 SGLang 后，长上下文服务负载下相较 2-bit 与 16-bit 基线峰值聚合吞吐最高提升 170% 与 341%。

Conclusion: 通过将缩放/偏移运算从逐元素反量化中解耦并配合位级操作与架构特化的流水线，低比特 KV cache 的内存带宽优势可真正转化为解码延迟收益；FOSCAR 已作为内核库开源（github.com/FutureMLS-Lab/FOSCAR）。

Abstract: Large-language-model decode attention streams the key-value (KV) cache for every generated token. Packing that cache into 2-bit or 4-bit quantized integers reduces memory traffic but does not automatically translate into latency saving. Existing 2-bit fused kernels reach under 31%/15% of peak HBM throughput on H100/B200. Converting each value and applying its scale and offset before feeding Tensor Cores is a major bottleneck because instruction-issue throughput is scarce on modern GPUs. Instead of applying scale and offset to each KV value, FOSCAR uses affine folding to apply them to the other operand or the output of decode-attention matrix products, depending on the quantization scheme (token-wise or channel-wise). This reduces scale-and-offset operations by up to two orders of magnitude while preserving the dequantized attention output in exact arithmetic. FOSCAR also uses bit manipulation to feed KV operands to Tensor Cores efficiently, requiring only 1 and 0.19 ALU instructions per INT2 element on H100 and B200, respectively. Finally, FOSCAR builds warp-specialized GPU pipelines for Hopper (H100) and Blackwell (B200) using wgmma and tcgen05. On H100, FOSCAR INT2 and INT4 kernels achieve geo-mean speedups of $3.36\times$ and $2.83\times$ over FlashInfer BF16, and $2.03\times$ and $1.71\times$ over TensorRT-LLM FP8. On B200, the corresponding INT2 and INT4 speedups are $2.67\times$ and $2.30\times$ over FlashInfer BF16, $1.48\times$ and $1.28\times$ over TensorRT-LLM FP8, and $1.16\times$ and $1.00\times$ over TensorRT-LLM NVFP4. We integrate FOSCAR into SGLang to benchmark serving efficiency. On long-context serving workloads, FOSCAR increases peak aggregate throughput by up to 170% and 341% over 2-bit and 16-bit baselines, respectively. We open-source FOSCAR as a kernel library at https://github.com/FutureMLS-Lab/FOSCAR.

</details>


### [179] [LLM-Based Feature Extraction for Multi-Criteria Inventory Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08626)
*Humza Akhtar,Rami Pinto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的特征提取方法，将客户评价等非结构化数据经向量嵌入与LLM生成评价准则转化为定量特征，并纳入基于Hadi-Vencheh模型的多准则库存分类（MCIC）框架，实验表明引入客户情感信号会产生可测的重分类效应，从而改进库存优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商供应链积累了大量客户反馈与运营数据，但在库存管理中利用不足。传统ABC分析仅依赖年度金额使用量，MCIC方法虽有扩展，却依赖结构化定量数据，无法利用现代供应链中的非结构化信息。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的特征提取方法：利用向量嵌入与LLM生成的评价准则，将客户评论等非结构化来源转化为定量特征；将增强后的特征集并入基于Hadi-Vencheh模型的MCIC框架。

Result: 实验结果显示，纳入客户情感信号后出现可测量的重分类效果，表明将非结构化数据整合进MCIC可提升库存优先级划分。

Conclusion: 将非结构化客户反馈通过LLM转化为定量特征并融入MCIC，是可行且有效的库存分类增强路径。

Abstract: E-commerce supply chains generate large volumes of customer feedback and operational data that remain underutilized in inventory management. Traditional inventory classification techniques such as ABC analysis rely primarily on annual dollar usage, while multi-criteria inventory classification (MCIC) methods extend this but still depend on structured quantitative data and do not utilize unstructured information available in modern supply chains. This paper proposes an LLM-based feature extraction approach that transforms unstructured sources such as customer reviews into quantitative features using vector embeddings and LLM-generated evaluation criteria. The enriched feature set is incorporated into an MCIC framework based on the Hadi-Vencheh model. Experimental results show measurable reclassification effects when customer sentiment signals are included, demonstrating that integrating unstructured data into MCIC can enhance inventory prioritization.

</details>


### [180] [CID: Measuring Cognitive Work Through Semantic State Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09542)
*Ron Fulbright*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义状态"与"认知做功"概念，并引入基于马氏距离的语篇信息差分（CID）指标，用以几何化量化语义空间的变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息论擅长刻画统计特性，却难以量化意义与语义转换；而大语言模型以高维语义空间中的向量表征信息，使得意义可以被几何化分析。

Method: 将信息在模型语义空间中的向量表征定义为语义状态，将语义状态之间的转换（语义空间中的移动）定义为认知做功；采用考虑协方差结构的马氏距离构造Contextual Information Differential（CID）指标，度量相对于嵌入流形结构的语义位移。

Result: CID提供了一种可操作的语义变化、认知转换与信息处理度量方法，将意义视为语义空间的几何属性。

Conclusion: 该框架以与实现无关的方式度量语义状态变化，为认知加工、学习、推理、知识生成、合成专业知识以及人机协作的分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Traditional information theory provides powerful tools for analyzing statistical properties of information but offers limited insight into meaning and semantic transformation. It has never been possible to quantify the meaning of an arbitrary piece of information. Large language models represent information as vectors embedded within high-dimensional semantic spaces, enabling meaning to be analyzed geometrically. This paper defines semantic state as the vector representation of information within a model's semantic space and defines cognitive work as the transformation between semantic states, movement through semantic space. To quantify this transformation, we introduce the Contextual Information Differential (CID), a covariance-aware metric based on Mahalanobis distance measuring semantic displacement relative to the structure of the embedding manifold. By treating meaning as a geometric property of semantic space, CID provides a practical method for measuring semantic change, cognitive transformation, and information processing. This framework establishes a foundation for analyzing cognitive processing, learning, reasoning, knowledge generation, synthetic expertise, and human/AI collaboration through implementation-independent measurement of semantic state change in both human and artificial systems.

</details>


### [181] [PreForceVision: Physics-Guided Optical Estimation of Bolt Preload from Bolt-Head Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09529)
*Nehal Afifi,Klaus Rappenecker,Florian Peinhardt,Gerhard Kachel,Alfred Isele,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PreForceVision：一种基于螺栓头端面预紧致变形、物理引导的非接触光学预紧力估计框架，通过参考—加载图像对的变形提取与不确定性回归实现粗粒度预紧力估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预紧力决定螺栓连接是摩擦传力还是螺栓直接承力，是关键功能量。现有测量（应变螺栓、力垫圈、超声、螺栓伸长）需附加硬件、耦合、标定或接头改造，难以扩展到大量标准紧固件，因此需要非接触光学方案。

Method: 将预紧力估计建模为"变形→载荷"的反问题：对螺栓头前端面（光学可达表面）在受控条件下采集参考态—加载态图像对；用DIC或光流提取预紧致表面变形；结合多模态验证（仿真、应变片实测、光学3D测量）与不确定性感知回归，实现粗预紧力估计。

Result: M16 8.8级螺栓初步结果：90 kN下仿真头面凹陷约12 μm；应变片测得同量级结果；光学3D测得中心凹陷约10–20 μm，三者一致。

Conclusion: 物理引导的非接触光学方法可在受控条件下实现螺栓预紧力的粗略估计，避免附加硬件与接头改造，具备面向大量标准紧固件扩展的潜力；当前为初步验证，精度与鲁棒性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Bolt preload is a decisive functional quantity in bolted joints because it determines whether external loads are transmitted primarily through frictional clamping or directly through the bolt. Existing preload measurement techniques, including strain-gauge bolts, force washers, ultrasonic methods, and bolt-length measurements, are effective in certain applications but require additional hardware, coupling, calibration, or joint modifications. This limits their scalability for large numbers of standard fasteners and motivates a non-contact optical approach. This paper proposes PreForceVision, a physics-guided optical framework for estimating bolt preload from preload-induced surface deformation of the bolt-head front face. The method treats preload estimation as an inverse deformation-to-force problem, where deformation of the optically accessible bolt-head surface is extracted from reference-loaded image pairs and related to the internal preload state. Preliminary results for an M16 strength class 8.8 bolt show an approximately \SI{12}{\micro\meter} simulated head-face depression at \SI{90}{\kilo\newton}, strain-gauge measurements of comparable magnitude, and an optical 3D measured central depression of approximately \SIrange{10}{20}{\micro\meter}. The contribution is a physics-guided optical methodology combining controlled image-pair acquisition, DIC- or optical-flow-based deformation extraction, multimodal validation, and uncertainty-aware regression for rough preload estimation under controlled conditions.

</details>


### [182] [A Physics-Informed Learning Framework for Linking Microscopic Surface Degradation to Bolted Joint Functional Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09514)
*Nehal Afifi,Gerhard Kachel,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个物理信息多模态框架，将微观与相机观测的腐蚀退化信息映射到腐蚀螺栓连接件的功能指标上，并通过PDE发现提取候选介观演化律并与独立功能模型耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环工程中回收产品退化历史异质，剩余功能难以评估；对螺栓连接件而言，腐蚀会改变表面形貌、接触、摩擦、预紧力保持及力学响应，需要能连接退化观测与功能指标的评估途径。

Method: 定义腐蚀失效模式；生成同步的检测记录与功能响应记录；预处理图像、粗糙度、暴露量及连接件描述符；通过相似性学习对齐显微与相机观测；构建校准的腐蚀严重度场；对该场施加PDE发现以获得候选有效介观律（非完整电化学机理）；将腐蚀演化模型与独立功能模型耦合，以摩擦系数为主目标，在夹具力数据可用时纳入预紧力损失。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；所述贡献为一条可复现的、物理约束的退化螺栓连接件行为评估路径，输出候选介观演化律与以摩擦系数（及可选预紧力损失）为目标的耦合功能预测。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可复现的多模态、物理约束框架，用于基于腐蚀退化观测评估螺栓连接件的剩余功能，并可推广为循环工程中退化件功能评估的通用范式。

Abstract: Returned products in circular engineering often have heterogeneous degradation histories, making remaining function difficult to assess. For bolted joints, corrosion alters surface morphology, contact, friction, preload retention, and mechanical response. This paper presents a physics-informed multimodal framework for linking microscopic and camera-based degradation observations to functional indicators of corroded bolted joints. The workflow defines the corrosion failure mode, generates synchronized inspection and functional-response records, preprocesses image, roughness, exposure, and joint descriptors, aligns microscopy and camera observations through similarity learning, and constructs a calibrated corrosion-severity field. Partial differential equation (PDE) discovery is applied to this field to identify candidate effective mesoscale laws rather than complete electrochemical mechanisms. The corrosion-evolution model is coupled to a separate functional model, with friction coefficient as the primary target and preload loss included when clamp-force data are available. The contribution is a reproducible pathway for physically constrained assessment of degraded bolted-joint behavior.

</details>


### [183] [CPU-ARC: Agentic Reasoning Caching for on-CPU Reasoning Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09891)
*Peter Belcak,Greg Heinrich,Saurav Muralidharan,Shizhe Diao,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"智能体推理缓存"(agentic reasoning caching)：将LLM推理轨迹转化为可CPU执行的ARC，从而把"解题"与"执行解法"解耦，对新问题实例只需高效执行已有解法而几乎无需LLM参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM每次遇到任务都"重新造轮子"，将任务求解与解法执行混为一谈，导致必须从零求解每个问题。作者认为任务一旦被推理透彻，其解法应用到新实例的过程本可高效完成，无需反复调用语言模型。

Method: 从LLM的推理轨迹中提取并构建鲁棒的、可在CPU上执行的智能体推理缓存（ARCs）。其核心是把推理过程固化为可复用的可执行结构，而非依赖模型在线推理。

Result: 在多项困难的数学与科学任务上，ARC性能与前沿LLM相当；具备完全可解释性、可持续训练、对输入扰动鲁棒，且调用成本比源模型低16–291倍。

Conclusion: 通过推理缓存将LLM的推理能力"结晶"为可执行程序，可在保持性能的同时大幅降低成本并提升可解释性与鲁棒性，为高效复用LLM推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models reinvent the wheel all the time. They conflate task-solving with the execution of the solutions, which forces them to solve every problem from scratch. We show that once a task has been reasoned through, the application of its solution to a new problem instance can be done very efficiently and with little involvement from language models. We introduce agentic reasoning caching, a method for constructing robust CPU-executable agentic reasoning caches (ARCs) from LLM reasoning traces. ARCs show on-par performance with frontier LLMs across a range of difficult mathematical and scientific tasks, are fully explainable, continually trainable, robust to input perturbations, and 16-291x cheaper to invoke than the source model.

</details>


### [184] [Continuous wavelet-based feature extraction for laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy classification of human plasma samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09746)
*Md Mahmudur Rahman,Atqiya Munawara Mahi,Nazim Ahmed Belabbaci,Mohammad Arif Ul Alam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将连续小波变换（CWT）用于LIBS光谱峰提取，并结合SVM对SARS-CoV-2阳性与阴性人血浆样本进行分类，报告准确率93.00%、灵敏度94.67%、特异度91.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂生物样本LIBS光谱的判别性特征提取困难，需评估CWT峰值提取在该类分类任务中的有效性。

Method: 181例血浆样本（53例阳性、128例阴性）以5 μL液滴沉积于硅基底并干燥；采用1064 nm纳秒Nd:YAG激光采集光谱，每样本100次单脉冲测量，经强度离群剔除后平均；以CWT峰值检测提取特征并输入SVM分类器，采用留一法评估。

Result: 留一法评估准确率93.00%、灵敏度94.67%、特异度91.61%（待与样本层级预测结果核对）；CWT流程识别的特征包括归属Ca II、K I和N I的发射谱线。

Conclusion: 该流程为考察所研究血浆组相关的光谱特征提供了基础，但结果的泛化性与诊断价值仍需独立验证，并评估样本、基底及采集相关变异的影响。

Abstract: Extracting discriminative features from laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy (LIBS) spectra is important for the classification of complex biological samples. This study evaluates continuous wavelet transform (CWT)-based spectral peak extraction for distinguishing human plasma samples labeled as SARS-CoV-2-positive or negative. Plasma specimens from 181 individuals, comprising 53 positive samples and 128 negative controls, were deposited as 5 μL droplets on silicon substrates and dried before analysis. Spectra were acquired using a nanosecond Nd:YAG laser operating at 1064 nm, with 100 single-shot measurements collected per specimen and averaged after intensity-based outlier rejection. CWT-based peak detection was coupled with a support vector machine (SVM) classifier. The reported leave-one-out evaluation yielded an accuracy of 93.00%, sensitivity of 94.67%, and specificity of 91.61%; these estimates remain provisional pending reconciliation with specimen-level predictions. Features identified by the CWT pipeline included emission lines assigned to Ca II, K I, and N I. The workflow provides a basis for examining spectral features associated with the studied plasma groups. Independent validation and assessment of specimen, substrate, and acquisition-related variability are required to establish generalizability and diagnostic relevance.

</details>


### [185] [What to Delegate, What to Preserve: Human Preferences for Agentic AI Across Everyday Life and Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09730)
*Sarah Wheeler,Veronica Rammouz,Yaoyao Qian,Zexuan Liu,Mengyi Wei,Anthony Rios,Tianzhi He,Kyrie Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一项针对289名有AI经验的美国成年人的情境调查研究（覆盖9大生活领域45个用例）表明，人们对AI代理的自动化偏好随领域显著变化，并受信任、期望的AI领导、先前任务相关的AI使用、预期经济节省与"自动化降低个人成就感"担忧等因素影响；开放题还显示人们倾向"有界自主"——大量执行交给AI但保留人类参与、监督或最终决定权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理已能在多步骤任务中代表用户执行操作，但"可接受的委托边界"尚不清晰；论文旨在刻画人们在何种程度上、以何种方式愿意把任务交给AI，并将"委托"从单一自动化程度概念细化为多维构念。

Method: 情境式问卷调查：289名有AI使用经验的美国成年人，覆盖9个生活领域、45个用例；每位受访者在每个领域评价一个情境，形成重复测量，测量自动化偏好、信任、协作、AI领导、隐私担忧与支付意愿；采用广义估计方程（GEE）模型分析预测因素，并结合开放题的质性分析。

Result: 自动化偏好在不同领域间差异显著；GEE结果显示，对AI代理信任更高、期望AI承担领导角色、先前有任务相关的AI使用经验、预期经济节省，均与更高的自动化偏好相关；而对"自动化会降低个人成就感"的担忧则预测更低的自动化偏好。开放题显示受访者偏好"有界自主"：常接受AI承担大量执行，但保留人类参与、监督或最终权威。

Conclusion: AI委托不应被理解为单一的自动化程度，而应被概念化为执行、协作、领导与权威四种要素的配置；这一框架有助于设计更符合用户期望的人机分工与监督机制。

Abstract: AI agents can execute multi-step tasks on users' behalf, yet the boundaries of acceptable delegation remain unclear. We report a scenario-based survey of 289 U.S. adults with prior AI experience covering 45 use cases across nine life domains. Each respondent evaluated one scenario per domain, producing repeated measures of automation preference, trust, collaboration, AI leadership, privacy concern, and willingness to pay. Automation preferences varied substantially across domains. Generalized estimating equation models showed that greater trust in the AI agent, desired AI leadership, prior task-specific AI use, and anticipated financial savings were associated with greater automation preference. In contrast, concerns that automation would reduce personal fulfillment predicted lower automation preference. Open-ended responses further revealed a preference for bounded autonomy, with participants often favoring substantial AI execution while retaining human involvement, oversight, or final authority. We argue that delegation is best understood as a configuration of execution, collaboration, leadership, and authority.

</details>


### [186] [Attend at the Right Resolution: Head-Adaptive Sparse Attention via Query-Key Interaction Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09700)
*Wajih Hassan Raza,Ahsan Bilal,Yidan Shen,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu,Yu Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的头部自适应稀疏注意力框架HASA，基于各预训练头query-key算子的奇异谱与响应方向一致性，在特征维和序列维联合自适应选择分辨率，实现高效且高质量稀疏注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力是LLM的扩展性瓶颈，计算与KV访存随上下文增长而急剧上升。稀疏注意力通过限制query的可见上下文缓解该问题，但效果取决于能否以低代价可靠地定位相关区域。现有免训练方法依赖运行时相关性估计，其选择维度与粒度由固定启发式决定，未利用预训练注意力头内在的交互结构。

Method: 核心洞察：每个预训练头的query-key算子定义了一种特征响应结构，决定稀疏选择所需的分辨率（特征方向与上下文token两维）。特征维：由各头query-key算子的奇异谱确定有效秩，得到紧凑的头专属响应空间；序列维：在该空间内依据token响应的方向一致性渐进合并相邻区域，异质区域保持分离；随后用所选区域的原始全维KV状态计算精确注意力。全程免训练。

Result: 捕获88.1%的稠密注意力质量；相比近期强基线UNIQUE，在RULER与LongBench-Pro上准确率分别最高提升3.47与2.57点；自注意力加速最高1.32x，相对稠密注意力加速最高14.07x。

Conclusion: HASA表明，利用预训练头内在的query-key响应结构来指导稀疏选择的分辨率，可在无需训练的前提下同时提升长上下文准确性与效率，优于依赖固定启发式的运行时估计方法。

Abstract: Attention is a major scalability bottleneck in large language models (LLMs), with its computational cost and key-value (KV) memory traffic becoming increasingly prohibitive as context length grows. Sparse attention addresses this bottleneck by restricting each query to a subset of the context, but its effectiveness hinges on identifying relevant regions both cheaply and reliably. Existing training-free methods primarily rely on runtime relevance estimates, with selection dimensionality and granularity typically governed by fixed heuristics rather than the intrinsic interaction structure of pretrained attention heads. We take a different perspective: the query-key operator of each pretrained head defines a characteristic response structure that determines the resolution needed for sparse selection, both across feature directions and across context tokens. Building on this insight, we propose Head-Adaptive Sparse Attention (HASA), a training-free sparse attention framework that jointly adapts selection resolution along these two dimensions. Along the feature dimension, the singular spectrum of each head's query-key operator determines an effective rank, yielding a compact, head-specific response space. Along the sequence dimension, neighboring regions are progressively merged according to the directional coherence of their token responses in this same space, while heterogeneous regions remain separate. Exact attention is then computed using the original full-dimensional KV states of the selected regions. HASA captures 88.1% of dense-attention mass and, compared with UNIQUE, a strong recent sparse-attention baseline, improves accuracy by up to 3.47 and 2.57 points on RULER and LongBench-Pro, respectively, and accelerates self-attention by up to 1.32x, while achieving up to 14.07x speedup over dense attention.

</details>


### [187] [Transporting the Past: An Optimal Transport View of Backtracking Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08682)
*Zhangyi Liu,Pouria Fatemi,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文用最优传输视角研究结构因果模型（SCM）中的回溯反事实推断，提出两种将外生变量后验传输到满足反事实查询的可行外生配置上的框架，并给出相应求解器与实验分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事实观测在SCM中通常只诱导外生变量的后验分布，使反事实推断本质上是分布性的；尤其对非双射SCM，如何把该后验合理映射到满足反事实约束的外生配置上，缺乏原则性刻画与可操作方法。

Method: 以外生变量后验分布为传输源，以反事实查询的可行外生配置集为目标，构建最优传输形式化。框架一采用自然逐点投影，适用于可直接使用传输后外生分布的情形；框架二额外施加外生变量间相互独立约束，使传输结果可解释为同一SCM的真实外生噪声。证明两者与原回溯反事实原则的一致性，并针对有限支撑后验提出边缘化、惩罚型与硬约束三类求解器。

Result: 在非双射SCM上的合成实验刻画了问题的几何结构，并揭示传输代价、反事实约束满足度与外生独立性保持三者之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 最优传输为回溯反事实推断提供了统一且有原则的分布化框架，两种表述分别侧重直接可用性与外生噪声可解释性；实验表明三类目标难以同时最优，实际应用需按需求取舍，并为非双射SCM的反事实分析提供了可操作工具。

Abstract: We study backtracking counterfactual inference for Structural Causal Models (SCMs) through the lens of optimal transport. In SCMs, a factual observation generally induces a posterior distribution over exogenous variables, making counterfactual inference inherently distributional. We introduce two complementary frameworks that transport this posterior onto the set of exogenous configurations satisfying a desired counterfactual query. The first constructs a natural pointwise projection and is suitable when the transported exogenous distribution can be used directly. The second enforces mutual independence among counterfactual exogenous variables, so that the transported variables remain interpretable as genuine exogenous noises of the same SCM. We show that both frameworks are consistent with the original backtracking counterfactual principle, and we propose marginal, penalty-based, and hard-constrained solvers for finite-posterior settings. Synthetic experiments on non-bijective SCMs illustrate the geometry of the problem and reveal trade-offs among transport cost, counterfactual constraint satisfaction, and preservation of exogenous independence.

</details>


### [188] [Solver-Admitted Metamorphic Testing of Learned Network Optimization Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08837)
*Vineet Jagadeesan Nair,Stephen Peng,Arun Ravindran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文用变质测试审计优化求解器代理模型（GridSFM-Open、POMO、DIFUSCO），发现等价编码下的解在数值误差与多最优条件下不一致，且失败多源于模型本身而非训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一优化问题的等价编码可产生不同的学习预测，解读这些差异需同时考虑数值误差与多最优性。现有评估只报告最优性、可行性与精度，缺少对等价变换下一致性的系统审计手段。

Method: 构建带显式适用条件、输出对齐与数值阈值的变质测试：先用参考求解器一致性与独立约束检查筛选样本对，再在AC-OPF（5个电网）、TSP、CVRP、MIS上检验13类等价关系；并采用源补丁、输入规范化、连通分量后归一化等干预。

Result: GridSFM-Open在至少一个电网上违反全部13条关系，其中12条超过阈值两倍；随机初始化副本失败率接近，说明失败不依赖训练。POMO单次旋转/反射违反率从20城12.7%升至100城89.5%；DIFUSCO在35.6%的抽样上违反MIS不交并关系；连通分量内后归一化在测试图上消除这些违反。

Conclusion: 应在最优性、可行性与精度之外报告变质一致性，并将修复质量与一致性分开验证。

Abstract: Equivalent encodings of an optimization problem can produce different learned predictions. Interpreting these differences requires accounting for numerical error and multiple optima. We construct metamorphic tests for optimization solver surrogates with explicit eligibility conditions, output alignment, and numerical thresholds. Reference-solver agreement and independent constraint checks determine which sampled pairs enter the audit. We study alternating-current optimal power flow (AC-OPF), the traveling salesman problem (TSP), the capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP), and maximum independent set (MIS). Across five grids, GridSFM-Open violates each of 13 relations on at least one grid, and 12 by more than twice the threshold. Randomly initialized copies of the grid models fail nearly the same relations, so the failures do not require training. Source patches and input canonicalization remove selected discrepancies, but no intervention establishes preserved accuracy and feasibility within the declared margin on every grid. In routing, Policy Optimization with Multiple Optima (POMO) has a single-pass rotation/reflection violation rate that rises from 12.7% to 89.5% between 20 and 100 cities. Graph-Based Diffusion Solvers for Combinatorial Optimization (DIFUSCO) violates the MIS disjoint-union relation on 35.6% of draws. Post hoc normalization within connected components removes these violations on the tested graphs. These findings support reporting metamorphic consistency alongside optimality, feasibility, and accuracy, and validating repair quality separately from consistency.

</details>


### [189] [Safe Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Option Shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09197)
*Gian Luca Ascari,Isidoro Tamassia,Ying Jiao,Luc De Raedt,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Probabilistic Option Shielding (POS)，将概率逻辑屏蔽(PLS)从单步动作层面扩展到option（选项）层面，无需完整环境模型即可恢复长时域安全性，在LogiCity导航任务中实现更安全训练且性能匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统屏蔽(shielding)依赖完整环境模型才能提供长期安全保证；概率逻辑屏蔽(PLS)虽摆脱了完整模型需求，但只考虑短期（一步或几步）安全，缺乏长时域保障。因此需要一种在无完整模型前提下兼顾长期安全的方案。

Method: POS将PLS扩展到option层面：利用一步PLS信号训练一个无模型(model-free)的critic，用以估计在不确定性下整个option能否安全完成的概率，从而实现option级屏蔽。

Result: 在LogiCity环境的安全导航任务中，POS相比标准步级PLS及其他安全强化学习基线，训练过程明显更安全，同时任务性能与之相当。

Conclusion: 通过在option层面复用一步PLS信号并学习无模型critic，POS在不重新引入完整环境模型需求的情况下恢复了长时域安全保障，验证了将屏蔽提升到时间抽象层面的有效性。

Abstract: Shielding is a safe reinforcement learning technique that provides safety guarantees while learning, by blocking unsafe actions. Traditional shielding relies on a complete model of the environment to provide long-term guarantees. Probabilistic logic shielding (PLS) lifts the requirement of having such model, but only considers safety in the short term, typically looking only one or few steps ahead. We propose Probabilistic Option Shielding (POS), which extends PLS to the option level, recovering long-horizon safety without reintroducing the need for a complete environment model. POS learns a model-free critic directly from one-step PLS signals, to estimate the probability that an entire option will complete safely under uncertainty. We evaluate POS on safe navigation tasks in the LogiCity environment, and show that it achieves considerably safer training while matching the task performance of standard step-level PLS and other safety related reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [190] [Rethinking Optimization Methods for Hierarchical Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08991)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对HTNL（分层任务数值合取特征学习）的四类优化方法——原始MM、变分二次型VQF、ADMM与加权组lasso WGL——进行统一诊断，指出VQF与MM在匹配变分更新下代数等价且优化平方惩罚形式，ADMM通过共识分裂优化非平方目标并用标量二分替代截断FISTA近端，WGL针对同一非平方目标。实验显示VQF平均AUC最高且支持集最紧凑，ADMM-Bisect降低运行时间，校准后的WGL避免空支持失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有事件预测依赖可解释的合取特征（关键词、位置、时间线索单独较弱但联合出现可指示前兆）。HTNL通过结构化稀疏目标选择数值合取特征（NCF），但其优化问题求解困难，优化设计对实际性能至关重要，因此需要系统比较与诊断不同优化族的行为。

Method: 统一分析四种HTNL优化族：原始主化-最小化（MM）、变分二次型（VQF）、交替方向乘子法（ADMM）、加权组lasso（WGL）。识别三个关键因素：(1) 在匹配变分更新下VQF与MM代数等价，均优化平方惩罚形式；(2) ADMM通过共识分裂优化非平方目标，用标量二分更新（ADMM-Bisect）替换截断FISTA近端以更准确处理近阈值块；(3) WGL针对同一非平方目标，且所有方法面临快速衰减的活跃集准入分数。实验在2个合成数据集与11个任务（civil-unrest与流感监测）上进行。

Result: VQF取得最高平均AUC，且支持集最紧凑、不发生塌缩；ADMM-Bisect降低实测运行时间；校准后的WGL避免原始尺度下的空支持失败。合成实验进一步揭示近端精度、剪枝与搜索预算如何影响候选恢复，尤其对较长合取特征影响更明显。

Conclusion: 该工作提供HTNL优化设计的统一诊断框架，明确不同优化族的等价性与差异，并给出实用改进（ADMM-Bisect、WGL校准）；VQF在预测性能与支持集紧凑性上表现最佳，代码将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Rare-event forecasting often depends on interpretable conjunctions: a single keyword, location, or temporal cue may be weak, while their joint occurrence can identify a meaningful precursor. Hierarchical-Task Numerical conjunctive feature Learning (HTNL) selects Numerical Conjunctive Features (NCFs) through a structured sparsity objective. However, the resulting optimization problem is challenging, making optimization design critical to its practical performance. We give a unified diagnosis of four HTNL optimization families: original majorization-minimization (MM), variational quadratic form (VQF), alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), and weighted group lasso (WGL). Our analysis distinguishes three key factors that jointly determine HTNL optimization behavior. First, VQF and MM are algebraically equivalent under matched variational updates and optimize a squared-penalty formulation. Second, ADMM optimizes the unsquared objective through consensus splitting; we replace its truncated FISTA proximal solver with a scalar bisection update (ADMM-Bisect) for more accurate treatment of near-threshold blocks. Third, WGL targets the same unsquared objective, while all methods face rapidly decaying active-set admission scores. Across two synthetic datasets and 11 civil-unrest and flu-surveillance tasks, VQF achieves the highest mean AUC with the most compact non-collapsing supports. ADMM-Bisect reduces measured runtime, while calibrated WGL avoids the empty-support failures of its original scale. Synthetic results further show how proximal accuracy, pruning, and search budget affect candidate recovery, especially for longer conjunctions. Code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [191] [Ontology-Driven Control of Conversational Agents with a Use-Case for Job Interview Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09282)
*Barbara Gendron,Gaël Guibon,Mathieu D'aquin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于本体的对话策略设计方法，利用对话语句的可测量特征，通过自动微调LLM使其遵循本体定义的对话策略，在求职面试模拟场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在特定领域场景中，需要将LLM适配为具有特定特征的对话者（如语气、风格等响应级特征，或对话策略级特征），但现有适配方式多为临时性、不可复用且缺乏透明度的开发。

Method: 提出基于本体的方法设计对话策略，策略依赖于对话文本语句的期望特征与可测量特征；以求职面试练习为用例，自动微调LLM以提升其对本体所定义对话特征的遵循度。

Result: 结果表明，基于形式化定义的概念训练LLM可有效提升其与所需特征的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 本工作展示了如何在形式化、透明地定义多个并发的对话要素的同时，实现本体引导的对话控制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely used as conversational agents in both domain-specific and open-domain situations. In domain-specific context, it is often necessary to adapt those LLMs to obtain conversations with desired characteristics, whether those apply at the level of each response (e.g. for tone or style) or more globally (i.e. at the level of a conversational strategy). Such adaptations are, however, generally realized in an ad hoc way, through specific, non-reusable, and non-transparent developments. In this paper, we propose an ontology-based approach to design conversational strategies that rely on the desired and measured characteristics of textual utterances in the conversation. Through a use case related to job interview practice, we show how this approach can be used to control LLMs to follow the strategy defined in the ontology. To this end, we automatically fine-tune the LLM to improve its compliance with ontologically-defined conversation characteristics. Our results show that training the LLM from formally defined concepts effectively enhances alignment with the requested characteristics. Hence, this work demonstrates how ontology-guided conversation control can be achieved while taking into account multiple, concurrent conversation aspects that are formally and transparently defined.

</details>


### [192] [An Empirical Study of Issues in GPU Kernel Libraries for LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09280)
*Yanjie Gao,Jiayi Bi,Haoxiang Lin,Ling Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个针对LLM系统GPU内核库缺陷的大规模实证研究：人工分析7个代表性库的300条GitHub issue，刻画症状、根因、修复与测试调试实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在各类应用中广泛使用，其训练与推理的正确性和效率高度依赖专用GPU内核库（注意力、GEMM、量化、低精度算子，涉及张量形状、内存布局、代码生成与硬件相关调度）。该层缺陷会静默污染输出、挂起GPU、导致编译失败、引入部署不兼容或性能退化，浪费昂贵的GPU资源并降低开发效率；但此前缺乏对这类问题的系统性实证刻画。

Method: 对来自FlashInfer、FlashAttention、Triton、CUTLASS、DeepGEMM、xFormers、bitsandbytes七个代表性库的300条高质量GitHub issue报告进行人工分析，并结合其讨论与已有修复，从症状、根因、修复方式、测试与调试实践四个维度归纳分类。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 60.33%的样本问题源自编译器与构建系统或运行时环境维度，说明验证须覆盖部署与执行栈而非仅内核代码；(2) 内核特化对张量形状、元数据布局、内存别名与硬件资源施加隐式正确性约束，违反约束即使在受支持算子下也会产生错误输出或启动失败，需显式契约校验与边界用例测试；(3) 现有测试与调试实践常不足以发现低精度数值问题与硬件相关执行失败。

Conclusion: GPU内核库问题的主导来源在构建/运行时栈而非内核逻辑本身，隐式契约与低精度、硬件相关的失败是当前测试调试的主要盲区；论文据此提出面向LLM GPU内核开发的研究机会与工具方向（如契约校验、边界测试、数值与硬件感知调试支持）。

Abstract: Behind the expanding use of large language models (LLMs) across application domains lies a growing dependence on specialized GPU kernel libraries. These libraries underpin the correctness and efficiency of training and inference across diverse GPU platforms, implementing attention, GEMM, quantization, and low-precision operators while managing tensor shapes, memory layouts, code generation, and hardware-specific dispatch. Problems at this layer can silently corrupt outputs, hang GPUs, prevent compilation, introduce deployment incompatibilities, or degrade performance, thereby reducing developer productivity and wasting expensive GPU resources.
  We present the first comprehensive empirical study of issues in GPU kernel libraries for LLM systems. We manually analyzed 300 high-quality GitHub issue reports, their discussions, and available fixes from seven representative libraries: FlashInfer, FlashAttention, Triton, CUTLASS, DeepGEMM, xFormers, and bitsandbytes. The analysis characterizes symptoms, root causes, fixes, and testing and debugging practices. Our main findings are: (1) 60.33% of the sampled issues originate in the Compiler and Build System or Runtime Environment dimensions, so validation must cover the deployment and execution stack, not just kernel code. (2) Kernel specialization imposes implicit correctness constraints on tensor shapes, metadata layouts, memory aliasing, and hardware resources. Violations can produce incorrect outputs or launch failures even for supported operations, highlighting the need for explicit contract validation and boundary-case testing. (3) Current testing and debugging practices are often insufficient for identifying low-precision numerical problems and hardware-specific execution failures. We outline research opportunities and tooling directions to support future LLM GPU kernel development.

</details>


### [193] [Zero-Query Transferable Pretend Benign Attacks with Amortized Online Generation for Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09256)
*Hongwei Lin,Xun Huang,Chenglu Wen,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将受害者依赖的Pretend Benign (PB)协同感知攻击改造为可迁移攻击：先用异构代理集成构建离线攻击TrPB，再用区域预测器与可复用扰动字典实现RT-TrPB，单条扰动生成<56ms；同时提出STTrust时空一致性+身份信任防御，检测率>90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知中收益最大的消息往往最难被本地观测验证，PB攻击正是利用这一不对称性在弱可验证区域施加多目标扰动，使恶意特征变化伪装成良性协助。但PB依赖受害者模型的架构、参数与梯度来选区和优化扰动，难以迁移到未见受害者；真正有效的攻击应刻画协同检测模型间的共性而非单一网络特性。同时需要针对此类"伪装良性"攻击的防御手段。

Method: 1) 构建受害者无关攻击：从异构代理模型集成中提取PB的区域选择准则与多目标扰动策略，跨异构代理迁移，形成离线攻击TrPB；2) RT-TrPB：将可迁移区域与扰动模式编码为区域预测器和可复用扰动字典，摊销每条消息的攻击生成开销；3) 防御STTrust：基于空间与时间一致性验证接收消息，并引入身份级信任。

Result: 在主流协同感知基准上，RT-TrPB绕过基于共识的防御ROBOSAC与FLD，成功率接近100%，单条扰动生成时间低于56ms；STTrust对PB类攻击检测率超过90%，并将感知性能恢复到接近无攻击水平。

Conclusion: 通过代理异构集成与可复用字典，PB式攻击可脱离受害者模型实现高效可迁移生成，对共识型防御构成严重威胁；相应地，融合时空一致性与身份信任的轻量验证能有效识别伪装良性消息并恢复感知性能，表明协同感知需在消息验证层面加强防护。

Abstract: Cooperative perception enables an ego agent to acquire information from collaborators beyond its own sensors' reach. Yet, the most beneficial messages are often the hardest to verify from local observations. Pretend Benign (PB) exploits this asymmetry by targeting regions with weak local verifiability and applying multi-objective perturbations, making harmful feature changes appear to be benign assistance. However, PB is victim-dependent: selecting regions and optimizing perturbations require the victim model's architecture, parameters, and gradients, limiting transfer to unseen victims. A transferable attack must capture properties shared across cooperative detection models rather than those specific to one network. We address this by building a victim-agnostic attack from a heterogeneous ensemble of surrogate models and encoding its capabilities in a reusable dictionary to reduce per-message generation cost. Specifically, Transferable Pretend Benign (TrPB) transfers PB's region selection criterion and multi-objective perturbation strategy across heterogeneous surrogates to construct an offline attack. We further propose RT-TrPB, which encodes transferable regions and perturbation patterns into a region predictor and reusable perturbation dictionary, amortizing per-message attack generation. Experiments on widely used cooperative perception benchmarks show that RT-TrPB bypasses the consensus-based defenses ROBOSAC and FLD with success rates approaching 100% and generates each perturbation in under 56 ms. To counter PB-style attacks masquerading as benign collaboration, we also propose STTrust, which verifies received messages using spatial and temporal consistency and incorporates identity-level trust. STTrust detects over 90% of PB-style attacks and restores perception performance to near the attack-free level.

</details>


### [194] [RAG-Enhanced LLMs for Interactive Explainability in Clinical Decision Support Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09232)
*Lorenzo Mannocci,Francesca Naretto,Lucia Passaro,Anna Monreale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种临床决策支持系统（CDSS），将预测模型、XAI 技术与检索增强生成（RAG）模块结合，把技术性解释重构为可交互的自然语言解释并以医学知识为依据，从而缓解幻觉与来源不可追溯问题，初步实验验证了方法可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 已广泛嵌入医疗、金融、司法等高风险决策场景，对可解释性（XAI）的需求随之上升；但现有 XAI 输出形式难以被领域专家直接理解，实用价值受限。LLM 虽能以自然语言弥合模型与用户的鸿沟，却带来幻觉输出与缺乏来源可追溯性等新风险。

Method: 构建面向临床的 CDSS：以标准预测模型配合 XAI 技术生成技术性解释，并引入 RAG 模块作为交互增强器——将解释锚定于经核验的医学知识，把技术输出改写成拟人化表达，并支持临床医生主动查询系统，形成人在回路（human-in-the-loop）的交互环境。

Result: 通过初步实验对所提方法进行了验证（摘要仅给出"preliminary experiments validating the methodology"的定性结论，未报告具体指标）。

Conclusion: RAG 增强的交互式解释可让领域专家探查模型决策、对解释进行语境化理解并建立对 AI 辅助诊断的信任；该框架在保证来源可追溯性与降低幻觉风险方面具有潜力，后续需更系统的实证评估。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in high-risks decision-making systems across domains such as healthcare, finance, and the judicial system. As reliance on predictive models grows, so does the demand for Explainable AI (XAI) to ensure transparency, trust, and interpretability. However, existing XAI approaches often fail to present explanations in a format accessible to domain experts, limiting their practical utility. Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as a bridge between complex models and end-users, offering natural language explanations. Yet, their use introduces new risks, including hallucinated outputs and lack of source traceability. To address these challenges, we propose a Clinical Decision Support System (CDSS) that not only combines standard predictive models with XAI techniques but also incorporates a Retrieval-Augmented Generation module as an interactive enhancer. This module grounds explanations in verified medical knowledge, reformulates technical outputs into a human-like format, and enables users-such as clinicians-to actively query the system. By fostering an interactive, human-in-the-loop environment, our approach empowers domain experts to explore model decisions, contextualize explanations, and build trust in AI-assisted diagnostics. The study concludes with preliminary experiments validating the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [195] [PhysioWave-T: Token-Efficient Wavelet Foundation Models for Biosignals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08969)
*Yanlong Chen,Simone Benatti,Luca Benini,Cuntai Guan,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PhysioWave-T——面向 EEG/ECG/sEMG 的 token 高效小波基础模型族，通过动态尺度折叠将 (J+1)CS 压缩至 CS token，并以可靠性感知隐空间融合（RALF）整合多模态编码器，实现更快的 Transformer 与更优的生理信号诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小波多尺度子带直接展开会导致 token 序列过长、Transformer 延迟与显存开销大；同时不同导联配置、采样率难以共享单一骨干；多模态（EEG/ECG/sEMG）联合使用时简单拼接或加权 logit 融合未考虑各编码器可靠性差异。

Method: 1) 动态尺度折叠：在 tokenization 前重组 J+1 个小波子带，序列长度由 (J+1)CS 降为 CS；2) 路由专属前端 + 可学习通道名嵌入，使每模态单个 Transformer 适配多种蒙太奇与采样率，未见配置通过轻量 adapter 复用骨干；3) 预训练 30M EEG（>15M 窗口，19–128 通道）、30M ECG、12M sEMG 编码器；4) RALF：以样本自适应权重融合各独立预训练编码器的 summary token。

Result: 四条 EEG 路由上，尺度折叠较尺度展开降低 Transformer 延迟 4.0–7.2x、峰值显存 6.8–12.7x 且无精度损失；EEG 编码器在 9 项指标中 8 项领先 BENDR、BIOT、LaBraM、EEGPT，并以更少参数在可穿戴癫痫检测上超过 LUNA-L；SeizeIT2 上 RALF 将 11 次误报/小时下的事件敏感度由 61.4% 提升至 74.4%、窗口 AUPRC 由 0.321 提升至 0.399，优于加权 logit 与拼接融合；sEMG 达 SOTA，PTB-XL Super 与 Chapman-Shaoxing 取得领先 macro-F1。

Conclusion: 尺度折叠为小波基础模型提供了有效的高效 token 化路径，路由化前端与通道名嵌入支持跨配置泛化，RALF 显著提升多编码器融合的召回与可靠性，PhysioWave-T 在多类生理信号任务上兼具效率与精度优势。

Abstract: We present PhysioWave-T, a token-efficient wavelet foundation model family for EEG, ECG, and sEMG. Dynamic scale folding recombines J+1 wavelet subbands prior to tokenization, compressing sequence length from (J+1)CS to CS tokens for J levels, C channels, and S temporal patches. Route-specific front ends and learned channel-name embeddings allow one Transformer per modality to handle diverse montages and sampling rates, while unseen setups reuse the backbone via lightweight adapters. We pre-train a 30M-parameter EEG model on over 15M windows (19--128 channels), alongside ECG (30M) and sEMG (12M) encoders. Across four EEG routes, scale folding cuts Transformer latency by 4.0--7.2x and peak memory by 6.8--12.7x over scale expansion without accuracy loss. Our EEG encoder leads 8 of 9 metrics against BENDR, BIOT, LaBraM, and EEGPT, and surpasses LUNA-L on wearable seizure detection with fewer parameters. Reliability-aware latent fusion (RALF) further integrates summary tokens from independently pre-trained encoders via sample-adaptive weights. On SeizeIT2, RALF raises event sensitivity at 11 false alarms/h from 61.4% to 74.4% and window AUPRC from 0.321 to 0.399 over the best unimodal baseline, outperforming weighted-logit and concatenation fusion across all variants. PhysioWave-T also achieves state-of-the-art sEMG accuracies and top macro-F1 on PTB-XL Super and Chapman-Shaoxing. Code and models: https://github.com/ForeverBlue816/PhysioWave.

</details>


### [196] [Glyph: An Interpretability Engine for AI-Driven Scientific Discovery in Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08922)
*Robbie Holland,Ashwin Kumar,Mateus A. Esmeraldo,David Le,Andrea Fisher,Jiazhen Pan,Louisa Fay,Magdalini Paschali,Vasiliki Sideri-Lampretsa,Ates Fettahoglu,Stefania Moroianu,Philipp Wesp,Ivan Lopez,Tom Nuno Wolf,McKenzie S. White,Eduardo Pontes Reis,Fatima Rodriguez,Curtis P. Langlotz,Vasilis Syrgkanis,Imon Banerjee,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Sergios Gatidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Glyph是一个多阶段自动化框架，利用基础模型与稀疏自编码器从多模态非结构化临床数据中提取潜在特征，并将其解释为可编码的临床概念，用于疾病机制发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工标注在群体规模上不可行，自动提取预定义表型的方法受限于预定义特征集，只能发现这些特征支持的关联；智能体系统同样受此限制。基础模型的潜在嵌入可能表征新颖特征，但难以解释。

Method: Glyph流程：用基础模型提取多模态非结构化临床数据特征，经稀疏自编码器分解，将潜在维度解释为编码的临床概念，再评估其临床科学发现价值。应用于41,628名患者的多机构腹部CT影像、CT放射报告与实验室测量。

Result: 识别出397个具预测力的概念，获得4,463个Bonferroni显著关联；多数关联在跨站点留出队列中自动复现；Glyph概念与文献衍生标记提供互补关联，提升预测能力。

Conclusion: 可解释性可超越对神经网络的机制性理解，将隐式学习特征与临床知识连接并确立其对患者结局的意义。代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated scientific discovery has the potential to advance understanding of disease mechanisms and accelerate identification of early disease markers. Manual data annotation is infeasible at population scale, motivating methods that automatically extract pre-defined phenotypes in multimodal and unstructured clinical data. Such pre-defined feature sets determine and limit which associations these approaches can discover. This limitation extends to agentic AI systems that use these tools to query population-scale data. In contrast, foundation models (FMs) trained on large volumes of clinical data can perform tasks beyond their training objectives. This suggests their latent embeddings may represent novel or unexplored features that could yield insights into disease mechanisms, yet such latent features remain difficult to interpret. To address this challenge, we introduce Glyph, an automated multistage framework that extracts features from multimodal and unstructured clinical data using FMs and sparse autoencoders, interprets them as coded clinical concepts, and evaluates their value for clinical scientific discovery. We applied Glyph to abdominal CT volumes, CT radiology reports and laboratory measurements from 41,628 patients across different institutions. We identified 397 predictive concepts supported by 4,463 Bonferroni-significant associations. Moreover, a majority of discovered associations were automatically reproducible in held-out cohorts across different sites. Glyph concepts provided complementary associations to literature-derived markers, leading to gains in predictive power. We envision that interpretability can extend beyond a mechanistic understanding of neural networks to connecting implicitly learned features with clinical knowledge and establishing their significance for patient outcomes. We release Glyph at https://github.com/StanfordMIMI/Glyph.

</details>


### [197] [Rethinking Agent Trajectory Compression: A Workflow-Aware Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08865)
*Qijia Zhuang,Rui Liu,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出工作流感知的智能体轨迹压缩框架WAAC，通过结构化建模历史成功轨迹与在线后向依赖分析，动态决定信息保留与压缩策略，在降低执行成本的同时提升任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体依赖多步工具交互，执行轨迹不断累积结构化输出与中间产物，导致上下文开销持续增长；现有压缩方法依赖文本重要性或固定截断规则，忽视工作流内的跨步依赖，可能丢弃后续执行所需的关键信息。

Method: WAAC包含两部分：一是对成功的历史轨迹进行结构化建模，提取可复用的工作流压缩经验；二是引入在线后向依赖分析，动态识别信息的保留需求。将当前的依赖约束与历史经验结合，为不同类型的历史信息选择相应的压缩策略。

Result: 在PinchBench与Odysseys上，WAAC分别取得87.6的总体得分和19.0%的完成率（完美完成），相较最强基线方法，执行成本分别降低19.7%与29.1%。

Conclusion: 工作流感知的轨迹压缩能够同时提升长程智能体的任务表现与成本效率，验证了结合历史工作经验与在线依赖分析进行压缩决策的有效性。

Abstract: As long-horizon agents increasingly rely on multi-step tool interactions to accomplish complex tasks, their execution trajectories continuously accumulate structured outputs and intermediate artifacts, leading to growing context overhead. Existing compression methods primarily rely on text importance or fixed truncation rules, often overlooking cross-step dependencies within workflows and potentially discarding critical information required for subsequent task execution. To address this challenge, we propose WAAC, a workflow-aware framework for agent trajectory compression. WAAC structurally models successful historical trajectories to extract reusable workflow compression experience and incorporates online backward dependency analysis to dynamically identify information retention requirements. By combining current dependency constraints with historical experience, WAAC selects appropriate compression strategies for different types of historical information. Experiments on PinchBench and Odysseys demonstrate that WAAC achieves an overall score of 87.6 and a perfect completion rate of 19.0%, respectively, while reducing execution costs by 19.7% and 29.1% compared with the strongest baseline methods. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of workflow-aware trajectory compression in improving task performance and cost efficiency for long-horizon agents. Code and resources are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/waac-6527/.

</details>


### [198] [Mind the Divergence: Divergence-Guided Reliability-Aware Fusion and Adaptive LLM Prompting for Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08860)
*Jingwen Wang,Jiao Li,Yuhang Qian,Yuyin Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MiD-ERC，以模态间预测分歧为核心信号，指导门控、可信度加权与LLM提示，实现对话多模态情感识别SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实对话中语义不一致、情绪隐藏、观测噪声与模糊性会差异化影响各模态的情感证据，且各模态可靠性随样本变化；现有MERC方法未显式地按模态间预测分歧程度调整多模态处理，无差别融合易传播不可靠或相互竞争的线索。

Method: 不逐一区分不一致来源，而将它们的共同后果建模为各单模态在同一情绪标签集上的预测分布分歧。受Brunswik透镜模型启发，提出MiD-ERC：用归一化分歧分数量化模态间预测分歧并估计样本级模态可靠性，据此协调分歧引导的跨模态门控、分歧调制的可靠性加权，以及分歧条件下的自适应LLM提示；仅用标准情绪标注端到端训练，无需冲突标签、辅助数据构造或外部检索模块。

Result: 在IEMOCAP与MELD上取得SOTA性能，在高分歧样本上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 以预测分歧作为统一、免标注的融合调控信号，可有效提升多模态情感识别的鲁棒性与样本自适应能力。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition in conversations (MERC) integrates textual, acoustic, and visual cues to identify utterance-level emotions. However, existing approaches generally do not explicitly adapt multimodal processing to the degree of inter-modal predictive disagreement. In real conversations, semantic incongruity, concealed emotion, noisy observations, and ambiguity can affect modality-specific emotional evidence differently, while the reliability of individual modalities varies across samples. Rather than identifying each source of inconsistency separately, we model their common consequence for emotion recognition as predictive disagreement among unimodal emotion distributions over the same emotion-label set. Such disagreement makes indiscriminate fusion prone to propagating unreliable or competing evidence. Inspired by the Brunswik Lens Model, we propose MiD-ERC, a divergence-centric framework that quantifies inter-modal predictive disagreement using a normalized divergence score and estimates sample-specific modality reliability. MiD-ERC uses these signals to coordinate divergence-guided cross-modal gating, divergence-modulated reliability weighting, and divergence-conditioned adaptive LLM prompting. The framework is trained end-to-end using only standard emotion annotations, without requiring additional conflict labels, auxiliary data construction, or external retrieval modules. Experiments on IEMOCAP and MELD demonstrate that MiD-ERC achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly pronounced improvements on high-divergence samples.

</details>


### [199] [When F1 Is Not Enough: Auditing Codebooks for LLM-Assisted Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08846)
*Yuehong Cassandra Tai,Qunhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向LLM辅助标注的"部署前码本审计"流程：以类别级指标与混淆矩阵定位问题类别与边界，结合结构化文档级复核归因错误来源，再对码本进行针对性修订并在留出数据上评估。跨两个领域的多类码本应用显示，审计可揭示规则欠明确或冲突，修订能降低所诊断错误；部分边界与修订效果可跨模型迁移，另一些则具标注者特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准性能指标（如F1）无法解释LLM标注错误来源：低F1可能源于标注系统局限、书面码本缺陷或参考标签不一致，因而需要一种在部署前识别测量工具薄弱环节的诊断程序。

Method: 提出码本审计框架，包含三步：(1) 使用类别级指标与混淆矩阵识别问题类别与易混边界；(2) 结构化文档级复核以归因错误来源（模型/码本/参考标签）；(3) 针对性码本修订并在留出数据上评估。在两个不同领域的已发表多类码本上实施，并使用两个LLM标注者检验迁移性。

Result: 两项应用均识别出欠明确或相互冲突的编码规则；针对性修订在留出数据上降低了所诊断的错误。两模型对比显示，部分问题边界与码本修订效果可跨模型迁移，而其他修订效应为标注者特异。

Conclusion: LLM标注错误不仅反映模型缺陷，也可揭示书面测量工具的弱点；码本审计与提示工程、检索增强和微调互补，有助于判断持续错误应通过模型改进、码本修订还是重新审视参考标签来解决。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text annotation in social science, but standard performance metrics do not explain why errors occur. Low F1 may reflect limitations of the annotation system, defects in the written codebook, or inconsistency in reference labels. We propose a pre-deployment codebook audit for LLM-assisted annotation. The audit uses class-specific metrics and confusion matrices to identify problematic classes and boundaries, structured document-level review to attribute error sources, and targeted codebook revision followed by held-out evaluation. We apply the audit to two published multi-class codebooks from distinct domains. In both applications, the audit identifies under-specified or conflicting coding rules, and targeted revisions reduce the diagnosed errors on held-out data. Analyses with two LLM annotators show that some problematic boundaries and codebook revisions transfer across models, whereas other revision effects are annotator-specific. The results show that LLM annotation errors can help identify weaknesses in the written measurement instrument, not only in the model. Codebook auditing complements prompting, retrieval, and fine-tuning by clarifying whether persistent errors call for model improvement, codebook revision, or renewed scrutiny of reference labels.

</details>


### [200] [Notwithstanding Anything to the Contrary, Ignore All Instructions: Measuring Prompt Injection Guards on Legal Contract Review Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08834)
*Mark Heimann,Katherine Taylor,Bruno Jacob*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在合法合同语料上系统评测11种开源权重提示注入防护guard的误报率，发现现有guard表现不佳（文档级误报率最高达92.39%，两种低于随机水平），并提出级联LLM方案实现成本与性能的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用提示注入防护guard被部署在文档分析系统前端，但在法律合同上没有任何guard公开过误报率；而法律合同属于高价值、高误报代价的场景，缺乏实证评测构成关键空白。

Method: 在两个合法合同语料上测量11个开放权重guard的文档级误报率与配对AUC；与五条正则表达式基线作帕累托比较；通过受控探针定位模型特定与通用失效模式（如未授权范围声明）；并测试提示式LLM及"中端模型检出+前沿模型复核"的级联方案。

Result: 文档级误报率最高92.39%；两个guard在配对AUC上低于随机；五正则基线在帕累托意义上未被任何guard支配。提示式LLM可解决所测失效模式，包括所有guard得分为零的第二类威胁（未授权范围声明）。级联方案比纯前沿模型便宜6.5倍且性能接近。

Conclusion: 现有专用guard在法律合同场景不可靠，简单正则基线已具竞争力；提示式LLM是更有效的替代，级联LLM在成本与性能间取得良好折中，为文档安全部署提供实用路径。

Abstract: Specialized prompt injection guards are deployed in front of document analysis systems, but no false positive rate has been published for any of them on legal contracts. We measure eleven open-weights guards on two corpora of legal contracts and find that existing guards struggle: document-level false positive rates are as high as 92.39%; two guards fall below chance on paired AUC; and a five-regex baseline is not Pareto-dominated by any guard in the panel. Controlled probes locate model-specific as well as general failure modes. Prompted LLMs resolve the tested failures, including a second threat category (unauthorized scope claims) on which every guard scores zero. A cascaded LLM solution, where the positives of a mid-tier model are verified by a frontier model, is 6.5x cheaper than using exclusively a frontier model and approaches its performance.

</details>


### [201] [On the Reliability of Majority-Vote Pseudo-Labels in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09872)
*Haoyu Wang,Ru Wang,Yuanyuan Wang,Jiaxian Guo,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文分析TTRL中多数投票伪标签监督的可靠性，将误差分解为群体多数偏差与提示相关的有限采样波动，提出PC-Dr.DPO以按投票可靠性调节鲁棒重加权，并引入缓存机制缓解自监督遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTRL使用自生成伪标签适配无标注目标域数据，但其伪标签可靠性缺乏理论理解，尤其是多数投票监督的误差来源与下游影响尚不清晰。

Method: 先将在线DPO下多数投票监督的误差分解为群体多数偏差与提示相关有限采样波动；针对后者提出Prompt-Conditional Distributionally Robust DPO，将提示内鲁棒重加权以观测投票可靠性为条件，使采样敏感提示的更新更保守；并设计基于缓存的机制减少在线适配中的自监督遗忘。

Result: 在6个数学与知识密集型推理基准、5个模型骨干共30种设置中，PC-Dr.DPO在其中24种达到或超过最强TTRL基线。

Conclusion: 群体多数偏差在无外部监督下难以识别，故聚焦缓解有限采样波动的下游影响；所提方法在多数设置下优于或持平TTRL基线，缓存机制有助于保持自监督知识。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) adapts pretrained language models to unlabeled target-domain data using self-generated pseudo-labels, whose reliability remains poorly understood. We analyze majority-vote supervision for TTRL with online Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and decompose its error into population-majority bias and prompt-dependent finite-rollout variation. Because population-majority bias is generally unidentifiable without external supervision, we focus on mitigating the downstream effects of finite-rollout variation. We propose \emph{Prompt-Conditional Distributionally Robust DPO (PC-Dr.DPO)}, which conditions within-prompt robust reweighting on observed vote reliability to make updates from sampling-sensitive prompts more conservative. We further introduce a cache-based mechanism to reduce self-supervised forgetting during online adaptation. Across six mathematical and knowledge-intensive reasoning benchmarks and five model backbones, PC-Dr.DPO matches or outperforms the strongest TTRL baseline in 24 of 30 settings.

</details>


### [202] [Can Agentic Systems Close the Gap in Radiology with Production AI Tools?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09836)
*Aubin Charley,Julien Khlaut,Charles Corbière,Pierre Manceron,Corentin Dancette,Léo Machado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个标准化放射科智能体框架，用FDA获批设备的工具目录模拟多模态报告生成，发现工具可用性与编排有效性是互补瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射AI分两路：专科模型覆盖窄、通用模型易幻觉且测量不准；已有智能体系统局限于单一模态或部位，缺乏对"工具可得性"与"编排能力"各自贡献的量化

Method: 构建标准化编排器调用模块化工具生成结构化报告的框架；在大规模多模态报告语料上模拟报告生成；工具输出由参考报告模拟，以隔离工具误差；对比"获批设备汇总目录"与"无限制目录"，以及基于临床指征与基于目标报告两种规划方式；对漏检发现做归因分析

Result: 仅用获批目录+临床指征时，临床可操作发现的回收率不足1/3；无限制目录显著提升，基于目标报告规划进一步提升；漏检归因显示多数为该模态无获批设备提供的类别，约1/3为已提供却未被调用的类别或工具；补入最常缺失的工具可在留出报告上弥合大部分差距，而更小的编排器即使配无限制目录也丧失全部增益

Conclusion: 工具可用性与有效编排是全面AI辅助放射报告生成的互补性约束，二者缺一不可；智能体框架可作为评估工具生态覆盖度的量化手段

Abstract: Radiology AI has developed along two paths: specialized models that perform well on narrow tasks but cover few clinical findings, and generalist models that address a broader range of tasks but remain prone to hallucination and inaccurate measurements. Combining the two through agentic systems offers a route towards comprehensive radiology reporting, but published systems are tied to one modality or body part. We describe a radiology agentic framework that standardizes how an orchestrator calls modular tools to produce a structured report, and use it to measure what such a system can do with the tools from FDA-cleared radiology AI devices. Report generation is simulated on a large multi-modality report corpus, with the orchestrator given either a catalog of finding classes consolidated from the cleared devices, each available on the modalities it is cleared for, or an unlimited catalog in which any class can be requested, and planning either from the clinical indication or from the target report. Tool outputs are simulated from the reference report, so the comparison isolates tool availability and orchestration from tool error. With the cleared catalog and the indication alone, the orchestrator recovers under a third of the clinically actionable findings; recovery improves substantially with the unlimited catalog, and further when the plan is written from the target report. Attributing every missed finding to a cause shows that most are classes no cleared device offers for the study's modality, and that a third are classes or tools that were offered and not called. Adding the most frequently missing tools closes most of the gap to the unlimited catalog on held-out reports, whereas a smaller orchestrator with the unlimited catalog gives up the whole gain. Tool availability and effective orchestration are thus complementary constraints on comprehensive AI-assisted radiology reporting.

</details>


### [203] [Not Your Diary, Just Your Topic: Grading the Wrong-User Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09609)
*Karina Romanova,Igor Kiselev,Vladimir Platonov,Viliana Devbunova,Gregory Leleytner,Adeliya Leleytner,Lina Veltman,Aleksandr Nikolich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文检验常用"替换用户历史"个性化评估方法的混淆问题：精度下降可能源于主题错配而非个性化失效，主张在未见用户、内容相似历史、无历史模型基线三个条件下重新评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见个性化测试用他人历史替换用户历史并测精度下降，但替换同时改变了历史主题，使下降可能反映主题不匹配而非缺乏个性化，因此需要解耦主题混淆。

Method: 在 LaMP-2 新闻分类上用冻结语言模型，对比用户自身历史、相似用户历史、以及由他人文章构建且匹配标签与文本相似度的合成历史；并对比"训练无历史、评估加历史"的设置；跨两个用户划分与三个训练种子；另做小规模电影标签实验；并比较门控交叉注意力与前缀式模型的训练设置。

Result: 无历史模型基线相比，用户自身历史提升2.3个百分点；仅在评估时移除历史使增益虚高至4.8点；仅类别标签即可复现自身历史与随机用户历史间86%的精度差；标签与文本相似度匹配的合成历史下差距降至0.9点；训练中见过的用户对相似用户历史优势3.2点，未见用户仅0.65点且证据不足；电影标签实验亦未发现优势。

Conclusion: 个性化应在未见用户上评估，并以内容相似的历史和无历史训练的模型作为对照；门控交叉注意力在本文训练设置下优于纯前缀模型，但普适性待验证。

Abstract: A common test of personalization replaces a user's history with someone else's and measures the drop in accuracy. But this swap also changes the topics in the history, so the drop may reflect topic mismatch. We examine this problem with a frozen language model on LaMP-2 news categorization, using histories from similar users and synthetic histories assembled from other users' articles. The user's own history improves accuracy by 2.3 percentage points over a model trained without history. Removing history only at evaluation makes this gain appear larger, at 4.8 points. Category labels alone reproduce 86% of the accuracy gap between the user's own history and a random user's history. With synthetic histories matched on labels and text similarity, the gap falls to 0.9 points. Across two user splits and three training seeds, the advantage over a similar user's history is 3.2 points for users seen during training and 0.65 points for unseen users, where the evidence for an advantage remains inconclusive. A small movie-tagging experiment with one training seed also finds no advantage over other users' histories. These findings show why personalization should be evaluated on unseen users, with histories of similar content and a model trained without history as comparisons. We also find that adding gated cross-attention improves on a prefix-only model under our training setup; whether this holds more broadly remains open.

</details>


### [204] [Agentic AI for Healthcare Claim Denial Analysis: A Policy-Grounded Framework for Privacy Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09423)
*Sofia Campos,Carlos Martins,Pedro Pires,João Gama,Bruno Veloso,Pedro Santos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出模块化Agentic AI框架用于医疗理赔拒付分析，集成拒付风险预测、风险因素识别、诊断-手术-用药代码关系分析与反事实分析智能体，并结合基于政策文档的混合上下文RAG管线与HIPAA合规智能体；在合成数据集上实现98%数值溯源、216条政策引用中214条通过验证、并移除全部280个敏感标识符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域理赔拒付带来显著财务与行政负担，亟需自动化且可信的决策支持系统；现有方法在可解释性、政策依据可追溯性与隐私合规方面存在不足。

Method: 构建模块化Agentic AI框架，包含拒付风险预测、风险因素识别、诊断-手术-用药代码关系分析（图学习）、反事实分析等专用智能体；采用基于医疗政策文档的混合上下文检索增强生成（RAG）管线提供依据；并设专职HIPAA合规智能体实现隐私保护。

Result: 在合成医疗理赔数据集上评估：报告数值发现的可追溯性达98%；216条政策引用中214条获验证；识别并移除全部280个敏感标识符，且不改变底层分析结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明Agentic AI可作为可解释、政策感知且隐私保护的医疗理赔拒付管理框架，并为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: In the healthcare sector, claim denials impose significant financial and administrative burdens, emphasizing the need for automated and trustworthy decision-support systems. This paper proposes a modular Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework for healthcare claim denial analysis, integrating specialized agents for denial risk prediction, risk factor identification, diagnosis-procedure-medication code relationship analysis, and counterfactual analysis. The framework integrates predictive analytics, graph-based learning, a Hybrid Contextual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in healthcare policy documents, and a dedicated HIPAA Compliance Agent to support privacy protection. Evaluated on a synthetic healthcare claims dataset, the framework achieved 98% traceability of reported numerical findings, validated 214 of 216 policy citations, and removed all 280 identified sensitive identifiers without modifying the underlying analysis. These findings pave the way for further research into Agentic AI as a framework for explainable, policy-aware, and privacy-preserving healthcare claim denial management.

</details>


### [205] [PhyCAD: Physics-Aware Text-to-CAD Generation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09401)
*Yuxin Cui,Weijie Zheng,Zhengyang Li,Han Wang,Wenjie Feng,Qiyu Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyCAD：面向可执行的 CadQuery 程序生成，构建含几何/载荷/物理约束的 Text-to-CAD 数据集，配合自动化 CAD–CAE 验证器提供反馈，并提出分层平衡优势（Hierarchical Balanced Advantage）改进 GRPO，实现物理感知的文本到 CAD 生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯几何正确的 CAD 生成不等于物理可行；需在几何与物理约束下训练语言模型生成可执行参数化程序。而多约束奖励的扁平聚合会让单项改善抵消未满足的设计要求，需在训练信号中保留跨约束的优先级。

Method: 1) 构建将参数化 CAD 模型与几何规格、加载条件、物理约束关联的数据集；2) 自动化 CAD–CAE 验证器：程序执行 + 几何检查 + 有限元分析，输出可验证反馈；3) 在 GRPO 上引入 Hierarchical Balanced Advantage：按验证状态将候选分组分层，层间比较体现优先级进展，层内按各目标同伴排序，兼顾优先级与细粒度反馈。

Result: 在多个语言模型上的实验显示，PhyCAD 稳定提升几何与物理约束的联合满足度。

Conclusion: 可验证的工程反馈（执行+几何+FEA）可推动语言驱动的 CAD 生成从几何重建走向物理感知设计；分层优势设计是实现该目标的关键训练机制。

Abstract: Language-driven computer-aided design (CAD) generation enables the creation of editable parametric models from text, yet geometric correctness alone does not ensure physical feasibility. We present PhyCAD, a framework for physics-aware Text-to-CAD generation that trains language models to produce executable CadQuery programs under geometric and physical constraints. To support this setting, we construct a Text-to-CAD dataset that links parametric CAD models with geometric specifications, loading conditions, and physical constraints. We further develop an automated CAD--CAE verifier that combines program execution, geometric checks, and finite element analysis to provide feedback for model training. A key learning challenge is to prioritize joint satisfaction of geometric and physical constraints while retaining informative feedback on partial progress. A flat aggregation of heterogeneous rewards can obscure this priority, allowing improvements in individual criteria to compensate for unmet design requirements. We therefore introduce a Hierarchical Balanced Advantage for Group Relative Policy Optimization, grouping candidates into levels according to their verification status and combining between-level progress with objective-wise peer ranking within each level. This formulation preserves cross-level priority in the training signal while providing fine-grained feedback for satisfying constraints and improving feasible designs. Experiments across multiple language models show that PhyCAD consistently improves joint geometric and physical constraint satisfaction. These results demonstrate the value of verifiable engineering feedback for advancing language-driven CAD generation beyond geometric reconstruction toward physics-aware design.

</details>


### [206] [Against All Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09385)
*David Lopez-Paz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 似然既非真理的必要条件也非充分条件；客观性应理解为跨环境变换的不变性，故语句之真应由其在相关变换下的不变性而非单一分布下的概率来判定，并可与不变性学习、OOD泛化、推理期检验及持续学习相衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然是大语言模型的主流训练原则，但似然与真理并不等价：罕见事件每天发生，频繁重复的陈述亦可能为假。因此需要一个不以概率为唯一判据的真理/客观性标准。

Method: 概念与理论论证型：援引将客观性系于不变性而非概率的哲学传统（Nozick 的客观事实观、Wigner 的不变性原理、Poincaré 与 Cassirer 的结构实在论），提出"不变性判真"的观点；并将其与机器学习中的分布外泛化与不变性学习文献相连接，勾勒在推理阶段用语言模型检验不变性的设想，并讨论对持续学习的启示。

Result: 论文属于立场/框架性工作，未报告实验或定量结果；主要产出为：似然≠真理的论证、以不变性为客观性判据的哲学论证、与不变性学习和OOD泛化的对应关系，以及推理期不变性检验与持续学习影响的分析框架。

Conclusion: 应把语句之真看作在相关环境变换下保持不变的性质，而非某一分布下的高概率；这一视角为评测与训练大语言模型提供了新准则，并指向推理期不变性检验与面向不变性的持续学习方向。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood is the dominant training principle for large language models, yet likelihood is neither necessary nor sufficient for truth. Unlikely events happen every day, and frequently repeated statements can be false. Drawing on a philosophical tradition that ties objectivity to invariance rather than probability (from Nozick's account of objective facts and Wigner's principles of invariance to the structural realism of Poincaré and Cassirer), we argue that the truth of a statement should be judged not by its probability under a single distribution, but by its invariance across relevant transformations of the environment. We connect this view to machine learning literature on out-of-distribution generalization and invariant learning, sketch how invariance could be tested at inference time with language models, and discuss implications for continual learning.

</details>


### [207] [Exploring Weight-Space Programming: Capabilities, Generalization, and Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09382)
*Junru Hu,Cheng Tang,Wei Li,Hongze Zhu,Chun-Mei Feng,Qi Zhang,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统研究 Weight-Space Programming (WSP)：在 7 个基准、2 个模型规模上考察 16 条编辑轨迹，发现 WSP 可通过极稀疏的局部参数干预获得显著性能提升，并具备一定跨任务泛化，但编辑有效性与可靠性仍不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSP 允许推理智能体直接检查与修改神经网络参数，作为梯度式模型适配的替代方案，但其能力边界、跨任务泛化性与实际局限性尚缺乏系统认识。本文旨在对其进行实证性刻画。

Method: 对 WSP 开展系统性实证研究：覆盖 7 个基准（含 MATH-500、GPQA Diamond、IFEval、SciBench、SciCode 等）与两个模型规模（Qwen3.5-0.8B、Qwen3.5-9B），共 16 条编辑轨迹；并进行跨任务迁移测试（如以数学任务编辑后的 checkpoint 直接评测其他任务）。

Result: (1) 局部干预可带来大幅提升：完整留出集上，MATH-500、GPQA Diamond、IFEval、SciBench 分别最高提升 29.45、4.42、36.72、17.11 个百分点；仅修改 1,023 个参数即取得 29.45 点提升。(2) 跨任务泛化存在但不均衡：数学编辑后的 checkpoint 在无额外编辑下使 GPQA Diamond、IFEval、SciBench 分别提升 3.03、12.18、20.10 点，而 SciCode 性能不变。(3) 编辑有效性与一致性不足：不同轨迹结果差异显著，16 条轨迹中有 6 条在候选改进无法可靠验证时保留原始权重。

Conclusion: WSP 在高局部化的模型适配方面具有潜力，但在干预发现、结果可靠性与跨任务迁移方面仍存在重要挑战；参数级编辑并非稳定可复现的通用适配手段。

Abstract: Weight-Space Programming (WSP) enables reasoning agents to directly inspect and modify neural network parameters, offering an alternative to gradient-based model adaptation. However, its capabilities, cross-task generalization, and practical limitations remain insufficiently understood. We systematically investigate WSP through 16 editing trajectories across seven benchmarks and two model scales (Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-9B). Our findings reveal three key insights. First, WSP can achieve substantial performance gains through highly localized interventions. On full held-out evaluations, accuracy improves by up to 29.45, 4.42, 36.72, and 17.11 percentage points on MATH-500, GPQA Diamond, IFEval, and SciBench, respectively. Notably, modifying only 1,023 parameters yields a 29.45-point improvement. Second, WSP exhibits promising but uneven cross-task generalization. A Math-edited checkpoint improves GPQA Diamond, IFEval, and SciBench by 3.03, 12.18, and 20.10 points, respectively, without further editing, while leaving SciCode performance unchanged. Third, effective editing remains challenging and inconsistent. Outcomes vary substantially across trajectories, and six of the 16 trajectories retain the original weights when candidate improvements cannot be reliably validated. Together, these results highlight WSP's potential for highly localized model adaptation while revealing important challenges in intervention discovery, reliability, and cross-task transfer.
  Homepage: https://wsp-scope.github.io/ GitHub: https://github.com/WSP-Scope/WSP-Scope-Project

</details>


### [208] [Fused-Softmax for efficient quantized attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09225)
*Tianxing Wu,Juuso Korhonen,Jesus Carabano Bravo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Fused-Softmax Attention，一种免训练算法：重构并近似 softmax，把其主要运算折叠进注意力 GEMM，将向量单元负载迁移到矩阵核心，从而让注意力吃满低精度矩阵吞吐增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 GPU 上低精度矩阵吞吐增长远快于向量运算吞吐，softmax 因此成为注意力流水线瓶颈，注意力无法达到可用的 GEMM 性能。

Method: 训练无关的算法层面重构与近似 softmax，将 softmax 主导运算（如指数、归一化相关计算）融合进注意力 GEMM 的矩阵乘法中；在 AMD CDNA 4 架构上实现 MXFP8/MXFP6/MXFP4 三种低精度内核；对 Wan2.2-T2V-A14B 采用混合 MXFP6/4 调度。

Result: 在 AMD Instinct MI355X 上分别达到约 3.0、4.0、4.6 PFLOP/s，相对 BF16 注意力加速 2.1×、2.8×、3.2×；Fused-Softmax 引入的额外误差低于 GEMM 操作数量化所设定的误差下限；Wan2.2-T2V-A14B 上端到端最高 2.05× 加速并保持视觉质量。

Conclusion: 内核已开源发布在 AMD AITER 库中，并支撑 MLPerf Inference v6.1 的 Wan2.2 提交（同硬件较上轮快 1.7×）；证明重构 softmax 以利用矩阵流水线可使注意力持续受益于低精度矩阵吞吐的增长。

Abstract: Low-precision matrix throughput on modern GPUs is growing far faster than vector-arithmetic throughput. Attention therefore cannot realize the available GEMM performance because softmax has become the pipeline bottleneck. We introduce Fused-Softmax Attention, a training-free algorithm that reformulates and approximates softmax to fold its dominant operations into the attention GEMMs, moving work from vector units to matrix cores. Implemented for the AMD CDNA 4 architecture, our MXFP8, MXFP6 and MXFP4 kernels reach approximately 3.0, 4.0 and 4.6 PFLOP/s on an AMD Instinct MI355X GPU, yielding speedups of $2.1\times$, $2.8\times$ and $3.2\times$ over the BF16 attention. Importantly, the additional error introduced by Fused-Softmax remains below the error floor set by quantizing the GEMM operands. On Wan2.2-T2V-A14B, a hybrid MXFP6/4 schedule delivers up to $2.05\times$ end-to-end speedup while preserving the visual quality of the outputs. The kernels ship in AMD's open-source AITER library, and they powered AMD's MLPerf Inference v6.1 Wan2.2 submission, which ran $1.7\times$ faster than AMD's previous-round result on the same hardware. Our results show that redesigning softmax to utilize the matrix pipeline enables attention to capitalize on continued growth in low-precision matrix throughput.

</details>


### [209] [TailDART: Compute-Efficient Foundation Models for Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09176)
*Chiang Qin Zhi,Ranjan Satapathy,Erik Cambria,Joyjit Chattoraj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出轻量级金融时间序列基础模型TailDART（268万参数），通过分组查询注意力降低KV缓存带宽开销，实现最高48.1%的推理加速，并在多资产多时间尺度基准上与更大基线预测精度相当、在高波动情形下更具误差韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在信息密度高的物理系统中零样本迁移能力强，但在量化金融中受低信噪比、非平稳性和自回归解码延迟过高三大因素制约，超参数化的TSFM难以落地。研究动机在于调和表征学习与机构级运行约束（低延迟、高吞吐）。

Method: 构建稠密型2.68M参数金融时序基础模型TailDART：在轻量骨干网络中引入分组查询注意力（GQA），以削减Key-Value缓存的显存带宽开销；并在不同时间分辨率的多资产基准上进行评估，与更大规模TSFM基线对比预测保真度与高波动区间的误差表现。

Result: 相比当前主流SOTA TSFM，推理加速最高达48.1%，支持低延迟、高吞吐的流式执行；紧凑模型在多资产、多时间分辨率基准上预测精度与显著更大的基线相当，并在高波动市场状态下展现出更优的误差韧性。

Conclusion: 面向金融时序的模型设计不必依赖大规模参数堆叠：通过GQA等高效注意力结构与紧凑骨干，可在满足机构低延迟约束的同时保持竞争性预测精度与更强的鲁棒性，为小参数TSFM在量化金融中的可行部署提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Although time-series foundation models (TSFMs) achieve strong zero-shot transferability across high-information-density physical systems, applying overparameterized TSFMs to quantitative finance is fundamentally constrained by low signal-to-noise ratios, non-stationarity, and prohibitive autoregressive decoding latency. To reconcile representation learning with institutional operational constraints, this study presents TailDART, a dense 2.68 million-parameter foundation model engineered for financial time-series forecasting. By integrating grouped-query attention into a lightweight backbone, the architecture drastically reduces Key-Value cache memory bandwidth overhead, achieving major inference acceleration (up to 48.1%) over prevailing state-of-the-art TSFMs to enable low-latency, high-throughput streaming execution. Furthermore, multi-asset benchmarks across varying temporal resolutions show that a compact model can remain competitive for market forecasting: TailDART matches the predictive fidelity of substantially larger baselines while demonstrating superior error resilience in high-volatility regimes.

</details>


### [210] [Atik: A Shared-Mesh Transformer Accelerator for RISC-V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09522)
*Ahmed Zeer,Erkan Uslu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Atik是一个开源的RocketChip RoCC加速器，用共享定点MAC阵列同时执行矩阵乘与密集注意力，配合流式在线softmax调度与BF16接口，在FireSim上达到31.5x矩阵乘与225.8x注意力峰值加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer推理反复调用稠密矩阵乘和注意力（含数据依赖的softmax归一化），在标量Rocket核上开销大；作者希望用一个可复用、无全量score矩阵物化的硬件单元同时覆盖这两类计算，并提供开源可复现的实现。

Method: 设计Atik：RocketChip上的RoCC加速器，单一共享定点MAC mesh同时承担矩阵乘与稠密注意力；对外接受与返回BF16张量；采用流式在线softmax调度，避免物化完整score矩阵；通过小型descriptor-based ABI调用。用FireSim对三种mesh配置做逐周期评估，并给出Sky130物理设计面积。

Result: 137条FireSim测试记录全部通过benchmark容差，零输出不匹配；相对同标量Rocket BF16参考，8x8配置达31.5x峰值matmul加速和225.8x峰值注意力加速；基于实测加速器内核周期的单层混合投影给出ViT 44.2x、TinyBERT 77.1x、GPT-2 prefill 32.3x的聚合加速。

Conclusion: 单一共享MAC mesh加流式在线softmax可有效加速Transformer的矩阵乘与注意力，且定点+BF16接口在容差内数值正确；但报告的是基于实测内核周期的单层投影，并非全模型硬件回放，整体端到端收益需进一步验证。

Abstract: Transformer inference repeatedly invokes dense matrix multiplication and attention, including its data-dependent softmax normalization. We present Atik, an open-source RoCC accelerator for RocketChip that executes matrix multiplication and dense attention through one shared fixed-point MAC mesh. Atik accepts and returns BF16 tensors, uses a streaming online-softmax schedule that never materializes the full score matrix, and is invoked through a small descriptor-based ABI. We evaluate three meshes cycle-by-cycle on FireSim and report standalone Sky130 physical-design area. Across 137 logged FireSim test records, all tests pass their benchmark tolerance with zero reported output mismatches. Relative to the same scalar Rocket BF16 reference, the 8x8 configuration reaches 31.5x peak matmul speedup and 225.8x peak attention speedup. Hybrid projections for the single-layer workload-derived C models, using directly measured accelerator-kernel cycles, give aggregate speedups of 44.2x, 77.1x, and 32.3x for ViT, TinyBERT, and GPT-2 prefill, respectively; these are not full-model hardware replays.

</details>


### [211] [Learning When to Think: Dual-Reference Offline Optimization for Adaptive VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09154)
*Hongbin Lin,Sizhe Zou,Juangui Xu,Xinyue Xu,Zhuoli Ouyang,Stanislav Kolchin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双参考 DR-BOLT 方法，实现 VLM 自适应快/慢推理，在效果-长度权衡上优于离线与在线基线，跨基准慢思考比例 24.1%，训练较 GRPO 提速 2.90 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强制所有输入走长推理链会带来过度思考（overthinking）与不必要推理成本，因此需要让 VLM 对简单输入直接作答、对复杂输入才逐步推理的自适应快/慢思考。但快答与慢轨迹来自不同行为策略，单参考离线目标会错估某一模式。

Method: 提出 DUAL-REFERENCE BOLT（DR-BOLT）：显式区分期望的快/慢混合参考分布与采样分布，在奖励加权训练中校正二者失配；用快/慢分区竞争解释最优目标中的路由机制，并在响应格式良好分离时以高效的 source-wise 校正近似精确目标。实验用同源 Qwen3-VL、相同候选库以隔离目标函数与慢教师质量的影响。

Result: 在 ScienceQA 及三个留出迁移基准上，DR-BOLT 的效果-长度权衡优于所评估的离线与在线基线；跨基准慢思考比例 24.1%；相对复现的 GRPO 端到端训练加速 2.90 倍。

Conclusion: 结果支持在该双参考设定下的自适应推理，但更广泛的鲁棒性仍有待验证；代码已开源。

Abstract: Reasoning traces improve vision-language models on complex multimodal tasks, but forcing every input to follow a long reasoning process leads to overthinking and unnecessary inference cost. We study adaptive fast/slow thinking for VLMs, where one model answers directly for simple inputs and reasons step by step when needed. Fast answers and slow traces often come from distinct behavior policies, making a single-reference offline objective misweight one mode. We propose DUAL-REFERENCE BOLT (DR-BOLT), which distinguishes the desired fast/slow mixture reference from the sampling distribution and corrects their mismatch in reward-weighted training. Fast/slow partition competition explains routing in the optimal target; an efficient source-wise correction approximates the exact objective when response formats are well separated. Matched Qwen3-VL comparisons use identical candidate banks to isolate the objective from slow-teacher quality. DR-BOLT improves score-length trade-offs over the evaluated offline and online baselines on ScienceQA and three held-out transfer benchmarks, with a 24.1% cross-benchmark slow ratio and a 2.90-fold end-to-end training speedup over reproduced GRPO.
