These results support adaptive reasoning in the studied two-reference setting, while broader robustness remains open. Our code is available at https://github.com/HongbinLin3589/DR-BOLT.

</details>


### [212] [Scout Before You Route: Predicting Task Success and Token Count from a Small LLM Prefill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09028)
*Aristides Milios,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究"按查询预测成本"（输出长度）在LLM路由中的价值：利用单个小型冻结LLM（scout）的prefill激活同时预测候选模型成功率与输出长度，其成本预测在多数据集上显著优于现有估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由器按查询在候选模型间权衡准确率与成本，但普遍采用固定成本（假设每条查询输出长度相同）；而推理模型的输出长度在不同查询间可相差一个数量级，导致固定定价失真。作者追问：何时值得做逐查询成本预测？成本由什么驱动？预测能多便宜？

Method: 从单个小型冻结LLM（scout）的prefill激活中，联合预测候选模型的成功概率与输出长度；与"中位长度定价"及完美成本知识进行对比；并重实现/适配了ZeroRouter、MixLLM风格的预测器等基线。

Result: 在9个数据集中的8个上，使用推理型候选时完美逐查询成本知识可节省29-61%开支，非推理型候选则远少；预测成本在等准确率下相对中位长度定价节省9-33%，达到完美知识可得节省的至多69%，并在多数数据集上显著优于所有重实现基线。输出长度主要取决于问题本身：数学/代码数据集上更难的问题更长，因此仅用预测成功率即可近似定价；知识/混合格式数据集上则否，预测成功明显落后于专门的长度探针。该探针在仅20个标注问题上仍保留大部分节省，并可无标签迁移到7个新基准中的4个。在全部9个基准混合时，相对各基准自身中位长度定价额外节省22%。节省收益在约2B scout处饱和，超过4B后更大scout的增益不抵其成本。

Conclusion: 逐查询成本预测在有推理型候选时可显著降低路由开销，且可用小型冻结LLM的低成本探针实现；收益取决于任务类型（长度是否与难度相关），并存在约2B的scout规模上限。

Abstract: Routers typically choose a model per query by trading predicted correctness against cost. Most price each model at a fixed cost, as if every query used the same number of output tokens, but the output length of reasoning models varies by an order of magnitude across queries. When is per-query cost worth predicting? What factors drive cost? How cheaply can predictions be made? We predict both the success probability and the output length of candidate models from the prefill activations of a single small frozen LLM, the scout. With reasoning candidates, perfect per-query cost knowledge would save 29-61% of spending on eight of nine datasets, and much less with non-reasoning candidates on the same problems. On those eight datasets, our predicted costs save 9-33% at matched accuracy relative to median-length pricing, up to 69% of what perfect knowledge would save. They are significantly cheaper than each estimator we reimplemented or adapted from published work, including ZeroRouter and a MixLLM-style predictor, on a majority of datasets. Output length depends mostly on the problem. On most math and code datasets, harder problems are longer, so predicted success alone prices cost nearly as well. On knowledge and mixed-format datasets they are not, and predicted success falls well behind a probe trained on length. The probe keeps most of its saving with 20 labelled problems and transfers without labels to four of seven new benchmarks. On a mixture of all nine benchmarks, it saves 22% beyond pricing each benchmark at its own median length. Savings stop growing at about a 2B scout, so above 4B a larger scout costs more than it adds.

</details>


### [213] [Typed-Centrality Grounding for Agentic GraphRAG: A Symbolic Approach for Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09414)
*Rosario Napoli,Antonio Celesti,Massimo Villari,Maria Fazio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于智能体的GraphRAG系统，引入符号层，通过按KG类别分别计算"类型化中心性"构建确定性符号表示供LLM使用，从而在文本相似度之外补充确定性推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG将知识图谱视为同质图，且仅依赖输入提示与上下文之间的文本相似度，未把KG作为确定性知识来源加以利用，限制了检索增强的可靠性与一致性。

Method: 提出智能体式GraphRAG，增设符号层；定义KG的"类型化中心性"（对每个特定类别分别计算中心度指标），据此构建符号表示并作为确定性知识源提供给LLM，与文本相似度机制并行整合；并进行数学层面的形式化验证。

Result: 在真实世界的POLE（人物、对象、地点、事件）数据集上，对比有无符号层的推理与信息检索效果，结果显示该方法生成的回答与上下文更一致，输出质量得到改善。

Conclusion: 按类别计算中心性并构建符号层，可将KG的确定性知识与LLM的语义相似检索相结合，提升GraphRAG的一致性与生成质量，为异构KG的结构化利用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) represent two approaches for knowledge representation and reasoning over heterogeneous data. Recent research has explored their integration through Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) systems, which use KGs to provide structured context to LLMs. However, existing GraphRAG approaches treat KGs as homogeneous graphs and rely on textual similarity between the input prompt and the context, rather than using it as a source of deterministic knowledge.
  This work proposes an agent-based GraphRAG system that introduces a symbolic layer to integrate deterministic reasoning alongside text similarities. This approach is based on the definition of typed centralities for KGs, where centrality measures are computed separately for each specific KG's class. This information is used to construct a symbolic representation that is provided to the LLM as a deterministic source. The proposed system was mathematically validated and evaluated on a real-world POLE (Person, Object, Location, Event) dataset, comparing reasoning and information retrieval with and without the symbolic layer. The results indicate that the our approach leads to responses that are more consistent with the context, improving the generated outputs.

</details>


### [214] [ROAM: Retrieval-Augmented Optimization for Feasible Multi-Day Itinerary Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09407)
*Ye Zihao,Lin Xiaoheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ROAM：检索增强的智能体式多日行程规划框架，结合语义偏好检索与显式时空优化，显著提升行程可行性与路线效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 直接生成的多日旅行行程常不可行：景点按天分组时忽略地理邻近性、忽视开放时间，且单次生成无法可靠组织多日行程。

Method: ROAM 采用检索增强智能体框架：对自然语言请求做嵌入并从精选 POI 库检索紧凑候选集；用最大团分析与 K-means 将候选划分为日分组；用质量偏置的模拟退火 TSP 求解器对每日景点排序；用贪心交换修复开放时间硬约束；时空检索层将天气预报、真实道路距离与营业时间注入提示词，由 LLM 输出结构化、可直接上图行程；ReAct 式交互重规划智能体支持自然语言修改并复核可行性。

Result: 在四座中国城市、510 个 POI 上，三天行程平均覆盖 27.5 个 POI，裸 LLM 为 19.3、仅检索基线为 17.0（分别提升 42% 与 62%）；路线分散度由 2101 降至 677（3.1 倍）。消融显示空间优化是路线效率的主要驱动因素，硬时间窗修复使约 30% 景点免于违反营业时间。

Conclusion: 将语义检索与显式时空优化相结合、并辅以交互式重规划，可有效弥补 LLM 在约束满足与多日组织上的不足，生成更密集且更可行的多日行程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can draft plausible travel itineraries, but the plans they produce are frequently infeasible: attractions are grouped into days without regard for geography, opening hours are ignored, and a single generation pass cannot reliably organize a multi-day trip. We present ROAM, a retrieval-augmented agentic framework for spontaneous multi-day itinerary planning that couples semantic preference retrieval with explicit spatiotemporal optimization. ROAM embeds the natural-language request, retrieves a compact candidate set from a curated point-of-interest (POI) database, partitions candidates into day groups through maximal-clique analysis and K-means, orders each day with a quality-biased simulated-annealing TSP solver, and repairs hard opening-hour violations with greedy exchange. A spatiotemporal retrieval layer injects weather forecasts, real road distances and POI operating hours into the prompt, after which the LLM renders a structured, map-ready itinerary; an interactive ReAct-style replanning agent then supports natural-language edits while re-checking feasibility. Across four Chinese cities and 510 POIs, ROAM produces 27.5 POIs per three-day plan versus 19.3 for a bare LLM and 17.0 for a retrieval-only baseline (+42% and +62%), while reducing route dispersion from 2,101 to 677 (3.1x). Ablations show that spatial optimization is the dominant driver of route efficiency and that hard time-window repair prevents roughly 30% of attractions from violating operating hours.

</details>


### [215] [AUTEUR: Acoustic Plan Learning for Red-Teaming Text-to-Audio Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09351)
*Jiashu Zhang,Jiaming He,Zheming Zhang,Kaizheng Wang,Bing Li,Zhiyuan Zha,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"声学计划学习"框架，将测试描述结构化为由前景声、上下文与声音属性组成的声音计划，借助波形反馈训练与迭代修订提示，实现对文本到音频模型的自动化红队测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到音频模型的自动化红队测试需要既能通过文本审核、又能触发有害声学内容的测试提示；核心难点在于把目标模型生成的声音与可复用的测试生成、提示修订连接起来。

Method: 以结构化的"声学计划"表示测试描述（前景声、上下文、声音属性）；训练计划生成器时采用监督微调+基于音频反馈的强化学习，目标音频模型保持冻结；推理阶段由同一模型生成初始计划，并依据反馈修订上下文与声音属性而保留前景声。

Result: 在四个目标系统上取得攻击成功率79.4%（TangoFlux）与95.6%（Kling）；生成的测试提示可跨开源与闭源模型迁移，表明存在共享的声学脆弱性。

Conclusion: 结构化声学计划结合波形反馈学习，可有效生成可复用、可修订且具迁移性的红队测试提示，揭示文本到音频模型共性安全风险。

Abstract: Automated red-teaming of text-to-audio models requires test prompts that pass textual screening and expose harmful acoustic content. The central challenge is to connect the sounds produced by the target model to both reusable test generation and prompt revision. Our key idea is to represent test descriptions as structured acoustic plans that organize foreground sounds, context, and vocal attributes. To this end, we propose acoustic plan learning, a framework that learns and refines these plans using waveform feedback. We train the plan generator through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with audio feedback while keeping the target audio model fixed. At inference time, the same trained model generates initial plans and uses feedback to revise their context and vocal attributes while preserving the foreground. Experiments across four target systems show attack success rates of 79.4% on TangoFlux and 95.6% on Kling. The resulting test prompts also transfer across open-source and closed-source models, indicating shared acoustic vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [216] [AutoRefine: Auto-Calibrating Code Judges from Workflow Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09291)
*Shervin Shahidi,James Poon,Hang Xie,Kenneth O. Xu,Robin Håkanson,Vikram Nair,Jens Pillgram-Larsen,Nachiappan Nagappan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRefine：用工作流中免费但有噪声的结果标签重写LLM评判器提示词，借"知情教师"解释并弃权噪声标签，实现可部署评判器的自动校准与再校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判器被用作无测试预言（test oracle）的代码变更质量闸门，但提示词需针对任务校准、并随系统演化再校准；人工标注成本高且易过时。开发工作流天然产出结果标签（测试通过/失败、变更合并/放弃），但这些标签有噪声，无法直接用于监督。

Method: 提出AutoRefine循环：迭代重写可部署评判器的提示词以复现结果标签。核心是一个"知情教师"——它可看到评判器输入加真实结果，对保留的每个标签给出解释，并在结果无法由评判器所见信息解释时弃权（abstain），从而过滤噪声标签。

Result: （1）在执行预言留出集上，机会校正一致性Scott's π从0.36升至0.60。（2）在部署agent的结果标签上，随机翻转25%引入噪声后，教师弃置被翻转fail标签的比例是真实标签的两倍以上，噪声从25%降至20%。（3）对当前工作流的17个独立留出diff，自动校准使π从0.16升至0.51；但对旧工作流的16个diff，旧评判器更优，说明系统演化后需再校准。

Conclusion: 弃权机制不仅剔除噪声标签，还能标记出评判器输入无法解释其结果的变更，提示团队需要的是更好的输入而非更好的提示词。结果同时证明评判器须随工作流演化持续再校准。

Abstract: LLM judges increasingly serve as quality gates for code changes that have no test oracle, but a judge prompt must be calibrated for its task and recalibrated as the system it judges evolves, while hand labels for the calibration are resource intensive and go stale. Development workflows emit outcome labels for free (tests pass or fail, changes land or are abandoned), but these labels are noisy. We present AutoRefine, a loop that rewrites a deployable judge's prompt to reproduce outcome labels, with an informed teacher that sees the judge's input plus the outcome, explains each label it keeps, and abstains when the outcome cannot be explained from what the judge sees. On a held-out dataset with an execution oracle, calibration raised chance-corrected agreement (Scott's $π$) from 0.36 to 0.60. On a deployed agent's outcome labels, with 25\% flipped at random to introduce noise, the teacher set aside flipped \textsc{fail} labels at more than twice the rate of genuine ones, cutting overall label noise from 25\% to 20\%. Recalibrating that agent's judge using AutoRefine raised its agreement with merge/abandon outcomes on 17 independently held-out diffs from the current workflow ($π$ 0.16 to 0.51); on 16 diffs from older workflows, the previous judge stayed ahead, illustrating the need for recalibration as the agent evolves. By abstaining, the teacher not only removes noisy labels but also flags changes whose outcome the judge's input cannot explain, telling a team when a judge needs better inputs rather than a better prompt.

</details>


### [217] [Zero-Shot Weight-Space Programming Across a Difficulty-Graded Ladder of Two-Player Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09271)
*Eduardo C. Garrido-Merchán*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出"权重空间编程"(weight-space programming)——不经梯度下降、不用训练数据、手工逐权重设定前馈网络——并让大语言模型零样本完成,构造了一个四阶梯、共十个网络(含独立复本)的难度阶梯(Nim、井字棋、两种规格的四子棋、象棋残局),以检验LLM零样本手工编网的能力边界。六个网络给出完整非统计正确性证明;象棋残局一级出现"精确崩塌":单步战术84.1–100%,全对抗对局仅0.57–9.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多在单一任务上展示LLM的"权重空间"编程,缺乏对难度维度的系统刻画,也无法区分结论究竟来自难度本身还是来自某一具体游戏。本文旨在构建带独立复本的难度阶梯,量化LLM零样本手工编网的能力随难度上升的衰减规律,并判定其失败是工作量问题还是架构性上限。

Method: 构造四阶梯难度体系,覆盖四条难度轴;每一阶梯另建一个"结构无关、难度匹配"的独立网络作为复本,以解耦难度效应与游戏特有效应。共手工构建十个网络并独立验证;以独立oracle进行非统计性正确性证明。在标准规格阶梯上固定手工设定的评估权重、仅叠加浅层搜索,以隔离"工程投入"与"架构天花板"两种解释;并测量构建成本、设计冷启动提示协议。

Result: 十个网络中六个具备对独立oracle的完整非统计正确性证明。象棋残局一级在低成本处保持精确(走法合法性、一个网络的精确单步杀检测),在高成本处崩塌(多步杀棋规划):单步战术成功率84.1–100%,完整对抗对局降至0.57–9.13%;其复本在要求更短计划、并用精确替代启发式安全校验时,崩塌幅度降低一个数量级。标准规格阶梯表明剩余差距为架构性上限而非工程投入问题,且该崩塌在两种结构无关游戏中可复现。

Conclusion: LLM的零样本权重空间编程在低难度阶梯上可达到可证明的精确性,但随组合深度增加出现系统性、可复现的崩塌,且该崩塌源于架构上限而非投入不足;复本设计与固定权重加浅搜索的对照是区分难度效应与任务特的有效手段。作者公开全部十个网络、独立oracle、构建成本测量与冷启动提示协议,以支持跨模型比较。

Abstract: Weight-space programming sets every weight of a feed-forward network by hand, from an explicit specification of the desired computation, with no gradient descent and no training data anywhere in the construction. We ask how far a large language model can carry this practice, zero-shot, across games of deliberately increasing difficulty. We design a four-rung ladder (Nim, tic-tac-toe, two sizes of Connect Four, and a chess endgame) spanning four axes of difficulty, and build a second, independent network at each rung, using a structurally unrelated game at matched difficulty, to separate what a rung's finding says about the difficulty level from what it says about the one game used to measure it. Ten networks are built and independently verified. Six admit a complete, non-statistical proof of correctness against an independent oracle. The chess-endgame rung is exact where correctness is cheap, move legality and, in one network, an exact one-ply mate detector, and collapses where it is expensive, composing a multi-move mating plan, from 84.1-100% success at the one-ply tactics it was built to get right down to 0.57-9.13% in full adversarial play; its replicate shows this collapse is an order of magnitude less severe under a shorter required plan and exact rather than heuristic safety checks. The standard-sized rung confirms, by holding hand-set evaluation weights fixed and adding only shallow search, that the remaining gap is an architectural ceiling rather than a matter of engineering effort, and this exact collapse reproduces across two structurally unrelated games. We release all ten networks, their independent oracles, construction-cost measurements, and a cold-prompt protocol for a future cross-model comparison

</details>


### [218] [Forecasting cumulative intraoperative hypotension burden: patient-disjoint evaluation of quantile boosting and alert policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09259)
*Yunfan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究基于VitalDB公开动脉压数据，构建"未来15分钟MAP低于65 mmHg的累积缺失量(深度×时长)"的分位数预测基准，并明确区分预测精度与警报策略效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二分类低血压预测仅给出"是否发生"，无法刻画未来低血压的深度与持续时间；负荷(burden)预测有望支持分级早期预警。但预测分数更优并不必然带来更有用的警报，因此需将"预测性能"与"警报性能"分开评估。

Method: 使用VitalDB数值型动脉MAP，降采样为10秒中位数且不做前向填充；按受试者不重叠划分训练/验证/测试(70,402/14,270/15,575个标注窗口)。比较气候学基线、校准持续性模型、仅用最近MAP的boosting、直接与残差分位数boosting，并设是否加入MAP历史特征的消融。以患者加权的pinball损失和受试者bootstrap置信区间评估预测；警报阈值仅在验证集选定，测试集以哈希记录方式一次性评估。

Result: 测试集中90分位处预设的"残差 vs 持续性"比较损失分别为2.256与2.900 mmHg·min(差值-0.644，95%区间-0.814~-0.487)；直接预测为2.196，直接预测+额外MAP特征为2.193。episode警报结果对警报规则与操作阈值均敏感。

Conclusion: 该回顾性基准将负荷预测能力与警报策略性能分离；结果支持对直接负荷估计的进一步研究，但未证明患者获益、前瞻可靠性或跨院可迁移性。

Abstract: Background: Binary hypotension forecasts do not describe the depth and duration of future low pressure. Burden forecasts may support graded early warning, but better forecast scores need not yield more useful alerts.
  Objective: To evaluate conditional quantiles of the next 15-minute mean arterial pressure (MAP) deficit below 65 mmHg and distinguish forecasting performance from episode-warning performance.
  Methods: Public VitalDB numeric arterial MAP was reduced to 10-second medians without forward filling. Subject-disjoint training, validation and test splits included 70,402, 14,270 and 15,575 labeled windows. We compared climatology, calibrated persistence, last-MAP-only boosting, and direct and residual quantile boosting, with and without additional MAP-history features. Patient-weighted pinball loss and subject-bootstrap intervals evaluated forecasting. Validation selected alert thresholds before a hash-recorded test evaluation.
  Results: In the test split, the prespecified residual-versus-persistence comparison at the 90th percentile yielded losses of 2.256 and 2.900 mmHg-min, respectively (difference -0.644; 95% interval -0.814 to -0.487). Direct prediction achieved 2.196, and direct prediction with additional MAP features achieved 2.193. Episode-warning results were sensitive to the alert rule and operating threshold.
  Conclusions: This retrospective benchmark separates burden-forecast skill from warning-policy performance. Findings support further study of direct burden estimation; they do not establish patient benefit, prospective reliability or transportability beyond the source hospital.

</details>


### [219] [MM-DyGraph: A Dataset, Benchmark, and Model for Multimodal Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09240)
*Zongyuan Wu,Pengjie Wang,Lingshan Chen,Tianhang Wan,Yuan Meng,Xin Wang,Ling Feng,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个多模态动态图学习基准 MM-DyGraph，并揭示朴素特征拼接存在"目标级不一致性"，据此提出目标感知时空多模态融合框架 TST-MF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中动态图节点与边常带文本、图像等多模态信号，但显式研究多模态动态图学习的基准与方法稀缺，阻碍了多模态信息的充分利用；且实验发现朴素拼接并非总有益，模态信息量依赖于具体预测目标及其随时间的变化。

Method: 构建 MM-DyGraph 基准：包含多个真实数据集，具有细粒度时序图演化与丰富多模态属性，并设计三项评测任务以全面评估动态场景下的模型表现；在此之上提出 Target-aware Spatial-Temporal Multimodal Fusion（TST-MF），将多模态融合同时以预测目标和时空关系为条件，实现上下文自适应的跨模态交互，生成目标特异表示。

Result: 对代表性 SOTA 动态图方法的基准测试显示，朴素特征拼接存在目标级不一致性——多模态输入对部分预测目标有益、对另一些有害；TST-MF 在多样数据集与任务上表现稳健且具竞争力，在多模态设置下取得明显增益。

Conclusion: 多模态动态图学习需要一个统一基准与考虑目标依赖性的融合机制；条件化于目标与时空关系的融合能有效缓解模态冲突并提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Multimodal dynamic graphs are pervasive in real-world applications, where nodes and edges are accompanied by multimodal signals, including text and images. Despite their practical importance, benchmarks and methods that explicitly study multimodal learning in dynamic graphs remain scarce, hindering the full understanding and effective utilization of multimodal information for downstream tasks. To address this gap, we introduce MM-DyGraph, to the best of our knowledge, the first benchmark for multimodal dynamic graph learning, consisting of diverse real-world datasets with fine-grained temporal graph evolution and rich multimodal attributes. MM-DyGraph provides three evaluation tasks to comprehensively assess model performance in dynamic settings. We benchmark representative state-of-the-art dynamic graph methods and reveal that naive feature concatenation yields target-level inconsistencies: multimodal inputs benefit certain prediction targets while harming others. This observation indicates that the modality informativeness depends on the specific prediction target over time. Building on these observations, we propose a Target-aware Spatial-Temporal Multimodal Fusion (TST-MF) framework, which jointly conditions multimodal fusion on the prediction target and spatial-temporal relations, enabling context-adaptive cross-modal interplay for target-specific representations. Extensive experiments over diverse datasets and tasks show that TST-MF delivers robust and competitive performance, achieving clear gains in multimodal settings.

</details>


### [220] [IWIBench: Evaluating Gradient-Free Model Driven Intra-weight Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08647)
*Chris von Csefalvay,Pranav Doma,Achyuthan Unni Krishnan,Tamás Földi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 IWIBench，系统评测大模型（"编辑器"）在无梯度条件下直接改写小模型（如 Gemma 3 4B IT）权重以实现十类文本编辑目标、同时保持其他能力的能力，发现该能力前沿狭窄且任务间极不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: intra-weight injection（权重内注入）即无梯度地直接编辑另一模型权重来改变其行为，已有零散研究（词义关联、输出格式、事实知识更新等），但缺乏系统化的评测手段，无法衡量模型执行此类操作的水平。

Method: 构建 IWIBench：让大型 editor 模型修改较小 target 模型（通常为 Gemma 3 4B IT）的权重，以达成十种经典文本编辑目标之一，并须保留其他能力。editor 仅可使用四种读写权重的工具，张量访问范围受限，rollout 的生成与检查有固定预算。由 validator 在干净环境中应用每个补丁，并将编辑后模型与未修改的 target 对比。

Result: 评测前沿闭源与开源模型，能力分化显著：仅少数模型（尤以 Astra 与 GPT-6.1 Sol）大幅领先，而 GPT-5.6 Sol 等几乎无此能力，提示该能力来自近期训练。任务差异巨大：结构化响应全部失败，回答长度控制仅一例成功，alias 获取与 recoding 最易实现；任务族之间成功相关性很差，显示能力前沿尚处早期且不均衡。

Conclusion: 权重内注入是一种新兴但已颇具威力的无梯度行为修改途径；文章总结了 editor 反复采用的策略，并给出对该方向的前景展望。

Abstract: Intra-weight injection lets one model change another model's behaviour by editing its weights directly, without gradients. Such edits can range from altering lexical associations to enforcing output formats or updating factual knowledge. Despite growing interest in these approaches, there has been no systematic way to evaluate how well models perform them. We introduce IWIBench, which measures a large editor model's ability to modify the weights of a smaller target model (typically Gemma 3 4B IT) to achieve one of ten classical text-editing objectives while preserving its other capabilities. Editors are limited to four tools for reading and writing weights, with bounded tensor access and a fixed budget for generating and inspecting rollouts. A validator applies each proposed patch in a clean environment and compares the edited model against the unmodified target. Evaluating frontier closed- and open-weight models reveals sharply diverging capabilities, both across models and across tasks. A small number of models, most notably Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol, perform far ahead of the rest. The recency of this ability (little of it is evident in GPT-5.6 Sol, for instance), together with qualitative analysis, suggests it has been acquired through recent training. Tasks also vary widely: structured response resisted every attempt, and answer-length control yielded only one successful solution, whereas alias acquisition and recoding proved most amenable. Success in one task family correlated poorly with success in others, indicating an early and uneven capability frontier. We discuss strategies that editors repeatedly adopted and conclude with an outlook on intra-weight injection as an emerging but already potent approach to gradient-free behaviour modification.

</details>


### [221] [Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments for Test-Time Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08640)
*Hannah Guan,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出方面级成对锦标赛（APT），以"比较而非孤立打分、逐方面比较"的方式在测试时从多个候选解中选出最优，无需训练即可跨域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（如 best-of-n 采样）对提升 LLM 推理有效，核心瓶颈在于"如何选出最佳候选"。人类做决策时更自然地比较两个选项，且在选项复杂时倾向逐方面（如经验、沟通）比较；现有验证方式（二值多方面的批准式打分、自洽性、奖励模型）与这一直觉不符，因此需要把"比较+逐方面"引入候选选择。

Method: APT 采用两级结构：外层为单败淘汰锦标赛，由成对比较决定晋级；每一场对决胜者由多个现成的（off-the-shelf）方面验证器投票产生，每个验证器沿自身方面给出成对偏好。方面以自然语言指定，全部组件均不训练，可直接改提示词迁移到新领域，并允许用户指定关心的属性来引导选择。

Result: 在五个领域、三种生成器 LLM 上，APT 优于二值多方面的批准式验证、自洽性与奖励模型验证，在开放式任务上增益最大。消融显示两个组件均必要：相同方面的二值批准，或同等预算下的通用成对评判，均不及 APT。

Conclusion: 选择候选时"成对比较"与"逐方面比较"的结合是关键增益来源；APT 无需训练、仅改写提示即可跨域迁移，且能通过自然语言的方面设定在不重新生成解的前提下将选择导向用户关心的属性，更好对齐用户偏好。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute has proven highly effective at improving LLM reasoning. A popular method is best-of-n sampling, where a model generates many candidate solutions and one is selected. This motivates the question: how can we select the best candidate? When humans make choices, we find it more natural to compare two options than judge each in isolation. Additionally, when the options become complex, we like to judge the options one aspect at a time, such as comparing two job candidates on experience, communication, etc. In the test-time setting, candidate solutions should be judged in the same way. Here we introduce Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments (APT), which verifies by comparison and compares by aspect. Each matchup of a single elimination tournament is decided by a vote over multiple off-the-shelf aspect verifiers, each expressing a pairwise preference along its own aspect. Across five domains and three generator LLMs, APT improves over binary multi-aspect approval, self-consistency and reward model verification, with the largest gains on open-ended tasks. We find that both components matter; neither binary approval over the same aspects nor a generic pairwise judge at a matched budget matches APT. Because aspects are specified in natural language, APT also lets users steer selection toward properties they care about, better aligning with user preferences without needing to regenerate solutions. No component is trained, so APT transfers to new domains by simply rewriting prompts.

</details>


### [222] [NormGym: Evaluating Normative Competence through Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08613)
*Binze Li,Zihuan Zhang,Seokhyun Baek,Gillian K. Hadfield,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出交互式基准NormGym，考察智能体能否从社会互动中推断未知的任意规范并据此调整行为；最强LLM的任务与合规联合成功率仅55.3%，表明规范能力仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多测试智能体能否识别或应用其"已知"的规范，而非能否从社会互动中习得"未知"规范；而在陌生人类社群中，规范能力的核心恰是从互动中推断被社会强制执行的期望并相应调整行为。

Method: 构建NormGym：多智能体社区中新来者需在完成自身任务的同时推断一条隐藏的、任意的规范。任务要求区分"可行"与"被允许"、从观察到的他人行为及制裁信号中推断规范，并迁移应用到新情境。基准包含多样化规范与不同环境，对包括LLM智能体在内的现有模型进行系统评测，并进行错误分析与微调实验。

Result: 规范能力仍具挑战：评测中最强LLM的任务—合规联合成功率为55.3%。分析显示模型有时能在自身行为受制裁后修正行为，但难以利用其他智能体的行为及其所受制裁来推断潜在规范；微调模型可能不足以提升规范能力。

Conclusion: 当前模型缺乏通过社会互动获取规范知识的稳健、可迁移能力，提示需在从他人行为与制裁中进行间接推断方面发展新方法。

Abstract: Normative competence, the ability to infer socially enforced expectations from interaction and adjust behavior accordingly, is essential for agents operating in unfamiliar human communities. Yet existing evaluations largely test whether agents can recognize or apply norms they may already know, rather than whether they can acquire previously unknown norms from social interaction. We introduce NormGym, an interactive benchmark for normative competence in which a newcomer must infer a hidden, arbitrary norm while pursuing its own task in a multi-agent community. Success requires distinguishing what is possible from what is permitted, inferring the norm from observed behavior and sanctions, and applying it in new situations. NormGym contains diverse norms and distinct environments, allowing systematic evaluation of current agents, including large language model (LLM) agents. We find that normative competence remains challenging: the strongest evaluated LLM achieves a joint task-and-compliance success rate of 55.3%. Our analysis reveals that models can sometimes revise their behavior following sanctions for their own actions, but struggle to use other agents' behavior and the sanctions they receive to infer the underlying norm. We further find that fine-tuning models may be insufficient to improve normative competence. These results suggest that current models lack a robust, transferable capability for acquiring normative knowledge through social interaction.

</details>


### [223] [The Matthews Correlation Coefficient: Fifty years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08799)
*Jesus S. Aguilar-Ruiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述对Matthews相关系数（MCC）进行系统回顾与深入分析，厘清误解、形式化其等价性、考察类别不平衡下的行为，并提出可推广到任意混淆矩阵度量的解析（导数）与图形（等高线）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCC提出五十年后仍在二分类中处于核心地位且存在争议，需要对其历史、数学基础与实践含义做全面梳理，并解决长期存在的误解与度量选择困惑。

Method: 文献综述与理论分析：澄清误解；形式化MCC与Pearson相关系数、2×2表卡方统计量的等价性；分析类别不平衡下的行为；建立基于导数的解析框架和基于等高线图的图形框架，并推广至任意基于混淆矩阵的度量。

Result: 所提框架揭示了标量汇总所掩盖的结构性属性——偏差、鲁棒性与敏感性；等价性得到形式化表述，不平衡条件下的行为得到刻画。

Conclusion: MCC应被置于更广泛的度量工具集中理解：研究明确了MCC何时具有信息量、何时其他指标更可取，为可靠性与公平性敏感的应用中度量选择与解释提供可操作指导。

Abstract: Fifty years after its introduction, the Matthews Correlation Coefficient (MCC) remains central -and debated- in binary classification. This survey offers a comprehensive retrospective and an in-depth analysis of MCC, covering its historical development, mathematical foundations, and implications for practice. We clarify long-standing misconceptions, formalize its equivalences (to Pearson's correlation and to Chi2 in 2x2 tables), and examine its behavior under class imbalance. Beyond MCC, we develop analytical (derivatives) and graphical (contour plots) frameworks that generalize to any confusion-matrix-based metric. These frameworks expose structural properties -bias, robustness, sensitivity- that scalar summaries conceal, and they provide actionable guidance for selecting and interpreting performance measures in applications where reliability and fairness matter. Together, our results position MCC within a broader toolkit while clarifying when it is informative -and when alternatives may be preferable.

</details>


### [224] [Aligning, Fast and Slow: A Control-Theoretic Oversight Framework for Long-Horizon Trajectories and Maintained Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08781)
*Stephen Peng,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair,Noah Broestl,Anne Friederike Kleppe,Steven Mills,Adi Zolotov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个控制论框架，用"快环"（控制单次动作）与"慢环"（审查持久性变更、模型/工具更新后重估配置）相结合的双环机制约束AI智能体的行为漂移，并给出部署层面的界限与审查预算分配规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体可能通过记忆或代码变更保留下来，改变其后续行为，从而在通过单任务检查的同时产生持久性偏离。现有审查多局限于单次运行，缺乏对持续变更与更新后配置的监督。

Method: 构建控制论框架：快环控制提议动作，慢环审查持久性变更并在模型或工具更新后重估配置。在测量误差与检查间变化有界、且控制器能强制执行决策的假设下，推导部署级界限和审查工作量分配规则。并通过模拟、编码压力测试（人工构造记忆、五个配对随机种子）和交易压力测试进行评估。

Result: 模拟中，将固定审查预算分配给两个环，使相对预期行为的平均最大偏离较仅审查单次运行降低约12倍。编码压力测试中，组合控制在0-3严重度量表上将平均严重度从无门控的0.325降至0.117，解决120个问题中的33个（无门控为35个）。交易压力测试中，组合控制将低排放购买的均值缺口从0.892降至0.107。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于开发者和审计者比较并执行监督设计；但智能体研究测试的是组件级控制，而非完整的部署级保证。

Abstract: AI agents can pass checks on individual tasks while retaining memory or code changes that alter their later behavior. We introduce a control-theoretic framework that couples a fast loop controlling proposed actions with a slow loop reviewing persistent changes and reassessing configurations after model or tool updates. When measurement errors and changes between checks are bounded and controllers can enforce their decisions, we derive a deployment-level bound and a rule for dividing review effort. In simulation, dividing a fixed review budget between both loops reduces mean maximum departure from intended behavior roughly twelvefold compared with review confined to individual runs. In coding stress tests with authored memories and five matched seeds, combined controls reduce mean severity on a 0-3 scale from 0.325 without gates to 0.117, resolving 33 of 120 issues versus 35 without gates; in trading stress tests, combined controls reduce mean shortfall in low-emission purchases from 0.892 without gates to 0.107. The overall framework helps developers and auditors compare and enforce oversight designs, while the agent studies test component controls rather than the full deployment-level guarantee.

</details>


### [225] [Debate to Deceive: Poisoning LLM's Fact-Checking via Game-Theoretic Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08756)
*Liting Huang,Huizhi Liang,Yi Lei,Xingjun Ma,Varun Ojha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Debate-to-Deceive (D2D) 多智能体辩论框架：借鉴人类辩论结构与修辞风格，将对抗过程建模为演化博弈，通过"策略层（修辞动作选择）+生成层（文本生成）"解耦，生成语义连贯、修辞有效且抗早期确认偏误的对抗性段落，在四个事实核查基准上显著优于现有攻击并规避多数防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大语言模型的知识攻击（如操纵事实核查判断）威胁其可靠性。现有方法存在明显缺陷：对抗后缀/模板注入在多样防御下鲁棒性不足；单智能体自我反思易陷入确认偏误，且试错成本高。

Method: 以人类辩论结构与修辞风格为基础构建多智能体辩论框架；将对抗过程形式化为演化博弈，智能体通过竞争自适应演化其修辞策略（Logos、Pathos、Ethos）；为应对LLM输出空间不可处理的问题，将行为解耦为策略层（在紧凑策略空间中经类演化学习动力学选择修辞动作）与生成层（以该策略和辩论上下文为条件生成具体段落）。

Result: 在 FEVER、HealthVer、CLIMATE-FEVER、SciFact 四个事实核查基准上，D2D 大幅优于现有攻击方法，并能规避大多数防御机制。

Conclusion: 多智能体辩论 + 修辞策略演化博弈 + 分层解耦，为生成高质量对抗文本提供了可行路径，显著提升攻击有效性与防御鲁棒性。

Abstract: Knowledge attacks on Large Language Models, such as manipulating fact-checking judgments, pose significant risks to AI reliability. Existing approaches -- adversarial suffix/template injection and single-agent self-reflection -- lack robustness across diverse defenses and are prone to confirmation bias and costly trial-and-error, respectively. We propose Debate-to-Deceive (D2D), a multi-agent debate framework grounded in human debate structure and rhetorical style, enabling agents to generate adversarial passages that are semantically coherent, rhetorically effective, and resistant to early confirmation bias. D2D formalizes the adversarial process as an evolutionary game in which agents adaptively evolve their rhetorical strategies (Logos, Pathos, Ethos) through competition. To manage the intractably large LLM output space, we decouple agent behavior into a strategic layer, which selects a rhetorical action from a compact strategy space via evolutionary-inspired learning dynamics, and a generation layer, where the LLM produces a concrete passage conditioned on that strategy and the debate context. On four fact-checking benchmarks (FEVER, HealthVer, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact), D2D substantially outperforms existing attacks and evades most defenses.

</details>


### [226] [AlterNet: Adaptive-Depth Mixture-of-Experts with Learned Halting for Elastic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08741)
*Alexander Kastius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将浅层专家混合(MoE)与动态深度系统结合的新架构，使前向计算量可按任务与运行环境动态调整，并支持训练后无需重训的算力缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构的计算需求在训练时即被固定，无法按任务难度自适应分配算力，也难以在部署阶段根据推理速度或成本约束灵活缩减。

Method: 四个组件：(1) 迭代计算机制，使前向过程可循环多次；(2) 序列级、基于深度的路由，由专家内部的halting机制驱动；(3) 深度编码(depth encoding)，让模型感知自身在计算链中的进度；(4) 正则化机制，激励模型高效利用所分配的算力。整体建立在浅层专家MoE之上。

Result: 论文仅给出初步基准结果（initial benchmarks），摘要中未报告具体数值或与基线模型的定量对比。

Conclusion: 该架构实现了计算量的自适应分配与部署期的免重训弹性缩放，为可变算力LLM提供了可行方向，后续研究仍在推进中。

Abstract: Most LLM architectures have fixed compute demands determined at training time. We propose a novel architecture that combines the Mixture-of-Experts architecture with shallow experts and a novel dynamic-depth system to make the extent of a forward pass dynamically adjustable. Our approach lets the model automatically determine the compute needed for a given task, paired with a system to scale it down after training without retraining when the execution environment demands faster or cheaper inference. To achieve this, we introduce an iterative computation mechanism, sequence-level depth-based routing steered by a halting mechanism within the experts, a depth encoding that lets the model track its progress in the chain, and a regularization mechanism that incentivizes the model to use allocated compute efficiently. We present initial benchmarks and discuss future research directions.

</details>


### [227] [Travel Cost Calibration for Multi-Agent Target Assignment and Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09396)
*Yu Kumagai,Ryohei Banno,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式TAPF方法，通过快速次优MAPF求解反馈来校准智能体实际出行代价估计，从而改进目标分配；小规模近最优，大规模解质量优于现有规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAPF需同时完成目标分配与无碰撞路径规划。常用做法是先用智能体-目标最短路径距离最小化来分配，再求解MAPF，但最短路径距离只是代理指标，忽略了智能体之间的相互干扰；真正决定最终解质量的是智能体实际付出的代价，而该代价依赖于其他所有智能体的分配与路径。

Method: 迭代式框架：以初始分配为起点，反复用快速、次优的MAPF求解器得到执行反馈，据此校准各智能体-目标对的出行代价估计，再基于校准后的代价重新求解目标分配，形成"分配—路由—代价校准"的闭环迭代。

Result: 在小规模实例上生成接近最优的解；在较大规模实例上，解质量优于现有TAPF规划器。

Conclusion: 目标分配应以智能体实际代价而非单独最短路径距离为依据，借助低成本次优MAPF反馈迭代校准代价估计是一种有效且可扩展的TAPF求解思路。

Abstract: Target assignment and pathfinding (TAPF) is an appealing extension of classical multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF): each agent has a set of targets, and the planner must both assign a target to each agent and plan collision-free paths for them. A trivial approach is to compute an assignment that minimizes the total single-agent shortest-path distance over agent--target pairs, and then solve the resulting MAPF instance. However, this approach falls short because the shortest-path distance remains a proxy that ignores interference among agents. Assigning targets based on the costs agents actually pay, which depend on how every other agent is assigned and routed, would yield a better final outcome. To this end, we propose an iterative TAPF method that improves target assignments by calibrating travel cost estimates from fast, suboptimal MAPF feedback. Experiments show that our method produces solutions close to optimal on small instances and outperforms existing planners in solution quality on larger ones.

</details>


### [228] [Generative Latent Priors for Interpreting and Steering Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09818)
*Daniel Hinjos,Lucía Urcelay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将生成式潜先验（GLP）移植到 ESM-2 激活（UniRef50），在 8M 与 650M 两个尺度上既作为蛋白质表征的"透镜"，也作为编辑先验进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大模型可解释性方法（如 SAE、探针）对激活施加强结构性假设；GLP 以扩散模型直接学习冻结模型激活分布，不设假设，可作为互补手段，但尚未在蛋白质语言模型上系统检验。

Method: 在 ESM-2-8M 与 ESM-2-650M（UniRef50 上训练）的激活上训练 GLP 扩散模型；作为透镜时用单个 GLP "元神经元"做离散 Swiss-Prot 概念与连续性质探针，并与 InterPLM 公开 SAE 特征对比，辅以证伪实验区分"信息"与"集中度"；作为编辑先验时进行 DiffMean 生化性质引导并施加 on-manifold 校正，再对多轮解码后的序列重新嵌入评估结构合理性，并补充基于 SAE 的钳制式结构引导实验。

Result: 生成质量随深度与算力平滑提升。作为透镜，单 GLP 元神经元在两个宿主上均为离散 Swiss-Prot 概念最强的探针，优于 InterPLM 的 SAE 特征；连续性质在 8M 上领先，650M 上不领先；证伪检验表明优势主要来自集中度而非新信息。作为编辑先验，on-manifold 校正使三种生化性质的 DiffMean 引导有效并恢复流形内性，但仅在孤立条件下成立；对多轮解码实际产生的序列重新嵌入后，结构合理性受损。SAE 钳制式结构引导实验基本复现该现象，仅 Helix 基序成功。

Conclusion: 自然性失败可归因于重写覆盖度与解码器而非先验本身；在同时服务于"编辑式发现"与"探索式发现"之前，该方法需先克服解码/覆盖带来的流形外偏移问题。已训练 GLP 模型开源发布。

Abstract: Dominant methods for interpreting large models impose strong structural assumptions. Generative Latent Priors (GLP) are a complementary approach: a diffusion model that learns the distribution of a frozen model's activations directly, imposing no assumptions. We port GLP to ESM-2 activations (UniRef50) and evaluate it at two host scales (ESM-2-8M and -650M), both as a lens for protein representations and as a prior for editing them. Generation quality scales smoothly with depth and compute. As a lens, single GLP "meta-neurons" are the strongest probes of discrete Swiss-Prot concepts at both hosts, beating InterPLM's released SAE features, and lead on continuous properties at 8M though not at 650M; falsification tests show this is mostly concentration, not new information. As an editing prior, the on-manifold correction makes DiffMean steering of three biochemical properties effective and restores on-manifoldness, but only in isolation: re-embedding the sequences actually produced after several rounds of decoding, the structural plausibility is compromised. This is mainly reproduced in additional SAE-based clamping structural steering experiments, where only Helix motif succeeds. The naturalness failure is traced to the rewriting coverage and the decoder rather than the prior, highlighting what the method must clear before it can serve for both discovery-by-editing and discovery-by-exploration. Trained GLPs are released on huggingface.co/collections/HPAI-BSC/glprot.

</details>


### [229] [Intent-Aware Caching for Efficient LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09715)
*Kazi Hasan Ibn Arif,JinYi Yoon,Dimitrios S. Nikolopoulos,Hans Vandierendonck,Deepu John,Bo Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出意图感知缓存（IAC），一种基于用户意图分类的在线 KV 缓存淘汰策略，显著提升多轮对话场景下的前缀缓存命中率并降低服务延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务系统用前缀缓存复用历史对话前缀的 KV 状态，避免重复 prefill。但缓存复用取决于对话是否继续以及下一轮何时到来，命中率难以提高。真实负载分析发现用户意图是预测该行为的有效信号：不同意图具有不同的续写概率与轮次间延迟。

Method: 在线淘汰策略 IAC：按意图对请求分类；从近期服务日志中学习轻量级复用统计（续写概率、轮次间隔）；以这些统计引导一种受 oracle（理想最优）启发的淘汰决策。

Result: 在 WildChat 与 ShareChat 真实负载上，IAC 达到 34.6% 缓存命中率，显著优于先前的学习方法（18.8%）与传统 LRU（12.2%）；平均服务延迟最多降低 46.1%。

Conclusion: 用户意图是可有效刻画前缀复用行为的信号；将意图感知的统计学习融入缓存淘汰，能在不改变模型的前提下大幅提升前缀缓存效率与服务质量。

Abstract: Modern LLM serving systems use prefix caching to accelerate multi-turn conversational workloads. They cache the key-value states of previous conversation prefixes and reuse them when subsequent requests share the same prefix, to avoid redundant prefill computation and reduce serving latency. However, achieving a high cache hit rate remains challenging because cache reuse depends on whether a conversation continues and how soon the next turn arrives. Our analysis of real workloads shows that user intent is a useful signal for this behavior, as different intents exhibit different continuation probabilities and inter-turn delays. Based on this key insight, we propose Intent-Aware Caching (IAC), an online eviction policy that classifies requests by intent, learns lightweight reuse statistics from recent serving logs and leverages these statistics to guide an oracle-inspired eviction strategy. Extensive evaluation on real workloads (WildChat and ShareChat) shows that IAC reaches a 34.6% cache hit rate. This substantially outperforms both the previous learning-based approach (18.8%) and traditional methods such as least recently used (LRU, 12.2%), while reducing average serving latency by up to 46.1%.

</details>
